Apotheosis – Ascension to Godhood
Vol.13

Chapter 1201
Even so, Luo Zheng didn't dare to underestimate these four Dragon Taming Arhats.
The power of the flashing dragon scales had reached a terrifying level. If an ordinary warrior of the Divine Sea Realm was hit by this palm, they would be torn apart!
Leaning against the courtyard wall, Luo Zheng's competitive spirit rose in his heart!
Both of them had the power of dragon scales. He wanted to see who was stronger!
The real dragon on the shoulder of the Dragon Taming Arhat was probably something of great origin. Unfortunately, he was in the Illusory Battlefield. Although he could use the power of dragon scales, Qinglong's soul couldn't be projected here, so he couldn't communicate with Qinglong. Similarly, Luo Zheng could use the Sword Spirit, but he couldn't communicate with Xun. Even if he summoned the Sword Spirit, it didn't contain Xun's soul.
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng didn't hesitate at all. The nearest Dragon Taming Arhat had already rushed towards him. The Dragon Taming Arhat suddenly clapped its hands. The real dragon on its shoulder began to flicker with a green light. The power of dragon scales poured into the Buddha statue!
Luo Zheng crossed his hands. The power of dragon scales also poured into his body. He didn't retreat at all. Instead, he clapped his hands.
"Roar!"
In contrast, the Dragon Taming Arhat clapped its hands. Accompanied by a dragon roar, the real dragon statue on its shoulder also raised its head and roared. The power of the Dragon Taming Arhat was stronger.
In the blink of an eye, the four palms collided!
"Boom!"
Accompanied by a loud noise, Luo Zheng's body was pushed back again!
Luo Zheng felt his blood and Qi tumbling in the process of being pushed back. It was very uncomfortable. The power of the Dragon Taming Arhat couldn't be underestimated!
However, even though Luo Zheng was pushed back, so was the Dragon Taming Arhat.
If it were the previous Arhat Buddha statue, it would have been smashed into powder by Luo Zheng's palms.
However, this Dragon Taming Arhat was many times stronger than the previous statues. Luo Zheng could understand this. After all, this Dragon Taming Arhat's body had to bear the power of the dragon scales of the True Dragon Statue. If this body was not built to be extremely strong, it would have collapsed before he could use this power.
Just like Luo Zheng's previous physical body, there was a limit to the amount of dragon scale power he could bear. If he forcibly drew the dragon scale power into his body, his physical body would disintegrate first.
"Boom!"
"Boom!"
Two loud sounds reverberated in the inner courtyard.
It was the sound of Luo Zheng and Arhat Taming Dragon crashing into the wall of the inner courtyard.
The Dragon Taming Arhat crashed into the wall of the inner courtyard. Under the tremendous impact, thick cracks appeared on the surface of its body, as if it was unable to withstand the impact and was about to collapse!
However, a golden Buddha's light was emitted from the cracks. Then, the cracks were repaired at a visible speed. In an instant, the cracks closed up, and the golden Buddha's light naturally disappeared …
This time, Luo Zheng was also confused by the impact. He felt a slight sweetness in his throat, and he couldn't help but spit out a mouthful of blood.
"It can still heal?"
Seeing the Dragon Taming Arhat's reaction, Luo Zheng couldn't help but frown. He wiped the blood from the corner of his mouth, but he didn't have the chance to think too much. This was because the other three Dragon Taming Arhats once again slapped at Luo Zheng. Each of them had a True Dragon coiled on their bodies. At this moment, the dragon scales on the True Dragons' bodies were shining …
If he was really hit by the three palms of these three Dragon Taming Arhats, even if he didn't die, he would be seriously injured!
After the impact, Luo Zheng actually retreated to a corner of the inner courtyard. Luo Zheng clenched his teeth, and his figure once again penetrated through space. In an instant, he passed by the three Dragon Taming Arhats and flashed to another corner!
"This is a bit troublesome." Luo Zheng frowned.
However, it wasn't that Luo Zheng didn't have a way to deal with it. If he couldn't break through the situation with pure strength, he could naturally use other methods!
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng slightly raised his head …
At this moment, in the starry sky of this universe, a total of 300 stars began to shine!
These 300 stars were distributed in different areas. There were stars in the Middle Sky Region, Western Sky Region, Southern Sky Region, Northern Sky Region, and Eastern Sky Region!
Under Luo Zheng's command, they began to emit intense starlight. These starlight were headed straight for the large world where the Illusory Battlefield was located. These 300 stars were one of the results of Luo Zheng's diligent cultivation in the past few years.
In fact, Luo Zheng was confident that he could deal with these four Dragon Taming Arhats by using the Yi Shen Sword.
However, Luo Zheng used the 《Stellar Battle Body》 at this moment to verify his guess …
When the starlight began to condense around Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng already had an answer in his heart.
"Sure enough, this Illusory Battlefield isn't a real illusory space. It's a super large world that originally existed in this universe … Otherwise, I'm afraid it would be very difficult for me to communicate with the power of the stars here!"
The many stars in the universe were able to shine here. Luo Zheng could be considered to have confirmed this.
The power of the Stellar Battle Body was enough to become one of Luo Zheng's trump cards. Therefore, in the past six years, Luo Zheng had never given up on communicating with the stars. He even spent a lot of time cultivating the 《Stellar Battle Body》! If he couldn't use it in the Illusory Battlefield, it would be a great loss for Luo Zheng.
Fortunately, Luo Zheng found that his worries were unnecessary. At least for now, he could successfully condense starlight!
When Luo Zheng's body was coated with a layer of magnificent and colorful starlight, he stepped forward and went straight for the Dragon Taming Arhats again.
The Dragon Taming Arhats that were smashed by Luo Zheng just now had been repaired. At this moment, the four Dragon Taming Arhats stood in a row and put their palms together neatly!
"Roar, roar, roar, roar …"
Accompanied by the roars of four dragons!
The four True Dragons that were entrenched on the four Dragon Taming Arhats roared at the same time!
Green light blossomed, and the light was so bright that it was hard to see!
How terrifying was the power contained in the eight palms?
However, Luo Zheng didn't want to retreat or dodge …
Maybe Luo Zheng had other ways to deal with it, but Luo Zheng also understood that the Illusory Battlefield had just opened and he would be facing all the geniuses in the universe. He didn't underestimate the other geniuses in the Divine Ocean Realm! In the past few years, even Ai Anxin of the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace might have made great progress. Luo Zheng might not be able to defeat her. Not to mention, there were also the geniuses of the Ten Thousand Buddha Sacred Realm, the Gods Without Thought, the Demon Night Clan, the Demon Race, and thousands of other strange races in the universe.
Therefore, Luo Zheng needed to review himself, to review the fruits of his hard work over the past few years.
"Buzz, buzz, buzz …"
Streaks of starlight began to flash in Luo Zheng's hands.
The shapes of these streaks of starlight were like cotton wool floating in his hands. They looked weightless, but the power contained in them couldn't be underestimated.
The three hundred streaks of starlight blended together. Red, orange, yellow, green, white, purple, blue, and red. The colorful streaks of starlight blended with each other and began to become extremely unstable!
After this cluster of colorful starlight was formed, Luo Zheng immediately slapped it out. If he hesitated for even a second, Luo Zheng would be sucked into it and be devoured by the power of the starlight!
"Glory of the Stars!"
"Buzz —"
A sharp sound burst out from this cluster of colorful starlight!
At this moment, the cluster of starlight just arrived in front of the four Dragon Taming Arhats …
"Explode!"
This cluster of starlight blended with the power of the three hundred stars. When it exploded, it repelled, blended, and reacted with each other. The burst of light was already extremely bright!
The entire Inner Yard was completely covered by the light of the Glory of the Stars. The situation inside couldn't be seen clearly!
However, the 30,000-foot-tall golden Buddha in the depths of the ancient temple opened its eyes and stared at the Inner Yard. Its expression was neither happy nor sad.
In the eyes of the golden Buddha, it saw the four Dragon Taming Arhats shattering continuously. The four True Dragon Stone Carvings on the Arhats' shoulders also began to shatter. Although rays of golden light emerged from their bodies in an attempt to repair themselves, as soon as the golden light appeared, the starlight took advantage of the gaps and annihilated it …
"The Dragon Taming Arhats can't be destroyed. Help me test this little guy. I want to see how far he has gone with the Emotion Severing Divine Dao …"

Chapter 1202
The golden light of these four Dragon Subduing Arhats had been annihilated by the starlight.
However, under the intervention of the Golden Buddha, the golden light within the Dragon Subduing Arhats flourished, and the fragmented stone statues quickly fused together and recovered to their original state!
The starlight diffusing in the Inner Court gradually dimmed.
Luo Zheng's figure gradually appeared. When he looked at the four intact Dragon Subduing Arhats, his eyes revealed a trace of surprise, and his face became grave and stern.
The four Dragon Subduing Arhats' Buddha statues were actually this powerful?
This was the first time Luo Zheng had used the Glory of the Stars, but it was the move with the strongest explosive power in the 《Stellar Battle Body》. Luo Zheng did not doubt its powerful destructive power in the slightest.
Such power was unable to tear these four Dragon Subduing Arhats to shreds. This was clearly beyond Luo Zheng's expectations, and even made him feel puzzled.
These eighteen Arhats came from the Inner Court one by one. The strength of each Arhat in the Inner Court was slightly stronger, but the gap in strength between each Arhat was not large.
This Dragon Subduing Arhat was the last Inner Court Arhat, so it was normal for him to be slightly stronger!
However, Luo Zheng's method was actually unable to kill these four Arhats. This was somewhat troublesome.
He naturally could not see that the reason these Dragon Subduing Arhats recovered to their original state was because someone had intervened from behind …
The four Dragon Subduing Arhats did not stop at all. As the dragon scales on their shoulders flickered, they once again surrounded Luo Zheng from several directions.
If it were any other martial artist, facing this situation, they would most likely be on the verge of despair.
However, for Luo Zheng, the trouble in front of him was just trouble, nothing more.
Facing the four Dragon Subduing Arhats, Luo Zheng's face was unperturbed. He once again raised the Thundergale Ghost Sword high up, the long and narrow blade hanging on one side. A faint sword intent lingered around the blade like a viper's temperament, constantly swallowing and spitting!
Luo Zheng's gaze became incomparably cold, as if he had become an expressionless stone statue at this moment.
Just as Luo Zheng raised the Lightning Wind Ghost Sword, the eyes of the giant Buddha in the depths of the ancient temple faintly flickered …
"Sure enough, this kid has cultivated the Emotion Severing Divine Way … Even though it hasn't reached perfection, but to comprehend such a cold sword will with his Divine Sea Realm cultivation base is already the limit. If he completes the last trace of emotion severing and comprehends the ultimate sword will, then this sword will definitely reach perfection!"
After saying that, the giant golden Buddha raised the lotus in his hand, and the lotus spun in front of him.
There was a fork on the stem of the lotus, and at the tip of the fork was a lotus seed pod that grew side by side with the lotus.
The Giant Golden Buddha reached out with his huge Buddha hand and plucked out a golden lotus seed from the lotus seed.
"As I've heard, all things originate from the origin. The heart of the past cannot be obtained, the heart of the present cannot be obtained, and the heart of the future cannot be obtained …"
When the Giant Golden Buddha chanted, the golden lotus seed seemed to melt, turning into a drop of pure golden liquid that kept rolling in front of the Giant Golden Buddha.
As the drop of golden liquid kept rolling, it began to transform into countless shapes: four limbs, a body, a head …
In the end, the golden lotus seed turned into a little person, and this little person's body shape was very similar to Luo Zheng's.
The Giant Golden Buddha used the Scripture of the Void to calculate Luo Zheng's fate!
Such a method of calculation was also a great divine ability. In comparison, the calculations of the many Empyreans under the Heavenly Dao were child's play!
However, when the giant golden Buddha was halfway through its calculations, a strange look appeared in its huge eyes. Moreover, there was also a trace of resentment in that strange look …
After a long time, the Giant Golden Buddha opened his mouth and said, "It's actually a Fateless. How could a Fateless appear …"
This calculation was halfway through and could no longer continue.
The golden lotus seed turned into Luo Zheng's appearance, but that face should have been vivid and lifelike, looking exactly like Luo Zheng. However, at this moment, the little golden person's face seemed to be covered by a layer of mist, unable to condense into a shape!
"Forget it, this seat wants to see how long you can last."
While the Giant Golden Buddha was calculating, Luo Zheng had already made his move …
The God Yi Sword was unleashed, and the sword light was unstoppable!
The four Dragon Subduing Arhats relied on the true dragon stone carvings on their shoulders to erupt with power, but they could not block the attack of the sword light!
This emotionless sword suddenly slashed out, and a thin line appeared on the waists of the four Dragon Subduing Arhats that were originally rushing toward Luo Zheng!
"Puff puff puff puff!"
Immediately after, four muffled sounds could be heard.
These four Dragon Subduing Arhats were cut in two by Luo Zheng, their heads and tails separated, and fell to the ground.
However, the wounds that were cut open by Luo Zheng immediately burst out with golden light. Under the golden light, a strange power was produced between their bodies, and they were once again glued together.
This magical ability was almost comparable to the Indestructible Body. No matter how Luo Zheng killed them, these four Dragon Subduing Arhats could always be restored!
Facing this strange scene, Luo Zheng's eyes were cold, but his state of mind was not the slightest bit moved.
At this moment, Luo Zheng's heart was as cold as ice, without any thoughts, only an extremely calm killing intent. He only wanted to kill these four Dragon Subduing Arhats.
"Puff puff puff puff …"
Several muffled sounds could be heard again!
Before these four Dragon Subduing Arhats could get up, Luo Zheng's long sword slashed out once again.
The sword light contained a cold and cruel sword intent, spreading into the shape of a fan, and once again slashed on the body of the Dragon Subduing Arhat …
"Again!"
Without waiting for these Dragon Subduing Arhats to be restored, Luo Zheng's sword had already slashed out!
"One sword …"
"Two sword …"
"Three sword …"
Luo Zheng's sword slashed out again and again, and the fan-shaped sword light spread out again and again.
At first, he cut these Dragon Subduing Arhats into two pieces, then four pieces, eight pieces … and finally, they were cut into a pile of stones.
If there were outsiders here, they would not be able to tell what this pile of stones was originally. Even the real dragon stone sculpture was also cut into palm-sized stones by Luo Zheng.
However, these irregular cross-sections of stones were still shining with a little golden light, and each stone was constantly trembling, attracting each other to stick together and restore the previous Dragon Subduing Arhats!
But Luo Zheng did not give them a chance!
The sword in his hand did not stop at all, nor did he show any mercy.
After severing his emotions, Luo Zheng's mentality would undergo a drastic change. Either he would take the initiative to leave the state of severing his emotions, and these four Dragon Subduing Arhats would have no possibility of restoring, or else Luo Zheng would not stop.
Under Luo Zheng's sword slashing, the golden light on the cross-sections of the stones became dimmer and dimmer.
This golden light was unusually overbearing, and could restore the wounds of the Dragon Subduing Arhats to their original state!
The golden light was the power of the Buddhist scripture "Solemnity of the Past"!
The Buddhist heart embraced the past, and through the calamity of life and death, it could be restored to its original state …
The golden light restored the Dragon Subduing Arhat's stone statue to its original state before it was cut into pieces!
However, the stone statue was still a stone statue, and the golden light was not endless. Under Luo Zheng's repeated slashes, the golden light gradually dimmed!
"If you still want to restore it, I will cut you into powder!"
Luo Zheng's emotionless voice rang out, and the Thunder Wind Ghost Divine Sword in his hand was still slashing out, cutting the stones even finer!
Seeing the light getting dimmer and dimmer, Luo Zheng also understood that it should be over soon.
He thought so in his heart, but at this moment, the golden light in the cross-sections of the stones soared in an instant, and the golden light was ten times brighter than before!
At the same time, the frequency of the trembling of the fine stones increased, and it was about to crazily stick together!
"Hey!"
Luo Zheng also noticed that something was wrong!
The golden light that had clearly dimmed suddenly released a bright light in an instant?
The sword in his hand did not stop at all, but his eyes looked towards the depths of the ancient temple, and he felt that something was wrong. Just now, it seemed that someone had interfered?
It was also this Golden Buddha who had a trace of thought. His Buddhist heart had reached the Great Realization stage, but when he saw Luo Zheng display such a cold sword move, and it seemed to be done casually, he had a thought in his mind.
This little guy at the Divine Sea Realm, the size of his Divine Sea Realm's Divine Sea was only one to nine. It seemed that he could display such a martial skill without True Essence?
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1203
This Golden Buddha could have let Luo Zheng go.
He had indeed relied on his own strength to pass through the eighteen arhats.
However, at this moment, this Golden Buddha had a hint of curiosity in his heart …
After a Divine Sea Realm martial artist opened up a Divine Sea in his Dantian, he would liquefy his True Essence and store it in his inner world.
Although the amount of True Essence stored in this way was countless times more than before, there was still a limit.
In Buddhism, the size of the Divine Sea Realm's Divine Sea was called "one nine".
According to this Golden Buddha's deduction, Luo Zheng could only use the Yi Shen Sword twice at most.
One sword was enough to draw half of the True Essence Sea in his body.
Two swords and the True Essence Sea would dry up …
Even if this Luo Zheng was gifted and his Divine Sea was wider than ordinary people, he could not escape the number of nine. Three swords was the limit.
If Luo Zheng had a fortuitous encounter and opened up a second Divine Sea, he could only use one more sword at most …
In this universe, if there was a Divine Sea Realm martial artist who could use the Yi Shen Sword four times, he would already be a genius of the major forces, a top existence!
However, this Luo Zheng calmly used seventeen swords!
The Golden Buddha couldn't figure it out at all. How did this guy do it?
Unless Luo Zheng had a treasure on him that could instantly replenish the True Essence in his body, or cultivated the Flower Grafting Technique that could continuously extract True Essence from somewhere … But with the Golden Buddha's eyes, how could he not see through it?
Under such doubts, the Golden Buddha wanted to see through the key points in order to replenish the golden light contained in the four Dragon Subduing Arhats …
Several years ago, when Luo Zheng met Second Senior Brother on the Path of True Extinction, Second Senior Brother also had the same doubts.
It was not that Second Senior Brother's eyes were more vicious than this Golden Buddha's. It was just that Second Senior Brother had always been by his master's side, so he knew the conditions for his master to accept a third disciple.
It was not difficult to guess that Luo Zheng's Divine Sea was filled with the Qi of Primal Chaos. That was the Primal Chaos Sea, which could not be mentioned in the same breath as the True Essence Sea.
Although this Golden Buddha knew the existence of the Qi of Primal Chaos, the Qi of Primal Chaos was known as the origin of all living beings. It was also a poison to all living beings.
How could he imagine that someone in this world could comprehend the Secret Primal Chaos Technique?
And how could he imagine that someone in this world could practice Primal Chaos Secret Technique?
Looking at the Dragon Taming Arhat that was rapidly healing, Luo Zheng's face once again revealed a helpless expression.
Luo Zheng was still able to cut the Dragon Subduing Arhat into pieces, but the golden light was always able to restore these things.
If this went on, when would it?
Go Luo Zheng '!
Luo Zheng was not a person who gave up easily. Since he had come this far, giving up so easily was not in line with his personality!
Luo Zheng Luo … Luo Luo … Go of the Buddha go go go of Go of Luo ….
Taking advantage of the fact that they had yet to take form, the sword intent on the Thundergale Dark Divine Sword began to flicker once more!
"Break!"
"Puff, puff, puff, puff …"
After slashing out ten times in a row, these Dragon Subduing Arhats once again turned into fragments, and the light between the fragments began to dim again.
"Again?"
Luo Zheng looked at the Dragon Subduing Arhat with a complicated expression. His eyes revealed a trace of seriousness. If the golden light were to turn from weak to strong again, he would really have no way to stop it.
After a breath, the golden light on the cross-section of the stone shone brightly again.
This scene made Luo Zheng speechless.
His gaze passed through the door of the Inner Academy. However, there was no focus in his eyes. There seemed to be a deeper meaning behind his gaze!
It was also Luo Zheng's gaze that made the giant golden Buddha's heart tremble slightly.
"This kid is very sharp. He didn't see me, but he knows I'm here … This is the last time. If I can't find out your limit, I'll stop," the giant golden Buddha said calmly.
Unfortunately, Luo Zheng didn't cooperate this time!
"Eight Twists Flying Smoke!"
Luo Zheng's body emitted wisps of white mist!
After he chose not to kill the Dragon Subduing Arhat, those extremely fine stones quickly stuck to each other. In a short time, the four Dragon Subduing Arhats were restored to their original state.
However, Luo Zheng used the Eight Twists Flying Smoke to easily wander among these Dragon Subduing Arhats. No matter how fierce their strength was, they couldn't get within three inches of Luo Zheng. No matter how the four Dragon Subduing Arhats cooperated perfectly, Luo Zheng was like a slippery fish, easily swimming among them.
The Dragon Subduing Arhats' attacks were fierce and their punches were heavy. However, when these punches came out, accompanied by the sound of dragon roars, the power of dragon scales all fell on empty space!
Luo Zheng became more and more relaxed. He stretched his body easily, as if he was floating in the water. He was pushed around by these Dragon Subduing Arhats, but his body didn't come into contact with any of them, nor did he receive any attacks.
His gaze fell on the door of the Inner Academy from time to time, and his face occasionally revealed a sarcastic expression.
"This kid is acting shamelessly with me …"
Luo Zheng's actions, in turn, made the Giant Golden Buddha helpless.
In the end, the Giant Golden Buddha shook his head. He reached out his hand and gently pinched, extinguishing the incense power of the four Dragon Subduing Arhats. At this moment, the four Dragon Subduing Arhats maintained their original posture and stood motionless.
"Creak!"
In the end, the door of the eighteenth Inner Academy was opened for Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng's face revealed a natural expression. He lifted his leg, walked around the motionless Dragon Subduing Arhat, and walked towards the door of the Inner Academy!
When Luo Zheng used the Eight Twists Flying Smoke, he had already made a decision in his heart. He was going to waste time with the person behind the scenes.
He didn't expect the effect to be so good. The other party probably didn't want to make things difficult for him and opened the door.
After the eighteenth Inner Academy, it was still the Inner Academy.
However, there were no more Arhat statues in the Inner Academy. In the middle of the Inner Academy, there was an offering table.
On the offering table, there was a token made of white jade.
"This is the reward of this cave?"
Luo Zheng's gaze swept over and found the word "Teleport" written on the jade!
"Teleport token?"
The greater teleportation technique in the universe could only be comprehended by sector lords.
When one's cultivation reached that level and had the experience of controlling a realm, one could comprehend the Greater Teleportation Technique!
This Greater Teleportation consumed a lot of True Yuan. Even Sector Lords would have to stop after four or five teleportations to replenish their True Yuan.
In the 100,000 Great Worlds, Greater Teleportation was probably the only choice besides relying on some extremely fast Godly Beasts. When Luo Zheng was trapped in the Immortal Abode, those Empyrean Lords used Greater Teleportation Technique to rush over.
"With this Teleportation Token, I can use Greater Teleportation and move my position in a large area?"
Luo Zheng originally had high hopes for this cave. After all, in order to enter this cave, he had spent 300,000 Fantasy points. Moreover, he had wasted a lot of time in this cave!
When he saw this Teleportation Token, he felt that this token was worth the 300,000 Fantasy points he had spent.
Now, the trouble Luo Zheng was facing was that those warriors refused to leave the city. Or rather, after these warriors discovered Luo Zheng, they would hide in the main city. Luo Zheng had no way to deal with them!
The Illusory Battlefield Luo Zheng was in was also divided into 50 Great Worlds.
Without relying on the passage, wanting to cross a Great World was a very time-consuming matter for Luo Zheng!
Relying on this Teleportation Token ….. it was enough to allow Luo Zheng to travel back and forth between Great Worlds!
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng's heart suddenly burned with passion!
He extended his hand and grabbed the token. Then, he looked into the depths of the ancient temple once more with a profound gaze.
Within the inner courtyard, there seemed to be some almighty expert. However, no matter who it was, it didn't have much to do with Luo Zheng.
After placing the Teleportation Token into Sumeru Space, Luo Zheng turned and left!
This time, stepping out of the inner courtyard, he didn't need to go through the 18 layers of inner courtyards. Opening the door led to the outer courtyard!
The moment Luo Zheng left the outer courtyard, the six-winged light bug that had disappeared for a long time appeared by Luo Zheng's side. The Maitreya Buddha also quietly changed from a fierce face to a smiling face.
As he walked out of the cave, the light vortexes appeared every 30 meters. Luo Zheng didn't leave and left the cave with ease.

Chapter 1204
Outside the cave, there were seven or eight Demon Race martial artists fighting with the three Heavenly Rats!
Before Luo Zheng entered the cave, he had killed the three Heavenly Rats, but not long after, another three appeared …
If Luo Zheng could discover this cave, then the other martial artists could as well!
The first martial artist had probably discovered the cave but was unable to defeat the Heavenly Rats here, so he returned to the main city to call for his companions!
Currently, there were still seven or eight martial artists alive, but when they saw the Fantasy Points all over the ground condensing into balls and floating in the air, the corpses of the dead martial artists were gradually disappearing …
Luo Zheng casually counted the balls of light condensed from the Fantasy Points. There were about ten balls. Presumably, ten martial artists had already been killed by the Heavenly Rats and were sent back to the main city to be resurrected.
When a martial artist killed another martial artist, they could absorb the other's Fantasy Points for their own use!
However, these Heavenly Rats could not absorb Fantasy Points, but after a martial artist died, half of their Fantasy Points would dissipate. Therefore, the Fantasy Points in front of them had already become ownerless objects!
To Luo Zheng, these were simply free Fantasy Points, so how could he let them go?
His figure flashed and he floated towards the first ball of Fantasy Points. His body lightly touched it, and the yellow Fantasy Points entered his body and were easily absorbed by him.
"Audacious!"
"Courting death!"
"Daring to absorb the Fantasy Points of our Eighteen Demonic Shadow Sons, we will chase you to the ends of the earth!"
The seven or eight Demon Race martial artists were still trying their best to deal with the three Heavenly Rats, but when they saw Luo Zheng's actions, they immediately flew into a rage, and their roars resounded through the wilderness!
These three Heavenly Rats continued to move at an incomparably fast speed. From time to time, they would use their sharp incisors to viciously bite down on the bodies of these Demon martial artists. With these bites, even divine artifact level armor could be pierced through. Even though these Demon martial artists had thick skin and thick flesh, at this moment, their bodies were still riddled with wounds so deep that their bones could be seen!
The Eighteen Demonic Shadow Sons knew that there was a great treasure buried in this cave, but the strength of the three Heavenly Rats was too strong, so they could only join forces.
In the fierce battle, more than ten lives were sacrificed!
Fortunately, these Heavenly Rats could not absorb the Fantasy Points. The dead martial artists were forcibly sent back to the main city. After they were resurrected, they could come back again and absorb the Fantasy Points that belonged to them.
The Eighteen Demonic Shadow Sons were very close, so they could be trusted. Therefore, none of them coveted the Fantasy Points on the ground!
Who knew that a cultivator would emerge from the cave while they were in the midst of a fierce battle and take the opportunity to absorb their dead brother's Fantasy Points? How could they tolerate this?
"Eighteen Demonic Shadow Sons?"
The corners of Luo Zheng's mouth curled up slightly when he heard their angry shouts. However, Luo Zheng did not take their warning seriously at all. He continued to charge towards the balls of dreamy light, rapidly absorbing the scattered balls of light on the ground.
Three thousand points …
Eight thousand …
Four thousand Fantasy
Although there weren't many scattered light spots on the ground, each one was worth a few thousand fantasy points.
As Luo Zheng absorbed the Fantasy Points, the number of Fantasy Points in his body rapidly increased!
After absorbing these ten Fantasy Points, he already had fifty thousand Fantasy Points. In addition to the ten thousand Fantasy Points from before, Luo Zheng's Fantasy Points instantly recovered to sixty thousand.
"Ah!"
Following a miserable scream, another Demon Race martial artist died on the spot! The Demon Race martial artist's head had been pierced through by the Heavenly Rat!
The red Fantasy Points wrapped around the Demon Race martial artist's soul and flew towards the main city. Another Fantasy Point burst out and floated above the corpse.
The remaining six Demon Race martial artists were slightly stunned when they saw this. They reflexively looked at Luo Zheng. This Luo Zheng wouldn't want to swallow the Fantasy Points, right?! This was too much!
Just as this thought appeared in their minds, Luo Zheng's figure disappeared. In the blink of an eye, he appeared beside the corpse of the Demon Race martial artist and swallowed the Fantasy Points...
. The six Demon Race martial artists were filled with anger when they saw this scene. Their eyes bulged, and they wished they could eat Luo Zheng alive!
"Kill this guy first!"
"Kill him, then deal with the Heavenly Rat …"
"Too hateful!"
The six Demon Race martial artists ignored the three Heavenly Rats and said they wanted to kill Luo Zheng first!
Coincidentally, two of the three Heavenly Rats also pounced on Luo Zheng at the same time.
Luo Zheng's expression was indifferent when he saw this scene. In his heart, there was no such thing as hateful or excessive in the Fantasy Battlefield.
Everyone was fighting for their ranking. This was a great elimination. The weak would be eliminated from the Fantasy Battlefield, and the strong would stand in the center of the stage! This was the meaning of the Fantasy Battlefield's existence.
Facing the siege of the Demon Race martial artists and the Heavenly Rat, a white mist appeared around Luo Zheng's body!
"Eight Twists Flying Smoke!"
It was still Eight Twists Flying Smoke!
When this movement technique was used, no matter how ferocious the opponent's attacks were, they were unable to touch Luo Zheng's sleeves.
"This guy's movement technique is so strange!" a Demon Race martial artist roared.
"Let's surround him..."
"You go behind this guy..."
However, an even stranger scene appeared before the eyes of the Demon Race martial artists.
The Heavenly Rat's speed was originally extremely fast. Luo Zheng's own speed might be far inferior, but the instant the Heavenly Rat pounced on Luo Zheng, the Eight Twists Flying Smoke turned Luo Zheng into a wisp of smoke. With the slightest fluctuation, Luo Zheng would be pushed out! The distance between Luo Zheng and the Heavenly Rat was always three inches!
Therefore, in the eyes of the Demon Race martial artists, Luo Zheng's speed suddenly increased. His speed was almost the same as the Heavenly Rat's!
Because this speed was too fast, they were almost unable to catch sight of Luo Zheng's figure. How could they surround Luo Zheng?
While using Eight Twists Flying Smoke, Luo Zheng did not forget to strike the Primal Chaos Qi into the ground, forming a mysterious white halo!
Luo Zheng had already used the Lightning Calamity Annihilation twice to deal with the Heavenly Rat. This time, he did the same. After all, the three Heavenly Rats also had three thousand fantasy points. The only difference was that this time, Luo Zheng 'encircled' the Demon Race martial artists in front of him.
Not long after, a terrifying aura suddenly came from the sky, accompanied by a burst of wild heavenly lightning. Everything in the white halo was baptized by the heavenly tribulation...
Accompanied by a roar of unwillingness, the six Demon Race martial artists turned into six red dots of light and went straight to the main city. The three Heavenly Rats in front of him also exploded at the same time, turning into countless tiny fantasy light dots.
These fantasy light dots floated in the air for a while, then flew towards Luo Zheng's body!
The six Demon Race martial artists were able to last until the end. They were stronger than the Demon Race martial artists who had been killed before. Naturally, they had more fantasy points. On average, each of them had ten thousand fantasy light dots.
Adding the fantasy points from the three Heavenly Rats, Luo Zheng's fantasy points had recovered to one hundred and thirty thousand!
"My luck is not bad," Luo Zheng smiled slightly. His body gently floated up, and he looked into the distance.
There were countless main cities in this large world, and the Illusory Battlefield was divided into fifty large worlds. Now that he had the Teleportation Token, there was no need for him to return to the main city. Now was the time for Luo Zheng to crazily plunder fantasy points!
However, whether the Teleportation Token was as effective as he had deduced still needed to be verified.
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng took out the Teleportation Token made of white jade. Then, he extracted a trace of Primal Chaos Qi from the Primal Chaos Sea and poured it into the Teleportation Token!
The moment the Teleportation Token was activated, it released a faint white light. Then, the white light extinguished.
Seeing this, Luo Zheng frowned slightly.
"This Teleportation Token … is useless?"
Just as he was thinking this, the Teleportation Token in his hand began to vibrate violently in the next breath!
An intense power of the Law of Space was transmitted from the Teleportation Token. The blazing white light instantly wrapped around Luo Zheng, as if a small sun had risen in the wilderness!
After a long time …

Chapter 1205
After activating the teleportation token, Luo Zheng escaped into a mysterious space.
Below Luo Zheng's feet, there were countless red dots. At this moment, Luo Zheng was somewhat stunned.
According to Luo Zheng's conjecture, this teleportation token might be used to help him use the Greater Teleportation Technique, allowing him to instantly cross thousands of miles of space and move freely in this great world.
He never expected that after activating the teleportation token, it would allow him to escape into a mysterious space. This space was completely beyond the scope of common sense!
"The red dots below should represent all the main cities in this great world … My position is here!"
The red dots below the space were distributed according to a certain distance. Among them, there was a cyan dot marked in a corner. Not far from this cyan dot, there was a main city.
That main city should be the one that Luo Zheng arrived in after entering the dreamland. He didn't expect that he would be assigned to the edge of this great world.
Just like what was marked on the map, the main city wasn't very far from him …
Apart from this main city, the other main cities were still quite a distance away from him.

If it was only marked as a main city, it wouldn't be able to teleport Luo Zheng away. It was equivalent to a map. Spending so much effort to obtain a map was not worth the investment!
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng carefully groped about!
After floating in this space for a while, Luo Zheng went straight to one of the red dots.

This space was not big, only about a few dozen miles in length and width. Luo Zheng circled around this space. Every time he passed by a red dot, that red dot would spread out a transmission channel, waiting for Luo Zheng to arrive. If Luo Zheng left completely, the transmission channel would disappear again …
A large world had tens of thousands of main cities. Luo Zheng had too many choices.

When Luo Zheng flew above the three light spots, the light spots didn't condense into a transmission channel. There was no reaction at all.

In this large world, the closer the main cities were to each other, the farther they were from each other. The closer the main cities were to each other in the center of the large world, the more intense the collisions between the warriors there! This was also the reason why Luo Zheng chose this red light spot.

The moment Luo Zheng drilled into the red light spot, a transmission channel spread out from the red light spot and firmly wrapped around Luo Zheng!
"Buzz …"
The moment he passed through the transmission channel, Luo Zheng's eardrums suddenly roared. At the same time, a feeling of extreme discomfort came over him.
After all, this type of "transmission channel" was different from ordinary transmission channels. Ordinary transmission channels only connected two places and could compress the distance between two places by a hundred times. However, the area of a large world was too large. Even if the distance was compressed by a hundred times, the transmission channel still wasn't a short distance.
However, this transmission command directly transported Luo Zheng. No matter how far the destination was, it could be reached in an instant. This type of ability could only be used by Sector Lords. Currently, Luo Zheng was using the transmission command to reach the Illusory Battlefield. Under the pressure of the space law, it was normal for Luo Zheng to not be able to adapt.
As the sound of his eardrums vibrating became more and more intense, Luo Zheng suddenly fell downwards!
In the center of the main city, dozens of cultivators were sitting cross-legged on the spot, meditating.
Every warrior participating in the Illusory Battlefield was fully prepared. Their clans had prepared large amounts of top grade Zhen Yuanyu and Zhen Yuanyu. In any case, the Illusory Battlefield was only a projection. Even if the Zhen Yuanyu was consumed, the Zhen Yuanyu outside wouldn't disappear.

After the white light dissipated, Luo Zheng discovered that he had fallen into the center of the main city. At this moment, Luo Zheng also understood why he couldn't fly. This main city had a Forbidden Space Restriction. With his strength, he naturally couldn't get rid of it.

These cultivators whispered to each other, their faces full of puzzlement.
Luo Zheng didn't care about these discussions.
He didn't know if the Teleportation Token could be taken away from the Dream Battlefield or if it could be used in the outside world. However, Luo Zheng wouldn't easily reveal it to others. As for how he appeared in this main city, he would let these people guess.

The warrior looked at Luo Zheng and said, "This main city has rules. Newcomers have to register first, then pay half of your fantasy points to the city lord. Only then can you safely leave."
"City lord? This main city isn't a city in the Dream Battlefield. How can there be a city lord? My fantasy points can't be divided. How can I pay the city lord? "Luo Zheng was slightly surprised.
The warrior replied, "I'm not sure if other main cities have City Lords, but our main city has one. As for the payment of Fantasy Points, the method is very simple. If you let the City Lord kill you once, you will naturally pay half of it to him …"
Hearing this, Luo Zheng suddenly understood. He didn't know whether to laugh or cry. He was intimidated by the two words' city lord '. Presumably, this city lord was a character similar to the young master of the demon race. They relied on their own power to squeeze other warriors' fantasy points.

Chapter 1206

Chapter 1208
The blue-robed cultivator was greatly alarmed!

The three long swords were of different colors. One was gold, one was silver, and one was bronze!
The three swords were engraved with beautiful patterns and their appearance was very eye-catching!
The black long sword that Luo Zheng had broken was a gift from the Ji Family's Young Master. As for the gold, silver, and bronze swords, they were his usual swords. These three swords had followed him for many years and were connected to his heart!
The gold, silver, and bronze swords flew out and resonated with each other from a distance. They began to rapidly rotate!
