Under the constant rotation of the three swords, a gold, silver, and bronze tornado appeared beside the blue-robed cultivator!
"Triple Mountain Dragon Twist!"
After this tornado was formed, it blew violently like a soaring dragon and charged towards Luo Zheng at the rear!
Luo Zheng's figure was like a sharp sword. Without dodging, he charged straight into it!
"Ping ping …"
The three tri-colored longswords that were hidden in the tornadoes clashed against his body, producing a series of metallic sounds!
Luo Zheng did not need a protective armor. His body was comparable to a first-grade divine artifact. Although the swords were sharp, how could they do anything to Luo Zheng?
The only troublesome thing was Luo Zheng's clothes. Under this twisting attack, the green clothes on his body were torn into strips and then shattered. However, this was not the first time Luo Zheng had encountered such a situation. Basically, Luo Zheng had to change his clothes every once in a while …
Luo Zheng forcibly passed through the three-colored tornado and charged towards the blue-robed cultivator. His hands shot out like lightning!
Seeing that he could not escape, the blue-robed cultivator flipped over like a sparrow hawk. A golden pill appeared in his hand and he suddenly crushed it!
"Hmph, you think you can obtain my fantasy points … Dream on! My fantasy points are all for the City Lord! Hahaha … "
Hearing this, a trace of confusion appeared on Luo Zheng's face. He only needed to kill this person to obtain half of his Fantasy Points. Why couldn't he obtain them?
After the blue-robed cultivator crushed the golden pellet, a violent aura erupted from the golden pellet, and an enormous aura spread out from it!
"Boom!"
Seeing that the situation was not right, Luo Zheng quickly retreated!
The distance between him and the blue-robed martial artist increased by several tens of feet!
After a breath of time, the golden ball suddenly shattered, and then blood red lightning that was fine like hairs emanated from within it!
This power of thunder was as thin as a strand of hair, but as it spread out, it was like a dense tree root that spread outwards. It completely covered the 100 foot area around the blue-clothed martial artist, turning into a massive ball of thunder!
Seeing the power of this golden pill, Luo Zheng's eyelids twitched violently!
"The power of this golden pill is actually so terrifying!"
If Luo Zheng had been caught in it just now, he would probably have been torn apart by this terrifying blood red lightning!
Although he wouldn't really die in the Illusion Battlefield, he would probably lose half of his fantasy points … If this wasn't the Illusion Battlefield, wouldn't he have really died?
In addition to the chill in his heart, Luo Zheng was also thinking about the blue-robed cultivator's words before he died. He was unable to obtain his fantasy points?
According to the rules of the Illusion Battlefield, even if one committed suicide, they would lose half of their fantasy points. Just like the cultivator killed by the Sky Rat in the wilderness before, after the fantasy points exploded, they condensed together and turned into a yellow ball of light. Luo Zheng only needed to go up and absorb it!
However, what Luo Zheng didn't expect was that after the blue-robed cultivator died, a large ball of fantasy points indeed appeared!
The densely packed Fantasy Points were twice as many as the Dragonfowl that Luo Zheng had killed back then! In other words, just within the blue-robed cultivator's body, there were at least 200,000 fantasy points of light!
However, after these yellow fantasy points of light exploded, they didn't enter Luo Zheng's body because this person wasn't killed by Luo Zheng!
Not only that, but these fantasy points of light actually flew towards the main city!
"What's going on?"
Luo Zheng's face was filled with shock …
Could it be that the City Lord could change the rules of the Illusion Battlefield?
This was simply impossible!
On the city wall, the City Lord's guards didn't stand up.
That blue-robed cultivator wasn't even a match for Luo Zheng. If they rushed out of the city, they would only be delivering food to Luo Zheng …
However, these guards had mocking smiles on their faces as they stared at Luo Zheng, seemingly mocking Luo Zheng's childishness.
As for the various cultivators at the city gate, each of them had a different expression. They were the subordinates of the City Lord, so naturally, they weren't willing to tell Luo Zheng the reason why.
Floating ten feet above the ground, Luo Zheng frowned as he watched this scene. Seeing the large number of fantasy points disappear, Luo Zheng felt somewhat helpless!
In an instant, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed, and he flew along the city wall!
This so-called City Lord definitely had more than one subordinate. A main city covered a large area. News of him being here might not spread out. Right now, he had to find his next target, which was the City Lord's other subordinates …
The dense yellow dots of light drifted all the way into the main city and finally flew into a courtyard in the southwest of the main city.
In the Illusion Battlefield, all the cultivators were fighting with their lives on the line. They were either fighting with other cultivators to obtain fantasy points or killing various fierce beasts in the wilderness …
However, this residence appeared to be very serene!
Little snowflakes were like weightless cotton that gently drifted down.
Under the scorching sun, these snowflakes were naturally man-made …
In fact, when one approached the entrance of this courtyard, one could feel the biting cold. Ordinary cultivators also knew that this was not a place to stay for long, so they all retreated carefully.
In the courtyard, a thick layer of snow had been laid on the ground. Standing in the courtyard was like standing in the middle of winter, giving off a quiet and peaceful atmosphere.
A woman wearing a purple veil was in a corner of this snow-covered courtyard, holding a palm-sized silk book in her hands.
There were no drawings on this silk book. Instead, it was filled with small, densely packed words. It was a contemporary novel …
In the entire universe, in the Hundred Thousand Great Worlds, cultivators occupied an absolute ruling position. To put it bluntly, they valued martial arts and despised literature.
Here, scholars could only be considered as mortals. They were insignificant existences. In the mainstream culture of any race, they didn't exist.
However, in these few years, there was a craze for contemporary novels in the Upper Realm …
It wasn't that the status of scholars had increased. Things like poems and songs were still not popular among cultivators. Instead, all kinds of supernatural novels were very popular.
The reason was still Luo Zheng …
Although many cultivators were annoyed by his stories over the past few years, there were still some cultivators who heard the stories in their minds and became interested.
However, Luo Zheng still needed to cultivate. Furthermore, the story was told for the mysterious girl. Everyone was just a listener. If the mysterious girl came out of seclusion, everyone could still gather by her side and listen. They could be considered listeners. But if Luo Zheng stopped telling the story, everyone would have nothing to listen to.
However, in the universe, there were many living beings. The number of mortals was even more than ants. In terms of culture, these powerhouses who stood at the top of the pyramid were actually not as good as those mortals.
If one asked around a little, they would be able to buy mountains of novels by spending a single True Essence Stone. They could even hire several hundred storytellers to tell vivid stories in their ears every day. In the last few years, this had become one of the most fashionable activities among martial artists.
Because of this, the status of some mortals rose. For example, in a small city under a sixth-rank Holy Land in the Broken Heavens Realm of [Gods No Thoughts], there was a storyteller named Zhang Shisan.
Zhang Shisan's stories were all made up by himself. When he told the stories, whether it was his emotions or his demeanor, they were all very accurate. He was recognized by a certain cultivator in this Holy Land and was recommended all the way up. Zhang Shisan's status rose steadily. It was said that it even caused a fight between two Holy Lands …
Later, Zhang Shisan was even given a Longevity Pill, which gave him hundreds of years of life. This was already very amazing among mortals. Even the rulers of some small countries among mortals didn't receive such treatment.
But in general, all scholars had to thank one person, and that was Luo Zheng!
If he hadn't used the Roaring Token to inject his stories into the minds of many cultivators and arouse the interest of some of them, this wave of activity wouldn't have happened …
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1209
This woman could be considered to be infatuated with the novel. At this moment, she was lying on a chair with her slender legs resting on a small stool. She was flipping through the silk book in her hands with great interest.
With the strength of a Divine Sea Realm warrior's soul, as long as they released their senses, they could instantly memorize the hundreds of thousands of words in the silk book in the blink of an eye.
But if they did so, they would lose the joy of reading!
She was still reading word by word. Only by doing so could she be affected by the characters in the novel and be concerned about their fates …
Just as this woman was engrossed in reading, a large ball of yellow dreamy light rapidly flew towards her. In the blink of an eye, the dreamy light was absorbed by this woman.
"Eh?"
The purple-clothed woman frowned. Her face that was whiter than snow revealed a trace of displeasure. It was indeed displeasing to be disturbed at the most exciting part of the story.
"Who is so daring … to kill my people."
The purple-clothed woman carefully folded a page of the silk book in her hands and placed it into her Sumeru Space.
Then, she quietly got up and left a series of small footprints in the rustling snow. With light steps, she left her residence …
Luo Zheng circled around the edge of the main city at high speed …
His guess was correct. The 'City Lord' had more than one subordinate.
In fact, the City Lord had sent a total of eight people to help her collect dreamy points at the eight exits of the main city.
In the outside world, these eight people were the personal attendants of the 'City Lord'.
This time, they came to the Illusory Battlefield and naturally served the 'City Lord' with great care. They had to hand over all the dreamy points they collected!
And the location where these eight people were located was jokingly called the 'Slaughterhouse' by the warriors in the main city …
With Luo Zheng's speed, he arrived at the other side of the main city in less than five minutes.
He saw a tall Human Race warrior standing at the city gate with a pair of golden hammers in his hands.
The human martial artist was as strong as a bull, and his voice was very loud. He shouted loudly, "Stand still, all of you. You won't feel any pain when I hit you with my hammer. Just close your eyes and you'll be revived in the city!"
In front of this human warrior, there was naturally a row of warriors who planned to hand over half of their dreamy points to him!
Seeing this tall human warrior, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed and a trace of joy appeared on his face!
He drew out a trace of primal chaos energy from his body. Then, a gust of wind blew around him and his speed increased. He flew towards the human warrior at an extremely fast speed!
This tall human warrior raised his hammers and was about to kill the people in front of him.............................
.......... Niu... .......
This tall human martial artist's name was Niu Jin. He was naturally fond of fighting. When he entered the Illusory Battlefield, he wanted to take advantage of this opportunity to kill!
............................................
Niu Jin had no interest in slaughtering sheep that couldn't resist. However, his young master's orders were more important. He could only stand guard here and act as his young master's executioner, helping her accumulate dreamy points.
At this moment, Niu Jin saw a figure flying towards him at high speed. He immediately became excited!
When the Illusory Battlefield had just opened, there were people who dared to challenge Niu Jin …
However, after those warriors were killed by him a few times, they immediately became as obedient as sheep.
Among them, there were a few who were stronger than him. However, after being taught a lesson by his young master, they also became sheep.
Facing a group of docile sheep, Niu Jin felt that this trip to the Illusory Battlefield was very boring. Therefore, he was powerless to do anything!
Now that he saw someone coming to find trouble with him, Niu Jin was naturally happy!
"Clang!"
Niu Jin collided the two hammers in his hands!
A visible ripple spread out from the hammers. When the sound reached the warriors' ears, they felt their souls tremble!
A smile appeared on Luo Zheng's face when he saw Niu Jin's fearless demeanor.
He was afraid that Niu Jin would escape into the main city. He was also afraid that Niu Jin would crush a golden pill like the warrior in green. If that happened, all his efforts would be for naught!
"I'll kill this person as fast as I can!"
After Luo Zheng made up his mind, he did not hesitate. When he was still a hundred feet away from Niu Jin, he took a step forward and crossed the space. He planned to approach Niu Jin's side and kill Niu Jin with a thunderous attack to cut off any backup plans.
However, just as Luo Zheng passed through space, he heard Niu Jin snort coldly. He suddenly waved his hammers and smashed them into the void!
"Boom!"
A loud sound came from this smash!
Luo Zheng's figure staggered out from the void. He looked at the boorish man in front of him with surprise.
Niu Jin's face was also full of surprise …
Shuttling through space for a short distance was only a basic method of using the Law of Space. With Luo Zheng's current understanding of the Law of Space, he could hide himself perfectly. At least, it would be difficult for the opponent to catch his shuttling path!
However, Niu Jin's hammers were not only able to accurately see through his teleportation technique, the hammers were also able to hit him in the void!
Just now, Luo Zheng was smashed out from the void by Niu Jin. The power contained in the hammers made Luo Zheng's Qi and blood tumble.
At this moment, Niu Jin was even more shocked than Luo Zheng!
His hammers really hit Luo Zheng's body …
With his strength alone, his hammers could flatten a ten thousand feet tall mountain. Even if the opponent was a warrior in the Divine Sea Realm, there was no way they could survive being hit by his hammers. At least, they would be smashed into meat paste!
However, after he smashed his hammers, he only saw Luo Zheng stagger out from the void. He didn't seem to be weakened at all. He wasn't even injured.
"What the hell is this guy!"
At this moment, Niu Jin had the same feeling as the blue-clothed cultivator. It was as if he wasn't looking at a Human Race cultivator, but a monster, a monster that could withstand the force of his hammers without being injured!
Even the young master couldn't withstand his hammers. This guy …
However, his character was completely different from the blue-robed warrior. Seeing Luo Zheng like this, he didn't have the slightest intention of retreating. This aroused his monstrous fighting spirit!
This kind of opponent could only be encountered by chance. Moreover, he wouldn't die in the Illusion Battlefield. This kind of opportunity was too precious. How could he let go of Luo Zheng?
The outcome was still undecided. However, he only wanted to fight to his heart's content. As for the large amount of fantasy points he had accumulated for the young master, he had already thrown this matter to the depths of his mind …
"Haha, your body is really enviable. However, I don't believe that I can't smash you! Don't run! "
"Clang! Clang! "
After Niu Jin finished speaking, he slammed his hammers together a few more times. Ripples spread out in the air. His thick and powerful legs took a mysterious step as he rushed towards Luo Zheng!
The pair of golden hammers in this person's hands danced, forming thousands of hammer shadows!
This hammer technique was extremely mysterious. Not to mention that ordinary warriors couldn't see through these hammer shadows, even Luo Zheng couldn't see through them in an instant!
He was like a mad bull, rushing towards Luo Zheng with great power. He wanted to crush everything in front of him into dust!
Such a big commotion attracted many warriors to stop and watch! Even if they had to endure the sound of Niu Jin's hammers striking, they still didn't want to miss this wonderful duel!
"That guy really didn't run!"
"Could it be that he was scared silly? Niu Jin will smash him into dust …"
"Previously, Niu Jin lost his temper with the City Lord and smashed his hammers on the city wall. I heard that the main city wall was shaken by him!"
Shaking the main city was already an exaggeration.
All the main cities in the Illusion Battlefield were built by the Heavenly Dao. They were where the rules were. In reality, they couldn't be shaken at all. Thus, it was impossible to peel off a piece of the city wall!
However, it was enough to show that everyone had a deep impression of Niu Jin's hammers and his terrifying strength.

Chapter 1210
The golden hammer shadows in front of Luo Zheng were profound hammer techniques that had been accumulated for thousands of years!
These golden hammer shadows covered the sky and covered the earth, making it impossible to distinguish between the real and fake ones. It was not easy to break them.
However, Luo Zheng had no intention of breaking them. He still intended to use brute force to resist, which was also the fastest method.
However, Niu Jin was originally very strong. How many martial artists of the same level could resist him with brute force? Even warriors who were stronger than Niu Jin, such as the "Young Master" that Niu Jin served, who was also the "City Lord" of the main city, could not use brute force to fight him. That would be too disadvantageous.
However, Luo Zheng would not be disadvantaged. With the body of a divine artifact combined with the power of the dragon scales, not to mention martial artists of the same level, even martial artists of a higher level than Luo Zheng would not be able to resist him!
Therefore, not only did Luo Zheng not retreat half a step, but he also faced Niu Jin head-on …
"You have guts! I'll grant you a quick death! "
Seeing that Luo Zheng did not run away, but instead came straight for him, Niu Jin poured all of his strength into his arms! At the same time, the Spirit Sea began to set off monstrous waves. His cultivation spread out like a god of strength, and all the golden hammer shadows came down on Luo Zheng!
At this moment, Luo Zheng also opened up the power of the dragon scales to as many as ten thousand pieces!
The strength in his body was like a tide, flocking together. Although the hammer shadows were dazzling like flowers, making it difficult to distinguish between the real and fake ones, Luo Zheng did not intend to defend against the hammer strikes. His only target was Niu Jin himself!
"Whoosh!"
Luo Zheng's figure suddenly sped up by 30%. He clenched one hand into a fist. This very ordinary Forward Punch contained far more power than anyone could imagine!
In Niu Jin's view, Luo Zheng's fighting style was somewhat desperate.
With a laugh, he grabbed with his right hand, and half of the golden hammer shadows in the sky disappeared. It turned out that for insurance, he had blocked Luo Zheng's attack with a golden hammer in front of his chest. He had smashed Luo Zheng twice before, so he knew that Luo Zheng must be extraordinary. The Forward Punch in front of him looked very simple, but he was afraid that there was a trick behind it! He did not want to perish together with Luo Zheng …
If they perished together, half of the fantasy points in his and Luo Zheng's bodies would be left here. If they were snatched by some meddlesome martial artists, he might be scolded by the Young Master..
Niu Jin looked reckless on the outside, but his mind was actually very meticulous. He kept one of the golden hammers, but the other one was still transforming into countless hammer shadows and smashing down on Luo Zheng's head. He was determined to smash Luo Zheng's head into smithereens!
It could be said that Niu Jin made the most correct judgment in an instant.
But no matter how correct his judgment was, it was useless under the suppression of absolute strength!
When Luo Zheng's fist smashed into Niu Jin's defensive golden hammer, Niu Jin immediately understood ….
The might of Luo Zheng's punch wasn't very strong. His entire aura had been completely overshadowed by Niu Jin. However, when Luo Zheng's punch made contact with the golden hammer, Niu Jin felt an earth-shattering force from his hammer.
This force smashed into the golden hammer, and the golden hammer turned around and smashed into Niu Jin's chest ….
"Ka!"
A cracking sound was heard.
Luo Zheng had smashed the circular golden hammer into pieces from the middle! Luo Zheng's Forward Punch did not stop. It went through the cracks and smashed into Niu Jin's chest!
"Clang!"
When Luo Zheng's fist hit Niu Jin's chest, another loud sound was heard!
Hearing this sound, Luo Zheng's eyes flickered slightly. However, he sighed in his heart. Not good. This guy actually has a treasure to protect himself!
A defensive treasure that could withstand the power of ten thousand dragon scales had to be at least a grade one divine artifact. However, even if he had a defensive treasure protecting his chest, he would still be severely injured. After all, a defensive treasure could only reduce the damage he received. It couldn't completely block the attack, unless the defensive treasure protecting his chest was a supreme divine artifact.
Luo Zheng was worried that this guy also had a golden pill. If he crushed the golden pill now, all his efforts would be for naught ….
Under the huge inertia, Niu Jin flew backward. He knew that he was seriously injured. That terrifying force still remained in his internal organs. It even shook the world in his body, and a part of his spirit sea was on the verge of collapse!
At this time, Niu Jin was not thinking about saving his life, but protecting the Young Master's fantasy points!
If he lost his life, he could revive. If this person snatched his fantasy points, he would most likely face the Young Master's harsh punishment!
Therefore, the moment he flew backward, a golden pill appeared in Niu Jin's hand!
However, the moment Niu Jin took out the golden pill, before he could crush it, he heard a cold chuckle beside his ear.
Luo Zheng seemed to have anticipated that Niu Jin would take out the golden pill and forced himself to step closer to him. Niu Jin was just about to crush the golden pill with his hand, but before he could exert any strength, a blue ray of golden light flashed …
Niu Jin could no longer feel the existence of his right hand!
That hand was instantly cut off by Luo Zheng's Thunder Wind Ghost Divine Sword, and he flew up high with the golden pill in his hand.
Luo Zheng's movements were extremely nimble. His figure flashed, and he grabbed the golden pill from his severed hand. With a roll, his knee pressed against Niu Jin's chest, and the blade of his sword was already aimed at Niu Jin.
"The Young Master won't …."
"Pfff!"
Luo Zheng didn't let this person finish speaking. If it was elsewhere, he might have been able to make this person speak a few more words.
This main city was strange everywhere. He was afraid that the fantasy points he was about to obtain would fly away. It was better to kill this person first. It couldn't be that the fantasy points of the person he killed would fly away, right?
In fact, until now, Luo Zheng still couldn't understand why the fantasy points would automatically fly away after the blue-clothed cultivator died ….
Once Niu Jin died, he was wrapped in a red light and brought back to the main city to revive. At the same time, a large amount of fantasy points exploded. This person's fantasy points were actually more than the blue-clothed cultivator's!
These yellow light spots exploded from Niu Jin's body and flew towards Luo Zheng's body. Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng let out a sigh of relief. He really thought that someone could affect the rules of the Illusion Battlefield!
On the city wall, many cultivators saw this scene. Most of their gazes were filled with envy and pity.
They were envious because Luo Zheng actually had such strength. Not only was he not at a disadvantage in a direct confrontation with Niu Jin, but he was also able to kill Luo Zheng in one blow and obtain so many fantasy points!
The reason for their pity was very simple. They clearly knew what Luo Zheng's fate was ….
Previously, when the "City Lord" wanted to take control of this main city, it caused many cultivators to retaliate. Among them, there was no lack of powerful cultivators. Some of them were even more valiant than Niu Jin and the others. Some of them had killed Niu Jin and the others several times.
But in the end, they were killed by the City Lord again and again!
Now, not only did those cultivators have a pitiful amount of fantasy points, but it was also very difficult for them to even leave the main city!
As long as those cultivators left the city, they would face the City Lord's pursuit. And with the City Lord's speed …. no Divine Sea Realm cultivator could escape from him!
In their opinion, this Luo Zheng's fate wouldn't be any better.
"This Niu Jin is dead. The City Lord …. will probably rush over!"
"This guy still hasn't left. It's not too late to slip away now!"
"This person should be a cultivator from another main city. He doesn't know the City Lord's strength!"
What they didn't know was that Luo Zheng didn't intend to leave this place at all. His target was the City Lord ….
The City Lord's subordinates were already filthy rich. Wouldn't the City Lord himself have accumulated an astronomical number of fantasy points? Thinking of this, how could Luo Zheng be willing to leave?
Since this City Lord was rumored to be so tyrannical and he had killed two of his subordinates in a row, this City Lord would probably come looking for him in the future ….
Luo Zheng was still standing outside the city. His eyes were focused on the golden pill in his hand!
This golden pill was most likely an ultimate treasure. The power it could unleash was actually so terrifying. Fortunately, this golden pill was a one-time consumable item! But even so, if he released this golden pill when facing an enemy, he would be able to obtain a miraculous effect under unexpected circumstances!
But this golden pill could only be activated after being crushed. Could it be that this golden pill could only be used to commit suicide?
Just as he was looking at this golden pill, Luo Zheng suddenly felt that this golden pill was boiling hot. A great power was about to burst out from it. Almost as if it was a conditioned reflex, he threw this golden pill far away!
"Pu!"
After the golden pellet flew for more than ten feet in the air, a dense amount of blood-colored lightning escaped from the golden pellet!
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng's face turned ugly. If it wasn't for his quick reaction, he would have been involved in it!

Chapter 1211
This woman was wearing a smoky purple gauze overcoat, with ivory white ribbons fluttering behind her. She looked like a fairy in a painting.
She stepped on a pair of crescent moon embroidered shoes and walked along the edge of the city wall. With every step she took, a flight of stairs made of ice crystals would be constructed under her feet. Her toes would lightly borrow the force of the steps to float forward for a distance, and the ice crystal stairs would shatter with a loud bang. Therefore, every light step she took was accompanied by the crisp and melodious sound of ice crystals shattering …
"Huala … Huala …"
Looking at this woman in purple, Luo Zheng's eyes were calm, but he was very vigilant in his heart. He quietly stretched out his limbs.
Cultivation was a standard to measure a warrior's strength, but in the Upper World, this standard was very broad.
For example, a powerful Divine Sea Realm warrior might be much stronger than a Divine Extreme Realm warrior with average talent!
Before this, Luo Zheng had easily killed a Divine Extreme Realm warrior. Moreover, Luo Zheng was not the only one who could do this. Many of the core warriors in the universe, as well as the geniuses of various races, could do the same.
When Luo Zheng felt this woman's sharp coldness, he also realized that this person's strength was extraordinary. She might be the strongest warrior he had encountered since entering the Illusory Battlefield.
"The City Lord is here …"
"Is this woman the City Lord of this main city? She has such a temperament! "
"Hehe, what's so strange about a member of the Ji Family having such a temperament?"
"…"
Many warriors on the city wall saw this woman's appearance, and their eyes shone brightly. It was an expression that would only appear when they saw a perfect object and their hearts throbbed!
The woman in front of them was indeed very perfect!
In Luo Zheng's eyes, it was the same. In terms of appearance and temperament, perhaps only Xun could compete with her!
However, her temperament was completely different from Xun's.
Xun gave people a sense of supreme nobility. No matter where she stood, she was like a goddess descending to the mortal world. Her every movement revealed decisiveness and killing intent. Under that cold appearance, there was a fiery heart hidden, like a frozen volcano.
But this woman in front of them gave people a sense of tranquility and peace.
Just like the rustling snowflakes, this kind of sharp aura not only did not make people resist, but also made people calm.
However, Luo Zheng felt that under this calmness, there was a faint murderous intent hidden. This murderous intent was very arrogant, as if it had control over the lives of all living beings, and this "living beings" naturally included Luo Zheng.
The woman in purple was getting closer and closer to Luo Zheng, and the coldness that made people calm became more and more serious. The snowflakes falling around her became bigger and bigger.
Inside the city, the Illusory Battlefield........ com.............................
The temperature in the Illusory Battlefield wasn't low. To mortals, it could be considered extremely hot. However, the snowflakes didn't seem to melt. They piled up at a speed visible to the naked eye, and soon, a thin layer of snow covered the ground.
........ Through the falling snow, the woman in purple's gaze fell on Luo Zheng. Like her temperament, this woman's gaze was exceptionally pure and clear.
"Who is Jia Fan?" Luo Zheng smiled. He looked up at the woman and said, "You mean that blue-clothed Illusory Battlefield cultivator?"
The woman in purple nodded, "Yes."
"You can say that." Luo Zheng did not deny it. He pointed at the place where the golden ball had exploded and said, "But he should have committed suicide. At least I didn't obtain any fantasy points."
The blue-clothed Illusory Battlefield cultivator committed suicide, but his fantasy points were absorbed by someone else. Luo Zheng could not understand. This did not conform to the rules of the Illusory Battlefield!
"Those fantasy points were naturally absorbed by me. They should have been my fantasy points," the woman in purple said indifferently.
"How did you do it?" Luo Zheng was the most curious about this matter.
The woman in purple did not answer Luo Zheng's question. Instead, she continued to stare at Luo Zheng with her pure gaze, "You also killed Niu Jin."
Hearing this, Luo Zheng was speechless. This was something that could be asked in one sentence, but this woman had to ask two separate questions. Luo Zheng grinned and said, "Yes, I did kill him! You are the 'City Lord'! Are these two your subordinates? "
Luo Zheng's words carried a clear provocation. Although he valued this opponent, he was not afraid to fight.
The woman in purple did not answer Luo Zheng's question. She did not seem to care about what Luo Zheng said. Instead, she was completely immersed in her own thoughts, "You took half of Niu Jin's fantasy points. I have to kill you three times to earn them back. After three times, I can let you leave the main city … …"
Hearing these strange words again, Luo Zheng understood that it was impossible to communicate with this woman.
However, this didn't affect Luo Zheng's goal!
Now, it seemed that this woman was indeed the City Lord. Killing the City Lord and taking her fantasy points was Luo Zheng's only goal!
"Whoosh!"
The Thunder Wind Ghost Divine Sword was suddenly unsheathed, and the blue sword body was suffused with a faint sword light. Luo Zheng restrained his emotions, and the long sword in his hand was already placed horizontally in front of his chest, "If you can defeat this Luo, not to mention three times, even thirty times is possible."
The woman in purple indifferently glanced at Luo Zheng's sword. Although she did not know the origin of the Thundergale Ghost Sword, she knew that this sword was not ordinary. Her clear eyes revealed a trace of surprise.
"Your … surname is Luo? What is your name? "The woman in purple suddenly asked.
"Luo Tianxing," Luo Zheng reported his name.
After hearing Luo Zheng's name, the woman in purple revealed a trace of disappointment.
There were hundreds of millions of cultivators in the Illusory Battlefield. If there weren't tens of millions of cultivators with the surname Luo, there were at least millions. The possibility of encountering that guy by chance was too small …
Following which, she lightly exhaled a breath of cold air. Thinking about how that guy had dug a few holes, a trace of fire appeared in her cold heart. If that guy entered the Illusory Battlefield, he definitely wouldn't be a nobody. She should be able to find him! As for this Luo guy in front of her, since he had disobeyed the rules she had set, she naturally had to kill him!
"I … am not called 'City Lord'," the woman in purple said. Her slender fingers that were whiter than snow opened slightly. Following which, a rippling light wrapped around her fingers.
After the light dissipated, five rings appeared on her fingers!
These five rings were old in style and didn't have any decorative patterns on them. However, they were extremely incompatible with Ji Luoxue's temperament!
Between these five rings, there was a thin chain necklace. She suddenly stretched out her five slender fingers, and the thin chain began to sway unceasingly ….
At this time, Ji Luoxue continued, "My name is Ji Luoxue. Remember my name, and then … go die …"
Just as she finished speaking, the slowly falling snow suddenly sped up by more than ten times!
If it was said that a snowstorm had quietly descended on a winter night, then now it was a blizzard in the middle of winter!
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
Not only did the snowflakes become bigger, but the wind also became fiercer!
Amidst this frenzied blizzard, Ji Luoxue's figure suddenly floated towards Luo Zheng!
"Whoosh!"
This woman was as quiet as a virgin, but once she moved, she had the power of rushing thunder and was not inferior to a man in the slightest.
Her figure suddenly enlarged in Luo Zheng's eyes. In the blink of an eye, the distance between Luo Zheng and her was less than ten feet!
Within ten feet, Luo Zheng's longsword suddenly struck out!
"Serene Divine Shadow!"
"Whoosh …"
"Crackle!"
As Luo Zheng poured chaotic energy into the Thunderstorm Serene Divine Sword, a winding lightning bolt had already charged towards the woman!
After this lightning bolt, there was a sharp wind blade!
After the wind blade, there was a dense swarm of Serene Divine Shadows. Each Serene Divine Shadow used a different fist technique and attacked Ji Luoxue!
On the city wall, all the warriors stared intently at the scene before them!
Many of the warriors in the main city had seen Ji Luoxue's strength. They did not dare to cross the city wall even half an inch because they knew that only the rules of the main city could protect them!
"Why do I feel that it's a little unfair? The City Lord suddenly became kind and only killed this kid three times before letting him go?"
"The City Lord's personality has always been very strange and temperamental. I heard that it has something to do with the books she reads. She seems to be easily affected by the stories in the books!"
"Sometimes gentle, sometimes cruel … I think that this kind of personality is very charming, haha!"
"Charming? Heh, she's a prodigy of the Ji Family. Are you worthy of her? "
The battle had just begun, and many warriors took advantage of this gap to whisper among themselves.

Chapter 1212
Luo Zheng's first round of attacks was merely a test.
Although it was only a test, it wasn't something that could be casually received.
Under the full power of the Dark Lightning Wind Divine Sword, both the lightning and wind blades were extremely sharp. Under the control of Luo Zheng's mind, the Dark Divine Shadows all had different moves. Every punch and kick was meticulously planned, so it wasn't that easy to break through them.
Facing these three attacks, Ji Luoxue's originally clear eyes were tainted with a trace of turbidity!
At this moment, her right hand formed the shape of an orchid, and her slender little finger was the most prominent. The ring on her little finger also emitted a faint yellow light at this moment!

The faint yellow light gradually condensed into a giant that was several hundred feet tall and enveloped Ji Luoxue within it.
A heavy aura was transmitted along with the faint yellow light.

The foremost bolt of lightning heavily struck the light and only left a faint burn mark on it, which instantly disappeared without a trace.
As for the wind blade, it only cut a distance of an inch …
As for the Dark Divine Shadows, although their fist techniques were exquisite and their coordination was flawless, they were unable to break through this powerful defense. As for Ji Luoxue, who was within it, it was even more difficult to harm her!

After blocking Luo Zheng's attack, Ji Luoxue raised her palm towards Luo Zheng. The corners of her mouth lifted slightly, and the palm that had just been raised came crashing down from the sky!
As this palm came crashing down from the distance, the faint yellow giant that enveloped Ji Luoxue also slammed its enormous palm down!
"Rumble!"
An earth-shattering sound rang out! At the same time, it was accompanied by a violent vibration!

"This slap is too exaggerated!"

Hundreds of thousands of warriors had already gathered on the city wall. Many of them were attracted by the huge sound just now.
Although Ji Luoxue's palm strike had caused the ground several hundred kilometers away to tremble and the cracks to extend to several hundred kilometers away, the city to the side did not tremble at all. Only the sound of the strike managed to reach the city.
Ji Luoxue's eyes blinked slightly when his slap did not hit its target. He raised his hand again and slapped the air with a backhand.
The first palm came down from the top, directly covering the ground.
The second slap was like a slap to the thin man's face. It swept across with the flat of the palm.
Her slap was not fast, but the giant was countless times bigger than her. The speed of the slap was the same as her speed.
The speed was the same, but the difference between her and the giant was countless times bigger. The absolute speed of the giant's slap was naturally countless times faster!
Half of the giant's hand was buried in the ground. When the slap came across, it lifted the soil and grass on the ground and slapped towards Luo Zheng!
If this slap landed properly, Luo Zheng would be directly slapped onto the city wall. Under the pressure of the palm and the city wall, Luo Zheng would be directly smashed into pieces!
Luo Zheng naturally would not let this happen. With a kick of his legs, he rolled and easily avoided the palm that was like a city wall.
"Hmph …"
Seeing Luo Zheng's action, Ji Luoxue only snorted lightly. His other hand slapped down from the air again!
In front of the giant, Luo Zheng was like a fly. Just as he rose into the air, he was caught off guard and was slapped to the ground by the other hand.
"Your strength … is weaker than I thought. You only used the power of the earth element …"
As soon as she finished speaking, she retracted her left hand and dug fiercely downwards!
The giant's left hand dug up the soil within a radius of ten feet. Luo Zheng, who had just been slapped into the soil, naturally fell into the giant's hand.
At the same time, Ji Luoxue's right hand clapped towards her left hand. Her hands were lightly clapping, as if she was applauding for someone. If one listened carefully, one could even hear her soft clapping!
The reason why one had to listen carefully was because the giant's hands were constantly clapping together, erupting with deafening booms!
Luo Zheng was being slapped up and down by this pair of giant hands!
Every time she slapped, the warriors on the city wall could not help but feel their eyelids twitch!
"What terrifying power. That kid … has probably been smashed into meat paste?"

"Clap clap clap …"
"Rumble, rumble, rumble …"
Ji Luoxue lightly slapped the pair of clean, white, jade-like hands, while the giant slapped its own enormous hands.
Ji Luoxue's clear eyes looked through the pale yellow light and sized up the situation in the palms.
As she continued to slap, the soil on the giant palms formed by the pale yellow light gradually scattered, revealing Luo Zheng's figure …
The soil and gravel in the giant palms could help reduce some of the power of Luo Zheng. However, after the soil and gravel scattered, the giant palms actually covered them!
"Clap clap clap …"
However, as Ji Luoxue slapped the palms again and again, the surprise in her clear eyes became more and more intense!
Although Luo Zheng was being slapped up and down by the giant palms, Ji Luoxue clearly saw that Luo Zheng's aura did not weaken in the slightest. She also did not see any injuries on Luo Zheng's body!
This guy … No wonder he could easily kill Niu Jin. His body was actually so strong?
Her beautiful eyebrows slightly furrowed.
Then, she gently raised the fingers of her left hand and bound Luo Zheng to her left hand. Then, she raised her right hand and stretched out her index finger, fiercely pointing it at her palm!
After she did this, Luo Zheng lay in the giant palms. Above his head, the finger of the giant palms contained a terrifying power and pressed down on him.
The power of this finger was extremely concentrated. It was clearly much more terrifying than the two palms slapping just now!
However, at this moment, Luo Zheng smiled slightly. He suddenly raised his fist towards the sky!
Luo Zheng's fist was countless times smaller than the giant's finger. The tip of the giant's finger was several times thicker than Luo Zheng's body …
This scene was extremely shocking!
In front of the giant, Luo Zheng was just a tiny ant. An ant that could be easily crushed with the tip of a finger!

Chapter 1213

Thus, the light yellow giant that enveloped her also put its hand to its mouth, looking very comical.

The huge hand started to block Luo Zheng's advance. With each slap, a huge palm print would appear on the ground, and Luo Zheng's body would constantly tumble within it. Thus, the giant's foot ferociously kicked at Luo Zheng's face!

Ji Luoxue was slightly stunned as she gazed at the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain.
While she was stunned, the enormous Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain had already pressed down towards her!
"Bang!"
Ji Luoxue raised her hand with the intention of directly smashing the seven-colored mountain into pieces.
As she swung her fist, the giant clenched his fist at the same time and smashed it forcefully towards the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain!

Its body was extremely tough, and ordinary forces were utterly unable to destroy it, let alone the brute force of the giant.
So, merely a rumbling sound resounded, and the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain trembled in midair before continuing to press down towards the giant!
The hardness of the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain far surpassed an ordinary mountain, so its weight was naturally countless times heavier than an ordinary mountain!
So, the pressure it exerted upon the giant's shoulder was obvious!
Even Ji Luoxue who was within the giant's body was able to sense the terrifying pressure, and she was actually unable to move at all under the pressure.
Crack! Crack! Crack!
Cracks started to appear on the giant's shoulder, and it probably wouldn't be able to hold on for long before it collapsed on its own.
After the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain pressed down towards the giant, Luo Zheng had a faint smile on his face, and he seized the opportunity to shoot towards Ji Luoxue.
If this move was effective, then he would be able to annihilate her right now. I wonder how many Fantasy Points are hidden within her body ….
However, Luo Zheng hadn't been able to approach her when Ji Luoxue's gaze flashed slightly, and then she flicked the little finger on her right hand lightly. All the Earth Laws dispersed, and the giant formed from the Earth Laws had naturally vanished at this moment as well!
The Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain that had lost its support directly pressed down towards the ground!
If Ji Luoxue hadn't been able to dodge in time, then she would have probably been crushed beneath the mountain ….

Chapter 1214
The sudden appearance of the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain pressing down was indeed beyond Ji Luoxue's expectations!

The method to refine the Divine Essencefused Mountain was something Luo Zheng had found from the Ten Thousand Wonders.
The Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain's five elements were heterogeneous. No matter which law was used, it couldn't be refined …
Some cultivators in the Upper Realm had refined large mountains before. They could refine ordinary mountains and use the earth element law to collect them. Ordinary mountains couldn't be considered sturdy and were even more of a joke when used against enemies. How difficult would it be for a Divine Ocean cultivator to split open a mountain? Only a sturdy mountain like the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain could be used against enemies. However, since ancient times, how many people had been able to refine the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain?
However, the blood refining technique in the Thousand Wonders had a miraculous effect on the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain.
Luo Zheng was able to find the blood refining technique in the library because he owned this immortal abode. However, he didn't know that this technique wasn't circulated in the Upper Realm.
Ji Luoxue possessed the Five Elements Bracelet. She cultivated the cultivation method of the Five Elements, so she was extremely familiar with the Divine Essencefused Mountain. This mountain was able to evenly mix the Laws of the Five Elements together. It was also a wondrous object in the universe.
Cultivation of the five elements was to completely fuse the five types of Source Laws and transform them into Dao Arts of the five elements. This was a method to enter the Divine Dao, the Five Elements Divine Dao!
................... There were countless cultivators in the Soul Transformation stage.................. realm..
The lifespan of the trillions of creatures in the world would eventually come to an end. Even for a Celestial Venerable powerhouse, after receiving the Mandate of Heaven, their lifespan would be around 300 million years, which was less than one-tenth of an era. Even if a Celestial Venerable used all kinds of methods to avoid the descent of the Five Decay of Celestial Beings, they would still fall in the end.
It could be said that in this universe, the greatest enemy of all living beings was not a certain opponent, but themselves and time!
. There would be a day when the end of one's life would come.
Someone once said that the Five Elements Cultivation Technique was the name of a sword. This sword always floated above everyone's head. When the time was right, this sword would take the lives of many experts. No one could avoid the power of this sword. This sword was forged by the Heavenly Dao. It stood at the highest point among the many karmic laws in the universe. It was a supreme law that couldn't be violated!
But if one could surpass the Heavenly Dao and enter the Divine Dao, they could escape the fate of the Five Elements Cultivation Technique. They could escape the sword of death hanging above their heads and obtain eternal life!
The Ji Family had Ji Luoxue cultivate the Five Elements Cultivation Technique for the same reason. They hoped that she would continue on the Five Elements Divine Dao and eventually become an existence that could surpass the Heavenly Dao and become a True God.
Ji Luoxue cultivated the Five Elements Cultivation Technique to enter the Divine Dao. If she went through the Soul Transformation stage and became a Realm Lord, she might be able to refine the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain. After refining it, she would be able to extract the magnetic force within and continue refining her Five Elements Cultivation Technique.
In fact, the Ji Family had already reserved a Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain for Ji Luoxue.
This Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain was very rare in the Upper Realm. The Ji Family had paid a great price to obtain this Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain. However, she was a genius of the Ji Family. This price was nothing!
The Divine Essencefused Mountain was slightly smaller than the Divine Essencefused Mountain that Luo Zheng had summoned. Ji Luoxue had been cultivating within the Divine Essencefused Mountain before he entered the Illusory Battlefield!
With Ji Luoxue's current strength, she could shatter a small piece of the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain with a single palm strike. It was almost impossible for other Divine Sea Realm warriors to do so. Even Luo Zheng found it difficult to shatter a piece of the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain!

Luo Zheng had never cultivated the Five Elements Cultivation Technique.
Yet, he had refined the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain.
How could Ji Luoxue not be shocked?
The size of the Divine Essencefused Mountain could change at will, and Luo Zheng could control it with ease. He had completely refined the Divine Essencefused Mountain!
Perhaps even the Ji Family's ancestor, who was half a step into the Five Elements Divine Dao, was unable to refine the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain to such an extent.

Luo Zheng couldn't be blamed for this.

However, it didn't tell Luo Zheng how to use the Divine Essencefused Mountain. Thus, up until now, Luo Zheng had only used the mountain to smash people!
This method of "smashing" might be clumsy, but its actual might couldn't be underestimated. Even if Ji Luoxue was smashed at the foot of the mountain, she probably wouldn't be able to survive.
"You … What method did you use to refine the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain?"
Ji Luoxue's heart started to beat violently when she heard Luo Zheng's words!
Even though she was indifferent by nature, she couldn't suppress her excitement at this moment!
As a genius of the Ji Family, she had too many responsibilities on her shoulders. If she could refine the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain, it would be immeasurably beneficial to her cultivation of the five elements!

Even though the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain destroyed my earth element power, Ji Luoxue didn't care.
This young man's strength was not bad, but it was absolutely impossible for him to defeat me! Therefore, Ji Luoxue was curious. Where did this guy get his confidence from?

Gods Without Intentions was one of the three major human factions, and the Ji Family was the third. Not to mention a Divine Ocean Realm martial artist, the Ji Family was a colossus even compared to many races in the Upper Realm!

Luo Zheng could also teach her the blood refinement technique.

Chapter 1215
Ji Luoxue raised her wrist again. She waved her hand lightly in the air, and the thin chains hung down from the gaps between her fingers.
"Metal Element Power!"
The ring on her ring finger emitted a faint golden light that condensed into a golden sword!

Ji Luoxue made a decision in an instant!

Once she made this decision, Ji Luoxue didn't hesitate anymore. She would kill him once. If she angered him, she would just compensate him with 500,000 fantasy points!
Whoosh!
A golden wind swept out. Ji Luoxue's figure entered the golden wind and instantly disappeared before everyone's eyes.

Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh …
The moment Luo Zheng flew backward, golden swords fell from the sky like a golden rainstorm.

The golden sword rain covered a huge area. No matter how fast Luo Zheng retreated, he couldn't rush out of the sword rain in time …
However, the number of golden swords was too great. Even if Luo Zheng managed to dodge them in time, he was unable to dodge all of the golden swords!
Pfft!
As Luo Zheng dodged, a golden sword stabbed into Luo Zheng's collarbone!
If it were any other cultivator, this golden sword would be enough to completely penetrate their body and sink into the ground.
However, when this sword stabbed into Luo Zheng's body, it only cut through his skin. The tip of the golden sword was embedded in his shoulder blade!
The onlookers on the city wall sighed at Luo Zheng's tyrannical body, but Luo Zheng was slightly surprised. He was surprised that the penetrative power of the golden sword was so strong.
The golden sword had only been summoned by Ji Luoxue through one of the rings in the Five Elements Bracelet. It could be considered as a profundity of the Laws of Metal. Putting aside how powerful the golden sword in Ji Luoxue's hand was, how could the rain of golden swords that filled the sky be able to break through his God Artifact?
"Sou!"
Ji Luoxue's figure floated in the distance. Her clear and cold eyes stared at the rapidly flickering Luo Zheng as she calmly said, "If you can't hold on any longer, you can fly back to the main city. I can discuss … conditions with you!"
For ordinary martial artists, cooperating with the Ji Family was an excellent opportunity. It could indeed be considered a blessing. However, how could Luo Zheng's goal be limited to this?
"Ji Luoxue already said to let that brat raise the conditions himself, but he actually refused!"

Many martial artists couldn't help but shake their heads when they saw Luo Zheng's slightly wretched appearance.

Fresh blood flowed down his right shoulder and left leg, making Luo Zheng look somewhat wretched.

At this moment, it was as if Luo Zheng had grown a pair of eyes on his back. His body began to flutter in a spiral shape, spiraling upwards along the extended golden sword. In the process of spiraling upwards, he maintained a distance of three inches away from the ten feet long golden sword!

Since the two of them had started fighting, this was the closest they had ever been to each other!

Chapter 1216
To measure a martial artist's strength, from a macroscopic perspective, it was aura and might.
But from a microscopic perspective, it was the control of strength and speed. This was called Minute Subtlety.
When a martial artist placed a bird in his hand, just as the bird was about to fly away, he would move his hand slightly downwards. The bird would be unable to use its legs to kick and would fall back into the martial artist's hand. This was a manifestation of a martial artist's Minute Subtlety.
However, this method wasn't very advanced. Initial stage martial artists could grasp it. It was commonly known as the "Stopping Bird Technique." In other words, the bird would stop in one's hand and be unable to fly away …
Some superior martial artists could even place a bird on their head, shoulders, or chest. They could still stop the bird and prevent it from flying away.
Furthermore, not only could they stop the bird, but they could also stop flies and mosquitoes …
Mosquitoes and flies were extremely small. To sense the moment they took off and remove the force supporting them was countless times more difficult than stopping the bird.
However, compared to what Luo Zheng was doing now, this was merely a small trick.
Previously, when she was looking down at Luo Zheng from high up in the sky, she saw that Luo Zheng's movement technique gave off a slippery feeling as he casually shuttled through the golden sword rain. She had already sensed that his movement technique was extremely special.
Now, her sword suddenly pierced towards Luo Zheng. Only now did she discover the mystery behind it. This fellow maintained a distance of three inches from the descending golden sword rain and coiled around her sword. He was also three inches away, not an inch less, not an inch more. Not only had his control of strength reached the pinnacle, his control of speed had also reached the pinnacle …
To be able to control Minute Subtlety to such a degree, she, Ji Luoxue, had practically never heard of anyone!
How did this fellow do it?
Through this strange movement technique of Luo Zheng's, Ji Luoxue finally took Luo Zheng seriously for the first time and viewed him as a worthy opponent!
Whoosh!

"Whoosh!"

Among those of the same level, Luo Zheng had always been the one to pinch the swords of others. This Ji Luoxue was probably the first to pinch his own sword.
When Ji Luoxue grabbed Luo Zheng's sword, the five rings on her right hand all shone with a strange light. The light was very similar to the color of the Divine Origin Mountain. It was the power formed after the five origin laws fused together.
However, Ji Luoxue's cultivation level was insufficient, so the degree of fusion naturally couldn't compare to the Divine Origin Mountain!
Even so, the Five Elements power was already extremely powerful. In her opinion, Luo Zheng had no chance of escaping now that she had pinched his sword …
However, in the next moment, Ji Luoxue's cold eyes suddenly flashed with astonishment!

The dragon scales were lighting up one by one …
The tide-like power also rapidly surged into the Lightning-Wind Ghost Sword!

However, the power transmitted from the Lightning-Wind Ghost Sword forcefully suppressed the words Ji Luoxue originally wanted to say!
This Lightning-Wind Ghost Sword, which was only four inches wide and four feet long, contained power comparable to the weight of a star …

Among cultivators of the same level, only Ji Luoxue could look down on others. Now that she was looking up at Luo Zheng, a trace of stubbornness finally surfaced on her originally cold face.
Ji Luoxue's personality seemed to dislike competing with others, but that was because there were too few people who had the strength to compete with her …
She was a prodigy of the Ji Family, and her status was extraordinary. What could others use to compete with her?

A vortex appeared amidst this churning!
There was a limit to the speed at which vital essence could be extracted!
Therefore, when cultivators used powerful moves that consumed too much vital essence, they needed to prepare for a period of time, or even a relatively long period of time.
However, Ji Luoxue's inner world was somewhat different. Her Divine Ocean was extremely special and could empty out all the vital essence in a short period of time …
This Divine Ocean was called the Sea of Moon Tide. Among the billions of Divine Ocean Realm cultivators, she was the only one who possessed the Sea of Moon Tide!
The vital essence in her inner world turned into a huge vortex that was extracted from Ji Luoxue's body and poured into her Five Element Chain. The five rings all flashed with a resplendent light. She never relaxed her grip on Luo Zheng's sword for a moment!
Since it was a competition of strength, although Ji Luoxue was a woman, she would definitely not lose to a man!
With the support of this majestic power of the Five Elements, Ji Luoxue gradually gained the upper hand. Her figure rose higher and higher, gradually surpassing Luo Zheng.

What was the limit of this fellow's strength? His strength could actually double …

What was even more troublesome was that the vital essence in her body had been completely depleted. As the enormous vortex in her inner world absorbed vital essence, more than 99% of her vital essence sea had been depleted!
"It should be over, right?"
Luo Zheng smiled slightly. A strand of extremely lively sword intent suddenly brewed within the Thunderwind Dark Sword. This strand of sword intent had just taken form when it shot towards Ji Luoxue along the blade of the sword!

Chapter 1217

Because Luo Zheng was destined to be unable to catch her. At worst, she could just repeat it a few more times. With a flash of the golden wind, she planned to once again distance herself from Luo Zheng.
But at this moment, a shackle appeared in Luo Zheng's hand!
It was a bright silver shackle that looked new and shiny. When Ji Luoxue saw this shackle, her face flickered with a trace of confusion.
What is this guy planning to do?
Wants to use this shackle to lock me down? How is this possible ….
However, just as this thought flashed through her mind, she had just entered the golden wind and was preparing to quickly distance herself from Luo Zheng!
But one end of the shackle turned into a silver belt of light and swept towards Ji Luoxue. One end of the shackle locked onto her feet at a speed that exceeded Ji Luoxue's imagination!
And the other end was locked onto Luo Zheng's body!
Seeing this scene, Ji Luoxue instantly became anxious ….
She couldn't figure out how this shackle could be so fast no matter how hard she tried. Why couldn't she escape even with the Wind Flash, the profundity of the law of wind?
Whoosh ….
Her figure was still frantically flashing about!
But no matter how Ji Luoxue dodged, she still couldn't shake off the shackle on her feet.
She was like a bird whose feet were bound. No matter how high she spread her wings and flew, she would still be pulled back to her original place ….
Ji Luoxue naturally couldn't imagine that Luo Zheng's Life Ending Shackle could even bind a Heavenly Venerable. Although she was a prodigy of the Ji Family, no matter how strong she was, she couldn't possibly be stronger than a Heavenly Venerable, right?
This Life Ending Shackle was obtained from the fragments that Master had given her.
It could be considered a miniature version of the Life Ending Battle!
The Life Ending Battle locked several dozen people together, and in the end, only one person could survive!
And this Life Ending Shackle in Luo Zheng's hand was used to bind himself to his opponent. After being bound by the Life Ending Shackle, there was only one choice between the two parties — to kill the other party!
If the other party was stronger than Luo Zheng, using the Life Ending Shackle would definitely be an act of suicide.
But after all, the Life Ending Shackle belonged to Luo Zheng. Whether or not to bind and who to bind was up to Luo Zheng himself!
Ji Luoxue's speed was extremely fast. Luo Zheng dodged while thinking about how to break out of this situation. Suddenly, he remembered that he still had this Dharma treasure …. He had never used it before!
Whoosh —
Rays of golden wind flashed continuously.
Ji Luoxue's figure could be seen intermittently in this golden wind. But no matter how she struggled, she couldn't get more than three meters away.
No matter how fast she was, she couldn't shake off this shackle ….
Although Luo Zheng looked a little miserable, he knew that when the Life Ending Shackle locked Ji Luoxue, he had already won!
This Life Ending Shackle could only extend a distance of three meters. With a light tug, Luo Zheng pulled Ji Luoxue over by Luo Zheng's immense strength.

Chapter 1219
In the face of Ji Luoxue's five-colored palm print, Luo Zheng's face brimmed with a warm smile!
In that instant, Luo Zheng activated the power of 30,000 dragon scales!
He'd similarly slapped his palm towards Ji Luoxue as well. However, this extremely ordinary palm strike contained unimaginable strength!
Ji Luoxue used this Five Element Palm Print against Luo Zheng purely out of helplessness …
Just as some knowledgeable bystanders had said, the current Ji Luoxue was actually not a complete body!
At this moment, there was another Ji Luoxue in another major world in the Illusory Battlefield!
Two bodies in one mind …
Although this cultivation technique was rare in the universe, there were a few similar cultivation techniques.
For example, Yun Luo could even sever one million avatars.

Only then could one be considered a true cultivator of the five elements!

Countless dreamy light spots spread out, covering an area of two to three zhang!

This was too much …
Even Luo Zheng was shocked by the number of fantasy points!

Those fantasy points flew towards Luo Zheng and entered Luo Zheng's body.

This woman's accumulation had far exceeded Luo Zheng's imagination.

Amongst the trillions of warriors, the top 100,000 were extremely dazzling existences!

In every corner of the world, people were discussing fervently, and the attention paid to Luo Zheng was unprecedentedly high.

Snowy City, Ji Family.

At this moment, Ji Luoxue's ranking was very eye-catching!
She was already in the top ten of the pyramid!

Any family would throw all their resources on their Daoists!

If one wanted to destroy a powerful family, one had to first kill the Daoists of that family! If one didn't do so, that Daoist would definitely rely on his monstrous fortune and extremely powerful talent to kill his way back one day!
Therefore, to the various large families and races, the family could be destroyed, but the Daoists couldn't die. Although the Daoists were seedlings, they were the future of the family …
This saying might have some elements of mystery in it. After all, some large families already had such deep foundations. If a Daoist really died, these families with powerful foundations could still support another one.
However, from these sayings, it could be seen how much importance these families and races attached to their Daoists.
It was already very impressive for Ji Luoxue to be able to enter the top ten of the Illusory Battlefield. However, she had an innate advantage in the Illusory Battlefield.
This was because everyone else was fighting alone in the Illusory Battlefield, while Ji Luoxue had two people. Both Ji Luoxue and Ji Luoxue were extremely powerful, so the number of Illusory Points they collected was naturally twice as fast as other prodigies and even some Daoists!

Chapter 1220
The ranking competition in the Illusion Battlefield was rather intense!

For example, after Ji Luoxue controlled a main city, she collected Fantasy Points through the method of "paying taxes." Every cultivator in the main city had to pay the taxes and couldn't escape!
If she only controlled a main city, Ji Luoxue's ranking wouldn't be so high!

The points accumulated by the two of them were counted on one person!

However, at this moment, Ji Luoxue's ranking rapidly dropped! He actually slipped from the seventh place to the hundredth place!

The Ji Family patriarch said coldly, "Someone killed Xue 'er once!"
It was a pity that the people outside couldn't observe every movement in the Illusion Battlefield. He didn't know who had the strength to kill a prodigy of the Ji Family.
"Hmph, to be able to defeat Xue 'er, this person probably isn't a nobody. I wonder what backing he has to dare to provoke my niece!" An elder of the Ji Family said coldly. A large family clan was ultimately domineering. Ji Luoxue was the apple of the entire family's eye. How could they tolerate someone attacking Ji Luoxue?
"I already said that Ji Luoxue should have merged with Black Snow. Splitting into two bodies with a single thought greatly reduces Ji Luoxue's strength. Why bother using such a method! Another elder said angrily.
As Ji Luoxue's clone, Black Snow's strength was also extraordinary. The key was that after the two of them fused, Ji Luoxue would be able to display her strongest strength!
Before the opening of the Illusory Battlefield, the Ji Family had been debating whether or not to fuse them!
Fusion had its benefits. With Ji Luoxue's complete form, there were few opponents in the Illusory Battlefield. Ji Luoxue even had the strength to fight those Dao Children.
However, if two clones entered the Illusory Battlefield, the speed at which they earned Fantasy Points would double. The only drawback was that their strength would decrease significantly.
The final decision was made by Ji Luoxue himself!
In reality, it was extremely advantageous for Ji Luoxue to enter the Illusory Battlefield with his avatar.
For example, when Ji Luoxue was killed by Luo Zheng, an ordinary person would have lost half of their fantasy points.
On the other hand, Ji Luoxue had only lost half of her main body's Fantasy points, but Black Snow hadn't lost anything. Thus, she had only lost a quarter of her Fantasy points.
If Ji Luoxue was killed even once in her peak condition, her ranking wouldn't just drop to the 700th position, it would directly drop below the 200th position!
However, how many people could kill Ji Luoxue when she was in her peak condition?
The Illusory Battlefield was divided into 50 major worlds. There were probably only a dozen or so people who could defeat Ji Luoxue. These dozen or so people might not be in the same major world as Ji Luoxue. In other words, the possibility of her being killed was very small.
Thus, the question of whether it was good or bad to have a clone was indeed somewhat unsolvable.
Some of the Ji Family's elders actually started debating about this question.
"Alright …"
The Ji Family's patriarch, who was sitting at the highest point, waved his hand indifferently.
When the patriarch's voice was transmitted, the entire Ji Family became extremely quiet. Even the elders, including the patriarch, were sitting upright and still.
"Xue 'er is already very good. Furthermore, being killed once doesn't mean anything. I believe that she will climb up again," the Ji Family's patriarch said indifferently.
"Patriarch is right. We should have confidence in Luoxue. Perhaps Luoxue wasn't killed. Logically speaking, there are some blessed lands to explore in the Illusory Battlefield. She might have encountered some danger that she couldn't resist. The cultivators who die in the Illusory Battlefield can be revived. Ji Luoxue still has many chances!" the Ji Family's patriarch said indifferently.
Quite a few people nodded slightly.
The rankings in the early stages didn't mean anything. Xuanyuan Chenfeng, who had been leading all the way, had already disappeared. Now that he had caught up again, everyone still had to be patient with Ji Luoxue.
In another corner of the Illusory Battlefield …
A female cultivator holding a saber tilted her head and stood at the top of the city wall.
This cultivator's appearance was exactly the same as Ji Luoxue's. They were practically identical.
Although they looked exactly the same, their auras were completely different.
Just like how white snowflakes often fell around Ji Luoxue, this woman also had snowflakes around her. However, what fell was black snow.
Although she and Ji Luoxue were two bodies, Black Snow had her own thoughts and thoughts. Even her personality was completely different from Ji Luoxue's.
The cultivators in this main city …
Most of them had already escaped.
Ever since the Illusory Battlefield opened, Black Snow relied on her saber to mercilessly slaughter the cultivators in the main city.
She didn't care about observing how many Illusory Points the other party had or what benefits she could obtain. As long as they were cultivators outside the city, they would be sent back to the main city by her saber.
After a period of time, many cultivators gathered in the main city and didn't dare to come out again.
However, staying in the main city wasn't a solution. After a discussion, many cultivators chose to escape at the same time.
Although Black Snow was extremely powerful, it was impossible for her to capture all the cultivators in one fell swoop. She wanted as many cultivators as possible to escape.
Throughout the entire process, Black Snow killed many cultivators. However, even more cultivators chose to escape the main city. Therefore, in general, the Illusory Points that Black Snow accumulated were inferior to Ji Luoxue's.
However, Black Snow didn't care.
Even though she had two bodies, she had a primary and secondary body. The primary body was Ji Luoxue and the secondary body was Black Snow. She only needed to kill to her heart's content. She didn't need to worry about anything else.
Currently, the cultivators in the main city had either fled or scattered. The remaining cultivators couldn't escape. Furthermore, their Illusory Points had been drained dry by Black Snow. Therefore, she planned to search for the next city.
When the cultivators in the main city saw that Black Snow was preparing to leave, they secretly heaved a sigh of relief. The sooner this plague left, the better.
Just as Black Snow was about to leave, she realized that her ranking had begun to drop rapidly.
"I … was killed by someone?"
Black Snow closed her eyes.
She and Ji Luoxue were telepathically connected. After a thought, Black Snow already obtained the answer.
A cultivator called Luo Tianxing, huh …
If I want to find this fellow, I'm afraid I'll have to cross the great world. I can't do that now.
However, with this fellow's strength, I'll eventually encounter him …
When she thought of this, a clear light flashed in her turbid eyes.
…
In the main city, a human figure gradually emerged from a green ball of light!
It was Ji Luoxue, who had been killed by Luo Zheng. At this moment, she was rapidly reviving.
Shortly after, the ball of light shattered, and Ji Luoxue's figure appeared from within. She looked in the direction of the main city's exit. Her clear eyes were turbid …
"Young Master!"
"Young Master!"
Niu Jin and Jia Fan had been waiting respectfully beside the green ball of light for a long time.

Ji Luoxue nodded slightly and walked towards the city gate.
On the city wall, all the Martial Artists looked at Luo Zheng with gazes that were filled with intense reverence.
This fellow really did it. He killed the prodigy of the Ji Family!
In the eyes of these Divine Sea Realm cultivators who had already accepted their fate, it was simply incomprehensible that Luo Zheng could kill Ji Luoxue.
In their eyes, Ji Luoxue was definitely an undefeatable legend. No one in the main city was qualified to be her opponent, let alone kill her.

Chapter 1221
Such an ordinary-looking fellow was able to kill Ji Luoxue. It was undoubtedly a miracle.

"That means she's admitting defeat!"
Although Ji Luoxue lost to Luo Zheng, many cultivators believed that Ji Luoxue would not give up. After reviving, Ji Luoxue would probably challenge Luo Zheng again.
After Luo Zheng killed Ji Luoxue, he floated outside the city wall and stood on the Divine Origin Magnet Mountain. He didn't choose to escape …
