Ji Luoxue shook her head and replied firmly, "No."
The goal of the first stage of the Illusion Battlefield was to accumulate Fantasy Points as much as possible. Before she had the confidence to defeat Luo Zheng, she naturally wouldn't rashly fight with Luo Zheng again.
"However, we will have the chance to fight again sooner or later." Ji Luoxue continued, "I hope that at that time … you won't be afraid."
Saying this, Ji Luoxue revealed a pure smile. At that time, she would merge with the black snow and become a complete body. At that time, she would win this battle!
Hearing Ji Luoxue's words, Luo Zheng shrugged his shoulders. "Sure!" Then he touched his head and said, "There's one more question …"
"Speak," Ji Luoxue said calmly.
"Why were you able to absorb the blue-clothed cultivator's Fantasy Points?" Luo Zheng asked.
Luo Zheng asked this question before, but Ji Luoxue didn't answer. Now, Luo Zheng asked again. He was indeed very curious about this matter and couldn't understand how Ji Luoxue did it. No matter how strong she was, she couldn't change the rules of the Illusion Battlefield!
Ji Luoxue smiled faintly. This time, she chose to help Luo Zheng. Then, a golden pill appeared in her hand. "This golden pill contains my imprint! .... Of course... His Fantasy Points............ of his Fantasy points................. His Fantasy points................................
Hearing this, Luo Zheng came to a realization …
He patted himself on the head. How could he not have thought of such a simple matter even after racking his brains?
However, the more obvious the rules were, the harder it was for people to wrap their heads around them.
However, Ji Luoxue's method gave Luo Zheng another idea …
If this was the case, a cultivator would set up countless traps in a main city. These traps would be imprinted with their own imprint. Then, any cultivator who was killed by these traps would obtain an endless amount of fantasy points that would converge towards them! He only needed to leisurely stay in the city and collect the Fantasy Points!
If Luo Zheng could think of this, others could naturally think of it as well! There were people who did this in the Illusion Battlefield. For example, Ai Anxin.
Now, Ai Anxin's ranking was soaring!
That was how she came up with this idea …
Ai Anxin continued to wander around the Illusion Battlefield. After discovering a main city, Ai Anxin didn't choose to enter the main city to challenge the strong cultivators there.
It was not that Ai Anxin was unwilling to fight against experts, but that would waste too much time. Furthermore, there was a risk of dying. Every time he died, half of his fantasy points would be deducted. With Ai Anxin's shrewdness, this was undoubtedly a losing business.
After pondering for a while, Ai Anxin thought of a method. That was to scatter seeds!
When a new main city appeared in front of Ai Anxin, she scattered a large number of seeds. These seeds were very unique. After being scattered on the ground, they were like living insects that crawled into the ground.
Within two hours, those seeds began to germinate and grow. Finally, they grew into dandelions!
These dandelions were about the size of a fist, and there was a circle of transparent needles on the outermost layer. They looked very beautiful and sparkled with a translucent luster …
The more beautiful something was, the more deadly it was!
These dandelions were the most deadly traps. When a cultivator approached them, the fine hairs on the surface of the dandelions would rapidly explode and scatter in all directions at an extremely fast speed, entering the cultivator's body.
These tiny needles would continuously travel along the cultivator's meridians and track the True Essence aura within the cultivator's body. Finally, they would penetrate the dantian and enter the cultivator's inner world. Then, they would release a fatal poison within the cultivator's body, polluting the entire True Essence Sea!
If a cultivator didn't revolve their dantian and release their True Essence, then it would be fine. But once it was released, they would immediately die.
After scattering these dandelion seeds along the path, Ai Anxin would leave this place and continue to wander through the Illusory Battlefield in search of the next main city.
During this process, fantasy points would constantly fly into Ai Anxin's body …
Although the number of fantasy points wasn't too great, with each cluster of fantasy points being only a few dozen or a few hundred points. Occasionally, there would be several thousand points!
But within two hours, dozens of fantasy points would enter Ai Anxin's body. And during this entire process, Ai Anxin didn't experience the slightest bit of danger …
"I understand." Luo Zheng smiled faintly and leaped up from the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain. As a ray of light flickered in his hand, the enormous Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain started to spin. Finally, it constantly shrunk and transformed into a miniature mountain that landed in Luo Zheng's hand.
Ji Luoxue's heart was filled with unwillingness when she saw the refined Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain. Previously, she had planned to use Luo Zheng as a bargaining chip to force him to hand over the method to refine the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain. Now, it seemed that her way of thinking was childish. He didn't even need her to let him go …
After Luo Zheng put away the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain, he glanced indifferently at Ji Luoxue and then prepared to fly into the distance.
The Illusory Battlefield was so large. Every cultivator had different methods of accumulating fantasy points. Luo Zheng possessed the Teleportation Token, which was a huge advantage for him.
Traveling between different main cities and hunting the "fat sheep" among them was also an effective method!
Ji Luoxue was the biggest fat sheep in this main city …
Luo Zheng had snatched away so many fantasy points from her, so she probably wouldn't fight again. Luo Zheng also didn't need to stay here and waste time. He could choose the next main city.
Seeing that Luo Zheng was about to leave, Ji Luoxue pursed her lips and finally spoke. "Can … you wait?"
Luo Zheng turned around and looked at Ji Luoxue with a puzzled gaze.
"I can make a deal with you!" Ji Luoxue said with a true essence sound transmission.
The method to refine the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain was too important to Ji Luoxue. If she refined her own Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain, it would be greatly beneficial to her cultivation of the five elements!
"What deal?" Luo Zheng replied.
As Ji Luoxue's thin lips moved slightly, a trace of interest appeared on Luo Zheng's face!
After 30 minutes, Luo Zheng appeared in a courtyard in a corner of the main city.
Stepping on the thick layer of snow, Luo Zheng felt the tranquility of this courtyard. He couldn't help but sigh in his heart. Ji Luoxue was able to leisurely earn so many fantasy points? People were really different. He worked himself to death fighting others outside, but the fantasy points he earned weren't even a tenth of what she earned!
Ji Luoxue stood in the center of the courtyard. She held a row of blank jade slips in her hand, and her index finger was constantly moving around the jade slips.
After a long time, Ji Luoxue's delicate voice sounded. She spat out the word "seal", and a jade slip was completed. She held the jade slip in her hand and said to Luo Zheng, "This jade slip only teaches you how to refine the origin magnetic force in the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain. As for how to use it, it's up to you to comprehend it yourself!"
Luo Zheng nodded and extended his hand towards Ji Luoxue.
Ji Luoxue originally wanted to hand over the jade slip, but a trace of hesitation appeared on her face …

Chapter 1222
Ji Luoxue didn't have any way to restrain Luo Zheng.
The method to use the magnetic force was a secret of the Ji Family. The ancestor of the Ji Family had taught it to Ji Luoxue in private!
She usually cultivated the five elements and sat quietly in the Divine Magnetic Mountain. She used this method to draw the magnetic force around her body and used it for cultivation.
But the Origin Magnetic Divine Mountain was incomparably heavy. Without refining it, it was impossible for her to carry the Origin Magnetic Divine Mountain everywhere like Luo Zheng.
Ji Luoxue and Luo Zheng hadn't even met before. Normally, she wouldn't casually teach this method to an outsider!
However, Luo Zheng's refining method was of great importance!
She couldn't just watch Luo Zheng leave. She could only grit her teeth and engrave the method on a jade slip. She chose to exchange it with Luo Zheng …
Seeing the hesitation on Ji Luoxue's face, Luo Zheng had a faint smile on his face. How could he not see through Ji Luoxue's thoughts?
However, Luo Zheng didn't express his opinion. He just wanted to see how this woman would choose.
If this woman didn't believe him, then Luo Zheng could flick his sleeve and leave. Since he already knew that the Origin Magnetic Force within the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain could be utilized, then he could enter the Book Collection Pavilion to search for it. With the vast reserves of the Book Collection Pavilion, finding this method would presumably not be difficult.
However, Luo Zheng was currently in the Illusory Battlefield and couldn't return to the immortal mansion. If he could obtain this method from this woman, the Divine Magnetic Mountain could become a powerful weapon for Luo Zheng.
Ji Luoxue hesitated for a moment before saying cautiously, "You can't teach this method to others. If you do, you'll be hunted down by the Ji Family. You won't be able to escape even if you flee to the ends of the earth!"
Hearing this, Luo Zheng couldn't help but roll his eyes in his mind …
This kind of threat might be effective against other Divine Sea Realm warriors, but it was useless against Luo Zheng. He had killed four Celestial Venerables. All the major races in the universe had sent people to guard the entrance of his immortal mansion. One more Ji Family didn't seem to matter.
After Ji Luoxue's warning, she handed the jade slip to Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng held the jade slip in his hand and injected his perception into it. Then, he began to rapidly memorize the incantation.
Just as Ji Luoxue had said, this technique was able to help him extract the Origin Magnetic Energy within the Divine Origin Mountain and utilize it for his own use ….
After memorizing it, Luo Zheng also understood something.
Seeing Luo Zheng's appearance, Ji Luoxue's clear eyes revealed a trace of nervousness.
Although she was indifferent by nature, this spell was still passed down from the Ji Family's ancestor. Passing it down to an outsider was indeed a lot of pressure.
If this Luo Zheng were to flick his sleeves and run away, she would want to cry but have no tears!
However, this didn't happen in the end. Although the blood sacrifice method to refine the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain was precious, it wasn't of much use to the current Luo Zheng. After all, he had already refined the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain.
Moreover, even if he imparted it to Ji Luoxue, she wouldn't leave the Illusory Battlefield right now. She wouldn't bring the Divine Origin Magnet Mountain to cause trouble for him.
"The five elements can't invade this Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain, but there's a method that can refine it. This method is called 'blood sacrifice' …."
Luo Zheng didn't inscribe the method of blood sacrifice into a jade slip, and he directly used True Essence voice transmission to explain it to Ji Luoxue.
With Ji Luoxue's memories, she was able to recite it backwards.
As Luo Zheng explained the method recorded in the Myriad of Wonders word by word, Ji Luoxue's gaze grew brighter and brighter, and her eyes revealed a watery color that seemed like two clear and translucent gems.
After he finished speaking, Luo Zheng asked. "Have you memorized it well?"
Ji Luoxue nodded, yet she sighed with emotion. "I wonder who invented this method of blood sacrifice to actually have such a thought …." At this moment, Ji Luoxue had a feeling in her heart. The person that invented the method of blood sacrifice seemed to have grasped the laws of all things, and only such a figure was able to come up with such a method!
This person's wisdom and vast knowledge were probably even greater than her Ji Family's ancestor!
Luo Zheng couldn't answer this question. "I've already given you the method of blood sacrifice. You have to fulfill the other condition as well."
"Of course …."
Not long after, outside the main city, two cultivators stood before Luo Zheng.
These two cultivators clearly revealed indignant expressions, and they glared fiercely at Luo Zheng ….
They didn't know what kind of deal their Young Master had made with Luo Zheng, and he actually wanted them to hand over the Fantasy Points that they had painstakingly collected to Luo Zheng!
Their strength far exceeded that of ordinary Divine Sea Realm cultivators, but since entering the Illusory Battlefield, they had silently collected Fantasy Points for Ji Luoxue, and then they were willing to let Ji Luoxue kill them and hand over the Fantasy Points to Ji Luoxue.
But now, Ji Luoxue had ordered them to let this kid kill them, and they weren't allowed to fight back!
How could they be willing?
Unfortunately, they had no choice. They couldn't disobey their Young Master's orders. Perhaps the deal between this kid and their Young Master was of great importance, so their Young Master had no choice but to make such a concession.
"Pfff pfff …."
As Luo Zheng punched out twice, the two cultivators immediately died, and two large balls of Fantasy Points of Light exploded.
Each ball of Fantasy Points was about 200,000. This way, Luo Zheng had more than 400,000 Fantasy Points out of thin air.
After absorbing these Fantasy Points, Luo Zheng's ranking rose again.
After Luo Zheng killed Ji Luoxue earlier, his ranking was already at the 309th position, and because of these 400,000 plus Illusory Points, Luo Zheng had charged into the 300th position and was ranked at the 279th position in this world. On the other hand, in the entire Illusory Battlefield, Luo Zheng was ranked at the 1096th position, and he was only a step away from the top 10,000!
There were too many cultivators participating in the Illusory Battlefield this time. Even Luo Zheng wasn't confident that he could reach first place. But since Luo Zheng had come to the Illusory Battlefield, he was bound to strive for this goal.
Perhaps Luo Zheng would fail at the final step, but throughout the entire process, he wouldn't relax at all. This had always been Luo Zheng's personality.
After killing these two cultivators, Luo Zheng didn't stay any longer and drifted away.
Ji Luoxue's originally pure expression became even more complicated as she watched Luo Zheng's figure disappear into the distance.
We should still have a chance to fight in this Illusory Battlefield …
The entire Illusory Battlefield's first stage was a competition of the number of Fantasy Points collected. But since it was divided into 50 great worlds, the top prodigies would definitely have an ultimate clash in the final stage.
Ji Luoxue was very confident. She believed that she would reach the final stage!
As for Luo Tianxing … With his strength, as long as he didn't encounter those Daoists from other races, he would probably be able to reach the final stage.
At that time, Ji Luoxue would fuse with the black snow and achieve perfection. She would face the various prodigies and Daoists in this universe in her strongest state. Of course, there was also Luo Tianxing!
Luo Zheng traveled through the air for a period of time. After confirming that no one was following him, he took out the Teleportation Token.
Buzz … Buzz … Buzz …
As Luo Zheng activated the Teleportation Token, he once again escaped into a strange space.
The Fantasy Points in this Illusory Battlefield were initially scattered. They were evenly distributed to every cultivator in the form of 100 Fantasy Points per person.
But as time passed, the Fantasy Points began to converge.
The Fantasy Points that "Grass" possessed were transferred to "Sheep", and the Fantasy Points of "Sheep" were plundered by "Wolves".
In the end, all the Fantasy Points of "Wolves" would be devoured by "Lions" and "Tigers"!
If one wanted to rank at the top, they had to continuously collect Fantasy Points!
Perhaps Luo Zheng now had 5,000,000 Fantasy Points and could stand around the 10,000th rank.
But if Luo Zheng were to stop collecting Fantasy Points, he would probably fall down by thousands or even tens of thousands of ranks in just a few hours. This was because the other martial artists weren't idling around either. Every martial artist was doing their best to climb up the ranks!
After entering this strange space, Luo Zheng's gaze swept across the map below. After choosing a main city, he once again descended into it!
Whoosh …
As Luo Zheng fell all the way down, he landed heavily on the ground. What entered his eyes was a stretch of emerald green.
On the towering trees that reached into the clouds, there were many treehouses surrounded by greenery. The city walls were formed by rows of intertwined roots of the old trees. Dense vitality was injected into the city walls …
"The main city of the Fiend Night Clan?" Luo Zheng's eyes flashed slightly.
It didn't matter to Luo Zheng what race it was. In any case, he only had one goal, and that was to collect Fantasy Points.
However, Luo Zheng didn't expect to meet an old acquaintance in this city.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1223
The two main cities that Luo Zheng had been to had different situations.
In truth, it was also related to the personalities and habits of the various races …
The Demon Race was naturally aggressive. Since they had entered the Illusion Battlefield, they had completely unleashed their bestial nature and fought fiercely against each other.
On the other hand, the human race was in a state of 'centralization'. For example, in Ji Luoxue's main city, although there were small fights between the cultivators, most of the Fantasy Points were taken by Ji Luoxue's eight subordinates, and the Fantasy Points obtained by her subordinates were given to Ji Luoxue.
She was the true City Lord. Everyone could challenge each other, but they all had to 'pay' to her.
There was a certain amount of order in the human race's main cities, but this order was maintained under Ji Luoxue's high pressure. If not for Ji Luoxue, the human race's main cities would have also entered a state of chaotic fighting.
However, the main city of the Yao Night Clan was completely in a state of order!
The Yao Night Clan was originally a race that obeyed order. There were very few conflicts between the clansmen, and they were usually united against outsiders.
There were not many Yao Night Clan cultivators in the main city. Nearly half of the Yao Night Clan cultivators had left the main city to explore the boundless wilderness outside the main city. They killed the monsters in the wilderness to obtain Fantasy Points.
Of course, the Yao Night Clan would also challenge each other. However, they would only fight outside the city after the other party agreed. Outsiders would definitely not participate, nor would they suddenly attack …
Seeing how orderly they were, Luo Zheng felt that it wasn't appropriate for him to directly go up and obtain Fantasy points.
Although there were large numbers of Yao Night Clan cultivators roaming outside the city, they were not too wary of Luo Zheng. However, in an orderly environment, people would involuntarily follow order.
There were very few Demon Race and Human Race cultivators going in and out of the city. However, they also followed the rules and did not randomly steal other people's Fantasy Points.
"It seems that I've come to the wrong place …"
Luo Zheng revealed a depressed expression. The Teleportation Token's greatest use was to allow him to teleport between the various main cities.
Under such circumstances, Luo Zheng only needed to constantly switch between main cities and harvest the experts in those main cities to obtain a considerable amount of Fantasy Points.
However, most of the cultivators in the main city of the Fiend Night Clan had gone out to hunt Vicious Beasts in the wilderness, so they didn't have many Fantasy Points. Or it could be said that most of the Fantasy Points were spread out among the cultivators and weren't gathered together …
Luo Zheng found a corner and once again took out the teleportation token. At this moment, this white jade teleportation token was still shrouded in a layer of black aura.
Luo Zheng shook his head. "This Teleportation Token can't be used continuously. After using it once, it needs to wait for the black aura to disperse before it can be used again. Now, even if we want to leave, we can't."
However, he remembered that there were two other cities to the north and northwest of this main city. Those two cities weren't far from here, so he should be able to rush over with some time.
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng didn't stay here any longer. He left the city gate and headed north …
He hoped that the main city in the north was a city of the Demon Race or some other race. Only places where the battle was more intense would have more Fantasy Points!
However, just as Luo Zheng left the city, he discovered that there were many cultivators gathered at the side of the main city!
Most of them were cultivators from the Fiend Night Clan. In addition, there were two or three human cultivators mixed in with them. They stood there and looked into the distance as if they were waiting for something.
This matter didn't have much to do with Luo Zheng. The strength of these cultivators was ordinary. Even if he killed them all, he would only be able to obtain twenty to thirty thousand Fantasy Points. If he attacked, he would probably be surrounded and attacked by them.
After thinking about it, Luo Zheng prepared to leave.
However, just as Luo Zheng was about to leave, he saw three streaks of light fly over. They were three cultivators from the Fiend Night Clan.
"There is a forest eight thousand miles away from the north gate. There are many iron-scaled spiders living in the forest. Killing one iron-scaled spider will give you 80 fantasy points. It is recommended that 20 martial artists form a party and travel there …"
After the Fiend Night Clan cultivators finished speaking, the cultivators gathered together and formed a group to head into the forest to kill the Iron Scale Spiders.
"This … is this still the Illusory Battlefield?"
When he saw this harmonious scene, Luo Zheng suddenly realized that his understanding of the Illusory Battlefield was somewhat narrow.
If the Illusory Battlefield was purely a battle between cultivators, there would be countless arenas set up for hundreds of millions of cultivators to fight.
The Illusory Battlefield was divided into fifty great worlds. Apart from the various main cities, there was still a vast space for people to explore.
Previously, when Luo Zheng was outside the Demon Race's city, didn't he rely on the Heavenly Mouse to obtain a lot of Fantasy Points?
Logically speaking, in this vast world, there should be countless secret places for cultivators to explore …
There were many ways to obtain Fantasy Points. The cultivators from the Fiend Night Clan clearly used another method. They divided up the work and cooperated to continuously explore this space, which also allowed them to accumulate a lot of Fantasy Points.
What Luo Zheng didn't know was that there was a cultivator ranked ninety-seventh on the leaderboard who had entered the top one hundred without killing a single person.
It wasn't that this person was weak. On the contrary, this person's strength didn't lose to the geniuses of the various clans at all. It was just that from the very beginning, he had been exploring outside the main city and had killed a large number of Vicious Beasts. There were even a few extremely powerful Divine Beasts among them. Naturally, the number of Fantasy Points he obtained was considerable!
The three cultivators from the Fiend Night Clan who had appeared just now had been exploring outside the main city. After discovering their target, they had returned to gather the other cultivators.
The Iron Scale Spider wasn't considered a very powerful Vicious Beast, so there were many cultivators who responded. Nearly half of the cultivators who had gathered were willing to go. Only a small portion of the cultivators remained silent, as if they didn't care about the eighty Fantasy Points.
The second cultivator from the Fiend Night Clan continued, "In the northwest, thirteen thousand kilometers away, there is an abandoned tower. There are many evil creatures in the tower. I killed the Shining Shadow Evil Spirit on the lowest floor and obtained three hundred Fantasy Points … We need at least nine people to go together. This tower has a total of seven floors."
The Shining Shadow Evil Spirit on the first floor was worth three hundred Fantasy Points. Without a doubt, this abandoned tower was much more difficult than the forest.
"I'll go!"
"I …"
"I'll go too!"
Even so, there were still many cultivators who were willing to go together. The two human cultivators also expressed their willingness to go together.
Luo Zheng also revealed an expression of interest …
In a situation where he couldn't use the Teleportation Token, he had originally planned to head to the two cities in the north. If he followed them to this abandoned tower, it wouldn't be a bad idea.
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng also decided to join this group.
But before Luo Zheng could say anything, the third cultivator from the Fiend Night Clan spoke again.
"In the north, twenty-four thousand kilometers away, there is a Withered Tree Gate that requires emergency rescue. If you are willing to join and successfully rescue Liu Yu and Aizen, you will receive a share of Fantasy Points. This share is worth five hundred thousand Fantasy Points!"
Many of the cultivators present were moved when they heard the Yao Night Clan cultivator's words.
Five hundred thousand Fantasy Points was a lot for many cultivators. This was enough for them to enter the top ten million in the Dream Battlefield!
But this Withered Tree Gate was a very dangerous place. Why would they need emergency rescue?
One cultivator asked, "Didn't Aizen and Liu Yu lead the elites of the main cities? Did they fail? "
"No." The cultivator shook his head, "They didn't fail. They were just ambushed by another group. Most of them were demons. Aizen and Liu Yu are trapped in the Withered Tree Gate. The situation is very dangerous!"
"Demons again!"
"A despicable race that only knows how to plunder …"
"I'm willing to go to the Withered Tree Gate!"
It wasn't that the Fiend Night Clan didn't have the strength to plunder other people's Fantasy Points. It was just that this race didn't like to fight or expand.
This was why this powerful race only occupied three hundred great worlds in the upper realm. Their territory didn't match their strength!

Chapter 1224
When Luo Zheng ascended, Liu Yu probably hadn't.
However, the Ascension Passage had been repaired at that time, so there shouldn't have been any obstacles to ascension. That was why the Great Thousand World welcomed a wave of ascension.
Many Divine Ocean Realm cultivators in the lower realms chose to ascend.
Later, Luo Zheng searched through the memories of the Great Thousand World, but he couldn't find any trace of Liu Yu on the Sea God Continent. She probably chose to ascend.
After all, Liu Yu's talent wasn't bad. Although she only stood in the second row of the Talent Stele, the Demi-Immortal Clan had gathered geniuses from many different clans. Even in the upper realms, Liu Yu's talent was quite good.
However, the upper realms were so vast that even Luo Zheng couldn't find any trace of her.
Now that he suddenly heard the name Liu Yu, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed.
"Is this Liu Yu the same person as her?"
There were so many Divine Ocean Realm cultivators, so it was inevitable that there would be people with the same name.
However, the Withered Tree Gate's direction was the same as Luo Zheng's route. What's more, the Withered Tree Gate offered five hundred thousand fantasy points!
Even Luo Zheng, who had several million fantasy points, was tempted.
However, because the reward was so high, the difficulty must be quite high as well. Not to mention that they had to face another team. There shouldn't be many cultivators who were qualified.
"Take me with you. I'm willing to help Aizen and Liu Yu!" A cultivator stepped forward and said sincerely.
The woman from the Yao Night Clan glanced at the cultivator and shook her head. "You … aren't strong enough. You'll only be wasting the fantasy points you've worked so hard to accumulate."
"I …" The cultivator's face reddened.
The woman didn't care about the cultivator's embarrassment. She glanced around and asked, "Who else thinks he's strong enough to come with me?"
"Me!"
A male cultivator from the Yao Night Clan jumped down from the city wall.
This cultivator was dressed in a pitch-black hedgehog robe, and a confident look appeared on his face. After leaving the city wall, he flew towards them.
The woman sized him up for a moment before she nodded, "You can do it!"
"Me too." A long-haired woman from the Nightfiend clan walked out from the crowd. She had red hair and seemed to be brimming with vitality. She carried a dark green bow on her back, and her bowstring radiated with the vigorous energy of the Laws of Life. It was extremely eye-catching.
Not long after, three Demon Night Clan cultivators chose to join.
Luo Zheng, who had been observing from the side for a long time, also slowly walked over and said loudly, "Can I participate?"
As soon as Luo Zheng said that, many Demon Night Clan cultivators looked at Luo Zheng.
The woman from the Demon Night Clan sized Luo Zheng up, and a trace of caution appeared on her face …
Although Luo Zheng didn't release his aura, he didn't conceal it either. After sizing him up, she could faintly sense that this human cultivator wasn't simple.
At the very least, she couldn't see through Luo Zheng's strength at all. A cultivator who could do this was definitely not simple.
"Human cultivator, what kind of joke is this? The Withered Tree Gate is a secret area that belongs to our Yao Night Clan. Other clans can't enter!"
"I also object. We don't need other clans' cultivators. Moreover, we still have to fight against the Demon Race this time …"
Several Demon Night Clan cultivators voiced their objections at the same time.
In this endless wilderness, there were also countless secret areas, just like the cave that Luo Zheng had entered before. After passing through the Inner Courtyard of the Eighteen Arhats, one would be able to obtain an extremely powerful item like the Teleportation Token.
The Withered Tree Gate was the same. Although it was extremely difficult to enter, one could obtain a large number of Fantasy Points from it!
Moreover, these secret areas could be entered repeatedly. As long as one's strength was enough, they could enter the Withered Tree Gate again and again to obtain Fantasy Points …
In fact, Luo Zheng's Teleportation Token could be obtained repeatedly as well.
However, one of these functional items was enough. After all, entering the cave required 300,000 Fantasy Points.
The Withered Tree Gate was firmly controlled by the Yao Night Clan of this main city. How could they easily give it to other clans?
However, this time, the Demon Race had set their sights on it, so they had no choice but to return to the city and ask for help!
When he saw the Yao Night Clan cultivators' objections, Luo Zheng's expression fell silent. He had already made up his mind.
Regardless of whether that person was Liu Yu or not, Luo Zheng would still head to the Withered Tree Gate to see what was going on.
If the Yao Night Clan cultivators didn't agree, Luo Zheng would just follow them. He didn't believe that they could shake him off.
Just as he was thinking this, the female Yao Night Clan cultivator in the lead said, "Human cultivators can enter the Withered Tree Gate with us!"
When the other Yao Night Clan cultivators heard this, they immediately objected!
"Didn't we agree before? The Withered Tree Gate is exclusively controlled by our Yao Night Clan. In fact, we were the first to discover it! "
"Why should we allow an outsider to enter?"
"If you bring this human cultivator with you, Aizen will definitely blame you!"
Hearing the objections, the female Yao Night Clan cultivator in the lead shook her head. She had returned from the Withered Tree Gate, so she naturally knew how serious the situation was.
If this human cultivator in front of her could come with them, he would be a great help.
"If Aizen blames us, I will take responsibility." The female Yao Night Clan cultivator walked towards Luo Zheng with a resolute expression and said, "My name is Bai Wei! I welcome you to join us! "
Luo Zheng smiled and nodded at Bai Wei. "Luo Tianxing."
When they saw that they couldn't stop Luo Zheng from joining them, the Yao Night Clan cultivators were extremely displeased.
"Why did you let a random human cultivator join us? I don't know what you're thinking, Bai Wei!"
"This brat is most likely a burden. Why bother bringing a burden to the Withered Tree Gate?"
When he heard this, Bai Wei coldly said to the Yao Night Clan cultivator, "If this brat is a burden, why are you afraid of him entering the Withered Tree Gate?"
Since he was a burden, he was definitely not strong enough. Even if he entered the gate, he would be easily killed …
When he heard Bai Wei's question, the Yao Night Clan cultivator was speechless and didn't say anything else.
As for Luo Zheng, he stood to the side with a faint smile on his face. He didn't say a word.
After fifteen minutes, Bai Wei had recruited seven cultivators from outside the main city. Only Luo Zheng was a human. The other six were all from the Yao Night Clan. Moreover, they were all outstanding members of the Yao Night Clan and were all extraordinarily strong.
Then, under Bai Wei's lead, the eight of them headed north.
Along the way, when the six Yao Night Clan cultivators glanced at Luo Zheng, there was a trace of disdain and vigilance on their faces.
Even though the Yao Night Clan had a good relationship with the human race, they were still a foreign race. It was inevitable for them to be vigilant.
This was because the Yao Night Clan was tolerant. If it were the Demon Race or the human race, they would have already fought with Luo Zheng. How could they possibly bring Luo Zheng along?
Whoosh whoosh …
The eight of them were flying rapidly in the air!
The speed of a Divine Sea Realm cultivator was extremely fast. 24,000 miles was extremely far for an ordinary person, but for a Divine Sea Realm cultivator, it was just a short distance. It should be known that when Luo Zheng was in the One Thought Sealed Tower, he had traveled almost 40,000 miles …
Four quarters of an hour later …
Withered trees began to appear in the wilderness below.
These trees had completely withered and were all alone in the ground. They were like lifeless tree stumps.
"These trees … seem to contain vitality," Luo Zheng said as he stared at the trees.
Bai Wei had been flying beside Luo Zheng. When he heard this, he smiled and said, "Yes, these trees have all become spirits. If you enter this forest, they will rise from the ground and attack."
"Is that so?" Luo Zheng's eyes flashed.
"However, although there are many of these Withered Tree Spirits, the reward is too little. Killing one Withered Tree Spirit only gives you two Fantasy Points," Bai Wei added.
"So that's how it is …" Luo Zheng was still thinking that with so many Withered Wood Spirits below, wouldn't the entire ground be filled with Fantasy Points? He didn't expect that the reward for killing these Withered Wood Spirits was too little. Two Fantasy Points … Even if he killed tens of thousands of Withered Wood Spirits, it would only be 20,000 Fantasy Points.

Chapter 1225
These two huge withered trees towered into the clouds, and a huge whirlpool appeared between them.
At this moment, Bai Wei led the charge and warned them, "Once you enter that vortex, you'll enter the Withered Tree Door. But be careful, the Withered Tree Door will attack you!"
Before she could finish her sentence, the two huge withered trees suddenly started to shake!
"Cough cough cough cough cough …."
The two giant trees started to shake, and the branches of the trees started to shake violently as well.
From afar, the giant withered tree seemed to move very slowly. However, when Bai Wei led the group closer, the withered branches whipped towards them at an extremely fast speed. Furthermore, the withered branches shimmered with a mysterious dark green light. It was the power of the Laws of Life!
"Whoosh whoosh ~ ~ ~"
The branches whipped towards them, and they were accompanied by a sharp whistling sound. Even space itself was trembling, so one could only imagine the power contained within them.
"Be careful!"
Compared to the giant Withered Wood Spirit, these eight martial artists were like eight tiny flies, constantly shuttling back and forth between the withered branches!
"Whoosh ~ ~ ~"
Accompanied by a whistling sound, a thick branch whipped towards Luo Zheng.
Facing the whip of the withered branch, Luo Zheng just lowered his body slightly and bypassed it from below.
However, as soon as he dodged the branch, 3 more branches whipped towards him from above, below, and above ….
Even though the branches were powerful, Luo Zheng didn't use the Drifting Smoke Technique. Instead, he used the Chasing Cloud movement technique to quickly pass through the gaps between the branches. Luo Zheng was able to deal with such an attack with ease.
However, right at this moment, a muffled groan sounded out. It was the woman from the Fiendnight Clan who carried the green longbow on her back who had been struck by a branch!
"Slap!"
The woman from the Demon Night Clan fainted from the impact, causing her to tumble about in the air.
Before the woman with the longbow could land on the ground, 3 more branches stretched out from nearby, and they actually wrapped around the woman and pulled her towards the Withered Tree Spirit.
Bai Wei's face froze when he saw this, and he shouted loudly, "Save her!"
At this moment, the two Demon Night Clan cultivators beside him charged towards the longbow-wielding woman from the left and right, trying to rescue her from the withered branch.
However, just as they rushed forward, ten withered branches appeared in front of them and forcefully forced them to retreat!
Even though the other cultivators from the Fiendnight Clan wanted to help, they were too far away ….
The members of the Fiendnight Clan revealed gloomy expressions. They'd come to rescue the woman, but they hadn't even entered the Withered Tree Door, and one of them was already dead.
Even though they wouldn't really die in the Illusory Battlefield, they'd come to rescue. So the more, the better.
"Sigh …."
At this moment, the members of the Fiendnight Clan members of the Fiendnight Clan.
Then, they saw a person turn into a black line and dash toward the longbow-wielding woman at an extremely fast speed!
"Luo Tianxing, it's useless! Come back quickly! "Bai Wei advised.
The branches of the Withered Tree Spirit were of different lengths, and the closer one got to the Withered Tree Spirit, the more branches there were!
Before entering the Withered Tree Door, everyone had tried their best to stay between the two giant Withered Tree Spirits. That way, they could avoid most of the branches ….
Even though the Withered Tree Spirit looked old and mottled, it was actually quite intelligent. It had purposely dragged a person away without killing him in order to lure other cultivators to save him. That way, it would be able to kill even more cultivators!
The first time they'd entered the Withered Tree Door, one of the members of the Fiendnight Clan had been dragged into the Withered Tree Spirit as well. After that, because they'd tried to save each other, they'd all been wiped out. Everyone had left half of their Illusory Points in the Withered Tree Door before being sent back to the main city.
That was why when the woman with the longbow had been dragged into the Withered Tree Spirit, Bai Wei had called for someone to save her. However, now that she'd been dragged into the Withered Tree Spirit, Bai Wei had tried to stop Luo Zheng from saving her.
However, Luo Zheng turned a deaf ear to Bai Wei's words and continued charging forward!
"Hu hu hu hu …."
When Luo Zheng approached the woman with the longbow, countless whistling sounds could be heard. Fifty to sixty branches flew towards Luo Zheng.
"Ka ka ka …."
All the branches wrapped around Luo Zheng and the woman with the longbow in an instant, wrapping the two of them tightly. It was like a giant cocoon made of branches!
As the branches shrank, the giant cocoon slowly shrank back towards the Withered Tree Spirit's main body.
"This human cultivator is an idiot. I told him not to save her, but he still foolishly charged forward!" a member of the Fiendnight Clan said expressionlessly.
"He's not bad, but he's a little stupid …."
"He's not of much use anyway. He can only be considered a burden. It's good that he left. He probably doesn't have many Illusory Points, so it's not a pity if he dies."
Bai Wei looked at the giant cocoon with a complicated expression. She had a faint premonition that this person wouldn't die because of this!
Her premonition was relatively accurate. When she first saw Luo Zheng, she felt that he wasn't ordinary. Although she couldn't estimate Luo Zheng's true strength, she felt that he was at least as strong as Liu Yu. Of course, he definitely couldn't compare to Aizen ….
Would he really be sent back to the main city like this?
However, just as Bai Wei thought this, she heard a faint sound.
"Ka …."
This sound came from the giant cocoon.
"Ka ka ka …."
The sound grew louder and louder, but she didn't know what was happening inside.
"Boom!"
She heard a loud sound!
The wooden cocoon formed by the withered branches instantly exploded. Then Bai Wei saw Luo Tianxing grab the longbow-wielding woman and run towards them!
Bai Wei's eyes widened when she saw this scene. She almost forgot to dodge when a withered branch came whipping at her.
This guy ….
Don't underestimate these branches. Although they looked like ordinary branches, the rich power of the Law of Life gave them unimaginable toughness.
Swords and blades couldn't break them, water and fire couldn't hurt them. Even an ordinary Divine Weapon wouldn't be easy to cut open one of these branches.
Just now, Luo Tianxing and the longbow-woman were wrapped in layers of branches. It was almost impossible for them to break free. Aizen was also entangled and couldn't break free.
But Luo Zheng only used two or three breaths of time to break free from the cocoon. How could Bai Wei not be surprised?
The other Demon Night Clan cultivators were the same ….
Just a breath ago, they were still mocking Luo Zheng, but then something so incredible happened. For a moment, their expressions were very unnatural.
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh ~"
Sharp whistling sounds surrounded Luo Zheng's ears.
He held the longbow woman in his arms, but his expression was still calm. Facing the branches whipping towards him, Luo Zheng didn't dodge. Instead, he punched out whenever a branch came close!
"Pa!"
Following a series of crisp sounds, the withered branches were shattered by Luo Zheng's attack.
With Luo Zheng's strength, he could easily tear apart these two giant branches with his bare hands ….
It's just that these two branches originally formed the Withered Tree Gate. He was afraid that if he tore them apart, the gate would disappear, so he suppressed this idea in his heart.
As for these branches, why would Luo Zheng care about them?
"Continue to rush over! Let's go! " Bai Wei was shocked, but she also understood that she chose the right person. This person's strength was much stronger than she imagined!
It seems that Aizen and Liu Yu's predicament can be solved!
But Luo Tianxing is an outsider. Will he lead the human race to take over the Withered Tree Gate?
At this time, Bai Wei's heart was filled with both joy and worry ….
The Withered Tree Gate is indeed a holy land for collecting fantasy points. Aizen and Liu Yu used this gate to enter the top three thousand of this year's Fantasy Gathering. No matter who it is, this place is very important.
But since she brought Luo Zheng here, she had no choice but to let him do as he wished.
But since this person was willing to save her Demon Night Clan member, his character is good. Now she can only pin her hopes on him.

Chapter 1226
They didn't waste any more time and entered the giant whirlpool between the two Withered Tree Spirits.
After passing through the whirlpool, they were safe for the time being.
Bai Wei took the longbow-wielding woman from Luo Zheng's arms and pulled out a long green arrow from the quiver on her back.
After changing the direction of the arrow, Bai Wei held the end of the arrow and stabbed it towards the longbow-wielding woman's forehead.
Luo Zheng was slightly taken aback when he saw this scene, but he soon understood what was going on.
The green arrow wasn't used to kill. Instead, it contained a powerful life force. Once it entered a cultivator's body, not only would it not harm them, it could even save them!
While Bai Wei was treating the longbow-wielding woman, the other cultivators from the Demon Night Clan were carefully sizing up Luo Zheng.
Just how strong was this person to be able to escape from the two Withered Tree Spirits so easily?
Their gazes contained traces of reverence. When they thought about what they had said, they felt a lingering fear.
Not to mention anything else, if they really angered this person, none of them from the Demon Night Clan would be able to survive.
After the green arrow pierced the longbow-wielding woman's forehead, ten breaths of time passed before she slowly regained consciousness.
When she woke up, a puzzled expression appeared on her face.
Previously, she had fainted after being whipped by the Withered Tree Spirits. Before she fainted, she knew that she would definitely die this time.
When she woke up again, she should have returned to the main city to revive.
She never expected that when she woke up, she would discover that she had entered the Withered Tree Gate. When she looked at her surroundings and Bai Wei, she felt as if she had just had an unbelievable dream.
When Bai Wei noticed the longbow-wielding woman's expression, he said, "It was that human cultivator who saved you."
"A human cultivator …" The longbow-wielding woman was startled. Her gaze fell on Luo Zheng with a trace of gratitude.
When he saw the longbow-wielding woman's grateful gaze, Luo Zheng only nodded slightly. However, he was sizing up the world behind the Withered Tree Gate.
In front of Luo Zheng was an extremely majestic and thick giant tree. Based on initial estimation, the diameter of this giant tree trunk was several hundred miles wide. Its height was even more difficult to estimate because one couldn't see the top at a glance!
When Bai Wei saw Luo Zheng's gaze, he took the initiative to explain, "This tree is the Ancient Divine Tree of the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe. It has 13 branches, and my people from the Demon Night Clan are trapped on the ninth branch......"
The Ancient Divine Ancient Sorcerer tribe?
The Ancient Sorcerer Tree Spirits was in the Ancient Sorcerer Tree Spirit in the Ancient Sorcerer tribe.....
....... and the Divine Ancient Divine Sorcerer tribe.
In other words, the Illusory Battlefield should be a collection of all the Ancient Sorcerer Tree Spirits in the universe.
However, the items obtained from each Ancient Sorcerer Tree Spirit were different. Luo Zheng didn't obtain a single Fantasy Point in the Ancient Sorcerer Tree Spirit. Instead, 300,000 Fantasy Points were deducted.
Luo Zheng's Fantasy Points weren't deducted from the Divine Ancient Divine Tree. According to Bai Wei, he could obtain a large number of Fantasy Points from the Ancient Sorcerer Tree.
"Then let's go up." Luo Zheng didn't want Liu Yu to be sent back to the main city when he came here.
Bai Wei nodded. He took the lead and flew up into the sky.
Luo Zheng naturally followed closely behind him. The other members of the Demon Night Clan also flew up..
Luo Zheng's speed wasn't slow. However, after flying up for an entire hour, all he saw was the bare trunk of the Divine Ancient Divine Tree.
According to Bai Wei, the Divine Ancient Divine Tree had a total of 13 branches. After flying up for an hour, they were at least a few million feet in height. However, they still hadn't seen a single branch. How tall was the Divine Ancient Divine Tree?
The higher they went, the more clouds there were. Even the nearby tree trunk was hidden in the clouds, making it difficult to see clearly.
Bai Wei, who was leading the way, slowed down slightly and reminded them. "Everyone, be careful. There should be Amberwood Voodoo Parasites further up. Remember to avoid the venom of the Amberwood Voodoo Parasites!"
Everyone's hearts shook when they heard Bai Wei's words, and they became more vigilant.
Not long after Bai Wei finished speaking, protruding tumors appeared on the bare tree trunk. When they passed by, countless cracks appeared on the surface of one of the tumors.
"Pa!"
With a crisp sound, a black, pointy-horned insect shot out from the tree tumor.
The pointy-horned insect spread its wings, and with a buzzing sound, it rushed towards the group!
The several Demon Night Clan cultivators didn't think much of it at first. This was because the massive tree trunk gave them a strange impression that the pointy-horned insect wasn't that big.
As the pointy-horned insect rapidly approached, they discovered that this pitch-black insect was ten feet long. To ordinary cultivators, it was a massive creature!
"This is the Amberwood Voodoo Parasite. Everyone, speed up and get rid of this thing!" Bai Wei said, and his speed increased even more …
Even if Bai Wei hadn't warned them, no one would be willing to face this terrifying insect. Even if the Amberwood Voodoo Parasite was only the size of a palm, it was enough to make one's hair stand on end. Moreover, this insect was so huge!
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
The several streaks of light that surrounded the Divine Ancient Divine Tree rapidly increased in speed.
"Pa, pa pa …"
With a series of explosions, more and more Amberwood Voodoo Parasites followed under their feet.
"What do we do? It seems we can't get rid of these Amberwood Voodoo Parasites!" A Demon Night Clan cultivator said.
To his surprise, Bai Wei's expression was very calm. "We couldn't get rid of them to begin with. The Withered Tree Door has been opened three times, and even Lord Aizen and Liu Yu couldn't get rid of them!"
"Then … how did they climb to the top of the Divine Ancient Divine Tree?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Someone has to make a sacrifice. Every time we climb a tree, someone has to be sacrificed." Bai Wei's eyes narrowed slightly, then fell on the black-robed Demon Night Clan cultivator.
This Demon Night Clan cultivator was flying at full speed, and his speed was probably second only to Luo Zheng and Bai Wei. When he saw Bai Wei looking at him, his expression changed. "What do you mean? Bai Wei, are you planning on making me …"
Bai Wei nodded. "These Amberwood Voodoo Parasites are easy to lure. Spark, go down!"
"I don't want to!" The Demon Night Clan cultivator called Spark shook his head. "I came here to help Lord Aizen! Why should I … "
"Hmph, your death would be the greatest help to Lord Aizen!" Bai Wei said coldly.
"Why don't you let this guy die!" Spark pointed at Luo Zheng. "He's an outsider!"
Being pointed at by Spark, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed, but he didn't say anything.
These Amberwood Voodoo Parasites should be highly poisonous, but Luo Zheng wasn't afraid. After all, he had a Divine Artifact body and was impervious to all poisons. He shouldn't have a problem dealing with this poison.
If Luo Zheng attracted the Amberwood Voodoo Parasites and then retreated, he could do the same.
But Luo Zheng wasn't happy to hear this. Since he was an outsider, what did the Demon Night Clan cultivator's life and death have to do with him? What's more, Luo Zheng only cared about Liu Yu. After all, Liu Yu helped him a lot back in the Seagod Continent.
Bai Wei's face darkened at Spark's words. He said coldly, "If you go down and attract the Amberwood Voodoo Parasites, I will report to Lord Aizen and make up for your lost Fantasy Points. Otherwise …"
Although there weren't many fights in the Demon Night Clan's main city, if someone disrupted the order, the consequences would be tragic.
Spark's face showed a trace of hesitation, but in the end, he clenched his teeth and swooped down.
"Swoosh swoosh swoosh swoosh …"
As it flew downwards, an orange light flickered around it. Within that light, countless long spears with majestic auras shot out.
The hundreds of Amberwood Voodoo Parasites that followed them were attracted by Spark's attack. They gathered together and rushed towards him.
"Die!"
Naturally, Spark was very unwilling to be forced to die. In his anger, he attacked the Amberwood Voodoo Parasites without restraint.

Chapter 1227
The black venom contained an extremely strong corrosive property!
In the blink of an eye, the protective orange light protecting Spark was completely corroded.
Then, everyone heard a violent roar coming from below!
Although one would not really die in the Illusory Battlefield, the physical pain was real. It was not a good experience to be corroded to death by the venom.
Everyone's hearts trembled slightly when they heard the sound.
"Let's leave quickly. After this, we should be able to reach the first branch!" Bai Wei reminded them.
Seeing Spark's miserable state, they did not stop at all, for fear that the Evil Bug Wood would catch up with them again …
Not long after, Luo Zheng's eyes flickered with a faint green light. Through the white fog, he saw a thick branch!
Just a single branch was enough to hold an entire city!
The Ancient Sorcerer Tribe was an extremely powerful tribe in ancient times, and the Great Toona Sacred Tree was the Holy Land of the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe.
The Great Sorcerer of the Sorcerer Tribe was also a Celestial Master. One of the Great Sorcerer Tribe's Great Sorcerer Tribe controlled the strongest Will of Heaven in the universe, the Sorcerer Incantation Killing Thought!
This Will of Heaven was the strongest, and it was an existence that made people's hair stand on end.
It was said that the Great Sorcerer who controlled this Will of Heaven only needed to know the other party's name and appearance to create a Will of Heaven in his mind and kill the other party in his imagination!
In other words, if Luo Zheng controlled this Will of Heaven, after seeing someone, he only needed to imagine himself killing that person in his mind, and that person would be killed!
The imagination of living creatures was boundless. Who could resist such a terrifying Will of Heaven?
This was really a method that could kill people with just a thought …
However, for some unknown reason, the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe had suddenly disappeared from the universe, and not even a trace of them could be found!
The Ancient Sorcerer Tribe, which had originally controlled thousands of Great Worlds, had been a colossal existence in the Ancient Times. However, the entire Sorcerer Tribe had mysteriously disappeared, and no trace of this tribe could be found in any of the Holy Lands … This matter could be considered a strange occurrence in this era, and until now, it had remained a huge mystery!
"We can go up directly. Why are we still on this tree?" Luo Zheng asked after stepping on the huge tree.
Bai Wei shook his head and said, "It's not that easy. Starting from the first tree trunk, it contains an extremely powerful curse. If you don't follow the path on the tree trunk and fly directly up, you will explode and die! No exceptions! "
The first time they opened the Dead Tree Gate, even Aizen himself exploded. Later on, they learned that the Great Toon Heavenly Tree wasn't something that could be casually climbed.
Under Bai Wei's guidance, they walked along the trunk for a while, but they didn't encounter any danger …
"Master Aizen and the others have already gone up. The trouble on the tree should have been dealt with. The next time the door opens, the beasts and insects will appear again," Bai Wei explained.
Without any difficulties or obstacles, the speed at which everyone advanced was naturally extremely fast.
Not long after, the group arrived at the end of the tree trunk. There was an ancient wooden plate at the end of the tree trunk, and on the wooden plate, fist-sized skulls were neatly arranged. These skulls were so small that they did not belong to an adult, but rather to a baby's head …
Coming to this conclusion, Luo Zheng couldn't help but frown.
The Ancient Sorcerer Tribe gave people a very strange feeling. It could even be said that they were disgusting.
"Step on the wooden plate and the curse will be broken. This is an ancient ritual of the Witch Tribe," Bai Wei said. He was the first to step on the wooden plate.
When Luo Zheng and the others stepped on the wooden plate, it began to slowly rotate. At the same time, the small skulls began to open and close their mouths, making "cough cough cough cough" sounds.
They were all warriors of the Spirit Sea Realm. With their powerful strength, they wouldn't be afraid even in the face of evil beings. However, this kind of sound still made their hair stand on end when they heard it echoing in this empty space.
Not long after, the wooden plate stopped spinning. Then, all the little skulls sank into silence.
Beneath everyone's feet, a faint light flickered, and these lights combined to form a series of profound words …
When the word appeared, Bai Wei said, "Okay, we can continue!"
It was these words that could break the curse on the top!
Everyone continued to climb.
Not long after, he encountered another tree trunk. He entered the tree trunk again, found the wooden plate, and obtained another line of words. Then, he went up again.
After repeating this process seven times, Luo Zheng and the others reached the seventh trunk!
Before Luo Zheng climbed up the tree trunk, he could already feel a strong aura coming from the tree trunk …
"We caught up to them!" At this moment, Bai Wei's face was filled with joy, "The Demon Race warriors didn't break Sir Aizen's Falling Leaf Array!"
What Bai Wei feared the most was that Aizen and the others were sent back to the main city by the Demon Race.
Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows slightly and suddenly turned into a streak of light. He drew an arc in the air and landed on the branch. Then, he stared ahead … …
…
At the end of the branch, Liu Yu held a thin sword. Her eyes glowed with vitality. Her entire body gave off a strange aura.
Since ascending, Liu Yu had been in despair.
She hadn't opened her eyes to see the vastness of the Upper Realm. She hadn't felt the violent energy of the world before she was thrown into prison by the Punishment King, never to see the light of day again.
But this time, the opening of the Dream Battlefield gave Liu Yu a chance!
She was like a prisoner who had been released from prison. She breathed in the air of freedom in this vast world.
However, Liu Yu also knew that the Dreamy Battlefield was just a stage. She could do whatever she wanted on this stage and compete with the warriors of the Divine Sea Realm. However, this stage would eventually come to an end. She would have to return to the prison and continue to suffer the pain of despair.
Liu Yu's outstanding performance and strength led to her being invited by Aizen as a companion.
When the Illusory Battlefield was activated, it was a projection of a cultivator's state before they entered the Illusory Battlefield. Liu Yu, who was imprisoned, did not even have a single weapon.
Her equipment was given to her by Aizen.
At first, when Aizen learned about Liu Yu's situation, he promised to give Liu Yu her freedom after the Dream Battlefield ended.
Aizen was a Sector Lord's successor of the Yao Night Clan. Her mother was a member of the Elder Council.
Aizen fell silent when he heard that the Punishment King was the one who had imprisoned Liu Yu and punished him with the Burning Cannon.
No one in the Yao Night Clan could fight against the Punishment King. A few years ago, even the head of the Elder Council was killed by the Punishment King!
But Liu Yu was happy that Aizen had such a plan. He was her only friend in the Upper World!
Liu Yu gave up her share of Dream Points and gave them all to Aizen. This was the only way Liu Yu could help her.
But this time, as soon as they set foot on the seventh tree, they were chased by a group of demons!
For the past few hours, they had relied on Aizen's Fallen Leaves Formation and Liu Yu's sharp sword skills to hold on.
In fact, even if she died, it wasn't a big deal. At the very least, this Dream Battlefield was a very happy memory for Liu Yu. She gripped her rapier and looked at Aizen controlling the Fallen Leaves Formation. She was unusually calm in the face of the Demon Race's attacks.
On the tree trunk, thumb-sized leaves were scattered in a certain pattern.
The edges of these leaves were like blades. If one wasn't careful, they could easily cut through one's Shield of Vital Essence and cut open one's body.
It was also because of the sharp leaves that blocked the attacks of these over ten fiend race martial artists!
All the demons were extremely irritable.
Once they stepped into the Fallen Leaves Formation, not only would they have to face the sharp leaves, but they would also have to face the Yao Night Clan girl's sword-light.

Chapter 1228
The demons in front of them were enraged.
After entering the Illusory Battlefield, these ten or so Demon Clan warriors had already plundered three cities.
Two of them were ranked in the top 500,000 in the Illusory Battlefield and in the top 10,000 in this great world!
By chance, they'd managed to enter the Withered Tree Gate!
But they didn't expect to encounter such a fierce resistance ….
After wasting several hours, they still couldn't break through the fallen leaves. They even lost two of their brothers, what the hell!
After the Demon Race cultivator roared, a giant shield appeared in his hand, "I'll charge in front, you guys follow me all the way. Brothers, let's do it in one go. I don't think these women can hold on any longer!"
"Let's go all out!"
"Fuck, what a waste of time. I wonder what kind of treasure is on top of this damned tree …."
The other demons responded as well. Unable to hold back, they gathered their strength and prepared to take down the enemy in one go.
A tall and slender woman from the Fiend Night Clan stood by Liu Yu's side!
She had a head full of sky blue hair, like the color of the sky was dyed on top of it ….
At this moment, Aizen's hand was surrounded by countless leaves. It looked like a bracelet made of leaves.
"Liu Yu …. I don't think he can hold on," Aizen said helplessly.
"It doesn't matter, we still have a chance to do it again." Liu Yu smiled faintly.
Aizen thought for a moment, then with a gentle wave of her finger, the edges of the leaves were dyed with a golden luster. "I like your optimistic attitude. If you can regain your freedom, you'll be able to shine even in the Yao Night Clan!"
Liu Yu smiled faintly. Aizen had said this many times already ….
Her assumption didn't exist. The Yao Night Clan didn't restrict their members too much, but the King's orders were ironclad.
Who told a nobody like her to offend the King of Punishment?
"Those filthy bastards are coming …."
"Then let's go …."
The two women from the Yao Night Clan had a resolute look on their faces.
Liu Yu did not care about the Fantasy Points. She only hoped that the time she spent in this Fantasy Space would slow down a little, just a little bit more...
The leading Demon Race cultivator carried a huge shield and was about to charge over.
A voice came from behind them. "Looks like we're not late!"
The Demon Clan cultivators were taken aback. They turned their heads and looked at Luo Zheng. They were all taken aback.
Human warriors?
How could a human martial artist appear here?
After Luo Zheng finished speaking, he walked over slowly with a smile on his face, as if he had turned a blind eye to these Demon Race cultivators.
"Bai Wei! She brought reinforcements, but what's up with that human martial artist? "
It wasn't just the Demons who were stunned. Even Aizen and the Yao Night clansmen behind him were puzzled.
There was an agreement in the main city that outsiders could not enter the Withered Tree Gate. What was Bai Wei thinking? It seemed that she was the one who brought this human martial artist ….
As for Liu Yu, her breathing suddenly became incomparably hurried when she saw Luo Zheng. The thin sword that flickered with a faint glow faintly trembled in the air. Her eyes were still filled with disbelief as she firmly locked onto Luo Zheng's figure.
In the prison, when she was being punished, Liu Yu often heard Luo Zheng's voice echoing throughout the universe.
This human youth had ascended at about the same time as her, but in just a few short years, he had already caused such a huge commotion. Liu Yu couldn't help but sigh.
When she first met Luo Zheng, this fellow was still a puny "Warrior" ….
In fact, Liu Yu also had a small hope in her heart.
She hoped that Luo Zheng would rise ….
She hoped that Kaoru would rise ….
That was the only way she could be rescued one day.
However, as time passed, Liu Yu also understood that she was already someone who had been forgotten.
The universe was so big.
Even a single great realm was unimaginably large.
Even if Luo Zheng really did rise one day, helping Kaoru ascend the throne, or even pushing Yao off her throne, so what?
Would Luo Zheng's attention be drawn to a prison in a corner of a great realm?
That was clearly impossible.
As soon as this thought crossed her mind, Liu Yu despaired.
She knew Luo Zheng was willing to help her, but the problem was that she couldn't leave the prison. How could she let Luo Zheng know?
When the Dream Battlefield opened, all the Divine Sea realm cultivators stood on it.
But Liu Yu never thought she'd have the chance to meet Luo Zheng.
The Dream Battlefield was divided into 50 great realms. The probability of her being in the same great realm as Luo Zheng was only one in fifty.
And there were countless main cities in a great realm. The entire Dream Battlefield had hundreds of millions of cultivators. The probability of meeting Luo Zheng by chance was almost negligible.
But such a negligible probability had appeared. Her meeting with Luo Zheng was originally a miracle!
She was overwhelmed with emotions. For a moment, she was so excited that she couldn't speak!
"Human Clan cultivator, get out of here. There's no place for you here!" a Devil Clan cultivator scolded.
Luo Zheng was still walking slowly. There was still a faint smile on his face ….
If these Devil Clan cultivators looked carefully, they would see that Luo Zheng's eyes didn't fall on them. Instead, he directly looked past them and met Liu Yu's eyes.
Luo Zheng didn't know how Liu Yu was doing recently.
In Luo Zheng's eyes, Liu Yu was an old friend. At least, she had helped him a lot on his journey to the Sea God Continent.
"Kill him," the Devil Clan cultivator with the giant shield decisively ordered. He felt a trace of evil from this Human Clan cultivator. This strange feeling made him faintly uneasy.
The leader of the Devil Clan cultivators, holding a mace, was the first to rush towards Luo Zheng!
The thick mace shone with a pitch-black metallic luster, and the sharp fangs on it were even more abnormally sharp!
The Demon Race martial artist gripped the spiked club with both hands, and the astral essence within his body exploded like an explosive. In that instant, the impact produced was terrifying. It was as if he wasn't wielding a spiked club, but a mountain!
"Not dodging?"
On the side of the Fallen Leaves Array, Aizen showed a touch of surprise. She couldn't understand the origin of this Human Clan cultivator at all.
Luo Zheng stretched out his hand, turned it into a knife, and chopped diagonally upwards!
This knife wasn't fast, it could even be said to be very slow. But the power it contained was unimaginable to any creature present!
"Peng!"
A dull sound was transmitted. The collision between Luo Zheng's knife and the mace wasn't surprising.
However, at this moment, the pupils of many cultivators widened. Almost at the same time, they sucked in a breath of cold air …
The thick mace was easily broken by Luo Zheng's knife.
Not only that, after the top half of the mace broke, it flew backward and turned into countless black fragments in the air. They scattered and formed a black drizzle on the tree trunk!
At the same time, the Devil Clan cultivator's hands that held the mace suddenly split open. Brown blood spurted out.
First, it was the area between his thumb and forefinger, then his entire palm, and then his arms. Soon, even his shoulders were shattered …
And the cause of all this was merely an ordinary palm blade.
Behind Luo Zheng, Bai Wei's eyes were like two stars rising! This guy's strength was still beyond her estimation!
As for Aizen, she came from a noble family, so her vision was extraordinary. After seeing this scene, her breathing became rapid, and at the same time, she felt a great threat!
She might have a chance against these Devil Clan cultivators, but after Aizen measured Luo Zheng's strength, she came to a very helpless conclusion. She was no match for this person.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1229
Aizen wanted to compare himself to Luo Zheng for the same reason as Bai Wei.
Warriors were always wary of those stronger than themselves. Sometimes, they would even be wary of their companions.
Besides, Aizen and Luo Zheng didn't know each other.
From the looks of things, Luo Zheng seemed to have been brought in by Bai Wei. But if he was so strong, why would he be willing to help her?
The Withered Tree Gate was a holy land for obtaining fantasy points. Every time one entered, they could obtain at least several million fantasy points. If this person was strong enough to occupy this place, the Yao Night Clan wouldn't be able to do anything.
From this point of view, it was natural for Aizen to be worried.
Luo Zheng's palm chop caused the hearts of all the Devil Race martial artists to tremble violently!
Although these Demon Race cultivators were irascible, their foresight wasn't bad. They knew they were on the verge of death.
There was only one path through this giant tree. One side was guarded by Luo Zheng while the other side was protected by the Fallen Leaves Formation. If they were surrounded, they would lose their last chance to resist.
"Let's kill this kid first!"
Making a prompt decision, the Demon Race cultivator with the giant shield on his shoulder rushed forward!
The other Demon Race martial artists also understood the situation they were about to face at the first moment, and they also moved!
These Demon Race cultivators were tall and sturdy. They were like small mountains as they rushed towards Luo Zheng ….
Bai Wei didn't lead the Yao Night Clan cultivators to follow. Her lips moved slightly as if she was communicating with Aizen.
Neither Bai Wei nor Aizen made a move.
As for Liu Yu ….
She simply looked at the youth in the distance.
When he first entered the Sea God Continent, he was still a young man. His body was slightly thin and he still looked a bit immature.
But now, Luo Zheng gave her a strong sense of confidence. These people in front of her could easily kill him ….
Someone who could defeat an Empyrean, how difficult would it be for him to deal with these Demon Race cultivators?
Since Liu Yu recognized Luo Zheng, he absolutely believed in his strength.
Boom ….
Accompanied by a violent explosion, the giant shield in the hands of the Demon Race cultivator at the front had already exploded!
And Luo Zheng's figure was like a ghost, constantly wandering around these Devil Clan martial artists.
Luo Zheng could slip through even the tiniest gap like a wisp of smoke. He kept a very close distance with the demon warrior as if he was sticking to the back of the demon warrior...
Then, he stretched out his hand and slapped the back of the Demon Race cultivator's neck!
"Pu!"
It was accompanied by a dull sound.
The leader of the Demon Race martial artists crashed to the ground, bursting into a ball of yellow dreamy light.
Before those dreamy light spots could enter Luo Zheng's body, Luo Zheng's figure circled around a large saber. He even stuck close to the edge of the saber and spiraled upwards, as if he wasn't afraid of being cut by the saber at all!
The moment he rushed forward, Luo Zheng's knee fiercely imprinted itself on the Demon Race martial artist's face …
Another ball of dreamy light spots exploded!
A sea of dreamy light spots crazily chased after Luo Zheng's figure, but they always maintained a distance of three inches from Luo Zheng!
The energy emitted by these dreamy light spots was almost negligible, but after Luo Zheng activated Eight Directional Flying Mist, his body was as light as a feather. It was also because these dreamy light spots were "boosting" behind Luo Zheng that his speed had more than doubled!
"Peng!"
"Pa —"
"Dong …."
Every time Luo Zheng shuttled back and forth, a muffled sound would erupt, and after each muffled sound, a Demon Race martial artist would fall.
The strength of these fallen Demon Race martial artists was not weak. The weakest among them was a core martial artist of a tenth-grade Holy Land. Because they were participating in the Illusory Battlefield, the weapons in their hands were at least second-grade divine artifacts, and there were even a few first-grade divine artifacts.
However, they were simply unable to catch Luo Zheng's ghostly figure. Even if they managed to catch a glimpse of Luo Zheng, he would be like a slippery loach that would float away in an instant!
If Luo Zheng was interested in fighting back, he would directly twist the divine artifacts in their hands into a pile of scrap metal!
This was a battle that was close to being toyed with. Even the spectators of the Fiend Night Clan felt a deep sense of powerlessness as they watched this scene — they would imagine themselves fighting against Luo Zheng, and after some simple mental calculations, they would quickly understand that they were no match for this person.
"Boom …."
The last Demon Race martial artist fell to the ground with a loud bang.
Luo Zheng's floating figure finally stabilized. At this moment, the dreamy light spots that had been chasing Luo Zheng all this time began to rapidly enter Luo Zheng's body!
"This group of dreamy light spots …" Luo Zheng's eyes flashed slightly. After completely absorbing the light spots, Luo Zheng suddenly opened his eyes and revealed a smile, "There are actually nearly a million of them!"
The strength of these Demon Race martial artists was actually inferior to the Demon Race's Dragon Bird.
However, as the Illusory Battlefield remained open for longer and longer, the dreamy light spots continued to converge on a small number of people. If Luo Zheng went to the main city of the Demon Race martial artists again, the points accumulated by the Fat Wolves would certainly be many times more than before.
Luo Zheng took less than thirty breaths of time to deal with these Demon Race martial artists.
Not far in front of Luo Zheng, the fallen leaves were still in place. The edges of the fallen leaves were suffused with a faint golden light, obviously unusually sharp.
When Luo Zheng's gaze swept past the fallen leaves and fixed on Aizen, he saw the vigilant look on Aizen's face ….
[So that's how it is, you didn't attack because you didn't trust me?]
Luo Zheng thought to himself, but he didn't care. After all, he had already found Liu Yu.
At this moment, all the Demon Night clansmen were nervous. Facing Luo Zheng like this, they had no power to resist.
The moment Luo Zheng took a step forward, all the fallen leaves rolled up at the same time, forming a mysterious fallen leaves array. These leaves made a soft rustling sound as they rolled on the ground. All the leaves maintained a single direction, facing Luo Zheng.
Although Aizen didn't speak, her warning was clear.
Luo Zheng didn't care at all and walked towards Aizen ….
"Don't come over," Aizen warned. Her True Qi spread out from her body as she stared at Luo Zheng with her pale blue eyes. Her fingers trembled slightly, as if she would attack if Luo Zheng took a step forward.
Luo Zheng was still walking slowly with a smile on his face. It was as if he didn't hear Aizen's warning.
Seeing Luo Zheng enter the fallen leaves array, Aizen clenched her teeth and her blue eyes flashed with murderous intent. This Luo Zheng was too strong. If he rushed out of the fallen leaves array, she wouldn't be able to deal with him!
Aizen was about to control the fallen leaves to attack Luo Zheng, but at that moment, Liu Yu also stepped into the fallen leaves array!
"Liu Yu?"
Aizen froze for a moment before seeing Liu Yu's faint smile.
Liu Yu nodded to Aizen, "Don't worry, he's an old friend."
"An old friend?" Aizen froze.
Liu Yu nodded and smiled, "Do you remember what I told you? Only one person in this world can change my fate, it's him …. "
Aizen froze ….
During this adventure, Liu Yu gave a general idea of what happened to her.
How did she offend the King of Punishment? How did she end up being locked up in the prison to endure the punishment of corrosion?
Liu Yu said that only one person in this world could save her. That person was the storyteller in the universe, the one with the infinite roar token. Even dozens of Empyrean Sovereigns couldn't do anything to him.
Since Liu Yu said so, was this person in front of her Luo Zheng?
Luo Zheng was now a legend in the universe!
In addition to being a legend, people also speculated about Luo Zheng's strength in private.
Some people said that Luo Zheng obtained the inheritance of Immortal Mansion and his strength was terrifying. He could easily deal with Dao Children and Heaven's Prides!
Others said that Luo Zheng's talent was average. He relied on external objects to kill Empyrean Sovereigns and his own strength was average. He was just a guy who disappeared into the crowd ….
But based on the strength Luo Zheng just showed, he definitely had the ability to fight against Heaven's Prides!
This legendary young man was standing in front of him. Even Aizen's state of mind fluctuated violently.

Chapter 1230
In front of Liu Yu, Luo Zheng didn't try to hide anything.
With a faint smile on his face, he nodded and admitted it.
Bai Wei and the other cultivators of the Yao Night Clan also caught up to them.

He was the human cultivator who killed Celestial Master Broken Shadow!

No matter what, since Luo Zheng was an old friend of Liu Yu's, there shouldn't be a problem.
Aizen and Bai Wei were shocked, but they also breathed a sigh of relief.
