As she spoke, she gently waved her finger, and a small black tree appeared in her hand. This small tree was four feet tall, and its branches were only as thick as a little finger. However, there was not a single leaf on the whole tree, only a lonely tree trunk.
When she summoned the small black tree, the branches of the small black tree shook, and all the leaves flew back in an instant. The leaves began to grow back neatly on the branches, and it looked very magical ….
In the blink of an eye, the bare black tree turned into a small tree with dense leaves. Aizen put the tree away and looked at Liu Yu. She thought about Liu Yu's situation and then looked at Liu Yu. She smiled brightly and said, "Old friends meet, why don't we talk? Liu Yu's situation isn't good. "
"Not good?" Luo Zheng's eyes flashed as he stared at Liu Yu.
Liu Yu's expression was a little unnatural.
In the prison, Liu Yu had thought of countless ways to spread the news that she was imprisoned.
But the universe was so vast. It was easier said than done to find a single person in a hundred thousand great worlds.
As for the Roaring Token, don't even think about it. Some Sector Lords couldn't even get a Roaring Token, let alone Liu Yu who was only a Spirit Sea Realm warrior. Plus, she had been chained up in prison.
Perhaps it was the will of the heavens that allowed him to meet Luo Zheng here.
But now that she was facing Luo Zheng, she didn't know what to say.
Aizen saw this and took the initiative to point to Liu Yu's situation.
"Yes, Liu Yu's been imprisoned and subjected to the punishment of corrosion. This punishment is second only to the Eternal Night Punishment! Every few days, their eyes would rot, their skin would fester, and their bones would corrode. Then, they would be healed by the power of life. The cycle would repeat itself, and every day would be a torment … "
Hearing Aizen's words, Flowing Feather's face turned pale.
She'd endured this for seven years!
She'd endured this pain for seven years.
Her time in the Dream Battlefield was probably the happiest time Liu Yu had ever been.
Here, she made friends with Aizen and the other members of the Yao Ye clan. Here, she was able to display her strength and compete with the other experts.
But soon, she'd have to go back to those days. Liu Yu shuddered.
That kind of pain was inhumane even for a cultivator.
Ordinary martial artists would usually not be able to endure for more than three years before going crazy …
Even for martial artists with strong willpower, once their willpower was exhausted within five years, their entire body and mind would directly collapse.
It was also because Liu Yu had grown up in the Sea God Continent. Cultivators who grew up in that chaotic continent were much more resilient than cultivators who grew up peacefully in the Holy Land. It was because of this resilient heart that Liu Yu had been able to endure these seven years. She had endured this cycle of pain for seven years.
Luo Zheng's expression darkened. His eyes flickered with coldness. "Because of … that bitch Yao? What a pity, I didn't kill her last time. "
That day when he had ordered the Life Risking Chaotic Battle, he had not locked Yao Yao within it. If he had locked Yao Yao within it, the things that happened below would have been much more interesting!
As soon as he said this, the surrounding Daydream Clan cultivators' expressions all changed!
Ignoring the upper echelons of the Yao Ye clan, ordinary cultivators were still very devout. Otherwise, the three Kings of the clan wouldn't have accumulated so much faith power.
It was normal for the Yao Ye clan to react this way when Luo Zheng suddenly insulted their Kings.
However, Aizen's expression was calm.
His mother was an elder and naturally knew about the conflict between the upper echelons of the Yao Ye clan. Many sector lords were already unhappy with Yao.
This Luo Congheng had already scolded Yao Yao before. Now that he heard it, it was actually quite normal.
Aizen didn't ask for anything, so the other Yao Ye clan members didn't do anything. They just looked at Luo Zheng with unnatural expressions, some even with hostility.
To most of the Yao Ye clan, they didn't care about the conflict between the Kings. In their hearts, the three Kings were supreme existences. They would use their lives to protect their Kings' dignity.
Liu Yu nodded.
Luo Zheng knew about the situation. Naturally, he knew that Liu Yu had offended Yao because of this.
However, he didn't expect the bitch to hold such a grudge. As a King of the Yao Ye clan, a supreme existence, she actually held a grudge against a Yao Ye clan member who ascended from a lower realm.
According to Aizen, the burning of the cannons was an extremely torturous punishment … …
Luo Zheng's eyes grew colder and colder.
Standing on this huge branch and looking at the boundless white fog in the distance, he suddenly turned his head and stared at Liu Yu, saying, "Three years! Just give me three more years and I will kill Yao and save you! "
Three years of punishment was also hard to bear.
Liu Yu did not want to endure the punishment of being burned by the cannon for even a day. However, when she heard that it would take three years, a look of anticipation suddenly appeared on her pale face.
What people feared the most was having no hope.
Liu Yu's martial heart was strong. As long as there was a sliver of hope, he would be able to support himself.
Actually, three years was a very short time.
Three years to remove the Yao Ye clan's King from her throne. If it was anyone else, they would think he was dreaming.
The fully armed Yao, with the help of the faith pool, was comparable to an Empyrean.
To remove Yao from her throne in three years, there were probably only a few people in the universe who could do this. And these people represented the strongest forces in the universe.
Before they could do this, they had to consider whether or not their own clans could withstand the backlash from the Yao Ye clan.
In the entire universe, the only person who had the qualifications to say this was Luo Zheng!
Because he had killed an Empyrean before!
Four true Empyreans had died at Luo Zheng's hands!
When Luo Zheng said this, the Yao Ye clan's martial artists' expressions became even more unnatural.
However, Liu Yu was their leader. If she didn't say anything, what could the other Yao Ye clan martial artists say?
However, the Yao Ye clan members were still a bit unconvinced. A Divine Sea realm martial artist wanted to kill their King in three years? These words were a bit too arrogant.
It had to be known that between the Divine Sea realm and the World Lord realm, there was the Divine Transformation realm and the Divine Sea realm.
Some martial artists had spent hundreds of thousands of years or even millions of years to cross this barrier!
But Luo Zheng said that he would only give him three years!
"Three years. I can endure." Liu Yu nodded, her eyes shining brightly.
Although she didn't believe that Luo Zheng could accomplish this in three years, at least Luo Zheng had given her hope. Hope that would allow her to endure three years of pain.
As for this fellow Luo Zheng, when he was on the Sea God Continent, he had turned the impossible into possible, turning the rotten into the miraculous.
In this world, there were only a small number of people who could create miracles. And Luo Zheng was one of these people.
Seeing Liu Yu's expression, Luo Zheng felt a bit of guilt.
After all, it was because of him that Liu Yu had fallen to such a state. It was because of him that she had suffered so much!

Chapter 1231
Aizen shrunk to the side, quietly observing Luo Zheng.
Not only her, but the other warriors, such as Bai Wei, also looked at Luo Zheng.

At least she made the right decision to enter the Dream Battlefield.

"Whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
Each leaf was comparable to a hidden weapon.

As soon as Aizen said that, she quickly retreated. The moment she retreated, the light in the ant's abdomen flashed to the extreme.

Following a violent explosion, a strong shockwave spread out with a flash of fire!
The air was filled with a burnt smell, and a mushroom cloud with a diameter of dozens of feet slowly rose.
The explosive ant's power was terrifying. If an ordinary warrior was hit by it, they would be turned into ashes.
Even if Luo Zheng's body was strong, if he was caught in the explosion, he would be seriously injured.
However, the ant itself wasn't difficult to deal with.
After killing the ant, Aizen turned around and looked at Luo Zheng. He smiled and said, "That's it, it's not difficult to deal with it."
What she did just now was just a demonstration.

Why are they looking at me?

But in the blink of an eye, he understood that they wanted to see him fight.
"I'll give it a try too."

But Luo Zheng didn't care. He continued forward.
Bang!
With a flip of his body, he flashed past another ferocious ant …
This way, Luo Zheng was being chased by two ferocious ants.
"What is Luo Zheng trying to do?"
"He's trying to lure the ants together!"
"He's crazy!"
When the other members of the Yao Night Clan saw this, they all revealed worried expressions.
Although the ants weren't strong, their explosive power was terrifying. No matter who it was, they had to be careful.
So if one wanted to pass through the eighth tree trunk, they had to use their strength. They had to lure the ants one by one and kill them one by one.
But now, Luo Zheng was swaggering into the ant swarm. Bai Wei and the others' hearts were in their throats.
Liu Yu had a faint smile on her face.
Strictly speaking, she didn't have much contact with Luo Zheng. But when she was in the Heavenly Feather Holy Land, she received detailed information about Luo Zheng's deeds from the Tower of Sin. Luo Zheng didn't stop at all along the way, and the number of ferocious ants following behind him kept increasing!
"If so many ants explode at once, how powerful will it be?"
"Isn't he afraid of being killed by the explosion?"
Aizen had been watching Luo Zheng's actions with curiosity. When she heard the discussion, her expression changed. She seemed to have thought of something. She said, "Everyone, retreat. Try to stay close to the main trunk of the tree."
Now, everyone could see that Luo Zheng was planning to lure these ferocious ants together!
The might of a single Ferocious Ant was already so shocking. If hundreds of Ferocious Ants exploded at the same time, how shocking would the scene be?
"Whoosh …"
As Luo Zheng's figure moved forward rapidly, a black mass of ferocious ants had gathered behind him.
"Now's the time!"
His figure suddenly swept forward and grabbed a ferocious ant in front of him.
The Ferocious Ant was ten feet long, much longer than Luo Zheng himself. Furthermore, the Ferocious Ant was innately strong. Even the Ferocious Ant that was as small as a grain of rice could lift something ten times heavier than itself!
As soon as Luo Zheng grabbed the ferocious ant, it opened its two pincers and tried to clamp down on Luo Zheng.
He grabbed one of the pincers and with a surge of power, he twisted the ferocious ant's head off.
The moment he twisted the head off, Luo Zheng's other hand grabbed the ferocious ant's abdomen and threw it behind him.
The ferocious ant was actually a very strange creature. Ever since it was born, it had been constantly consuming its own strength to stabilize the Explosive Flames in its abdomen. It was very careful not to let the Explosive Flames explode. When it was seriously injured or on the verge of death, it was unable to stabilize the Explosive Flames! After the Explosive Flames lost its restraints, it naturally produced a violent explosion.
Luo Zheng had already twisted the ferocious ant's head off. The Explosive Flames in its abdomen had naturally lost its restraints. When Luo Zheng threw the abdomen, it was still flying in the air. The Explosive Flames in its abdomen became brighter and brighter.
"Crack …"
The abdomen rolled on the ground and landed in the middle of the ferocious ants chasing Luo Zheng.
Following a flash of light …
"Boom!"
The sound of a violent explosion could be heard.
The ferocious ants at the side did not dodge and were naturally affected by the explosion. Hence, the Explosive Flames in their abdomens exploded again.
"Boom, boom …"
The ferocious ants exploded one after another. This was a chain reaction.
When dozens of ferocious ants exploded together, hundreds of them exploded.
"Boom!"
A series of explosions rang in everyone's ears. The power of the explosions was far beyond everyone's imagination!
A huge mushroom cloud rose up from the spot where he had been standing.
Along with this explosion, an extremely powerful shockwave spread out in all directions.
Even the Great Toon Divine Tree, which was comparable to a giant mountain, was gently swaying!
Luo Zheng maintained a certain distance from the explosion. His legs were like nails, firmly nailed to the ground, bearing the impact of the shock wave.
Fortunately, Aizen had suggested for everyone to retreat from the beginning. Behind them was the Great Toon Divine Tree.
When the shock wave spread, it was like an invisible giant hand, pressing everyone to the tree trunk, making them unable to move.
If they stood stupidly on the branches, they would have been pushed by the shock wave and hit the trunk of the Daitoon Heavenly Tree.
In that case, even if they did not die, they would be seriously injured.

Chapter 1232

The Human Alliance, in an Ascension Pool in the Three Pure Realms …

However, they did not know that the competition in the Upper Realms was even more brutal than in the Lower Realms. In the Lower Realms, they were peak existences. In the Lower Realms, they were absolute kings.
However, the Divine Sea Realm warriors in the Upper Realm were too ordinary. This kind of difference in status was a lesson that almost every ascender had to learn.

"My husband was at the Life and Death realm in the past. Now, more than six years have passed. I wonder what his cultivation has been like. Has he stepped into the Divine Sea …?"
This woman was the master of the Cloud Hall, Ning Yudie.
After several years of adventuring, her cultivation had broken through to the Divine Sea!
When Luo Zheng left that year, she was already pregnant with a son named Luo Nian. She was pinning her longing for Luo Zheng on her son.
However, after these years, Luo Nian was already seven years old. Looking at Luo Nian, who was almost the same as Luo Zheng, her longing did not decrease, but increased with each passing day.
When martial artists became partners, it was normal for them to be separated for several years, or even decades.
Some extreme people would go into seclusion for a hundred years. Although they were separated by one level, they could indeed be separated by a hundred years …
However, Luo Zheng and Xi Youqin's bone ages were not old. Their state of mind had not been settled by time, so how could they be indifferent and think hard during this time?
In order to abandon the torment of longing, she cultivated quietly, but she did not want to break through to the Divine Sea realm in these six years.
Luo Nian was seven years old. He was also extremely intelligent since childhood. Coupled with the status of the Cloud Hall in the Central Domain, the foundation of his small body was far stronger than that of ordinary children. He had broken through to the Xiantian Realm at the age of six. Now, Luo Nian's cultivation was at the Xiantian Realm Level Four.
She was reluctant to leave Nian 'er when she ascended to the Upper Realm, but in the end, she decided to ascend to find her husband!
"This Upper Realm is boundless, so vast that you can't imagine …"

"We belong to the Jade Pure Holy Land of the Three Pure Realms, a tenth-grade Holy Land! And the Realm Master of the Three Pure Realms is also the Holy Lord of our Jade Pure Holy Land … "
The Divine Sea Realm soldier in front of them roughly explained some things to this group of ascenders.
And Ning Yudie's heart was constantly shaken when she heard it!
She knew that the Upper Realm was vast and could not be compared to the Boundless World where she lived.
Even if she ascended to the Upper Realm, the journey to find Luo Zheng would be extremely difficult …
However, listening to the soldier in front of her, the area of these 100,000 Upper Realms was still far beyond her imagination!
Crossing a Upper Realm would take a hundred years with the speed of a Divine Sea Realm martial artist?
This Upper Realm was already ridiculously large, not to mention that there were 100,000 Upper Realms. Finding her husband among them would be harder than finding a needle in a haystack!
She had ascended and was actually in a tenth-grade Holy Land?
What kind of strength did this Holy Land have …
All kinds of thoughts immediately made Ning Yudie's mind confused!

Now, the entire mine is in a state of abandonment, and we urgently lack manpower. You need to go to the mine of our Jade Pure Holy Land to mine …"
It turned out that when the Illusory Battlefield opened, all Divine Sea Realm martial artists could participate!
Although the mine strictly prohibited ascenders from entering the Illusory Battlefield, it was impossible to stop them. After all, as long as they took a step, a Divine Sea Realm martial artist would be able to enter. There was nothing they could do about it.

"Mining? How long do we need to dig? "An ascender asked.

As soon as these words came out, all the ascenders were stunned.
Ascending to the Upper Realm would take more than a hundred years? This was too different from their expectations!
Ning Yudie followed behind the group in silence. She was also hesitant in her heart, not knowing how to face this …
Originally, Ning Yudie was a woman who was quite independent, but after marrying Luo Zheng, her thoughts changed. She had a special dependence on Luo Zheng.
But now, it was impossible for Luo Zheng to appear in front of her!
With her husband's talent, he should be able to become a core martial artist in a tenth-rank Holy Land, right?
It was just that there were so many large realms in the Upper Realm. She did not know which tenth-rank Holy Land he had entered.
Was she really going to mine here for dozens of years?
She regretted it in her heart. If she had known, she would not have ascended. After all, there was still Nian 'er in the Lower Realm for her to worry about …
These soldiers were the ones who brought Ning Yudie through the spatial tunnel, moving forward!

"Follow me. I'll do it," the soldier said again.
Ning Yudie's eyes flickered, but she did not know what had happened to her. Anyway, after ascending, she was a little confused.
It seemed like it was a good thing that she did not need to mine.

In this palace, there were already dozens of women gathered. These women all had the cultivation of the Divine Ocean Realm …
After listening to their conversation, Ning Yudie found out that these women were like her, ascenders.
Not long after ascending, they were brought here. No one knew what was going on.
There were guards outside this palace, and they could not go out easily.
At this moment, a bad premonition arose in Ning Yudie's heart.
Many of the women did not know what their fates were, so they could only concentrate on cultivating.

Chapter 1233
After the three men entered the palace, the leader let out a chuckle and said, "Second brother, these women are the cultivators that ascended from our Jade Pure Holy Land!"
"Lower realm cultivators, those that can cultivate to the Divine Sea realm are all absolute beauties. Hehe, second brother, do you think my point of view is correct?" The leader said.
The man standing in the middle was the most handsome. The moment he entered, he attracted the attention of all the women. He said indifferently, "Those that can ascend must be the most talented cultivators in the lower realm. Since young, they used face nourishing pills and face changing pills to maintain their youth. Naturally, this is the case …"
The Divine Sea realm women had been waiting here for many days. Now that they saw the three men enter, they naturally wanted to know what was going on.
The woman in the lead asked, "Young Masters, may I know why the Jade Pure Holy Land has arranged for us to stay here?"
One of the three men laughed, "There's no reason. I just feel that it's not easy for women to ascend, so I'm saving you from the pain of mining!"
Although the ascended cultivators in the palace were all women, they had experienced a lot of ascension. Naturally, they wouldn't be moved by this sentence.
On the contrary, they knew that this matter wasn't simple.
"If that's the case, why do you still need to imprison us here? Why don't you set us free?" A woman said with a smile.
Hearing the woman's words, Ning Yudie's eyes flashed. If she could obtain her freedom, she could stand up and find Luo Zheng. However, since the three men invited them here, it was impossible for them to leave casually …
As expected, the man laughed, "My Jade Pure Holy Land is the leader of the Three Pure World. You all ascended from thousands of miles away. Are you all going to give up the chance to join my Jade Pure Holy Land?"
The moment he said this, the women were all worried.
They knew that they were under someone else's roof. They could only wait and see what the man said.
"Martial uncle Hai of my Jade Pure Sacred Ground is recruiting disciples. Martial uncle Hai is a half-step Sector Lord. It's your fortune to be able to become his disciple. Follow me … however, martial uncle Hai has a limit to his recruitment of disciples. He can't accept just anyone. It'll depend on your luck to be able to catch his eye!"
"Take a half-step Sector Lord as his master?"
"I, Sun Linghua, have never had a master since I stepped onto the path of Martial Arts. I don't care what my master's cultivation is, I won't worship him …"
"I knew she was up to no good. There are so many women in the Spirit Ocean Realm in the Upper Worlds. Why did she insist on taking us in as her disciples?"
How intelligent were these women? When they heard this, they vaguely understood what was going on. Naturally, they were extremely unhappy.
But why would these three men at the Ultimate Divine Stage care about them?
Under his orders, a group of armored soldiers surged out from outside the house. These armored soldiers took out numerous grey bracelets and put them on one by one. Those were the Spirit Restriction Bracelets.
Those who wear this bracelet will not be able to circulate their True Yuan.
Some of the girls wanted to resist, but in the face of three Extreme God Realm warriors and dozens of Spirit Sea Realm warriors, they were only warriors who had just entered the Spirit Sea Realm. How could they resist?
Ning Yudie didn't resist. Instead, she rubbed the ring on her ring finger. She had given her first sumeru ring to Luo Zheng. Later, she asked the Cloud Hall's craftsmen to create a new one.
In her heart, she was silently praying that Luo Zheng would protect her …
…
…
In the Dream Battlefield, Luo Zheng naturally didn't know that Ning Yudie had already ascended.
He, Aizen, Liu Yu, and the others were still rapidly climbing the Toona Divine Tree.
This Toona Divine Tree had a total of 13 branches. On each branch was a strange insect!
On the seventh branch was a Ferocious Ant.
On the eighth branch was a Human-Faced Cicada. This cicada had a lifelike human face on its head. It could even speak human language. It looked extremely frightening.
On the ninth branch was a Poisonous Ice Toad …
On the tenth branch was a Twin Winged Wasp.
On the eleventh branch was a Divine Power Weevil …
Previously, Liu Yu and Aizen had led the Yao Ye Clan to the twelfth branch!
However, every time they climbed to the twelfth branch, many of the Yao Ye Clan members would fall.
This time, with Luo Zheng's help, not only did their climbing speed increase, but they also didn't lose a single person!
In the eyes of the Yao Ye Clan members, Luo Zheng was clearly a monster.
The Poisonous Ice Toad and the Twin Winged Wasp were both extremely poisonous creatures that had been lost for a long time …
Even Liu Yu and Aizen would undoubtedly die if they were stung by these poisonous insects!
However, Luo Zheng rushed forward like a horse, allowing the thousands of poisonous insects to stab and kill him. He was like a stone, completely unaffected. He didn't put these poisonous creatures in his eyes at all.
As for the Divine Power Weevil, it was far stronger than ordinary cultivators, but Luo Zheng's brute force was superior. In terms of strength, he completely suppressed it.
Not long after, the group finally stood on the twelfth branch of the Toona Divine Tree!
At the end of the branch, there was a circle of round red fruits, a total of forty of them.
"You're saying that these fruits contain a large number of Dream Points?" Looking at the fruits, Luo Zheng had a strange look on his face.
Aizen nodded and said, "Each fruit contains a hundred thousand Dream Points!"
"So many!"
Luo Zheng was slightly surprised …
Before, Luo Zheng thought that plundering other people's Dream Points was a shortcut.
Most of the participants in the Dream Battlefield thought the same, so the battles were concentrated around the main cities …
Now it seems that the Dream Battlefield's arrangements were far more plentiful than he thought.
No wonder Aizen and Liu Yu repeatedly entered the Withered Tree Door!
With Luo Zheng's strength, he could climb the Toona Divine Tree alone. If he obtained these fruits alone, he could obtain four million Dream Points. Aizen said that the Withered Tree Door had been opened three times. If he entered all three times alone, wouldn't he have accumulated twelve million Dream Points?
No wonder he killed Ji Luoxue once and accumulated five million Dream Points, but he was only ranked in the top ten thousand …
"Everyone here can have one of these fruits." Aizen walked over and picked one of the red fruits. Now it was time to distribute it.
The eyes of the Yao Ye clan members lit up, one fruit was worth a hundred thousand Dream Points!
In fact, they had been following Luo Zheng all this time, so they didn't have many opportunities to attack. Getting a hundred thousand Dream Points was already very good.
"As for the remaining Dream Points, I will take thirty percent and Luo Zheng will take seventy percent. Brother Luo Zheng, what do you think?" Aizen's blue eyes stared at Luo Zheng with a smile.
"Okay." Luo Zheng nodded, but then he asked, "What about Liu Yu? Why doesn't she need it? "
As soon as Luo Zheng finished speaking, Liu Yu shook her head, "I don't need it …"
"Why?" Luo Zheng asked.
Aizen answered on behalf of Liu Yu, "If Liu Yu accumulates enough Dream Points, she will appear at the top. The Yao Ye clan has so many eyes, she will be noticed … Right now, Liu Yu doesn't need to be noticed. It's better for her to be forgotten."
After all, Liu Yu was still in the Yao Ye clan's prison. The top five million in the Dream Battlefield would attract attention. With Liu Yu's strength, it wouldn't be difficult for her to enter the top five million.
If Yao Ye inadvertently noticed her, the consequences would be even worse.
Although the possibility wasn't high, it was better to be careful.
Luo Zheng looked deeply at Liu Yu and then nodded, "I understand …"
Luo Zheng took the fruits Aizen handed him and crushed them one by one …
Every time Luo Zheng crushed a fruit, it was equivalent to Luo Zheng "killing" it in the Illusory Battlefield. Therefore, the illusory light spots that burst out from the fruit rapidly entered Luo Zheng's body!
After Luo Zheng received the remaining 70% of the fruits, there were 21 of them. After crushing these 21 red fruits, he obtained another 2.1 million fantasy points. This number was already very objective, second only to the fantasy points obtained from killing Ji Luoxue!
However, when Luo Zheng checked his ranking, he found that it hadn't moved at all.
In this great world, his ranking had moved up by ten places, and he was now ranked 267th.

Chapter 1234
After checking his ranking, Luo Zheng let out a sigh.
In the Dream Realized Battlefield, he had too many competitors.
Putting aside their strength, each of the hundreds of millions of Divine Sea Realm cultivators had their own opportunities and luck!
Warriors with both strength and Luck might have monopolized holy lands like the Withered Tree Door that allowed them to plunder Fantasy Points. Thus, the speed at which they obtained Fantasy Points was extremely fast.
If it wasn't for his relationship with Liu Yu, Luo Zheng could've driven away the Demon Night Clan cultivators and monopolized the Withered Tree Door. The next time the door opened, he could enter alone and obtain 4 million Dream Points. Ji Luoxue probably wouldn't be able to compete with Luo Zheng even if he took over a main city and relied on millions of cultivators to "pay taxes".
Aizen had been wary of Luo Zheng because she was afraid this would happen. After all, she was the Realm Lord's successor and wanted to obtain a good ranking. This time, she had taken the initiative to give Luo Zheng 21 Dream Fruits.
"There is a stone plate at the top of the tree that leads to the 13th branch. Why don't we go up?" Luo Zheng asked as he stared at the stone plate.
"We can't go there," Liu Yu shook his head at Luo Zheng.
Aizen blinked and smiled, "Brother Luo Zheng, if you want to try, you can try. However, the 13th branch is the peak of the Great Toon Divine Tree. The difficulty is not something we can attempt, but the rewards must be amazing."
Luo Zheng nodded, "I want to try!"
Even though he was ranked in the top 20,000 of the Dream Battlefield, this wasn't the end for Luo Zheng!
"We've climbed up to the thirteenth level once. The top should be the Legacy Grounds of the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe. Once we enter, we'll be attacked by strange sorcery. We retreated after a few of us died. Luo Zheng, you have to think this through." Liu Yu's face was filled with worry.
"Mm," Luo Zheng nodded and smiled, "If it doesn't work out, I can still retreat."
Since Luo Zheng had made up his mind, Liu Yu didn't try to stop him. She knew it was pointless to stop him.
As for the other Demon Night Clan members, such as Bai Wei and Aizen, they were looking forward to it. They had tried the Withered Tree Door several times but didn't dare set foot on the top of the Great Toon Divine Tree. Would Luo Zheng be able to challenge it?
Under the watchful eyes of the crowd, Luo Zheng once again stepped onto the stone plate. Accompanied by a series of strange coughs from the little skulls, another row of words appeared under Luo Zheng's feet. Without much hesitation, he flew toward the crown of the Big Toon Divine Tree.
At the top of the Great Toon Divine Tree was a huge gray tree crown. At this point, the originally lingering white clouds had completely turned into a lead-gray color …
The moment he stepped onto the treetop, Luo Zheng had a very strange feeling in his heart!
This strange feeling even felt a little familiar …
'Where have I felt this before?'
Luo Zheng, who was standing in the same spot, spent a few breaths of time to recall, but he couldn't find the answer in his own memory.
Above the massive treetop, brown and yellow branches spread out, forming a flat ground. And on top of this treetop, there were skulls that were dozens of feet in size!
"These skulls … should be the skulls of the Giant Clan. They seem to have been used as houses by the Ancient Witch Clan?" Luo Zheng looked around.
Luo Zheng didn't have much contact with the Giant Race. The Giant Race's martial artists were twice as tall as the Demon Race's martial artists. However, their movement speed was slow, and the Giant Race's talent wasn't too high. The only thing they had was their innate divine strength. Almost every Giant had innate divine strength.
However, in the Upper Realm, where there were many powerhouses, they couldn't compete with other clans just by relying on their physical strength.
Therefore, in the Upper Realm, the Giant Clan was considered a small clan.
After looking around, Luo Zheng began to explore the tree crown …
Perhaps it was because he was alone, but he felt that the atmosphere around him was becoming gloomy.
The Great Toon Sacred Tree was very quiet. Luo Zheng didn't feel this when he was accompanied by the girls of the Night Demon Clan. Now that he was standing here alone, he could even hear his own heartbeat.
"Thump …"
"Thump …"
"Thump …"
After Luo Zheng walked a few steps along the treetop, he suddenly stopped in his tracks.
At the same time, he frowned slightly …
The sound of his heartbeat seemed to be too loud!
However, just as Luo Zheng stopped, the sound of his heartbeat gradually subsided. After a few breaths, it disappeared into his ears.
'What's going on?'
After thinking for a while, he moved his feet again.
"Thump, thump, thump …"
Just as he moved, he heard his heartbeat again!
"Heh …"
A cold smile appeared on Luo Zheng's face. He was now sure that he had fallen into some kind of strange witchcraft.
The witchcraft in the universe was now considered a relatively small cultivation method. Since the mysterious disappearance of the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe, their witchcraft inheritance had also disappeared completely. Only a few witchcraft inheritances had been passed down, but they were no longer systematic.
However, this witchcraft was powerful because of its strangeness. It could often kill people without them noticing, making it impossible to guard against!
Luo Zheng knew that he couldn't see through this witchcraft, but at this moment, Luo Zheng didn't think too much about it. He allowed his heartbeat to become louder and louder as he continued to move toward the tree crown.
"Thump, thump, thump …"
As Luo Zheng took more steps, the sound became louder and louder. It was as if someone was punching Luo Zheng in the heart.
However, Luo Zheng cultivated his body from the outside to the inside. The strength of his body wasn't limited to his muscles, bones, and skin. Even his internal organs had absorbed all kinds of flames and were as strong as divine weapons!
If it were any other cultivator, having their heart gripped by this strange force, they would likely be severely injured and unable to move, but Luo Zheng was completely fine. He even ignored the sound of his heartbeat ringing in his ears.
But after the time for an incense stick to burn, he stopped once more ….
"This path doesn't seem right … I seem to be walking in circles …"
On the path at the top of the tree crown, there were sixty to seventy giant skulls. After he walked a few rounds, he soon found that he was walking in circles.
It seemed that he was trapped here?
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed slightly. His pupils were dyed with a layer of green.
After activating the Clear Eyes Spirit Eyes, Luo Zheng's eyes swept around …
Through the light lead-gray fog, Luo Zheng's pupils widened slightly. He couldn't help but gasp!
In the depths of this lead-gray fog, there were actually huge eyeballs floating!
Because he couldn't judge the distance, Luo Zheng didn't know how big these eyeballs were. However, to Luo Zheng, they were undoubtedly huge. These eyeballs were floating in the air, silently watching him!
No matter what kind of creature these eyeballs came from, anyone who was stared at by these eyeballs would feel creepy. Luo Zheng was no exception!
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1235
"There are a total of 13 giant eyeballs. These eyeballs should represent some kind of symbol of the Ancient Sorcerer Race or even the supreme existence of their race …"
Previously, Luo Zheng had speculated that this huge Illusory Battlefield should have been constructed according to the will of the Heavenly Dao, and this will contained the memories of the Heavenly Dao.
Therefore, in this era, the civilizations that had been born, the civilizations that had been destroyed, and the civilizations that had survived would all appear one after another.
For example, the temple that Luo Zheng had entered came from the Ten Thousand Buddha Sacred Realm …
For example, the Great Toon Divine Tree in front of him came from the Ancient Sorcerer Race …
The huge universe had settled down over billions of years, and countless civilizations had accumulated.
Some civilizations had only appeared briefly before they dissipated in the long river of history, becoming specks of dust in history. However, some civilizations had tenaciously and thrived, such as the Human Race and the Demon Night Race …
In that temple, Luo Zheng had obtained a Teleportation Token and could freely travel through this great world.
Then, what was at the top of the Great Toon Divine Tree?
To be safe, Luo Zheng could have chosen not to enter this place. Now that the Teleportation Token could be used again, he could also use it to escape this predicament!
However, there was no danger to one's life in the Illusory Battlefield. All the cultivators were doing their best to fight, so Luo Zheng naturally would not choose to shrink back.
Moreover, he had just checked his ranking. Although he had obtained more than two million Illusory Points, his ranking had fallen instead of advancing!
The fellows in front of Luo Zheng had either used special methods to plunder Illusory Points or discovered something that Luo Zheng had not discovered …
If Luo Zheng did not fight now, his ranking would fall even more.
Outside the lead-gray fog, those giant eyeballs only floated quietly in the air without any movement, as if they were just a strange background.
Luo Zheng naturally did not pay attention to them. He hesitated for a while before suddenly leaping up!
Since he couldn't see through this strange maze, he didn't want to compete with it. With a leap, he flew into the air.
No matter how elaborate a maze was, it could not extend into the air. Moreover, there was no restriction on flying on the Great Toon Divine Tree …
"Huff huff …"
Although there were layers of thick lead-colored fog in front of him, Luo Zheng was able to see the environment at the top of the tree with the help of the Clear Eyes Spirit Eyes.
The top of the Big Toon Heavenly Tree was relatively open. It was a hill-shaped treetop. Along the way, the treetop was inclined upward at an angle of about 30 degrees. Its surface area was about the size of a main city. With Luo Zheng's speed, he could cross it in dozens of breaths.
At the top of the treetop, there was a ball of purple light swaying gently. However, it was too far away for Luo Zheng to distinguish.
"Phew …"
After Luo Zheng flew along the canopy for a long time, his eyes suddenly narrowed!
The reason he flew like this was to use the purple light as a reference. However, even after flying for a while, the distance between him and the purple light did not decrease at all!
When Luo Zheng looked down, he saw that the three skulls were placed together. It was where he had been standing before!
"There is some kind of power in this maze that binds me here. Is it constructed by the Law of Space?"
It was impossible for an ordinary maze to achieve such an effect. The only possibility for Luo Zheng to wander in the air was that only a maze constructed by the Law of Space could bind him.
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng suddenly took a step forward. This time, he traveled through space.
His figure was like a long shadow, disappearing from one place and reappearing in another …
After repeating this several times, the ball of purple light at the top of the treetop was getting closer and closer to Luo Zheng!
"It's effective?"
Luo Zheng's face lit up!
However, just as he was about to travel again, he heard a muffled groan. His figure staggered and slid out of the space!
But when he was shuttling through space, he hit an invisible barrier!
Looking at himself, he returned to his original place.
At this moment, Luo Zheng's face revealed a trace of a bitter smile. He was really helpless.
"Thump, thump …"
Accompanied by the sound of his heartbeat, Luo Zheng's eyebrows slightly wrinkled. The predicament in front of him was really troublesome …
At this moment, Luo Zheng once again landed on the treetop. His eyes were fixed on the big skulls.
These skulls were twice as tall as Luo Zheng. They were like houses.
Previously, Luo Zheng didn't pay too much attention to them. However, when he was traveling back and forth, he discovered that when he was far away from these skulls, the sound of his heartbeat would become softer. When he was closer to the skulls, the sound of his heartbeat would become louder.
It was this subtle difference that made Luo Zheng notice these skulls!
He walked forward, stretched out his hand, and rubbed the surface of these skulls …
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed slightly. His hand exerted force!
"Kacha!"
A crack appeared on the surface of these huge skulls, extending from the cheekbones all the way to the top of the skull.
Then, Luo Zheng once again struck out with his palm. This huge skull finally disintegrated …
"Boom …"
The moment the skull cracked open, a figure was revealed from within. It was a dark figure that was only one-third the size of an average human! That thing was wrapped in rags, and one could vaguely make out its bony body …
"Whoosh …"
However, that figure disappeared in a flash and disappeared into another skull.
"It's … a shadow creature!"
When Luo Zheng was traveling through the lower realms, he had seen this kind of creature!
With a glance, he had a very deep impression of this bony body. At that time, a group of shadow creatures was chasing him and several people of the Demi-Immortal Clan!
Why would he see a shadow creature here?
Could it be that this Ancient Sorcerer Clan was the Shadow Clan that was cursed by the God?
Just now, when he drove this thing out, the sound of his heartbeat disappeared. It seemed that this thing was most likely playing tricks on him!
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng no longer hesitated. His figure flashed and followed in front of the next giant skull. With his whole body, he directly crashed into it.
The skulls of these giants were not hard. In front of Luo Zheng's Divine Artifact Body, they were like paper. He directly crashed into them.
However, the moment Luo Zheng crashed into them, from the two huge eye sockets of this skull, two bony black shadows appeared …
"Two?"
"These creatures are hidden in these skulls?"
"Whoosh …"
This time, Luo Zheng did not intend to let this thing slip into the next skull. He wanted to catch one and find out what it was!
This black figure was not big, but its speed was extremely fast. Luo Zheng urged his body to move, and with a grab, he could only grab a piece of rags behind it!
"Sizzle …"
When Luo Zheng grabbed this piece of rags in his hand, it turned into a black gas and corroded Luo Zheng's fingers. This thing was also highly poisonous.
If it was another person's hand, perhaps even the bones would have been corroded. However, Luo Zheng's expression did not change at all. He was still chasing after the black figure!
"Gira-"
Luo Zheng chased after one of them, while the other black figure stretched out its bony fingers. The fingernails on its fingers were like the claws of a wild beast. It was chanting some kind of incantation.
Although Luo Zheng knew that the incantation of the Ancient Sorcerer Clan was powerful, he did not care so much. He had to catch this thing in front of him first!
Although the figure was agile, its speed was still much slower than Luo Zheng's. When Luo Zheng stretched out his hand, the black figure could no longer dodge and was pressed to the ground by Luo Zheng. He lifted the cloak!
The moment the cloak was lifted, it turned into a black gas and began to dissipate. This guy in front of Luo Zheng did look similar to a human in appearance, but its whole body was black. There was no skin on its hands and feet. Instead, it was replaced by a hard shell that glowed with light!
"Gira-"
The thing that was pressed down by Luo Zheng seemed to know that it could not escape, so it also chanted an incantation.
Then, two black vortexes appeared beside Luo Zheng, and two palm-sized skulls emerged from the vortexes.

Chapter 1236
Facing these palm-sized skulls, Luo Zheng punched them twice!
Luo Zheng didn't put these tiny skulls in his eyes. He only used 10% of his strength in these two punches!
According to Luo Zheng's plan, it should be enough to crush them.
But he never expected that these two skulls would only be sent flying by Luo Zheng …
After being sent flying several hundred meters by Luo Zheng and piercing through two giant skulls, they actually circled back and opened their big white mouths, biting at Luo Zheng!
These little skulls … were abnormal!
"Gila …"
"Gila …"
At the same time, Luo Zheng heard the sound of incantations coming from the giant skulls around him!
Black whirlpools appeared around Luo Zheng, and little skulls drilled out from these whirlpools one after another.
"They really can't be broken?"
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed, and he crushed the guy on the ground with his foot. Then, with a wave of his right hand, he grabbed one of the little skulls.
"Break for me!"
This time, Luo Zheng used the power of ten thousand dragon scales!
The surging power flowed from Luo Zheng's right arm into his five fingers. Under such a violent power, even a divine tool would be twisted.
However …
These little skulls were like boulders, completely withstanding Luo Zheng's power. Luo Zheng still couldn't crush them.
Twenty thousand!
Thirty thousand!
To deal with these little skulls, Luo Zheng almost exhausted all of his strength. He even used the power of the dragon scales to completely erupt the vital essence in his body!
But the little skulls still didn't budge …
At the same time, the other little skulls also bit at Luo Zheng.
"Cough, cough, cough …"
Luo Zheng spun in the air and kicked these skulls away. His figure suddenly flashed, and he wanted to kill the guys hiding in these giant skulls first.
"Boom …"
The giant skulls were broken apart one by one by Luo Zheng.
These ragged guys in black cloaks weren't difficult to deal with.
Under Luo Zheng's full strength, it was almost impossible to escape from his hands …
He went along the treetop, destroying these giant skulls one by one, and killing them one by one.
As a result, he found that he was getting closer and closer to the treetop.
If he wanted to get out of this maze, he had to destroy this maze. Luo Zheng finally understood!
Although Luo Zheng thought of a way to break this maze and it wasn't difficult to approach the purple light, a new problem arose. What should he do with these little skulls?
Since these skeletons were attracted by these strange fellows, Luo Zheng originally thought that the little skeletons would disappear after killing them. However, he had already killed more than half of these fellows, yet not a single one had disappeared. On the contrary, the number of little skeletons following behind Luo Zheng was increasing, and he couldn't shake them off at all.
Killing these fellows now had nothing to do with the Little Skeleton.
Half an hour later …
All the giant skulls on the treetop were smashed to pieces by Luo Zheng.
The guys from the Shadow Clan were also completely wiped out by Luo Zheng!
However, four to five hundred palm-sized little skulls appeared behind Luo Zheng. They were densely floating behind Luo Zheng, taking turns to bite Luo Zheng's body. Just looking at them would make one's scalp tingle!
"Scram!"
A majestic force gushed out of Luo Zheng's body!
That force slapped the densely packed little skulls behind him, and all the skulls were sent flying by Luo Zheng …
But after a while, they gathered around Luo Zheng and continued to bite him from behind!
The biting power of these little skulls couldn't be considered strong, and Luo Zheng had the body of a Divine Tool, so he didn't feel too much.
But if he couldn't get rid of these little skulls, wouldn't they always be behind him?
At this time, Luo Zheng finally remembered!
Previously, when he was surrounded by Celestial Venerables in the middle heaven domain, there was a Celestial Venerable who was followed by countless skulls. Those skulls were also constantly biting that Celestial Venerable.
That Celestial Venerable should have also killed a member of the Shadow Clan, so he provoked these skulls …
If a Celestial Venerable couldn't do anything to these skulls, then Luo Zheng would be even more helpless.
However, that Celestial Venerable was killed in the universe, and Luo Zheng was now in the Illusory Battlefield. But carrying so many skulls around the Illusory Battlefield wasn't a good idea!
After struggling for a while, Luo Zheng shook his head. He didn't care anymore, he could only take one step at a time. At most, he would just treat it as carrying a "shell" made of skulls. These little skulls were so hard, maybe they could help him block some attacks …
After no longer thinking about it, Luo Zheng walked towards the purple light.
After breaking through the maze, Luo Zheng was less than two thousand feet away from the purple light. In front of Luo Zheng was a flight of stairs made of tree trunks. These stairs were high in the air, extending all the way to the top of the Great Toon Divine Tree.
The moment Luo Zheng stepped on the stairs, a giant eyeball in the sky gently blinked.
A strange fragrance entered Luo Zheng's nose, and the surrounding fog turned from gray to pale yellow.
"Poisonous fog?"
The two most powerful methods of the Ancient Shaman Clan were voodoo poison and curse techniques …
That curse technique really gave Luo Zheng a headache. Those little skulls hanging behind him constantly grinding their teeth were proof of this. He simply couldn't deal with it!
As for the voodoo poison …
He completely ignored it.
Actually, this yellow poisonous fog was the Ancient Shaman Clan's long lost Tuo Luo Gu Poison!
The method to concoct this poison had long been lost, but according to some records, this poison was so overbearing that even warriors with three transformations couldn't resist it. Even some Sector Lords would pale when they heard about it. Back when the Ancient Sorcerer Clan was expanding, countless warriors had died from this poison.
But no matter how overbearing it was, it had no effect on Luo Zheng.
He was a weapon, but it looked like a human's weapon …
Luo Zheng walked forward another ten feet, and another giant eyeball blinked. The yellow fog dissipated, and what filled the air was a blue poisonous fog!
This blue poisonous fog made Luo Zheng frown!
It wasn't that he was afraid of this poison, but that this blue poisonous fog was simply too smelly!
That yellow poisonous fog was incomparably fragrant, and this blue poisonous fog was incomparably smelly. They were two extremes.
Luo Zheng could only cover his nose as he walked forward.
After that, every ten feet that Luo Zheng walked, the poisonous fog would change, and the smell would be completely different. But most of them were very smelly, either stinking or rotting. In short, other than the first poisonous fog, which smelled good, the rest of the poisonous fog made Luo Zheng uncomfortable.
If the Ancient Clan's almighty beings were still alive, and they discovered that Luo Zheng used this method to determine their Thirteen Killing Strange Poisons, they would probably vomit blood from anger!
The Thirteen Killing Strange Poisons were the pride of the Ancient Clan. The Thirteen Killing Strange Poisons each had different toxicity. Some poisons could cause a person's entire body to fester and die, while some poisons would hide in a warrior's body for several years before erupting. Once they erupted, there wouldn't be a complete corpse left, and the entire person would be swallowed by the poison!
But for Luo Zheng to use these poisons, it was a bit of a "reckless waste", too much of a waste …
These stairs filled with poisonous fog were like a heavenly moat to ordinary warriors, impossible to pass.
At this moment, Luo Zheng easily passed through and finally arrived in front of the purple ball of light!
That purple ball of light was like a ball of blue water floating in the air. After Luo Zheng sized it up, a trace of disappointment appeared on his face.
"Without those fruits and without the reward of fantasy points, what is this …"
Luo Zheng saw a rectangular metal certificate floating in the purple ball of light.
He stretched out his hand and passed through the purple ball of light, grabbing the rectangular metal certificate. His eyes flashed with curiosity.
After grabbing the metal certificate, Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed slightly. He only saw the words "Karma Hall" on the metal certificate.
At the same time, a thought was transmitted from the metal certificate!
"Pay five million fantasy points and you can enter the Karma Hall. The Karma Hall needs ten people to enter at the same time. There are three people left before the Karma Hall opens. Do you want to pay fantasy points …"
When this voice echoed in Luo Zheng's mind, Luo Zheng was stunned!

Chapter 1237
Luo Zheng was very unwilling to accept the fact that he had only obtained an iron certificate and a body full of skulls after wandering around the peak of the Great Toon Sacred Tree.
He stood at the top of the tree and looked around. There didn't seem to be anything special …
He activated his Clear Eyes Spirit Eyes and stared at the thirteen giant eyeballs!
"I wonder what's the reward for smashing these eyeballs?" Luo Zheng licked his lips.
In a fit of whimsy, he really leaped up and broke through the layers of fog, flying toward those giant eyeballs that were far away in the horizon!
However, he had only flown a few meters when a huge invisible force suddenly came from the sky and slapped down fiercely at Luo Zheng!
"Bang!"
With a crisp sound, Luo Zheng's figure fell down!
He fell from the top of the tree …
During this process, Luo Zheng tried to stop himself from falling!
At that moment, Luo Zhengcai realized that he couldn't defy the invisible force at all. He fell from the top of the Great Toon Divine Tree …
Liu Yu and Aizen had already descended from the tree and were waiting for Luo Zheng not far away.
After waiting for a long time, they were also guessing Luo Zheng's situation.
"That guy … Could he have been sent back to the main city?" Aizen looked up at the top of the tree. She couldn't see anything through the fog.
Being sent back to the main city meant that he died.
"It would be a pity if he really died at the top of the Great Toon Divine Tree. He had quite a lot of fantasy points. Half of his fantasy points were left at the top. No one would dare to go in and take it." Bai Wei shrugged her tall shoulders.
Liu Yu looked indifferent. "Probably not."
"But it's been so long …"
As soon as Aizen's voice fell, everyone saw a shadow falling down from the white fog.
"Boom!"
The force of this smash caused the ground beneath everyone's feet to tremble a few times!
Liu Yu's figure flashed and saw a big hole not far away. When she went over, Luo Zheng had already climbed out of the hole.
"Luo Zheng! Are you okay? "Liu Yu asked with concern.
Luo Zheng smiled. "Don't worry, I'm fine …"
Aizen was like a butterfly as he slowly floated over. Just as he was about to speak, he saw the skulls above his head. His face changed!
It wasn't just Aizen, the other cultivators, including Liu Yu, were also on guard.
"Be careful!"
Liu Yu's reaction was the fastest. With a slender sword in hand, an extremely sharp sword light shot out, piercing towards the small skulls in the sky.
"Pa pa …"
Dozens of small skulls were sent flying by this sword strike, but in the blink of an eye, they circled back and rushed towards Luo Zheng, biting his back tightly. Their white teeth were still gnawing at Luo Zheng's back, as if an evil spirit was grinding its teeth!
"This …" Liu Yu's eyes were filled with surprise. She knew very well how powerful her sword strike was, but there was not a single mark on the surface of the little skull that had been struck by her sword light!
Luo Zheng said helplessly. "It's useless, these skulls can't be destroyed!"
"What's going on?" Aizen's face was full of curiosity. He couldn't hide his smile. Although the skulls looked scary, Luo Zheng was carrying a bunch of skulls on his back. They looked like a huge tortoise shell. It was really funny.
"It should be the curse power of the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe." Luo Zheng tore off a small skull from his shoulder and threw it out. "This thing seems indestructible. Is the curse power so powerful?"
After Luo Zheng threw the small skull, it spun in the air and bit towards Luo Zheng again. Luo Zheng gently waved his finger and a sharp wind blade cut towards the small skull. The wind blade was fierce but it didn't cause any damage to the small skull.
However, just as the small skull flew towards him, he caught it in his hand. His eyebrows slightly raised, as if he had thought of something. He lightly pressed his spatial ring, and a pickaxe suddenly jumped out!
"Black Star Pickaxe! Supreme Artifact! "
Aizen's eyes shone with a bright light.
She was the heir of the Sector Lord. She had all the best treasures in the Dream Battlefield, but Aizen couldn't possibly have a sovereign artifact!
There were so many Sector Lords in a hundred thousand Great Worlds.
But there were only three thousand Supreme Artifacts …
These three thousand were accumulated over countless years. It took a million years for one Supreme Artifact to be born.
This guy had a Black Star Pickaxe!
Luo Zheng did not care about the Black Star Pickaxe's origin. All he needed to know was that it was very useful.
Since the pickaxe had the power to dig through everything, he wondered if it could break the small skull.
"Bang!"
If he smashed it down …
The small skull was still a small skull, and the pickaxe was still a pickaxe. It was useless!
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng's face was filled with shock. Even the Black Star Pickaxe could not break it?
The pickaxe used the Heavenly Dao's Causality Law. It should be able to break anything.
If even the pickaxe couldn't break it, it could only mean one thing. The Causality Law in the skulls was stronger than the pickaxe!
Luo Zheng couldn't figure it out.
Whether it was the Ancient Witch Race or the Shadow Race, were they that strong?
Those cloaked fellows shouldn't be very powerful in the Shadow Clan. At the very least, it shouldn't be difficult for Luo Zheng to kill them.
Yet, such trash was able to attract such a heaven-defying little skeleton. How did they do it?
Why would such a strong race escape to the other side of the world?
Even the Black Star Pickaxe could not break this little skull, so Luo Zheng could only put it away in his storage pouch.
Aizen reached out and "plucked" a small skull from Luo Zheng's back. After a while, she smiled and said, "I have an idea."
"What is it?" Luo Zheng asked.
"These small skulls have been biting you, right?" Aizen said.
"Yes," Luo Zheng nodded, "The problem is, I don't know how long they'll follow me."
"But the main city is ruled by the Heavenly Dao. Fighting is forbidden there. You can try using the power of the main city." Aizen suggested.
Luo Zheng's eyes lit up when he heard Aizen's words.
He didn't think of that.
According to the rules of the Dream Battlefield, fighting was forbidden in the main city. Since the rules were set by the Heavenly Dao, it couldn't be broken. He didn't believe these small skulls could break the rules.
So he should try entering the main city.
Luo Zheng had left from the main city of the Night Demon Clan and traveled 20,000 kilometers to the north before arriving at the Withered Tree Gate. There were two main cities to the north and west of the Withered Tree Gate, and they weren't far from each other …
If he could get rid of these small skulls, he would thank the Heavens.
"Aside from these small skulls, what else did you get from the top floor? Don't tell me you got a hundred fruits? "Aizen continued to ask.
She knew she couldn't pass the top floor but she was curious about what Luo Zheng got.
Upon hearing these words, Luo Zheng's eyes widened. If there were really a hundred fruits, he would have to thank the heavens for a total of ten million Fantasy points.
He didn't hide it and took out the iron certificate, "Don't even think about it. Don't mention a hundred fruits, you didn't even give me one. You didn't even give me a dream point. You gave me this! Five million fantasy points will be deducted! "
"Five million points deducted!" Aizen couldn't help but click her tongue. Her accumulation speed wasn't slow but after so long, she had only accumulated two million points.
What was so special about this iron certificate? It directly made him pay so many points.
According to common logic, just entering the Karma Hall would cost five million fantasy points. In this large world, there were probably only three hundred or so cultivators. It could be said that they were as rare as the morning stars. The rewards in the Karma Hall were definitely not cheap!
But Luo Zheng didn't have time to think about this problem. He had to get rid of these small skulls first.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1238
Luo Zheng didn't return to the main city of the Yao Night Clan with Aizen and Liu Yu.
To ordinary warriors, the speed at which he obtained Fantasy Points through the Withered Tree Door wasn't considered slow, but it was far from enough for Luo Zheng.
Also, Luo Zheng occupied the Withered Tree Gate, so Aizen's share of the points would decrease. Aizen probably didn't want Luo Zheng to stay either.
Outside the Withered Tree Gate, many people from the Yao Night Clan watched as Luo Zheng turned into a streak of light and disappeared.
Liu Yu looked at Luo Zheng's departing figure with a complicated expression.
Aizen glanced at Liu Yu and knew what Liu Yu was thinking.
After all, Luo Zheng was her only chance to regain her freedom, but the chances were slim.
"I think Luo Zheng is strong enough to compete with the prodigies of the major factions. He might even be able to compete for the top 100 in the Dream Battlefield," Aizen said.
The top 100 in the Dream Battlefield were treasures no matter where they went!
These people were favored by the heavens and earth!
They either had a rare bloodline, peerless talent, or even heaven-defying luck …
Out of a hundred million cultivators, there were only a hundred or so left.
Only such cultivators were qualified to be called prodigies!
Liu Yu remained silent for a long while. When Luo Zheng's streak of light completely disappeared from her sight, she simply said, "Luo Zheng will stand at the peak of the Illusion Battlefield …"
Aizen's eyes flashed with a strange light when she heard Liu Yu's words.
Stand at the peak? Compete for first place?
Liu Yu's view was a bit too optimistic.
She admitted that Luo Zheng was shockingly strong, far stronger than ordinary Divine Sea Realm cultivators. With Luo Zheng's current strength, ordinary Divine Sea Realm cultivators wouldn't last more than a single round against Luo Zheng.
However, his competitors were even more terrifying!
Let's not talk about the prodigies of the other races for now!
For example, their Yao Ye clan's Si Sha's strength was unfathomable. It would not be easy for Luo Zheng to win against him. Moreover, there were so many great clans in the universe, and every one of them was a strong competitor.
Liu Yu's confidence was a bit too blind.
However, Aizen wouldn't point it out. If Luo Zheng could really do it, she would be happy for Liu Yu. Perhaps … Luo Zheng could really take Liu Yu out of that hell in three years!
After the Withered Tree Gate's fruit was taken, it would take about twelve hours to open it again.
During this time, Aizen would lead the Yao Ye clan back to the city to rest and reorganize. Now, they were on their way back.
…
Luo Zheng's teleport token could be used again.
However, every time he used it, he had to wait for a long time. Now that he knew he'd reach a main city if he continued north, he wouldn't waste it.
Before he flew, Luo Zheng's body shook violently, shaking off the little skulls. Then he turned into a streak of light and sped forward!
The little skulls chased after Luo Zheng endlessly!
Their speed was almost the same as Luo Zheng's. They could fly as fast as Luo Zheng could. Whenever Luo Zheng paused, the little skulls would open their mouths and bite Luo Zheng. It was really annoying.
Luo Zheng couldn't do anything about it.
After flying for about ten thousand miles, a majestic main city appeared in front of him.
Only after throwing a glance at the structure of the city wall and the forms of those buildings, Luo Zheng knew that this was a major city of demons...
After arriving here, Luo Zheng slowed down a little and carefully observed his surroundings.
At the same time, there was a burst of "cough cough cough" behind him. The little skulls bit Luo Zheng's back.
"Whatever, I'll try and see if I can get rid of these little skulls first." Thinking of this, Luo Zheng's body suddenly shook, shaking off the little skulls. Luo Zheng turned into a streak of light and rushed towards the main city.
Outside this main city, many Devil Race martial artists were still fighting non-stop.
The Demon Race was originally a bloodthirsty and warlike race. In this kind of environment, their fighting spirit only grew stronger!
According to Luo Zheng's plan, after obtaining the teleport token, he would constantly shuttle between the various main cities and harvest the "fat sheep" outside the main city.
But right now, he was in a hurry to deal with the trouble behind him!
Therefore, he didn't even look at the Demon Race cultivators outside the city and directly shot towards the main city!
"Sou!"
The speed of the fleeing light that Luo Zheng transformed into was also extremely fast!
The Devil Race martial artists who were originally engaged in a chaotic battle suddenly saw this flight light, and a look of surprise flashed across their faces one by one.
After seeing that escape light, they actually saw a bunch of skulls following behind. These Demon Race cultivators also didn't understand the situation!
"What the hell is that!"
"The one at the front seems to be a Human Race cultivator …"
"What is he doing here?"
The Demon Race cultivators inside and outside the main city were also talking about this scene.
Luo Zheng naturally didn't care about their curiosity and just flew towards the main city …
But just as he approached the main city, a Demon Race cultivator flew out from the main city with a giant spear on his shoulder. He said with a sinister smile, "Hey, where did this Human Race brat come from? If you want to escape into the main city, then leave behind your dreams …"
Before this Demon Race cultivator finished speaking, in the eyes of many Demon Race cultivators, the escape light caused by Luo Zheng's flight took a turn and circled around that Demon Race cultivator!
"Pu!"
A spray of brown blood burst out!
The Demon Race cultivator, who intended to block the way, was cut in two!
The vast majority of Demon Race cultivators didn't even see clearly what method Luo Zheng used to kill him!
By the time they reacted, Luo Zheng had already arrived at the edge of the city wall!
This scene immediately caused the Demon Race cultivators present to reveal looks of horror. Those who were still talking about it were now silent like cicadas in winter, afraid that they would be targeted by Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng's expression was extremely indifferent. He wouldn't waste time here. He stepped onto the main city wall, turned around, and looked back. He wanted to see what would happen to these little skulls. Would they be obliterated by the rules of the main city?
Soon, the gloominess in his eyes became even more intense.
These little skulls were still grinding their white teeth, but they didn't enter the main city. They just wandered around outside the city wall, as if there was a strange force in the main city that kept them inside!
Luo Zheng clenched his teeth and walked out of the city wall once again. As soon as he left the city wall, the little skulls were like sharks that smelled blood, once again biting at Luo Zheng …
After these little skulls bit his body, Luo Zheng entered the main city once again. That invisible force once again isolated these little skulls outside!
As for the Demon Race cultivators inside and outside the main city, as they saw this scene, they all stared at each other in a daze.
"What the hell is this guy doing!?"
"I thought those skulls were his weapons. He seems to want to bring them into the main city?"
"I don't understand …"
Just now, Luo Zheng had killed a Devil Race expert with lightning speed. Now that they were discussing, they lowered their voices. The strong were always revered.
Luo Zheng frowned and thought for a while. Standing on the city wall, he suddenly reached out and grabbed a small skull in his hand like lightning. Then, he pulled it into his arms!
He was originally standing on the edge of the main city. If he took a step forward, he would be outside the city. If he took a step back, he would be inside the city. Luo Zheng wanted to pull the little skull into the main city!
But when Luo Zheng pulled the skull to the edge of the city wall, he suddenly felt a very obvious resistance!
This force obviously didn't come from these little skulls. Because when Luo Zheng grabbed a little skull, he could throw it thousands of feet away.
It seemed like the main city's restriction was preventing these little skulls from entering!
Aizen's idea was correct …
If he could bring these little skulls into the main city, he could rely on the power of the main city to kill them.
But the problem was that he couldn't bring them in!

Chapter 1239
Luo Zheng's feet were pressed against a brick on the city wall!
The power within his body that could destroy mountains and expand seas was surging out!
If he were to stand on a wall made of ordinary bricks, the wall would probably collapse like tofu.
However, the main city of the Illusory Battlefield was indestructible. Even when he used his power, the wall under his feet didn't budge at all.
The target of Luo Zheng's power was the small skeletons outside the city. The rules of the city that forbade fighting didn't take effect.
It was just that the invisible power was like an insurmountable wall! Even after Luo Zheng lit up all the dragon scales, the small skeletons still couldn't be pulled into the main city. The small skeletons could only cough and grind their teeth in his hand. They were even biting Luo Zheng's fingers.
"Hu …"
Helpless, Luo Zheng could only loosen his hands.
It was impossible to rely on the main city's power of law to destroy these small skeletons.
This main city was indestructible. Even if Luo Zheng used his full strength, he couldn't destroy the main city's walls because of the protection of the invisible power of law!
Similarly, Luo Zheng couldn't pull the small skeletons into the main city!
These little skulls were a curse cast by the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe. Once they were summoned, they would continuously "attack" Luo Zheng. This was also the reason why they were rejected by the main city.
Unless Luo Zheng had the power to destroy the main city, he wouldn't be able to bring the small skeletons into the main city. If Luo Zheng's power was so great, he wouldn't need to rely on the main city's power of law. He could directly crush the small skeletons!
Looking at the small skeletons dancing in the air, Luo Zheng shook his head and walked a few steps around the main city's walls. The small skeletons followed him like shadows, sticking close to the edge of the main city as he moved!
"These guys …"
Looking at the small skeletons baring their white teeth, Luo Zheng was speechless.
Many demon warriors stared at the walking Luo Zheng. The more they looked, the more confused they became. They couldn't understand what Luo Zheng was trying to do!
After Luo Zheng walked a few steps on the city wall, he frowned and looked at the small skeletons. Suddenly, he jumped out of the main city and picked up another small skeleton. The power in his arms surged out again, and he ruthlessly smashed the small skeleton towards the main city!
The small skeleton was like a meteor, smashing towards the main city with an extremely powerful force …
"Boom!"
When the skull smashed into the main city, it still hit an invisible wall and bounced back at an extremely fast speed. However, it bounced into the ground outside the city and directly drilled its head into it!
"Pop …"
It was unknown how far the small skeletons underground …
Not long after, the little skull's rebounding force disappeared, and it emerged from the ground far away. It quickly returned to Luo Zheng's back and bit him on the back.
"Heh! I understand now. That human brat seems to want to get rid of those skeletons behind him! "
"I don't know where he got these things. They're so sturdy. I'm afraid they're even more powerful than divine artifacts, right?"
"…"
Even though these Fiend Race martial artists suppressed their voices very softly, it still entered Luo Zheng's ears.
He was originally very depressed, but when he heard these words, his eyes flashed with killing intent …
"Since I can't clean up these broken things, I'll just plunder fantasy points first!"
When Luo Zheng's aura changed, the faces of the demon warriors inside and outside the city wall changed!
Especially some demon warriors outside the main city!
One of the demon warriors reacted the fastest. Seeing that the situation was bad, he fled towards the main city at an extremely fast speed.
Although this brat's strength was extremely abnormal, as long as he was in the main city, he would be safe!
However, Luo Zheng didn't give them a chance …
"Whoosh!"
A small skull whizzed through the air and smashed into the Demon Race cultivator's head with a tremendous force!
"Pfff …"
The Demon Race warrior was also wearing a helmet, but the small skull directly smashed the helmet to pieces. The Demon Race warrior's head was like a fragile watermelon, directly exploding!
After a ball of fantasy light scattered, it flew towards Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng was too lazy to count the number of dreamy light spots. He simply tossed them out and then pulled off the little skulls on his back and shot them at the surrounding Demon Race martial artists!
"Pa, pfff, bang …"
All kinds of collision sounds came. Luo Zheng was helpless. In any case, these small skeletons were terrifyingly sturdy. Coupled with his terrifying strength, they could become a terrifying weapon …
However, he would rather not have these things!
In a few breaths of time, Luo Zheng killed seven or eight demon warriors.
As for the other demon warriors outside the city, most of them had already fled back to the main city. Some demon warriors fled towards the wilderness, but they didn't dare to return to the city.
Luo Zheng was too lazy to chase after them. He circled around the main city, tightly clutching a small skeleton in his hand.
Whenever he encountered a demon warrior outside the city, he would kill them all, smashing them with this small skeleton, killing them one by one …
The stronger demon warriors might have to spend a bit more effort, but the weaker ones would be killed in one move by the small skeleton.
The Demon Race cultivators in the main city began to feel despair …
None of them had expected that such a fiend would appear out of nowhere outside the main city. And it was a human brat!
When Luo Zheng circled the main city for the second time, there was no one outside the main city.
The demon warriors who were still fighting each other had either been killed by Luo Zheng and returned to the main city to be resurrected, or they didn't dare to leave the main city, or they fled into the wilderness.
"It's almost cleaned up. It's time to change the main city," Luo Zheng shook his head.
Half an hour after Luo Zheng left the main city, some demon warriors cautiously walked out of the city wall, looking in the direction Luo Zheng had left with some fear on their faces …
In the wilderness, in a deserted corner, the Teleportation Token once again appeared in Luo Zheng's hand!
After activating it, accompanied by a fierce white light, Luo Zheng's figure disappeared.
In an instant, Luo Zheng appeared in that strange space. He glanced around and raised his eyebrows slightly, "Those skulls didn't follow me?"
The Teleportation Token could allow Luo Zheng to appear in the center of a main city in an instant. If he brought these skulls into the center of the city, who knew what would happen. However … these skulls didn't follow Luo Zheng into this space.
After choosing a main city, Luo Zheng once again descended into it …
The main city that appeared in front of Luo Zheng was very unfamiliar. The houses in the main city were very exquisite. Those buildings had pointed roofs and round roofs, but it was a style that Luo Zheng had never seen before.
"The Night Clan in the Upper Realm seems to be a small clan with no name, but the buildings in the clan are exquisite and meticulous." Luo Zheng walked out of the main city while looking around.
The Night Clan was a clan that produced a lot of assassins. On the Sea God Continent, Luo Zheng had fought with this clan before.
This clan was good at assassination, but concealment and assassination weren't a path to success in the world of warriors. They couldn't occupy the mainstream.
When Luo Zheng arrived at the city wall from the center of the main city, his eyebrows twitched. Those hundreds of small skulls were already waiting for him outside the city …
He had used the Teleportation Token this time, teleporting at least tens of millions of miles!
But these small skulls could directly cut through the boundary of space and arrive here almost at the same time as him? Wasn't the witchcraft of the Ancient Sorcerer Clan too heaven-defying?
"Sigh …"
Luo Zheng could only bring these small skulls again and continue to "clean up" the city of the Night Clan.
Even though the Night Clan in the Upper Realm still specialized in assassination, under Luo Zheng's Clear Eyes Spirit Eyes, those concealment and assassination techniques seemed very powerless.
This cleaning up was easier than cleaning up the cities of the Demon Clan previously …
After a few rounds, no one dared to leave the city.
"Let's change places." Luo Zheng's gaze was indifferent, and he quietly left the main city.
But this time, he didn't take out the Teleportation Token. Instead, he took out the iron voucher.
Just now, Luo Zheng had cleaned up the "fat sheep" of two cities and accumulated a lot of fantasy points, but his ranking was still dropping …
The Illusory Battlefield's overall ranking had already fallen to 17,000, and this great world's ranking had also fallen below 300!

Chapter 1240
Luo Zheng's efficiency in obtaining Fantasy Points wasn't slow.
He didn't need to travel long distances between the main cities. He could use the Teleportation Token to easily change his location.
However, the amount of Fantasy Points he obtained couldn't be compared to some martial artists.
It seemed that he had missed something.
Or did this group of people have a secret that he didn't know?
When Luo Zheng took out the metal voucher, another thought came from it.
"Pay five million Fantasy Points and you can enter the Karma Hall. The Karma Hall needs ten people to enter at the same time. There are six people missing before the Karma Hall opens. Do you want to pay the Fantasy Points?"
Six people missing?
When Luo Zheng obtained the metal voucher, there were still three people missing!
Now, there were more and more people missing?
