"No … there should be other martial artists who have already opened the Karma Hall and gathered ten people. The four people waiting inside should be from another group."
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. His guess shouldn't be wrong.
It seemed that many martial artists had already entered the Karma Hall.
Five million Fantasy Points …
Even for Luo Zheng, this was a large number. When he plundered a main city, those martial artists wouldn't obediently line up outside the city to let him kill them. Usually, he could only kill a few dozen martial artists and accumulate less than one million Fantasy Points.
After all, there were many main cities in this large world. Not every main city had someone like Ji Luoxue.
It seemed that he should enter and take a gamble.
After making this decision, Luo Zheng sent a thought into the metal voucher.
"Pay the Fantasy Points!"
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
At this moment, countless Fantasy Points of Light flew out from Luo Zheng's body!
Five million Fantasy Points of Light were extracted from Luo Zheng's body and flew into the metal voucher. Due to the large number of Fantasy Points of Light, this process lasted for a while.
Looking at the yellow Fantasy Points of Light, Luo Zheng was in deep thought.
The Fantasy Points of Light were only used to calculate the ranking …
It shouldn't be that simple. Otherwise, for some of the weaker warriors of the Spirit Sea Realm, participating in the Dreamy Battlefield could only be a group of lambs waiting to be slaughtered. The only purpose of participating in the Dreamy Battlefield was to experience life and death situations and hone one's mind.
As for the Heavenly Dao's arrangements and the other uses of these Fantasy Points, he could only wait until the Illusory Battlefield closed.
After the 5 million fantasy points were absorbed by the metal voucher, the entire metal voucher blossomed with intense yellow light!
"Pa!"
With a crisp sound, the yellow light began to expand and turned into a door of light!
Looking at the light in front of him, Luo Zheng took a step forward and entered it!
Inside the door …
It was a squarish little house.
When Luo Zheng entered, he glanced around and found four people sitting in the corner of the room.
One Demon Clan cultivator and three Human Clan cultivators. Among the three Human Clan cultivators, there were two males and one female …
The aura emitted from these people wasn't weak. Their strength definitely couldn't be underestimated. At the very least, they had the strength to fight against Luo Zheng!
After all, the martial artists who could activate the iron certificate must have passed through some ancient ruins or secret places to obtain this iron certificate.
As for activating the metal certificate, it required five million fantasy points.
For a martial artist who could accumulate more than five million fantasy points, luck was very important, but their strength was definitely not weak!
"Another one came in. There are still five people left." One of them glanced at Luo Zheng indifferently and immediately closed his eyes.
On the other hand, the Demon Race martial artist's eyes circled around Luo Zheng, sizing him up. There was a hint of provocation in his eyes. In fact, this Demon Race martial artist was the first to enter. He had provoked the people in front of him, but they all ignored him …
"This is the Karma Hall? It doesn't seem to have an exit? " Luo Zheng looked around and reached out to touch his shoulder. Those skulls had been biting his back. After he entered the door, the skulls didn't follow him in. He was relaxed but a little unaccustomed!
"This is just a waiting room. Once there are ten people, the Karma Hall can be opened. You just have to wait here." The human woman in white said expressionlessly.
It was obviously not the first time this human woman entered the Karma Hall. She was very familiar with the entire process.
In fact, besides Luo Zheng and the Demon Clan cultivator, this was not the first time the other three human martial artists had entered the Karma Hall.
In their opinion, Luo Zheng and the Demon Clan cultivator entering for the first time would definitely drag them down and become a burden. They were slightly disappointed.
But the Karma Hall required ten people to open. After opening, they would need to cooperate with each other to go further in the Karma Hall. Not to mention, fighting was prohibited in this room. They couldn't drive out Luo Zheng and the Demon Clan cultivator. It was also because of this that the small skulls biting Luo Zheng couldn't enter this room …
Luo Zheng nodded slightly. He didn't mind this woman's attitude.
But waiting here made Luo Zheng a little depressed. He didn't want to waste time. If he had known earlier, he would have investigated the metal certificate. It would have been better if he had waited until the nine people entered.
Since he had entered, Luo Zheng walked to a corner of the room.
After Luo Zheng sat down, the atmosphere in the room became oppressive. The woman in white sat in a corner with her legs crossed, seemingly resting with her eyes closed.
The two human men sat cross-legged, seemingly adjusting the True Qi in their bodies. Only the Demon Clan cultivator's bell-sized eyes were staring around, as if he wanted to fight someone here.
After Luo Zheng sat down, he gently patted the table. After a light "pa" sound, a miniature mountain began to spin out.
It was the Divine Essencefused Mountain that Luo Zheng had refined!
After defeating Ji Luoxue, Luo Zheng used the Blood Refining Method to exchange a jade slip with Ji Luoxue.
This jade slip recorded her Ji Family's secret method, which could be used to refine the Divine Essencefused Force.
Now that he had to wait for the other cultivators to enter this room, Luo Zheng used this time to try to refine the Divine Essencefused Force.
When Luo Zheng took out the miniature Divine Essencefused Mountain, the Demon Clan cultivator glanced at it slightly and showed a disdainful look on his face. Refining a mountain into a magic weapon was a very stupid act, at least in the eyes of this Demon Clan cultivator.
As for the other two human cultivators, when they saw the miniature mountain in Luo Zheng's hand, their eyes flashed slightly. After looking at Luo Zheng for a while, they closed their eyes again.
The two human cultivators felt the Divine Essencefused Mountain's Five Elements Force and also saw that it was extraordinary, but they didn't put it in their eyes.
The remaining woman in white glanced over indifferently. She originally wanted to look away, but suddenly, she seemed to think of something. Her delicate eyes abruptly widened as she stared blankly at the Divine Essencefused Mountain in Luo Zheng's hand.
"Is … is the miniature mountain in your hand a refined Divine Essencefused Mountain?" The originally cold voice became somewhat restrained at this moment.
Obviously, this woman knew very well how difficult it was to refine the Divine Essencefused Mountain, or in other words, it was impossible to refine! But the miniature mountain in front of her was clearly a complete Divine Essencefused Mountain, and it had been refined by this person! This was what she found inconceivable.
Luo Zheng nodded slightly and said to the woman in white, "Yes."
After softly saying this, Luo Zheng undid the seal Ji Luoxue left on the jade slip and began to absorb the secret method on the jade slip.
The woman in white kept looking at Luo Zheng and the Divine Essencefused Mountain in his hand, and she began to guess the origins of this young man. Although she was curious about how Luo Zheng had refined the Divine Essencefused Mountain, she knew that it wasn't appropriate to ask.
This Divine Essencefused Force …
It was a force formed naturally after the five elements were mixed.
One had to circulate the power of five Laws at the same time, and these five Laws had to be in a state of balance.
The more balanced they were, the easier it was to extract the Divine Essencefused Mountain, but it wasn't easy to control the power of five Laws in a state of perfect balance.
After Luo Zheng absorbed the contents of the jade slip, he obtained this information …
Circulate the power of five Origin Laws?
Maintain a state of perfect balance?
When he thought of this, Luo Zheng drew out five strands of Chaotic Qi from his dantian …
The strands of Chaotic Qi flowed into his hand through his meridians, but each strand of Chaotic Qi flowed into a different finger through his meridians.
Not long after, his five fingers flickered with light, and five different powers of laws appeared. However, the powers of laws were not balanced.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1241
The power of the five elemental laws was independent from the other origin laws. It was the most origin law power in this world.
Most of the matter in this world was made up of these five types of laws.
It was extremely simple for any martial artist to manifest the power of a single Law!
After all, many cultivation techniques were driven by the power of laws. In other words, they used the power of laws to deduce a special kind of law profundity.
Some Martial Skills even required the fusion of two or three types of Law Energies or the use of them alternately to achieve the desired effect. This required a large amount of practice and comprehension.
It was already quite amazing for an ordinary martial artist to comprehend five different types of laws. This was not a threshold for Luo Zheng.
However, the Ji Family's secret technique was to achieve an absolute balance between the five elemental laws of metal, wood, water, fire, and earth. This was quite a big test for Luo Zheng.
Actually, achieving an absolute balance between the five elemental laws was an extremely powerful martial technique.
Ji Luoxue's Five Elemental Palm that day had fused this secret technique into a palm martial technique. That Five Elemental Palm was also an extremely powerful killing move. However, Ji Luoxue's One Thought and Two Bodies had not merged, so the power of the five elements was not complete.
At this moment, Luo Zheng's thumb was dyed with a layer of pale gold. The golden light was very dazzling. If one looked directly at this color, it would give a glaring feeling. This was the power of the metal elemental laws.
A warm flame rose on his index finger. This was the power of the fire elemental laws.
His middle finger was suffused with a heavy aura. This was the power of the earth elemental laws.
Waves of water rippled on his ring finger. This was the power of the water elemental laws.
His little finger was overflowing with a pale green aura of life. This was the power of the wood elemental laws.
Although Luo Zheng could not completely balance these five types of laws, he still suppressed the five types of laws with the advantage of the Chaotic Qi!
The white-clothed woman in the room looked at Luo Zheng's hand with an even more surprised expression.
This woman was the same as Ji Luoxue. She came from Gods No Thought. She belonged to a colossus similar to the Ji Family. Although her status and talent were not as good as Ji Luoxue's, she knew that the fusion of the five elements was a secret technique unique to the Ji Family. It was also the most famous cultivation technique of the Ji Family's ancestor.
This secret technique is not to be divulged to outsiders …
Not only did the young man refine the Divine Essencefused Mountain, but he also seemed to be cultivating the Ji Clan's secret art?
She was even more curious about Luo Zheng's background.
Luo Zheng concentrated on the Power of the Five Elements in his palm. The Power of the Five Elements reinforced and restrained each other. It was extremely difficult to completely bind the Power of the Five Elements in his hand.
At this moment, he naturally wouldn't pay attention to anyone else. He also didn't know that the white-clothed woman was staring at him …
"Gathering these five types of Laws in one hand actually produced such a strong repulsive force!"
The five elements counter one another. Metal counters wood, wood counters earth, earth counters water, water counters fire, fire counters metal …
Metal begets water, water begets wood, wood begets fire, fire begets earth, earth begets metal …
"No wonder the first key point of this secret art is to completely balance the Power of the Five Elements. As long as one type of Law is slightly more than the other, it will affect each other, and then it will affect the other types of Law. After that, they will begin to repel each other … Only when they are completely balanced can they reinforce and restrain each other and coexist on the same palm!"
As Luo Zheng thought about this, he pushed his palm toward the mini mountain in front of him. This palm directly imprinted itself on the Divine Essencefused Mountain!
With this light pat, Luo Zheng wanted to use the Ji Clan's secret art to draw out the Power of Magnetism from the Divine Essencefused Mountain!
But at this moment, the water on his ring finger suddenly flashed and rushed toward the flame on his index finger. However, that wisp of flame was very afraid and desperately rushed toward his little finger …
"Pu …"
In the chaos, the Power of the Five Elements on Luo Zheng's five fingers finally collapsed.
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng smiled bitterly and then shook his head. As expected, it still didn't work.
The moment he withdrew his palm, the white-clothed woman took the initiative to speak. "This Ji Clan's Five Elements Fusion Technique is no small matter. You seem … to have just started cultivating it?"
Luo Zheng looked up at the white-clothed woman and nodded slightly.
If this was the outside world, Luo Zheng would have to avoid it. However, in this Illusory Battlefield, he was facing all the warriors of the Divine Sea Realm, so he couldn't be bothered to deliberately hide it.
"I remember that the prodigy of the Ji Clan spent a full six years to cultivate this technique before he reached Initial Attainment … I'm afraid it's impossible to cultivate it successfully in a short time," the white-clothed woman continued. In her opinion, the prodigy of the Ji Clan, Ji Luoxue, was already a top existence among the younger generation. How many people in the entire world were more talented than her?
When the white-clothed woman saw that Luo Zheng was grasping this little bit of time to cultivate, she was afraid that he was in a hurry to succeed. The most taboo thing for warriors to cultivate was to be in a hurry to succeed. She couldn't help but speak up to teach Luo Zheng.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng only smiled slightly and didn't speak anymore. With a thought, he extracted another five strands of Chaotic Qi from the Chaotic Sea and poured it into his hand.
Soon, five different rays of light appeared in Luo Zheng's hand and intertwined in the room.
"Pu …"
This time, Luo Zheng didn't forcefully suppress the power. The result was as expected. It collapsed even faster than the last time!
In the blink of an eye, the five types of Power of Law interacted with each other in Luo Zheng's hand and collapsed one after another.
When the white-clothed woman saw this scene, her eyes revealed an expression that said she had expected this. There was even a faint smile. Even Ji Luoxue needed six years. If you want to master this secret technique, it'll be enough for you to be proud if you spend ten years! If you want to master it in such a short time, it's undoubtedly wishful thinking!
Unfortunately, the white-clothed woman didn't understand Luo Zheng's character. The more challenging something was, the more he refused to believe it and would grit his teeth to overcome it.
After the second circulation of the Power of the Five Elements collapsed, Luo Zheng extracted another five strands of Chaotic Qi in the blink of an eye!
But this time, Luo Zheng adjusted the positions of the Power of the Five Elements!
The Power of Law of Water condensed on his thumb, the Power of Law of Fire gathered on his little finger, and the Power of Law of Metal was moved to his middle finger by Luo Zheng …
In this way, the powers that restrained each other were separated from each other!
Although Luo Zheng still couldn't achieve complete balance, the situation of the five types of Power of Law repelling each other was indeed reduced by a lot.
This time, Luo Zheng once again slapped the miniature hill in front of him!
As Luo Zheng gently imprinted his palm on the miniature hill, his mind began to activate the Power of Origin Magnetism in accordance with the secret technique.
"Buzz buzz buzz …"
On the other side of the Divine Origin Magnetism Mountain, a palm imprint also appeared. The shape and size of this palm imprint were exactly the same as the one left by Luo Zheng's palm on the front.
"Pu …"
When Luo Zheng persisted to this step, the Power of the Five Elements in his hand collapsed again!
The moment it collapsed, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. His hands quickly circled around the Divine Origin Magnetism Mountain, and the Divine Origin Magnetism Mountain continuously spun around.
As Luo Zheng's hands circled around the mountain, a green-gray light suddenly appeared in the center of Luo Zheng's palm!
This light was like a mark that lingered in the center of Luo Zheng's palm … but the light only lingered for a moment before it began to quickly dissipate. After a breath, the light had completely disappeared.
This was the Power of Origin Magnetism?
It was a pity that fighting was prohibited in this room. Otherwise, he would have been able to test the might of the Power of Origin Magnetism. Hearing Ji Luoxue's marvelous words, Luo Zheng himself didn't pay too much attention to it.
After all, Luo Zheng was guarding an Immortal Mansion, which was tantamount to a mountain of treasures. He didn't care about the other techniques in this universe.
However, Luo Zheng didn't know that he had underestimated the Power of Origin Magnetism …
In the end, the Power of Origin Magnetism was an important part of cultivating the Divine Dao of the Five Elements. To some extent, there might be a gap between it and Luo Zheng's Emotion Severing Divine Dao, but in the end, it was still a Divine Dao!
As for the white-clothed woman who had been secretly observing Luo Zheng, when she saw Luo Zheng really extract the Power of Origin Magnetism, her pale pink little mouth unconsciously opened slightly. She stared blankly at Luo Zheng, her face full of disbelief.

Chapter 1242
Strictly speaking, Luo Zheng hadn't completely succeeded.
Ji Luoxue had his back against the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain now, and when he struck out with his palm, he drew out a thousand times more magnetic force than Luo Zheng!
Luo Zheng had only suppressed the Five Elements Laws earlier and forcefully extracted the magnetic force …
But he was able to extract a trace of magnetic force after merely three attempts. Even if Ji Luoxue was here, he would probably be stunned when he saw this scene.
This was a little too fast …
When she was sent to the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain by the Ji Family's ancestor, she had stayed there for three years!
She had only extracted a trace of magnetic force at the end of the second year, and Luo Zheng had merely spent an hour in the entire process.
This white-clothed woman was a core disciple of another large family in Gods No Thoughts, and she was an existence second only to the prodigies. She had been influenced since she was young and knew how terrifying those prodigies were!
Among her peers, Ji Luoxue was like a proud phoenix that was always high and mighty and received the protection of her elders. The strength that Ji Luoxue displayed also far surpassed her peers.
The white-clothed woman was once unconvinced, and she secretly worked hard to cultivate in an attempt to close the gap between her and Ji Luoxue, and even wanted to surpass Ji Luoxue! But in the end, she saw the reality. Some people in this world were born different, and as she worked hard, the gap between her and Ji Luoxue grew larger and larger …
This was the strength of a prodigy!
If Ji Luoxue, as a prodigy, had spent six years to completely master this secret technique, then what was this young man before her …? Was he a monster?
She originally wanted to continue watching and see to what extent Luo Zheng's mastery of the Ji Family's mystic technique would reach in such a short period of time, but in the end, Luo Zheng put away the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain …
It wasn't that Luo Zheng wasn't willing to continue cultivating, but fighting was prohibited in this room, and even if he extracted a trace of magnetic force, it would only be a waste.
After putting away the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain, Luo Zheng didn't stop cultivating, and he just continuously used the chaotic energy to transform the five types of Laws into ceaselessly coiling around his palm, seeking that feeling of balance.
Only after the five types of Law Forces were completely balanced could the Prime Magnetic Force be at his fingertips.
At this moment, a thin line suddenly appeared on a wall somewhere in the room. The thin line extended from the corner of the wall all the way up. After about seven feet, it turned 90 degrees and finally formed the outline of a door.
"Buzz buzz …"
The entire door emitted a luster, and not long after, the door of light was pushed open by someone!
Then, someone pushed open the door and entered!
"The sixth person …"
Everyone's eyes flashed slightly, and their gazes focused on that person.
This person was also a Human Clan warrior.
The Human Race warrior wore a round hat on his head. His face was gloomy and arrogant. After meeting everyone's gaze, he coldly snorted, "What's there to see? Is there a flower growing on my face?"
Warriors who could collect the iron certificate and pour in five million fantasy points were not to be trifled with. They were either the inheritors of a Realm Lord or the Holy Son of a Holy Land. Warriors like this could be found in the hundreds of thousands in the entire universe.
The white-clothed woman and the other two Human Clan warriors didn't change their expressions when they heard the round hat warrior's words. The three of them just sneered in their hearts.
Luo Zheng smiled slightly, and then focused on controlling the balance of the five elements in his hand. He naturally wouldn't argue with this person.
However, the Demon Clan warrior couldn't get used to it. It was fine that the Demon Clan warrior didn't find trouble with the newcomer, but this guy wearing a round hat actually dared to speak rudely.
"Hehe, can't I look?" The Demon Clan warrior asked.
"Why don't you look again?" The round hat warrior stared at the Demon Clan warrior and said.
"I'll look again!" The Demon Clan warrior said with a straight face.
"If you look again, I'll …"
Hearing the meaningless conversation between the round hat warrior and the Demon Clan warrior, Luo Zheng, the white-clothed woman, and the other two Human Clan warriors couldn't help but roll their eyes.
They clearly knew that there were rules and restrictions in this room, and they couldn't fight. In any case, the two of them were geniuses in the Divine Sea Realm, but they were here talking nonsense. How bored were they?
Especially the Demon Clan warrior. His voice was loud and clear, and he was as vigorous as an ox. When he quarreled with the round hat warrior, his voice echoed in the room. This room wasn't big, so it was very noisy to the ears!
Luo Zheng didn't care. His mind was calm, and he focused on the five elements in his hand, as if he didn't hear anything.
However, the white-clothed woman frowned more and more. The faces of the other two Human Clan warriors also became gloomy!
"If you quarrel again, I will kill the two of you after leaving this room!" The short Human Clan warrior couldn't bear it anymore and finally shouted!
After saying that, the short warrior stretched out his hand, and a short sword floated in his hand.
The short sword's appearance was ordinary, but there was a unique engraving on the blade. There was a faint golden light on the blade of the short sword, and there were some incomplete Sanskrit characters in the golden light.
The round hat warrior and the Demon Clan warrior, who were quarreling, saw the short sword. Their eyes narrowed slightly, and a trace of surprise appeared on their faces.
"The Supreme Artifact, Buddha's Blade?" The round hat warrior stared at the short sword and asked the short warrior, "You are a cultivator from the Ten Thousand Buddha Sacred Realm!"
There were only more than 3,000 Supreme Artifacts in the whole world. Many warriors had already memorized them. Although they hadn't seen it with their own eyes, the round hat warrior had already guessed the origin of this short sword based on the momentum and appearance of this short sword.
Buddha's Blade …
Buddha's Golden Mouth, Sentence Death!
This Buddha's Sun Blade contained an extremely powerful sure-kill power!
This was the most famous weapon in the Ten Thousand Buddha Sacred Realm, the most precious treasure of the Black Face Buddha Hall. The short warrior must have an extraordinary origin to be able to bring him into the Illusory Battlefield.
Most of the warriors in the Ten Thousand Buddha Sacred Realm were monks. Naturally, they wore cassocks and had shiny heads.
But not everyone was like this. Although the Ten Thousand Buddha Sacred Realm was a Buddhist holy land, some cultivators who entered the world did not need to abide by the rules and precepts of the major temples.
The woman in white glanced at the short sword in the short warrior's hand indifferently, but her face remained calm. Although a Supreme Artifact was rare, it was not enough to make her afraid …
Buddha's Blade?
After hearing the Supreme Artifact, Luo Zheng couldn't help but take a few more glances, but all his attention was still focused on the Power of Five Elements in his hand.
After the short warrior drew his sword, it was quite effective.
The round hat warrior who came in later realized that these guys in front of him were not to be trifled with, so he covered his hat and pushed it to a corner of the room.
The demon warrior's eyes were still filled with provocation, but he didn't make a ruckus …
After the professional round hat warrior entered, another thin line appeared on the wall, and another door opened.
The seventh warrior had entered.
This seventh person was a woman from the Demon Night Clan. She didn't cause any trouble, but after glancing at everyone indifferently, she leaned against the wall and maintained a straight posture. Then, she took out a long arrow from the quiver on her back and stroked the shiny arrowhead with her fingers.
In this great world, there were actually many people who had obtained the iron voucher, and they were also paying attention to the number of people in the iron voucher.
Most people would hesitate before using the iron voucher. After all, if they wanted to enter the Karma Hall, they would have to deduct five million Fantasy points. Anyone would feel the pinch.
But as the number of people gradually increased, some warriors who were originally hesitant made up their minds.
As a result, the number of people in the room increased rapidly.
After the woman from the Demon Night Clan entered the room, in less than half an hour, another door opened!
The eighth person …
A demon warrior.
At the same time, another door opened.
The ninth person …
The tenth person!
Luo Zheng waited for more than half a day before the ten people in the Karma Hall were finally gathered.
"Finally gathered." The woman in white smiled slightly. After getting up, she took the initiative to say to Luo Zheng, "Okay, let's go."
The woman in white didn't have any hostility. Instead, she was very polite to Luo Zheng. Luo Zheng naturally smiled back. "But this room doesn't have a door. Where should we go?"
"No need, just stand here," the woman in white said.

Chapter 1243
After the four walls collapsed, they immediately dissipated, and the ceiling above their heads also disappeared in an instant.
A majestic mountain appeared in front of everyone!
This huge mountain range was submerged in thick white clouds, like a fairyland.
Ten people, including Luo Zheng, stood on a platform at the foot of the mountain.
"Isn't this iron voucher known as the Karma Hall? Why is there such a big mountain? "Luo Zheng asked curiously.
When the others heard Luo Zheng's words, they turned a deaf ear. After all, it was the first time most of them had entered, and they were unable to answer Luo Zheng's question.
"This is the Karma Hall. We'll understand once we go up the mountain," the white-clothed woman replied with a smile. "After leaving this platform, all the restrictions will be removed. However, I don't wish for everyone to fight against each other," the white-clothed woman said to the others.
"Why should we?" A sinister voice floated over.
Some warriors already had the intention to fight …
Since they could afford to spend five million fantasy points to enter the Karma Hall, they must have a lot of fantasy points!
The white-clothed woman said with a smile, "The fantasy points obtained in the Karma Hall are calculated in the tens of millions. If your luck is good, you can even obtain some Supreme Artifacts that have yet to appear. If you are willing to fight with each other here, there is no problem …"
Hearing the white-clothed woman's words, everyone's expression changed.
These warriors were able to enter the iron voucher, so they had a lot of fantasy points to spare. Even if they paid five million fantasy points, they would still have a few million fantasy points left.
However, these Fantasy Points were accumulated bit by bit after experiencing countless battles!
Could they obtain tens of millions of fantasy points in the Karma Hall?
Could they even obtain a Supreme Artifact?
This kind of information had no choice but to make those martial artists who harbored murderous intent reconsider.
After the white-clothed woman finished speaking, she didn't have too many misgivings. She nodded slightly at Luo Zheng and said, "Let's go!"
After speaking, she was the first to step out of the platform and onto the steps of the mountain.
Luo Zheng's eyes flickered slightly. From the white-clothed woman's words, it seemed that he could obtain a large number of fantasy points here.
In the tens of millions?
No wonder his ranking had not risen during this period of time and had instead fallen!
Presumably, most of the martial artists in front of him had already entered Karma Hall!
Compared to the Karma Hall, the speed at which he was plundering Fantasy Points could not keep up.
He didn't think too much about it and followed the white-clothed woman out, taking a step up the stairs …
However …
The moment Luo Zheng stepped on the stairs, he suddenly had a bad premonition. When he raised his head, he heard a burst of "Cough, cough, cough".
That group of damned skeletons actually appeared again!
After Luo Zheng entered the room, it was temporarily quiet for a long time. He didn't expect that just as he left the hall, the group of skeletons would actually follow him.
Previously, when Luo Zheng used the Teleportation Token to travel from one big city to another, these skulls had caught up with him at an incredible speed.
Now, Luo Zheng directly used the iron voucher to enter the Karma Hall. He didn't know how far away he was from his previous location, but these skulls still followed him …
Could it be that after he left the Illusory Battlefield, they could still follow him into the immortal mansion?
It didn't matter if they were in the Illusory Battlefield. After all, the Illusory Battlefield was both real and illusory. However, if they could follow him into the immortal mansion, that would be really bad.
As for the other nine martial artists, they were shocked when they saw this scene!
Even the white-clothed woman leading the way was somewhat dazed when she saw the surging skulls. "What, what are these things?"
Luo Zheng's expression was calm. He was more or less used to it. "I provoked them in a mystic realm …"
"Is that so? These small skulls can't be dispelled? "
After saying that, the white-clothed woman walked to Luo Zheng's side and plucked a small skull from his back. After carefully observing it, a thin bolt of lightning suddenly blossomed from her slender fingers!
"Crack!"
After that bolt of lightning suddenly shot out, it completely enveloped the skull!
Although this bolt of lightning was very small, the power contained within it made even Luo Zheng secretly apprehensive.
The power of the lightning was indeed astonishing, and the light it released was extremely dazzling. However … it was useless.
After the lightning dissipated, the skulls were still intact.
Luo Zheng wasn't too surprised by this result. These small skulls were indestructible. The Karma Law contained within them was probably as tyrannical as the Heavenly Dao. If they could be so easily destroyed, then there wouldn't be so many small skulls hanging behind the Empyrean that besieged him that day. If even an Empyrean couldn't do anything about it, how could a Divine Sea Realm martial artist dispel it?
"Hehe, let me try …"
The one who spoke was the demon martial artist who had been constantly provoking others. In that room, this guy seemed to want to fight everyone, but now he was very well-behaved …
The Demon Race warrior also plucked off a skull.
"Let me try too!"
"Hey, these skulls are divine tools. I wonder if I can destroy them!"
"Give me one …"
"There are so many behind that guy. You go and pluck them yourself …"
"…"
Luo Zheng, who was standing on the spot, was in a complete mess.
Didn't everyone come to the Karma Hall? How come all of their attention was on the skulls behind him?
Seeing Luo Zheng's expression, the white-clothed woman covered her mouth and smiled. Releasing her lily-white hand, the skull automatically flew behind Luo Zheng and bit into Luo Zheng's back. She then said, "If I'm not wrong, these skulls should come from a curse!"
"You guessed right." Luo Zheng shrugged his shoulders.
"Legend has it that the witchcraft of the ancient clans coincided with the Heavenly Dao Law. They used the method of cursing to steal the Will of Heaven. Therefore, their witchcraft is extremely powerful. That curse is almost impossible to break …" The white-clothed woman said.
"Steal the Will of Heaven?"
Hearing this, Luo Zheng was slightly taken aback.
The white-clothed woman faintly smiled. "Actually, I don't really understand … but there are only 128 Wills of Heaven. If you use unorthodox methods to obtain the Will of Heaven, I'm afraid you will be punished by the Heavenly Dao!"
The Will of Heaven was the supreme law of karma in the universe.
There were 128 Wills of Heaven in the universe, not one more, not one less!
When a Heavenly Venerable fell, the Will of Heaven dissipated, and then a new crown of Will of Heaven was born.
However, legend has it that the ancient clans used some unknown method to use witchcraft to execute the supreme law of karma. This method had already exceeded the limits of the Heavenly Dao, so it was not tolerated by the Heavenly Dao.
Today's ancient martial art practitioners were guessing that this was the reason why the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe had completely disappeared overnight!
However, more than a billion years had passed since the disappearance of the ancient clans. It was a matter of the past that had been covered in dust by time. It was inevitable that there would be some deviations in everyone's guesses.
"Let's go. I forgot to mention earlier that flying is prohibited in the Karma Hall. So, we can only climb up these stairs one step at a time." After the white-clothed woman finished speaking, she didn't stay any longer and directly climbed up the winding stairs.
Luo Zheng also followed behind the white-clothed woman.
At the same time, the eight warriors behind Luo Zheng were each using their own methods to break the small skull.
"Ah ah ah … I can't break it! It even broke my Dark Demon saber! "
The Demon Race warrior held the pitch-black saber in his hand, his face full of heartache. His saber was also a first-grade divine artifact, but when he slashed down, the small skull remained intact while a semi-circular gap appeared on the saber!
Fortunately, a weapon that was damaged in the Illusory Battlefield was still intact in the outside world. Otherwise, this fellow would definitely go crazy!
But now that his saber was damaged, he would have to face many battles. Without this first-grade divine artifact, it would be somewhat inconvenient.
Hearing the words of this Demon Race warrior, the others looked at him as if he was an idiot …
They had only taken the small skull from Luo Zheng's back out of curiosity. Was there a need to risk it like this?
Naturally, no one was able to break the small skull, so they all gave up. The small skull returned to its original owner and stuck to Luo Zheng's back, grinding its white teeth. The other eight warriors followed behind Luo Zheng and climbed up the stairs.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1244
This white jade staircase was about twenty feet wide. From the bottom of this majestic mountain, there were hundreds of thousands of stairs.
Although flying was prohibited, the speed of this group of ten was not considered slow.
With the white-clothed woman's warning, the vigilance between the ten people was temporarily lowered.
After all, the ten people present could be ranked in the top one thousand of this great world. But in the entire Illusory Battlefield, they could be ranked in the top one hundred thousand.
None of them were confident that they could defeat the others in one bite. Moreover, the white-clothed woman also said that the Karma Hall would reward them with tens of millions.
Along the way, the white-clothed woman walked at the front. Luo Zheng followed behind her. The other cultivators maintained a certain distance and climbed at a uniform speed. The short cultivator from the Human Race brought up the rear.
After climbing like this for the time it takes to burn an incense stick, everyone stopped in front of a hall. The staircase extended here and connected to the hall. If they wanted to continue climbing, they had to pass through this hall.
This hall was entirely white and had a magnificent appearance. It was unknown what material it was made of. There were no signs on the outer wall.
Among the ten people, only the white-clothed woman was entering the Karma Hall for the second time. Everyone's gaze rested on the white-clothed woman.
The white-clothed woman smiled faintly and said, "This is the first Karma Hall. The difficulty is not high. Everyone can enter."
Luo Zheng nodded. Since he had gritted his teeth and spent five million fantasy points, there was no need for him to hesitate.
Thus, everyone followed behind Luo Zheng and stepped into the hall.
In the empty hall, there was nothing.
The cultivators present had explored many secret realms in the Illusory Battlefield.
Facing this situation, a trace of vigilance rose in everyone's heart. Of course, this included Luo Zheng.

But even someone as strong as Luo Zheng attracted a pile of small skulls behind him.

..... Even Luo Zheng's eyes flashed.

. Why was a supreme divine artifact? The reason...

The test of the first Karma Hall was indeed not difficult.

But these light rays still gave everyone a kind of hidden threat.
It was like a person walking on the same spot as if he were walking on flat ground. If he were placed a hundred meters in the air, although the path in front of him was wide, he would still have some misgivings and slow down.
Many martial artists were currently in such a state of mind …
Every ten feet in the hall, they would encounter a white light ray.
And as everyone continued to move forward, the speed of the white light rays became faster and faster.
This speed was relative!
In the eyes of Luo Zheng and the others, the speed of the white light rays was not much different from that of a snail.
After all, with their speed, it would take less than a breath's time to cross this Karma Hall at full speed …
It was just that everyone was more or less afraid of these white lights, so they didn't dare to be rash. They just followed the rhythm of the light rays and waited until the light rays passed by before taking a step forward.
The speed and frequency of each light ray was not fixed.
The deeper they went into the hall, the faster the light rays penetrating through the walls.
Sometimes, the light rays would suddenly pass through the wall, stopping in the center of the hall for a moment, and then accelerate and disappear into the wall on the other side.
Sometimes, the light rays would directly enter from one side of the wall to the other at a speed that was difficult for everyone's eyes to follow. After waiting for a few breaths of time, it would repeat again …
There was a pattern to these light rays passing through. To many martial artists, it was just a little trick. The more familiar everyone was, the more relaxed they became, and the faster they passed through.

Chapter 1247
If it were an ordinary boulder, the ten people present wouldn't even bat an eyelid!

The square-shaped boulder was thirty feet in length, width, and height. Every time it rolled, it would cover a distance of thirty feet. Its rolling speed was also extremely fast, and the distance in the hall was limited. In just two breaths of time, it had rushed to the front of the human cultivator.
The human martial artist remained calm. It seemed that there was a limit to the direction in which the boulder rolled.
The floor of the Karma Hall was also divided into squares that were thirty feet long and wide. When the huge rock rolled, it strictly followed the square on the floor. Moreover, it could only roll forward, left, and right. If it wanted to move diagonally, it would need to roll to the left and then to the front. Only by doing so could it move diagonally.
With a slight sway of his body, the Human Martial Artist moved diagonally to the top of the square boulder. The boulder could not reach its original position in one step, so it could only roll to the side and then press down on the Human Martial Artist again.

"Rumble, rumble, rumble …"
As the ground continued to tremble, the human martial artist moved left and right, maintaining a diagonal line with the boulder. At the same time, he said, "Everyone, follow me and maintain a diagonal line with the boulder. Passing through the second Karma Hall won't be difficult!"
Hearing this, everyone understood tacitly.
It seemed that the Karma Hall was like this. Even the slightest mistake could lead to immediate death. For example, if one was accidentally crushed by the boulder, even the Demon Race cultivators with powerful physical bodies, or even Luo Zheng, would not be able to withstand it. However, as long as one grasped the rules, passing through the Karma Hall would not be difficult.
"Since that's the case, there's no need to waste time in this hall. Let's quickly pass through," a cultivator said and rushed into the depths of the hall.

From the beginning, she had told everyone that they needed to work together to pass through this Karma Hall. This was because as the difficulty of the Karma Hall increased in the later stages, the more people there were, the better.
However, it was almost impossible for all ten of them to pass through all the Karma Halls unscathed.
So, last time, of the nine people who had come with her, only two were left at the final stage, causing them to fail at the last step and fail to pass through the last Karma Hall …
Ji Nan clearly remembered that the second Karma Hall was by no means simple. Last time, two people had died in the second Karma Hall.

"We've been blocked? There should be some mechanism in this Karma Hall, or do we need to fulfill some conditions before we can pass? "Luo Zheng pondered as he dodged these square boulders.
"Ah, save me!"
At this moment, a person howled not far away. This person was constantly dodging, but he was blocked in a corner by three square boulders.
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng's figure turned into a ray of light and flew towards this person. The moment he approached this person, he quickly struck out with his palm!
"Pa!"
That person was directly sent flying by Luo Zheng's palm. At this critical moment, he flew out from the gap between two square boulders and rolled a great distance on the ground.
"Luo Tianxing?"
Ji Nan was shocked. Why was this guy showing off his ability?
He pushed others out, but he himself was in danger?
In her heart, she was still hoping that this person would be able to go further in the Illusory Battlefield. At the very least, she would have the opportunity to challenge him!
If he was crushed by these boulders at this moment, not only would he have wasted five million fantasy points, but he would also lose half of his fantasy points. As a result, it would be very difficult for him to get up again.
Everyone looked at Luo Zheng in surprise. Although these ten people had formed a temporary team because they had used the metal voucher at the same time, there was no tacit understanding between them. There was even a faint hostility between them. For example, in the first Illusory Hall, Luo Zheng wanted to break the small skull and almost killed someone. More than one person had wanted to kill him.
Seeing Luo Zheng step in to save someone and even sacrifice himself, they felt strangely moved in their hearts.

Chapter 1248
Just as the square-shaped boulder was about to press down on him, his face was full of calmness. Then, a white mist rose from Luo Zheng's body.
"Eight-Turn Flying Smoke!"
At this moment, Luo Zheng's body was as light as a feather!
Although the boulder's rolling speed was extremely fast, it was still very large.
Luo Zheng's Eight-Turn Flying Smoke was very special. It was a movement technique that danced on the tip of a knife!
The faster the opponent's speed and the stronger the momentum, the more Luo Zheng's body could move with the momentum!
Up until now, only the crescent blade under Ji Luoxue's feet had broken through the three-inch boundary of the Eight-Turn Flying Smoke.
Yet, Luo Zheng was confident that he could dodge the boulder in front of him …
"Whoosh …"

Someone blurted out in surprise.

If the others were crushed by the boulders, and the illusory light spots that exploded were ownerless, they would naturally have an opportunity to take advantage of it. Now, it seemed that if they wanted to go further here, cooperation was extremely important!

At that moment, the short Human Race martialist from the Ten Thousand Buddha Sacred Region suddenly said, "Perhaps the mechanism is on this huge rock …"
After saying that, he turned over and avoided the heavy pressure of the huge rock in front of him. A short sword appeared in his hand. It was the Supreme Artifact, Buddha's Sun Blade!
"Whoosh …"
His figure flashed as he circled around the boulder. Although he couldn't fly in the hall, his speed was still several times faster!
The Buddha Sun Blade in his hand lightly sliced across the giant stone!
"Whoosh!"

"Boom!"

After several times, a swastika was left on the surface of the boulder!

The swastika character on the surface of the boulder began to rotate rapidly at the same time.

Moreover, there was also the law of karma in the Supreme Artifact. Could the Buddha Sun Blade break this boulder?
Everyone avoided the crushing boulder while paying attention to the movements here …
"Boom, boom …"
The boulder slowly stopped rolling!

Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed slightly. When the light dissipated, he saw that the boulder was still intact. At the same time, a small ghost face appeared on the surface of the boulder again!
Everyone was also slightly stunned when they saw the ghost face!
The shape of the ghost face suddenly changed from a strange smiling face to a crying face!
Seeing this scene, everyone's hearts suddenly tightened.

At the same time, a mournful ghost face appeared on the surface of all the square boulders in the hall at the same time.

Just as Luo Zheng moved away, all the square-shaped boulders shot out a square-shaped beam of light in all four directions!
"Buzz buzz buzz …"
This square beam of light was three feet high and three feet wide. It covered a square on the ground and extended all the way to the end of the hall!
Every square boulder released a square beam of light at the same time. Under the crisscrossing of each other, there was very little space left for the warriors to dodge …
Luo Zheng stood in the center of the square. At this moment, the front and back of him were filled with light beams in all directions. He could only move left and right, and could not move forward or backward.

When the light beams disappeared, everyone saw two dreamy light spots floating in the air …

There were eight square boulders in the area where the two people had been standing. It was a dangerous place where there was no way to dodge.
When everyone saw the two dreamy light spots, their hearts sank. Under this round of illumination, two people had actually died. But they still had not found a way to break it!
The two dreamy light spots were not small in number. Everyone estimated that there were at least three million dreamy light spots each. But at this moment, no one was in the mood to absorb them!
"Rumble, rumble …"
Not long after, the boulders began to roll again. At this moment, they did not chase after the crowd, but lined up in a row!
If these boulders really lined up in a row and shot the square beam of light in front of them, there would be no blind spot in the entire Dream Hall. Everyone would undoubtedly die!
"There's one less square boulder over there!"

The situation changed too quickly. Everyone had no time to think. They only saw Ji Nan and the other six surviving warriors rushing toward Luo Zheng at the same time under Luo Zheng's command!
When the last warrior rushed into the square where Luo Zheng was, the row of square boulders released a square beam of light at the same time!

However, when these square-shaped boulders were arranged neatly, there were a total of 43 of them. In this huge square hall, the horizontal arrangement could accommodate 44 square-shaped boulders. With one less square-shaped boulder, it was also the only chance of survival left!
There was a way to break this Karma Hall. Naturally, this chance of survival would not be blocked. If it was blocked, then no one would be able to pass through.

Chapter 1249

This was a stupid method, but it was an effective one!

It was just that no one could think of a good method …
As they dodged, the two of them placed their traps on the square on the ground in rows.

Chapter 1250
The short cultivator and Ji Nan's speed was quite strange.

Chapter 1251
After the dark red ghost face was nailed to the ground, it suddenly shattered …
The cracks extended all the way until the entire square boulder was covered in cracks. Then, with a loud bang, the boulder exploded, sending stone fragments flying everywhere.
These square boulders were originally ordinary stones.
However, on the surface of these stones, there was some kind of extremely strong Karma Law that was protected by the rules of the Heavenly Dao, so they could not be destroyed.
Just like the city walls of the various main cities, this was also the reason. Therefore, no matter how powerful the power of a battle between cultivators was, it could not be destroyed.
"You scared me to death …"
The one who spoke was the Demon Race cultivator. Just now, in a moment of desperation, Luo Zheng had directly released the bowstring and casually shot the first arrow. It just so happened that the Demon Race cultivator's side was almost hit by Luo Zheng's arrow.
Luo Zheng also noticed this scene, but time was of the essence, so he could not care so much.
"Sorry," Luo Zheng smiled faintly.
"It's fine … at least we can pass through this damn hall!" The Demon Race cultivator laughed heartily, not caring at all. Besides, even if that arrow had hit, it might not have been able to take his life. Luo Zheng's bow was obtained from the lower realms and could only be regarded as a low-level treasure among Divine Tools. However, this Demon Race cultivator was fully armed.
A cultivator looked at the motionless boulder next to him and stretched out his hand to lightly slap it, causing the square boulder to explode …
Just now, they had been forced to flee in all directions by these boulders, and now they could not help but vent their frustrations. As a result, the square boulders were smashed into pieces by them one by one.
Ji Nan walked over to Luo Zheng and smiled sweetly at Luo Zheng, "Amazing!"
"I was lucky. I didn't expect this little skull to come in handy," Luo Zheng took off a small skull from his back. This thing could not be destroyed and gave Luo Zheng a terrible headache, but it could be used at a critical moment.
This time, Luo Zheng could be considered the hero of passing through the second Karma Hall.
In the first Karma Hall, Luo Zheng had almost killed several people, so they were naturally unhappy and even angry. However, at this moment, all of this was gone.
Although they were a temporary team, their cooperation would eventually shorten the distance between them.
"Bang bang bang …"
The Karma Hall exploded with loud noises. In the blink of an eye, more than forty square-shaped boulders were smashed into smithereens. Piles of stone fragments covered the ground.
At this moment, everyone's gaze fell on a certain place in the hall. There were still two balls of dreamy light floating there. These two balls of dreamy light were left behind by the two people who had fallen under the illumination of the square beam of light.
Because these two people were not killed by others, these dreamy light spots were considered ownerless. Previously, everyone had a tacit understanding and did not take advantage of the chaos to absorb these two dreamy light spots.
Now that everyone had passed through the Karma Hall, they naturally shifted their gazes to the Karma Hall. After all, the two balls of dreamy light added up to a few million. It wasn't a small number.
The only problem was how to distribute it …
"Luo Tianxing is the only one," Ji Nan said faintly. Although her face was indifferent, there was a kind of unnegotiable momentum in her tone.
"En, we'll divide the remaining portion equally." The short cultivator nodded.
The round-hat cultivator's face revealed an unconvinced expression, "Why …"
Just as he opened his mouth, Ji Nan and the short cultivator's gazes locked onto him, causing him to immediately shut his mouth.
The greatest contributor to passing through the second Karma Hall was Luo Zheng. At this moment, it wasn't excessive for him to receive an extra portion of dreamy light spots.
Luo Zheng naturally wouldn't refuse. With a faint smile, he walked towards one of the balls of dreamy light spots and absorbed all of them. This could be considered an additional income or a 'legacy'.
As for the other dreamy light spot, after everyone agreed, they all carefully absorbed it one by one. After each person absorbed several hundred thousand dreamy light spots, they would quickly leave and take another person's place. No one dared to take this opportunity to swallow it all. After all, if they did, they would be hunted down by the other cultivators. No one dared to take this risk, not to mention it was only several hundred thousand dreamy light spots.
After absorbing these two dreamy light spots, everyone began to move towards the end of the Karma Hall. When they arrived at the end of the Karma Hall, the white light barriers that had blocked their path had already disappeared.
However, there was a giant square boulder blocking their path.
This giant square boulder was the one that blocked the exit of the Karma Hall!
"Why is there another giant square boulder?"
"How did this giant square boulder roll here?"
"Let me!"
That Demon Race cultivator had smashed a total of fifteen giant square boulders. At this moment, when he saw the remaining giant boulder, he gathered his strength and strode towards the giant boulder. The sturdy muscles on his body swelled as he forcefully struck out with both palms.
"Pu!"
With this palm strike, everyone originally thought that the giant square boulder would shatter.
However, this square-shaped boulder stood there and did not move an inch!
Everyone frowned when they saw this scene …
Logically speaking, they had already broken through the Heavenly Dao Laws in the Karma Hall. This giant square boulder should be unable to withstand a single blow.
However, it now seemed to be completely undamaged!
If this giant square boulder was still shrouded in the formless power of the Laws, its weight was close to infinite. It couldn't be smashed or moved, and it blocked the exit. How could they pass through?
That Demon Race cultivator was also at a loss. He said in a daze, "What's going on? I can't move this boulder … "
As soon as his voice fell, he saw that the surface of the giant square boulder suddenly seeped out yellow light spots.
These yellow light spots fluttered around and immediately flew towards the Demon Race cultivator's body …
"This, why are there dreamy light spots emerging!" The Demon Race cultivator was shocked. Moreover, the number of dreamy light spots that escaped was not small. In an instant, he had nearly 400,000 dreamy light spots.
Seeing this scene, everyone's faces revealed expressions of interest.
Ji Nan said indifferently, "If I'm not wrong, this is the reward for the second Dream Hall. Everyone can obtain it. However, it seems that the reward should be based on the power of the technique!"
"Ah!" The Demon Race cultivator's eyes widened like a bull's and he immediately roared, "This won't do! Again! "
Just now, he only wanted to smash this giant square boulder with his palm strike. He only used half of his strength and didn't use any martial skills, so he obtained 400,000 dreamy light spots. Regretting it, he anxiously clenched his fist and punched the giant square boulder again …
"Dong!"
The power of this punch was much greater than before, but no dreamy light spots emerged from the surface of the giant square boulder.
The Demon Race cultivator's face revealed a mournful expression. As for everyone else, they all revealed looks of pity.
Everyone clearly knew what he had missed out on, but nothing needed to be said …
The Demon Race cultivator silently retreated to the side, his face full of resentment. At this moment, his intestines were green with regret. Why did he rush to smash this giant square boulder?
After the Demon Race cultivator left, the Demon Night Clan cultivator stood in front of the giant square boulder.
With the lesson learned from the Devil Race martial artists, everyone also understood that they should unleash their most destructive means.
Some techniques were powerful, but they didn't cause much damage. In other words, they didn't cause any actual damage.
The Demon Night Clan woman stood in front of the giant boulder and thought for a moment. Then, she took two steps back and took out a fiery-red longbow.
"This longbow … is also a supreme artifact?"
Luo Zheng hadn't paid much attention to this Demon Night Clan woman before. She was very quiet and didn't show much from beginning to end. All she did was give Ji Nan a Blue Luan feather.
For her to take out a supreme artifact meant that her status in the Demon Night Clan wasn't low. Her background should be even greater than Aizen's!
She skillfully held the bow and with a light flick of her slender fingers, she pulled the bowstring …
"Empty string?" Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows. The Demon Night Clan woman didn't have an arrow in her hand.
After she pulled the bowstring, the magnificent fiery red color on the longbow began to surge. At the same time, a flame vortex appeared in front of the longbow, and as the vortex continued to rotate, a crimson arrow appeared!
"This is the Broken Moon Bursting Flame Bow. It's ranked over 2100 on the Universe Tablet." Ji Nan explained when she saw Luo Zheng's curious expression.

Chapter 1252
When she released the bowstring, there was a buzzing sound.
Everyone watched as the bowstring vibrated back and forth on the bow frame …
The fire vortex floating in front of the bow didn't shoot out. Instead, it strangely floated in front of her and slowly rotated …
The Yao Night Clan woman bashfully smiled at everyone, "We … should retreat a little!"
Seeing the power contained within the fire vortex, everyone understood that the power of the explosion would be unimaginable, so they all retreated.
Even the Yao Night Clan woman put away the Moon-Shattering Bursting Flame Bow and retreated with everyone, leaving behind the fire vortex that slowly rotated in the air …
Luo Zheng, Ji Nan, and the others retreated several hundred feet before stopping.
"It's not enough. If we continue retreating, I'm afraid you'll be affected," the Yao Night Clan woman said with a smile.
Thus, everyone retreated to the middle of the Karma Hall with her.
"Alright, that's enough." As soon as the Yao Night Clan woman said that, her eyes flashed and the sound of an explosion could be heard!
Boom ~ ~ ~
The fire vortex shot towards the square boulder and violently collided with it. The power of fire contained within the vortex spread out and spread behind them. Nearly half of the Karma Hall was immersed in fierce flames.
The Yao Night Clan woman seemed to be very familiar with the bow. She told everyone to retreat, but not too far, just enough.
Ji Nan whispered, "Fifth level of the Law of Fire, and it contains a strange Karma Law that can greatly condense the power of fire. Is this the special characteristic of the Moon-Shattering Bursting Flame Bow?"
Within the raging flames, countless dreamy specks of light appeared on the surface of the boulder. As they danced in the air, they flew towards the Yao Night Clan woman!
The number of dreamy specks of light was more than double that of the Demon Night Clan cultivator.
When the flames dissipated and the dreamy specks of light were completely absorbed, the Demon Night Clan cultivator anxiously asked, "How many? How many dreamy specks of light did you obtain?"
"2.8 million," the Yao Night Clan woman said with a faint smile.
"Ahhhhh …" The Demon Night Clan cultivator roared. At that moment, he felt extremely unbalanced. However, there was no medicine for regret in this world. Who told him to slap that square boulder?
Hearing the woman from the Fiend Night Clan, the other cultivators' hearts burned with passion.
As expected …
Just as Ji Nan had said, it was too easy to earn Fantasy Points in the Karma Hall!
He had only passed the second Karma Hall, but he had already obtained so many Fantasy Points. What about the rest?
"I see …"
Luo Zheng thought to himself, "If I only rely on plundering other warriors, it will be very difficult to maintain my ranking. If I want to get a good ranking, I must enter the Karma Hall."
After the flames dissipated, the other cultivators impatiently rushed towards the square boulder.
Following that, another human cultivator launched an attack at the square boulder!
Since he knew the rules, he naturally didn't hold back in the slightest. Instead, he exerted all his strength to execute his strongest martial skill on that square boulder …
Although this human martial artist's strength was not bad, he did not have a supreme divine artifact in the end. The number of Fantasy Points that seeped out from the boulder was not as many as the female from the Yao Night Clan, but it was still more than a million Fantasy Points.
At the same time, the Demon Race cultivator let out another wail.
Everyone present took turns to attack the square boulder. Basically, everyone obtained more than a million Fantasy Points as a reward.
Every time a person absorbed a dreamy light spot, they would hear a wail of anguish.
The people in front of them all obtained their own rewards.
At this moment, Ji Nan appeared in front of the square boulder with a confident smile on her face.
She didn't take out any weapons or magic treasures. Instead, she clasped her hands together and gently rotated them in the opposite direction. A special aura was emitted from her palms!
Next, she lightly slapped the square boulder.
"Pa." A crisp sound was heard. It was as if she had slapped the square boulder.
The Demon Race cultivator's eyes widened as he looked at Ji Nan and said, "You … don't want Fantasy Points?"
The Demon Race cultivator didn't feel much power and couldn't understand why Ji Nan would do this. However, when he thought that someone would obtain less Fantasy Points than him, he felt a little comforted.
Ji Nan faintly smiled but didn't say anything …
As for Luo Zheng, the short cultivator, and the woman from the Fiend Night Clan, when they saw this scene, they all revealed surprised expressions. Clearly, Ji Nan's slap was not ordinary!
Not long after, yellow Fantasy Points of Light began to dissipate from the square boulder. At first, they only dissipated one by one.
Seeing this, the Demon Race cultivator laughed heartily. "This should be worth 100 Fantasy Points, right?"
But soon, the Demon Race cultivator couldn't laugh anymore …
The number of Fantasy Points of Light began to increase as they floated out one by one …
"3.1 million, not bad." Ji Nan faintly smiled at Luo Zheng and then retreated to the side, leaving the Demon Race cultivator standing there in a daze.
Of the eight remaining people, six of them had absorbed different amounts of Fantasy Points. Only Luo Zheng and the short cultivator were left.
After the two of them looked at each other, the short cultivator took a step forward. "This time, let me go!"
This short cultivator had a supreme divine artifact, the Buddha Sun Blade. He should have obtained a lot of Fantasy Points.
However, contrary to everyone's expectations, he didn't seem to plan on using the Buddha Sun Blade.
He stood in front of the square boulder, his hands clasped together. After closing his eyes, he remained motionless.
"This guy, what is he doing …" The Demon Race cultivator muttered.
No one else spoke.
The square boulder in front of them rewarded Fantasy Points of Light according to the strength of everyone's attacks. Then, in front of this boulder, everyone would display their strongest strength.
In this Karma Hall, everyone was temporarily cooperating. But once they left, with the strength of these geniuses, there would be a day when they would meet each other. Understanding each other's true strength in advance, as well as their trump cards, would be of great benefit!
After the short cultivator closed his eyes, he was like an old monk in meditation. Even his aura became tranquil.
At this moment, he seemed to forget time and space. It was as if he was the only person in this world …
After the time for an incense stick to burn, everyone heard a sigh.
This sigh was incomparably bleak, as if it came from a creature that had seen through everyone.
"Haa …"
As this sigh was heard, the short cultivator gently stretched out his hand and pushed towards the square boulder.
This push was very calm and could even be called 'gentle'!
Ji Nan slapped her hand on the surface of the square boulder, causing a crisp sound to ring out. But this short cultivator's push was silent. The moment his palm touched the square boulder, he withdrew his hand, like a dragonfly skimming the surface of the water.
But with this gentle push, a massive amount of Fantasy Points of Light blotted out the sky and shot straight towards the short cultivator!
"This strength is strange. The methods of the Ten Thousand Buddha Sacred Realm are truly profound!" Luo Zheng looked at the short cultivator with a dignified expression, sighing in his heart.
In the universe, there were three human forces. Each of them was strong in their own way. The Gods Without Thought was established by many families, and the Human Dao Alliance was an alliance of countless Holy Lands. But the Ten Thousand Buddha Sacred Realm was the most ancient of the human forces. They inherited the Buddhist Dao, and the path they took was completely different from the Gods Without Thought and the Human Dao Alliance. This was because their inheritance came from the Gods and Buddhas of the heavens!
"Three million nine hundred thousand," the short cultivator indifferently reported this number.
When everyone heard this number, they couldn't help but suck in a breath of cold air.
It wasn't that the Fantasy Points had frightened them. In fact, the Fantasy Points in these eight people's bodies were no less than four million.
But if the short cultivator could obtain close to four million Fantasy Points, didn't that mean that the power of this short cultivator's palm was several times that of theirs?
Just now, the woman from the Fiendish Night Clan had used a Supreme Divine Artifact and only obtained 2.8 million Fantasy Points. But this person had obtained 3.9 million Fantasy Points with just his bare hands?
Ji Nan pursed her lips. The record she had just set had been broken by this guy. She was unwilling to accept this, but she turned to Luo Zheng and said, "It's your turn!"
She hoped that Luo Tianxing's performance would be better than that short cultivator's.

Chapter 1253
Mu Ning was the same as many geniuses of the rich and powerful families.
From the day she was born, almost every incense stick's worth of time was arranged.
She cultivated diligently day and night and worshipped her sister. She paid little attention to the trivial matters around her, wholeheartedly wanting to become the second Mu Xuerong of the Mu Family.
Mu Ning rarely calmly appreciated the other beautiful things in the world.
These days could be considered as a rare opportunity in her life. She felt that time had slowed down, and the things around her were completely different.
"That snake is too cruel. Why must it feed on young boys and girls?" Mu Ning said with a frown.
Luo Zheng glanced at Mu Ning indifferently, "When did the Mu Family's eldest daughter become so sentimental?"
"You!" Mu Ning fiercely glared at Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng calmly looked at the mother and son and said indifferently, "This kind of thing, whether it's in the God's Domain or the universe, happens thousands of times every day. The eldest daughter of the Mu Family naturally finds it fresh."
Hearing this, Mu Ning's heart slightly trembled. Looking at Luo Zheng, her eyes revealed a complicated look.
Her sister said he was a stowaway from the Great Yan Universe.
Climbing step by step from the universe to the God's Domain, what kind of experience did he have on his way?
As she became more and more curious about Luo Zheng, she felt a strange feeling in her heart.
The next day.
The children selected from the Eagle Fort were sent to the back of the eagles.
Hundreds of eagles soared into the sky from the Eagle Fort.
At the front were thirteen King Eagles. Luo Zheng and Mu Ning sat on two of the King Eagles.
Under the lead of the Great Tribal Chief, they flew northwest for a distance before arriving at a circular mountain pass.
It was an extinct volcano. The volcano's crater had been deposited for thousands of years, forming a beautiful lake. Many eagles had already landed around the lake. The other tribes of the Eagle Clan had already arrived.
The five tribes of the Eagle Clan were the Kang, Wa, Chao, Lang, and Wu tribes.
Luo Zheng and Mu Ning's tribe was the Kang tribe.
"Why are there two outsiders?" The Great Tribal Chief of the Chao tribe stared at Luo Zheng and Mu Ning.
Luo Zheng and Mu Ning were not the only outsiders who had entered this world. Other than the Paragons, there were also other True Gods.
These tribes were very vigilant against these outsiders. Just like Mu Ning, some True Gods killed people without saying a word, which naturally led to the resistance of these natives.
The Chao tribe was attacked by two True Gods. In the end, they had to mobilize eleven King Eagles to kill the two True Gods!
They naturally did not have a good impression of these foreign True Gods who had suddenly appeared.
"They are our friends," said the Grand Chief of the Kang tribe.
"They are enemies."
"Don't let them step into the Temple of Sky Wheel. If they anger that snake, we will all be exterminated."
The Great Chieftains of the other four clans all voiced their objections.
The Great Tribal Chief of the Kang Tribe looked back at Luo Zheng. This was something he had expected.
After Luo Zheng nodded to the Great Tribal Chief, the Great Tribal Chief of the Kang Tribe brought the other four Great Tribal Chiefs to one side and took out the Golden Cores in his hands.
Not long after, the Great Tribal Chiefs returned again. Their expressions when looking at Luo Zheng and Mu Ning changed.
These Golden Cores were too important to them. If they brought them to the Temple of Sky Wheel, they would be able to obtain these magical Golden Cores. This transaction was undoubtedly very cost-effective. The Great Tribal Chiefs did not say anything more.
Then, under the constant shouting of the Great Tribal Chiefs of the Eagle tribe, everyone returned to the back of the King Eagles.
Accompanied by a melodious sound, all the King Eagles spread their wings and soared into the sky. The scene was very spectacular …
"Xiu!"
A King Eagle shot out and flew to the front of the group in the blink of an eye.
This King Eagle was smaller than the other King Eagles. The feathers on its neck flickered with a blood-red light. When it passed by Luo Zheng, the eagle's eyes only glanced at Luo Zheng indifferently before shooting away!
"This eagle is very powerful," Mu Ning said as she stared at the little King Eagle.
Luo Zheng nodded. The adult King Eagles of the Eagle tribe posed a great threat to Luo Zheng. If a fight really broke out, Luo Zheng was not very afraid.
But facing this little King Eagle, Luo Zheng did not have absolute certainty of victory.
However, if this King Eagle wanted to hurt him, it would be very difficult, let alone take his life.
Thousands of King Eagles soared in the sky, and from time to time, the screams of children could be heard.
The five Eagle tribes contributed a total of seventeen children. At first, these children felt very surprised and amused riding the King Eagles.
But after a long time, they grew tired of the scenery and began to make a ruckus.
The Eagle tribe men present all had heavy expressions. This kind of life and death parting was repeated almost every once in a while.
In the middle of the break, Mu Ning saw one of the boys crying non-stop, desperately calling for his mother. She went forward and took out a small mirror. The small mirror in Mu Ning's hand could change into a variety of colors, immediately attracting the boy's attention.
When the King Eagles took off again, Mu Ning simply held the boy in her arms and rode with him on the same King Eagle.
Several hours later, after flying over the mountains and lakes, Luo Zheng saw the huge sea of insects again!
Those huge insects were still wriggling day and night …
"Ah! How can there be such huge insects here, "Mu Ning looked down with a face full of surprise.
After entering this world, she directly appeared in the territory of the Eagle tribe. She could be considered much luckier than Luo Zheng. Naturally, she had not seen these Sky Wheel Insects before.
Although Luo Zheng now understood a lot about this world, he was also very curious about the origin of these insects.
These huge insects, even in the Realm of the Gods, were ferocious creatures that could not be ignored.
As the saying goes, it is difficult to raise a flood dragon in shallow water, and it is difficult to hide a fierce tiger in plain sun. It is reasonable to say that in such a world, there were one or two of these huge insects at most. But in this sea of insects, there seemed to be thousands, or even tens of thousands of huge Sky Wheel Insects gathered together. It was naturally hard to understand.
After passing through the sea of insects, there was a canyon that was like a scar on the earth. After passing through the canyon, they returned to the vast desert.
The King Eagle's physical strength was limited. After flying continuously for four to six hours, they had to stop for a while.
The Eagle tribe members often went to the desert to herd eagles, so they were naturally very familiar with this route.
After walking and stopping for two days and two nights, the people on the back of the King Eagle finally saw a brown-yellow building in the barren desert.
It seemed that this was once a city. After experiencing endless years, the buildings had almost collapsed, leaving only the barren walls stubbornly standing, experiencing the baptism of sand and time.
In the central area of this city, there was a huge altar, and behind the altar was a majestic hall.
Under the command of the chiefs, the eagles swooped down one after another. All of them folded their wings and stopped below the altar.
Luo Zheng jumped down from the back of the King Eagle and looked up at the altar.
In the center of the altar, there was a huge black snake statue. This black snake's appearance was exactly the same as the small snake on the Imperial Jade Seal!
"It's that snake," Mu Ning said, following closely behind Luo Zheng.
"Sky Wheel King Snake," Luo Zheng nodded. This was what the King Eagle tribe members called this snake.
The Tribal Chiefs and the other Eagle tribe members jumped down from the King Eagle's back, and then they also carried the children down.
These children had been crying and crying for their mothers the entire way. Now that they were tired, all of them were sleeping soundly. Their childish little faces were filled with serenity. They did not know the fate that they were about to face!
Mu Ning looked at the innocent face of the sleeping boy in her arms, and a trace of pity appeared in her eyes. She whispered, "Is there no way to save these children?"
"Let's take one step at a time," Luo Zheng replied indifferently.
He didn't know what kind of vicious creature this Sky Wheel King Snake was.
If this vicious creature in the Imperial Jade Seal was as powerful as the Shi Mi, not to mention him, even if those Perfection Stage Paragons came, it would be useless.
At this moment, the chirping of a bird could be heard in the sky.
A cloud floated over from the distant horizon. After careful observation, those clouds were formed by palm-sized multicolored birds. There were some people standing on those multicolored birds.
"The Black Bird Clan has arrived," the Tribal Chief said.

Chapter 1254
However, this Ji Nan had some kind of talent that allowed her to distinguish between the truth and lies.
