Luo Zheng just smiled and didn't explain. He believed that they wouldn't be too bothered by this issue …
Continuing to climb the stairs, the group arrived at the mountainside.
An ice-blue hall appeared in front of Luo Zheng's eyes.
The hall in front of them seemed to be built with blue crystals, and its entire body flickered with an ice-blue luster.
When they got closer, they could feel a cool breeze coming from the hall.
"How difficult is the third Karma Hall?" Jin Hai turned to look at Ji Nan.
Ji Nan glanced at the hall several times, her expression grave. "Every Karma Hall is different. I can't say for sure, but there are a total of five Karma Halls from the foot of the mountain to the peak! The last time we entered, there were two prodigies in the team! No one died in the first Karma Hall. Three died in the second Karma Hall. Four died in the third Karma Hall. I also died in the fourth Karma Hall … "
"Two prodigies, but so many people died?" The Demon Race martial artist's eyes widened.
Among the ten people present, there wasn't a single prodigy …
Although they hadn't competed with each other before, the top three in terms of strength were undoubtedly Luo Zheng, Jin Hai, and Ji Nan.
In their hearts, although Luo Zheng and the others weren't weak, they probably couldn't be compared to prodigies. To be called a prodigy, one had to have all the advantages of time, place, and people. They were people who gathered the great fortune of the entire universe.
Not only did they bear the fate of the Great Era Battle, but they also carried the hopes of a race and a force.
Even a team with a prodigy couldn't pass this Karma Hall. The difficulty was more exaggerated than they had imagined.
Therefore, when Ji Nan said this, some of the eight people wanted to retreat.

Ji Nan emphasized the words "work together".
There was one point that Ji Nan didn't mention. The last time she entered the Karma Hall, the two geniuses didn't want to give in to each other. They were constantly fighting each other and even fought on the mountain.
Furthermore, when they saw the other cultivators in danger, those two prodigies just stood by and watched them die …
If it wasn't for this, it was extremely likely that the 10 of them would have passed through the fifth Karma Hall!
This was also the reason why Ji Nan emphasized cooperation from the very beginning. It was also because of this that their team performed better than the previous team. At the very least, they only lost two people in the second Karma Hall.
"Ji Nan is right. If we want to obtain more fantasy points, we must cooperate with each other. Otherwise, no one will be able to go far. If we cooperate well, perhaps we can all charge into the top 1000!" Luo Zheng smiled. "Everyone, let's go in!"
After saying that, Luo Zheng took the lead and stepped into the hall.
The interior of the hall maintained the same color as the exterior. Whether it was the floor, the ceiling, or the two walls, the pillars were all icy-blue. When one touched them, they felt a comfortable coolness.
"This Karma Hall is so beautiful." Ji Nan looked up and sighed.
Luo Zheng and the others didn't feel anything. Women were naturally more interested in beautiful things. Not only her, but the woman called Huan Ling from the Fiend Night Clan was also the same.
However, they didn't walk far in the Karma Hall before stopping.
It wasn't that they didn't want to move forward, but that they couldn't. In front of them was a deep ravine that was three thousand feet wide.
Strange colors were constantly flowing in this deep trench. They were streams of pure energy flowing in this trench.
Azure Demon stood at the edge and stretched his neck to look down. Then, he looked forward and said, "If we fall, I'm afraid we'll be directly sent back to the main city! How are we going to cross this? "
Normally, not to mention this deep ravine that was a thousand feet wide, even if it was a hundred thousand feet wide or a million feet wide, they could still fly over it. The problem was that flying was prohibited in the Karma Hall.
"We should be able to jump over it." Jin Hai looked into the distance and said.
"It's not a big problem." Luo Zheng nodded.
It wasn't too difficult to jump three thousand feet with a sprint …
However, this deep ravine in front of them couldn't just be a test of their jumping ability. If they rushed over recklessly, they would most likely die.
Just like in the second Karma Hall, they quickly passed through the end of the hall, only to find a white light barrier there, blocking their path.
"There seems to be something here." Ji Nan pointed to the left side of the hall.
Luo Zheng looked in the direction Ji Nan was pointing. There were ten icy-blue tablets erected in the corner of the hall!
Luo Zheng walked closer to take a look. Through the icy-blue tablets, he found hooks in them.
"Why are there so many hooks?" the Demon Race cultivator asked curiously.
Each of the ten tablets had a long hook sealed in it. At the end of each hook was a string of thin chains. They were curled together and their length was unknown …
Ten tablets?
Luo Zheng stared at them for a while. Then, he reached out and slapped one of the tablets.
In theory, there were Heavenly Dao rules in the Karma Hall. Nothing could break them.
However, when Luo Zheng smacked it, he discovered that the tablet was extremely brittle. The huge tablet directly turned into crystal clear powder and scattered, and the hook that was sealed within fell out with a sound.
"These hooks …"
After Luo Zheng picked up the hooks, he pulled hard on the chains behind them.
Even though the chain was only as thick as a pinky finger, Luo Zheng was unable to break it even when he tried to force it.
"Sure enough, they are also protected by Heavenly Dao rules. These hooks can't be broken," Luo Zheng said.
"Clang …"
"Clang …"
"Clang …"
When everyone saw Luo Zheng take out the hooks from the obelisk, they all followed suit. They broke the obelisk and took out hooks from it.
The answer was becoming clearer. These exquisitely shaped hooks were prepared for Luo Zheng and the others.
The team that was supposed to challenge the Karma Hall was supposed to have ten people. However, with two of them dead, the hooks in the remaining two steles were now ownerless.
Cang Mo also smashed a tablet and obtained a hook. However, he wasn't satisfied. He looked at the remaining two tablets. However, when he wanted to smash them, they didn't move at all. They couldn't be broken.
It seemed that each person could only obtain one hook.
After everyone obtained the hooks, they focused their attention on Ji Nan again.
Ji Nan looked at Luo Zheng innocently. "I don't know what to do next …"
Luo Zheng smiled and walked to the edge of the deep trench with the hooks. His gaze fell on the other side.
The other side of the deep trench was hidden in darkness. He couldn't see the situation on the other side. However, as a green luster appeared in Luo Zheng's eyes, the black shadows on the other side gradually dissipated. He saw some faint shadows in the darkness. Those shadows were swaying!
"There seems to be something on the other side," Luo Zheng said.
"What is it?" Ji Nan asked immediately. Before they figured out the rules, everyone was in an unknown state of the Karma Hall. They might be killed instantly if they were careless. Therefore, everyone looked nervous.
"I didn't see it clearly," Luo Zheng said. The green luster in his eyes intensified. However, this time, when Luo Zheng activated his Clear Eyes Spiritual Eyes, his expression suddenly changed. "Get out of the way!"
Just as he finished speaking, from the darkness on the other side of the deep trench, a hook shot towards him at high speed ….
After Luo Zheng's reminder, everyone quickly moved to the side. However, the hook was extremely fast. It covered the distance of three hundred feet in an instant. The hook was thrown from the opposite side and directly hung on the body of the human warrior named Li Chengyan!

Chapter 1255
The human martial artist called Li Chengyan was hooked and pulled into the darkness.
"Ah!"
There was a scream.
Everyone saw a ball of yellow light suddenly explode in the darkness on the other side …
Clearly, Li Chengyan had died. Half of his Fantasy points had exploded!
The instant those fantasy points appeared, they disappeared without a trace, as if they had entered the darkness!
"Where are the Fantasy points?"
"They were absorbed? They seem to have been absorbed! "
"That's impossible!" Ji Nan also revealed a surprised expression. "What item in this Karma Hall can absorb fantasy points?"
In the Illusion Battlefield, only warriors could absorb Fantasy points from each other.
If Luo Zheng killed Li Chengyan, then no matter how far away they were, these Fantasy points would fly toward Luo Zheng.
Just like the golden pills that Ji Luoxue had bestowed upon her subordinates to commit suicide, those golden pills had Ji Luoxue's consciousness. Therefore, when her subordinates committed suicide, the Illusion Battlefield determined that they were killed by Ji Luoxue.
When warriors were killed by ferocious beasts in mystic realms or wilderness, their Fantasy points would float on the spot and become ownerless Fantasy points. Anyone could absorb them, but those ferocious beasts could not absorb them.
But after Li Chengyan was hooked, his Fantasy points were absorbed?
There was only one possibility. There was a warrior on the other side of the Karma Hall!
It was also that cultivator who had lured Li Chengyan over, killed him, and absorbed his dreamy light spots at the same time.
But every time the Karma Hall opened, only ten people could enter at the same time. Why would there be other warriors on the other side?
But that didn't make any sense at all …
"Everyone, retreat. Be careful." Luo Zheng frowned. He clenched the hook in his hand tightly, his eyes overflowing with green. Even though he had activated his Clear Eyes Spirit Eyes to the maximum, he still could not see through the darkness. This darkness seemed to be constructed using the Law of Darkness. The darkness was like a curtain that protected the other party.
"It's the Law of Darkness. The other party came prepared," Jin Hai said coldly. He held the hook in one hand and the Buddha Sun Blade in the other.
Luo Zheng's eyes scanned the darkness and finally locked onto a blurry shadow.
These hooks were placed neatly on the side, and each person had one. Obviously, they were for Luo Zheng and the others to use directly.
Since the other party could lure the people on this side over, Luo Zheng could also lure the people on the other side over …
"Now!"
After aiming, Luo Zheng stretched out his hand, and the hook in his hand flew straight to the opposite side!
"Whoosh …"
Although the hook was flying at an extremely fast speed, the "living beings" on the other side seemed to be very experienced. They kept circling around the edge of the deep trench.
"Missed!"
Luo Zheng frowned slightly. He reached out and pulled the hook back.
Just as Luo Zheng retracted his hook, he suddenly raised his eyebrows, "Watch out! Phantom … "
However, his warning was too late. As soon as his voice fell, another hook flew out of the darkness on the opposite side, wrapped around Phantom at an extremely fast speed, and pulled her over.
In fact, from the moment the hook was used on Li Chengyan, Luo Zheng had already discovered that the hook itself could not directly injure people, but once someone was hooked, it seemed to produce an irresistible force that would directly wrap around the martial artist and pull them over.
Seeing that the Phantom was hooked and was about to be pulled into the darkness, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. The hook that he had just retracted was once again thrown toward the opposite shore!
"Whoosh …"
His hook wasn't aimed at the shadow in the darkness, but directly at the phantom!
As soon as Phantom's figure disappeared into the darkness, she felt a strong threat. She planned to fight back at the first moment, but a hook wrapped around her body again and pulled her back directly …
"That was close!"
Seeing that Phantom was fine, Ji Nan and the others next to her also breathed a sigh of relief.
"Damn it, those guys on the opposite side are hiding in the darkness. How can we break through! It's not fair at all! "The Demon Race warrior raised the hook in his hand and stared at the opposite side of the river.
Phantom was also caught by the other party because of a moment of carelessness. At this moment, she had escaped a disaster.
Phantom was pulled back by Luo Zheng. She nodded slightly at him, and then her delicate face showed anger.
"There are indeed other warriors on the opposite side!"

"Whoosh …"
Just as the Phantom condensed the flame vortex, another hook flew towards the Phantom from the opposite side.
After being hooked once, Phantom naturally would not let this happen a second time. She grabbed the Moon-Breaking Bursting Flame Bow and snorted coldly. With a slight twist of her body, she moved slightly to the side, and at the same time, released the bowstring in her hand!
"Whoosh!"
The crazily tumbling flame vortex shot directly towards the opposite side!
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng, Ji Nan, Jin Hai, and the others also stared at the opposite side of the river.
The explosion of the Phantom's flame might be able to win them a slight chance …
"Boom!"
The flame vortex burst into fierce flames, and at this moment, the opposite side of the river was clearly illuminated.
At this time, Luo Zheng and the others saw clearly that there were ten people standing neatly on the opposite side of the river. Each of them was holding a hook in their hands, and they were looking at each other with sneers on their faces.
Although the power of the arrow shot by Phantom was not small, it seemed that it could not be used in the Law of Darkness. Under the devouring of the Law of Darkness, the flame vortex began to shrink rapidly …
In the center of the opposite shore, a warrior standing at the forefront pointed at Luo Zheng with a contemptuous smile on his face. At the same time, he made a throat-cutting gesture with his hand. After he made this gesture, the flame vortex was completely devoured, and the ten warriors on the opposite shore were once again immersed in darkness.
"There are still ten people in this team. Their strength cannot be underestimated!" Ji Nan said with a cautious expression.
According to Ji Nan's speculation, the ten people on the opposite side should also be warriors who were exploring the Karma Hall. But unfortunately, the third Karma Hall had arranged two teams on opposite sides!
The ten people on the opposite side should have also explored the first two Karma Halls, but none of them had fallen. The strength of these guys absolutely could not be underestimated.
What was worse was that they had arranged the Law of Darkness in advance, hiding them in the darkness, which invisibly increased the difficulty of Luo Zheng's team.
"Who can use the Law of Darkness?" Ji Nan asked. The Law of Darkness was a relatively unpopular Law, and Ji Nan did not understand it.
Although Luo Zheng had been baptized by the Law of Darkness, he had never comprehended it.
Hearing Ji Nan's question, Jin Hai sneered, "No need for the Law of Darkness! Watch me! "
Jin Hai gently clapped his hands, and the sound of Sanskrit rang out, accompanied by a faint fragrance.
Specks of psychedelic colors began to emanate from his body, spreading out in all directions …
Inside the fog, Luo Zheng and the others couldn't sense anything out of the ordinary.
But to the other side, there were countless illusions appearing out of thin air, both real and fake, making it difficult to distinguish.
"Let's see how they'll hook us!" Jin Hai sneered.
Although this method could not completely hide the seven of them, it could bring considerable interference to the other side.
Now, it seemed that the third Karma Hall's test was a contest between two teams.
In this fog, they had to think about how to fight back.

Chapter 1256

Because in this third Karma Hall, it was destined that only one team would be able to pass through. After the loser was killed, they would be directly sent back to the main city!

Azure Demon's meaning was simple. This person only lacked a single fate, but his strength still couldn't be underestimated!

No matter how strong this person was, this hook contained the rules of the Heavenly Dao; it was impossible for him to break free. The moment this fellow was pulled over, he would be forced into a passive position. If Luo Zheng were to attack, this fellow might not be able to avoid it.

He would have more than enough time to throw his hook behind him. As long as he could hook Big Brother Dugu over, these cowards would have reached the end of their road in the Karma Hall.

According to the rules of the Karma Hall, once a martial artist was wrapped around by a hook, they wouldn't be able to struggle free nor would they have the ability to fight back. Although the Astral Crystal Saint Body suddenly possessed an extremely strong defensive power, no matter how strong it was, it still couldn't compare to the rules of the Karma Hall!

The Karma Laws of the Karma Hall were often filled with hidden killing intent. Even the slightest violation would result in death. For instance, the river of energy in this deep trench or the square boulder in the second Karma Hall. But, if one could use them, they would be incomparably fierce …
This hook contained an extremely potent Karma Law. Anyone caught in this hook would enter a special state. In this state, a martial artist would be weightless and unable to resist. They would also be unable to use true essence or astral essence. At the same time, they wouldn't be harmed!
Only when this hook was released could both sides fight!
So, just as Big Dream condensed his Astral Crystal Saint Body, Jin Hai, who was hiding not too far away, directly used the hook.
The rhombus-shaped astral crystal that Big Dream had used his astral essence to condense was naturally destroyed by the Heavenly Dao rules on the hook!

Chapter 1258

Chapter 1259
Similarly, Luo Zheng was unable to resist the Laws contained within the hook.

The only Heavenly Talent among independent warriors.

Chapter 1261

Chapter 1262
The energy surging like a river began to corrode Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao's body!

The dark metal sword in his hand had the power to override the Heavenly Dao.
Before entering the Illusory Space, the sword had told Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao that the Illusory Battlefield had a special repulsive force towards him. It was possible that other than him, all hidden souls and living beings would not be able to enter together.
For example, when Luo Zheng entered the Illusory Battlefield this time, he wasn't able to bring Xun, Qinglong, and the others with him. The Illusory Battlefield's projection could only accept Luo Zheng alone. Even now, Luo Zheng couldn't communicate with them.
But the sword had forcibly broken this rule of the Illusory Battlefield and forced its way into the Illusory Battlefield.
The sword also knew that if he hadn't come, Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao wouldn't have been able to accomplish much with his own strength …
After entering the Illusory Battlefield with the sword, Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao was confident that he could compete for first place in the Illusory Battlefield.
Not only were those prodigies no match for him, but even a Dao Child who had truly comprehended the Dao was no match for him!
The Karma Hall was dangerous, and a moment of carelessness could lead to death. Just like the second Karma Hall he had just passed, there were countless life-taking ropes. According to the rules of the Karma Hall, these life-taking ropes were almost impossible to sever. But Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao had relied on the sword to forcibly sever the Heavenly Dao rules within …
This was the fundamental reason why not a single one of the 10 of them had perished in the Karma Hall, and it was also the reason why the 10 core geniuses of the various clans addressed him as Big Brother Dugu. It was because they noticed that this' Dugu 'who was traveling with them this time seemed to be an unimaginably thick thigh ….
Under the leadership of 'Dugu', they might have been able to easily complete the Karma Hall.
Given their gains in the Karma Hall, the ten of them would probably be able to enter the top ten of this world!
But just as they reached the third Karma Hall, they were arranged to clash with another group of people. At first, they even thought that the difficulty would be lowered. After all, with 'Dugu''s' strength, he could easily wipe out the seven of them.
But they never imagined …
Just like that, Dugu fell into the energy river.
"I can't accept this … bring me up!"
This energy river rapidly eroded the layer of golden light covering Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao's body. This dark iron sword was incomparably tyrannical. If it were any other martial artist, if they were to fall into this energy river, they would have died in the blink of an eye. They would have been completely eroded.
"Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao's Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao's's," the sword replied....
"Then, then … Let me float up, I want to say something to that guy!"
Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao had never imagined that he would be defeated in the Karma Hall. Moreover, it was caused by a brat of unknown origin. How could his proud heart accept this?
...
The dark sword's' surface suddenly flashed with a dazzling golden light. It dragged Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao up from the bottom of the energy river.
It was also because the dark sword was not at its peak. If it was at its peak, it would have been enough to protect Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao as he swam freely in the energy river. Not to mention that, it would have been easy for it to shatter the Heavenly Dao. But now, it could only protect Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao for a short while.
Even so, it was enough to stun the martial artists on both sides of the shore.
When the martial artists in the darkness saw Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao fall into the energy river, their hearts sank. After the battle in the second Karma Hall, the strength that Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao had displayed had completely convinced this group of proud geniuses. But now that he had fallen, the shock in their hearts was even greater.
As for Luo Zheng's' side …
Ji Nan had recognized him the moment Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao appeared from the darkness.
Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao …
That guy who had become famous in one battle.
It was also this person who had appeared out of nowhere and stolen much of Ji Luoxue's limelight.
It was a pity that Ji Luoxue had not fought Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao in the Four-Race Grand Competition.
Not only did Ji Nan recognize this person, but Jin Hai also recognized him. After all, Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao had become famous by stepping on that prodigy of the Ten Thousand Buddha Sacred Domain. When this news spread to the Ten Thousand Buddha Sacred Domain, it caused a huge commotion!
Therefore, the moment Ji Nan saw Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao, she was already in a state of despair. She sighed inwardly.
She was done for. Although she trusted Luo Zheng, in her heart, Luo Zheng's strength should be much stronger than hers, Jin Hai, Huan Ling, and the others. But it was still unknown if he could fight against the prodigies.
However, just like that, Luo Zheng had dragged Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao into a state where his life was hanging by a thread. At the same time, Luo Zheng himself was also in an extremely dangerous state. Then, before Ji Nan could understand what had happened, Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao fell into the energy river and disappeared in an instant. It looked like he was dead.
Although Luo Zheng did not rely on his own strength to win a head-on battle, but relied on luck and trickery to get this prodigy into the energy river, this was enough to make Ji Nan sigh incomparably. At the very least, Luo Tianxing's luck was probably much stronger than Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao's.
However …
Just as Ji Nan was sighing slightly, a sword shadow emerged from the bottom of the dark river. After the sword came out of the water, Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao's originally sunk figure actually emerged. But at this moment, his face had been corroded beyond recognition by the pure energy flow in the river. And that was the result of the sword's full protection.
"He can still get up like this! This guy is so freakish! "
The rude Azure Demon also sighed.
Ji Nan, Jin Hai, Huan Ling, and the others could not help but gasp. They were really afraid that this guy could fly up from the energy river.
It was just that flying was forbidden in the Karma Hall. In his current state, this possibility was not high.
As for the warriors on Dugu Jian's side, there was a glimmer of hope in their eyes.
However, Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao did not leave the river. It was already very strenuous for the sword to bring him up to the surface of the river. After all, this was almost a direct violation of the rules of the Heavenly Dao, and it consumed a lot of energy for the sword.
However, Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao did not leave the river. After he appeared, there was only deep hatred in his eyes. Accompanied by a wild laugh, he stared at Luo Zheng and said, "Boy, you win this time! But you won only because of luck. It has nothing to do with strength! "
Luo Zheng stared at Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao's head in the river, his face showing a noncommittal expression. To be honest, Luo Zheng himself was very surprised that this person could survive in the energy river for so long. Even with his miraculous body, he would probably be corroded to death instantly if he fell into the river. This person seemed to have an extremely powerful force protecting him.
A force that could resist the rules of the Heavenly Dao to a certain extent …
Either the Divine Race came in person, or it was an existence that surpassed the Heavenly Dao.
Was the problem with the sword?
"Do your best! I hope that you can have a smooth journey. This way, we will meet again sooner or later. At that time, I hope that you won't play such tricks again. I will make you lose to me fair and square … Gulong, gulong … "
The following words could not be heard clearly.
Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao's sword had already reached its limit.
He quickly sank into the energy river. After losing the protection of the sword, his body was instantly corroded and melted, becoming a part of the energy river.
At the same time, a large ball of dreamy light exploded!
"So many dreamy light balls!"
Everyone's eyes suddenly lit up!
According to their previous experience, the dreamy light balls that Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao had exploded were at least tens of millions!
Those yellow light balls quietly floated above the energy river. They did not float up or down, and they emitted a strong attractive force to the warriors on both sides of the river.
But so what if the attractive force was strong? Who would dare to step into this energy river and absorb it?
When Luo Zheng saw this scene, a regretful expression appeared on his face. He was afraid that such a large amount of dreamy points would be wasted here. It was a pity that he did not kill this person with his own hands. Otherwise, these dreamy light balls would automatically enter his body.

Chapter 1263
Luo Zheng threw out his long hook, and with a backhand pull, Jin Hai was the first one to be pulled over.
At the same time, he had already rushed into the darkness!
After Jin Hai was pulled over, he immediately pulled Ji Nan over, followed by Phantom, Azure Demon, and the other three cultivators …
"Bang!"
Just as the cultivators on Luo Zheng's side were being pulled to the opposite side one by one, a muffled sound came from the darkness, and then a ball of dreamy light exploded and flew towards Luo Zheng!
The cultivators who could enter the Dream Hall naturally collected a lot of dreamy light spots. After absorbing them, Luo Zheng instantly gained over a hundred thousand dreamy points.
At this moment, how could Jin Hai, Ji Nan, and the others be willing to fall behind Luo Zheng?
The Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao had died and returned to the main city to be resurrected. Their leader had fallen, and their morale had dropped to a freezing point.
Although the strength of the remaining seven people wasn't bad, under the circumstances that they didn't have the intention to fight, there was no need to fear them!
What's more, the strength of Ji Nan, Jin Hai, and Phantom could not be underestimated!
Accompanied by the flickering light of the Buddha's Blade, the cultivator who used the Law of Darkness was also killed. The darkness that shrouded this place dissipated, and the number of cultivators on the other side rapidly decreased, turning into dreamy light spots …
After the last person's dreamy points were absorbed by Ji Nan, everyone's eyes once again focused on Luo Zheng.
At this moment, this third Karma Hall didn't seem to be too difficult. From beginning to end, Ji Nan and the others didn't do much, and only Phantom helped Luo Zheng.
However, they also knew that this third Karma Hall was extremely dangerous. If it weren't for Luo Zheng bringing the Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao into this energy river, they would probably be wiped out here … The reason they could pass through this Karma Hall was none other than Luo Zheng alone!
"Why are you looking at me?" Luo Zheng looked at the exit of the Karma Hall. Their exit was the entrance of the Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao's group.
Ji Nan pursed her lips and smiled, "That guy is very unwilling."
"Who?" Luo Zheng pretended not to know.
Ji Nan pointed at the energy river in the deep trench.
Luo Zheng smiled. "I've already offended Ji Luoxue. Adding another one won't hurt …"
This kind of character, even if he had fallen once, he would probably be able to catch up quickly. With the evolution of the Illusory Battlefield, the warriors who were ranked at the bottom would be constantly eliminated, while the warriors who were ranked at the top would constantly collide with each other. Sooner or later, Luo Zheng would meet this person again.
"That's true …" Ji Nan looked at Luo Zheng's indifferent expression and could only smile helplessly.
"However, these dreamy light spots are wasted," Luo Zheng said as he stared at the dreamy light spots floating on the river.
Not only Luo Zheng, but Ji Nan, Jin Hai, and the others also looked at those dreamy light spots with regretful expressions.
At this moment, Phantom walked beside Luo Zheng, holding the long hook in her hand and saying softly, "You go down, I'll hook you up!"
"Hmm?" Luo Zheng looked at Phantom.
"Trust me, I won't let you fall in," Phantom said seriously.
This long hook could hook a warrior back in an instant, but the large group of dreamy light spots always floated one foot above the energy river. If one jumped down and absorbed all the dreamy light spots in an instant, and someone threw the long hook at the same time, with the right timing, it was not impossible to take away these dreamy light spots.
However, to absorb these dreamy light spots, the warrior who threw the hook had to have an extremely precise timing!
If he was a little faster, he might not be able to catch the falling warrior. If he was a little slower, the warrior would fall into the energy river. Regardless of whether he was a little faster or a little slower, the warrior who fell in would be destroyed.
Another problem was the issue of trust.
This group of warriors had entered the Karma Hall together and had built trust among each other, but it was not enough to put their lives in the hands of others. If the person on the shore did not throw the hook, then they would really die unjustly, even though they would not really die in the Illusory Battlefield …
"I can hit a flying butterfly from ten miles away. There is absolutely no mistake," Phantom added.
She had been a woman of few words along the way, but at this moment, Phantom was very confident.
"We … will also throw the hook," Ji Nan said with a smile when she saw Luo Zheng's expression change.
"It's better to let Phantom throw the hook alone. When the two long hooks collide, the Laws contained in them will cancel each other out. I'm afraid it will be self-defeating," Jin Hai said. Although he was tempted, he did not dare to jump into the energy river.
Now, everyone regarded Luo Zheng as their backbone and expected him to lead the team further in the Karma Hall. No one wanted Luo Zheng to fall. As for Jin Hai, he was far less important than Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng glanced at Phantom, then at the dreamy points in the energy river …
Now, he only had a total of more than ten million dreamy points in his body. The dreamy points in the energy river were definitely not a small amount for Luo Zheng.
After pondering for a moment, he nodded at Phantom. These tens of millions of dreamy points were worth Luo Zheng's risk.
Then, he walked to the edge. Phantom stood behind Luo Zheng, holding the long hook in her hand.
"I'm jumping!"
After saying that, Luo Zheng leaped into the air.
Then, he jumped towards the dreamy light spot in the energy river.
The distance of several feet was covered in an instant by Luo Zheng's rapid fall.
At this moment, Phantom looked straight ahead, staring at the height of Luo Zheng's fall. Calculating in her heart, she threw the long hook in her hand towards the dreamy light spot.
When Luo Zheng's figure passed by the dreamy light spots, these ownerless dreamy light spots immediately drilled into his body!
But while he absorbed the dreamy light spots, he was less than a foot away from the energy river below …
"Whoosh …"
At the same time, the long hook wrapped around Luo Zheng's body. Under Phantom's strong pull, Luo Zheng stopped falling at this moment. Under the pull of the long hook, Luo Zheng's head tilted forward, and the tip of his nose was only two or three inches away from the energy river!
If he fell any further, he was afraid that he would be eroded by this energy river.
Luo Zheng could resist the powerful flame force, but he absolutely could not withstand the erosion of this energy river …
"Huff … Huff …"
Under Phantom's strong pull, Luo Zheng's figure began to rise rapidly, and he returned to the shore almost instantly.
Seeing Luo Zheng come back intact, several cultivators showed envious expressions. How could they not be envious of the danger they took in exchange for tens of millions of dreamy points?
"Thank you," Luo Zheng, who returned to the shore, thanked Phantom.
Phantom just nodded slightly, "No need to thank me, this is what you deserve."
Although this Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao was not killed by Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng was the one who killed him. If not for him, all of them would have died at the hands of the Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao.
After collecting the Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao's dreamy points, Luo Zheng's ranking soared again …
Because cultivators from all over the Illusory Battlefield had obtained iron scrolls from various secret realms and entered the Karma Hall!
At this stage, everyone's ranking was fluctuating violently.
After all, entering the Illusory Hall required a deduction of five million dreamy points. For the cultivators in front, if tens of thousands of dreamy points were deducted, their ranking would drop a lot, let alone five million.
After Luo Zheng's initial deduction of five million dreamy points, his ranking fell directly.
But after he passed through the second Karma Hall, his ranking quickly returned. After absorbing tens of millions of dreamy points, his ranking in this Great World had reached the 23rd place!
And in the entire Illusory Battlefield, his ranking directly jumped from 9200 to 130 …
The top 100 was the place where the geniuses gathered!
This was already the top of the pyramid, the most outstanding existence in the universe!
"This Luo Zheng's strength is definitely not weak!"
"Who said he relied on an Immortal Mansion? His strength is probably comparable to the geniuses! "
"If this guy continues to rush forward, I'm afraid he will enter the top 100!"
Originally, many people in the universe had been paying attention to Luo Zheng. Even when Luo Zheng was ranked outside the millions, they had been paying attention to him.
Not long ago, Luo Zheng had entered the top 10,000 …

Chapter 1264

Three Pure World, Jade Pure Sacred Ground …
Qingling Martial Hall was the core cultivation sacred ground of the Jade Pure Sacred Ground.
Only the disciples with the most outstanding talent could be selected to enter this martial hall.
During this time, the Divine Sea Realm martial artists of Qingling Martial Hall had all entered the Illusory Battlefield, so Qingling Martial Hall was deserted.
However, it was very lively outside a large hall in the center of Qingling Martial Hall.
Life and Death Realm martial artists, as well as Extreme God Realm martial artists, were all gathered here, looking at the pyramid formed by countless names at the top of the hall.
The competition in the Illusory Battlefield was ongoing. As the cultivation sacred ground of the Jade Pure Sacred Ground, Qingling Martial Hall had high hopes for some of the core geniuses …
"Shen Qing's ranking has risen again. He has entered the two hundred and twenty thousand!"
"It seems like it's possible for him to enter the top one hundred thousand …"
"He really lives up to everyone's expectations!"
On average, there were three tenth-rank sacred grounds in the one hundred thousand great worlds …
As the most outstanding martial artist of the Jade Pure Sacred Ground, it would be a great achievement if Shen Qing could enter the top one hundred thousand.
These disciples were not the only ones paying attention to Shen Qing. Even the Sacred Lord of the Jade Pure Sacred Ground came to observe from time to time.
The pyramid floated above the large hall, which was the only illusory space in the Jade Pure Sacred Ground.
The operation of the illusory space required a large amount of True Essence.
Usually, True Essence would only be consumed when there were people in the illusory space. However, because of the pyramid, the illusory space needed to constantly replenish True Essence.
In the Upper World, there were two ways to replenish True Essence.
The first method was to use True Essence Stones or Zhen Yuanyu. This method consumed a lot of resources. The more economical method was to let martial artists directly supply True Essence from their bodies.
There were not many things in the universe, but the number of martial artists was extremely large.
The True Essence of martial artists came from the Yuan Qi of Heaven and Earth. As long as a certain number of martial artists gathered, they could continuously absorb the Yuan Qi of Heaven and Earth. After the Yuan Qi was converted into True Essence in the body, it would be supplied to the illusory space to maintain the operation of the illusory space.
This method wasn't only used in the Illusory Space, but also in some large-scale Divine Inscription Formations.
In the back of this grand hall, there were over a thousand people sitting side by side, pouring their true essence into the divine runes below. These divine runes absorbed the true essence of different people and then gathered it together, erasing the martial artist's mark. Finally, the pure true essence was turned into the purest form of true essence that was supplied into the dreamland, maintaining the operation of the dreamland …
If his True Essence was cut off, the illusory space would gradually dissipate, and the pyramid at the top would also disappear.
"Sigh, although we're free from mining, I don't know how long it'll take to absorb True Yuan all day," complained a red-clothed woman.
Ning Yudie sat at the side of the red-clothed girl. Her hands were placed on top of the Divine Rune that was absorbing True Essence. Her expression was calm. "Our strength is weak and we don't have the qualifications to enter the Clear Spirit Martial Hall. Supplying True Essence here is better than mining …"
As an ascender, Ning Yudie had been summoned by a half-step World Lord of the Clear Jade Holy Lands to select a disciple.
At that time, Ning Yudie had a bad premonition. This half-step World Lord had chosen a female disciple; he definitely didn't have any good intentions.
The result was as expected. Although it was incomparably mysterious, in the end it was some sort of male and female dual cultivation technique that was said to be comparable to a transcendent divine might, and would be greatly beneficial to female martial artists …
But the final request caused Ning Yudie to let out a breath of relief. The most important requirement was that she had to maintain her virginity.
She was already a married woman, so she had escaped this calamity.
However, with Ning Yudie's status as an ascender, she should have been forced to work in the mines. But now that she had been brought here, it wasn't convenient for her to go back to mining. In the end, she was sent here to absorb heaven and earth origin energy every day and then pour her True Essence into the divine runes.
This task continued day and night. Although it wouldn't cause any harm to a martial artist's body, it was still extremely boring. It was unknown how long she would have to endure this.
"This Clear Spirit Martial Hall only recruits the most core disciples of the Holy Land … entering is just a dream!" The red-clothed woman curled her lips, "It's better to be taken in as a disciple by that half-step World Lord. In any case, you'll have a chance to stand out in the higher realms! It's a pity that I wasn't chosen! "
Not every martial artist was disgusted by the male and female dual cultivation technique. On the contrary, this was a shortcut for female disciples to rapidly improve.
Hearing this, Ning Yudie curled her lips and lightly said, "My husband, I wonder if he has the qualifications to enter this Clear Spirit Martial Hall …"
"Your husband? Hehe, dream on … think about it, there are countless Divine Sea realm martial artists in this tenth grade Holy Land. Those that can enter are the core of the core, the most outstanding martial artists of the Clear Jade Holy Land … "The red-clothed woman sneered.
"Pa!"
At this moment, a whip shadow suddenly lashed out, exploding beside the red-clothed woman and Ning Yudie!
"If you make any more noise, I'll chase you out of this place and send you to the mine to mine!" A rough voice rang out. Not far away, a fat woman said coldly.
Although the whip didn't hit Ning Yudie and the red-clothed woman, the air waves from the whip were like knives, scraping the two women's faces painfully.
Some martial artists transferred their quintessential essence here in exchange for some benefits. As for Ning Yudie and the other ascenders, they were doing hard labor here …
Ning Yudie lightly glanced at the fat woman, but her eyes were filled with a trace of frost.
Ning Yudie knew that her strength and talent were not worth mentioning in the Upper Realm, so she had always been careful and patient. However, there were always people who would deliberately make things difficult for her, especially this woman, who had been looking for trouble for no reason.
There was no need to visualize the quintessential essence here. Their whispered conversation didn't disturb others, but this fat woman didn't allow it. She was deliberately looking for trouble.
Probably feeling the coldness in Ning Yudie's eyes, the fat woman slowly walked over. She looked down at Ning Yudie and sneered, "How is it? What do you mean by looking at me like that? "
"Nothing." Ning Yudie turned her face away.
"I heard … you ascended to the higher realms to find your husband? Your husband is also an ascender? "The fat woman asked. She didn't know where she heard this from.
Ning Yudie's expression was cold. She didn't want to speak.
"How childish. The universe is so big. Who knows where your husband has ascended to? Besides, he's just a nobody. I'm afraid he's still mining in some sacred land's mine! "The fat woman deliberately mentioned this to ridicule her.
However, if it was her, it was possible.
The efficiency of a Divine Sea Realm martial artist was naturally much faster than the average person. However, it wasn't easy to complete a mission that required millions of top-grade quintessential essence stones.
It was normal for some Divine Sea Realm martial artists to spend decades or even centuries digging.
Ning Yudie's heart trembled slightly. Her expression didn't change, but her heart was filled with helplessness …
This woman ridiculed her, but her logic might be correct. Previously, she thought that finding Luo Zheng was as difficult as finding a needle in a haystack. However, it seemed that finding a needle in a haystack was too easy. Finding information about Luo Zheng in the universe was thousands of times harder than finding a needle in a haystack!
The fat woman left with a cold smile …
Ning Yudie mechanically transferred her quintessential essence into the divine pattern. Her eyes gradually filled with sorrow.
However, at this moment, a vigorous voice suddenly sounded in her ears!
"Hehe, we have 16 people in the top 100 of [Gods Without Thought]. It's worth celebrating, worth celebrating …"
The voice that suddenly sounded in her ears gave Ning Yudie a fright.
In the days after she ascended, everyone in the universe had been busy paying attention to the Illusory Battlefield. No one had made a sound in the universe. This was the first time that Ning Yudie had heard the mysterious sound of the Roaring Command.
"This sound … what's going on?" Ning Yudie's face was filled with curiosity.
"It's the sound of the Roaring Command," a martial artist beside her lowered his voice and told Ning Yudie, "This Roaring Command can transmit its sound to the entire universe …" This martial artist wasn't an ascender. He had been forced to enter this place to supply his quintessential essence for other reasons.
"Roaring Command?" Ning Yudie frowned as she thought about it. She felt that these three words sounded very familiar, as if she had heard them before, but after thinking about it for a while, she couldn't recall where she had heard them before. However, a light flashed across her eyes. If she could get this Roaring Token, wouldn't she be able to tell her husband where she was?
If Luo Zheng knew where she was, he would definitely set out to find her!
"This Roaring Command is so mysterious. How many quintessential essence stones can I buy it?" Ning Yudie asked.
For this ascension, Ning Yudie had made thorough preparations. She still had a lot of quintessential essence stones in her hands, about 200,000 top-grade quintessential essence stones.
She didn't expect that as soon as she asked, the martial artist would look at her like she was an idiot. "How many quintessential essence stones? Hehe … "
Ning Yudie didn't know why this martial artist was laughing.

Chapter 1265
Looking at Ning Yudie's puzzled expression, the warrior said lightly, "The value of this Roaring Token can't be measured by Zhen Yuanyu. Ordinary warriors can't buy it. If you really want to buy it, I think it's worth at least a million Zhen Yuanyu, right?"
"A million Zhen Yuanyu!"
Although Ning Yudie had just ascended, she had heard that mining in the Holy Land required one to pay a hundred Zhen Yuanyu.
One Zhen Yuanyu could be exchanged for ten thousand supreme-grade Zhen Yuanyu.
Then, a million Zhen Yuanyu, wouldn't that be a hundred million supreme-grade Zhen Yuanyu?
Since the Cloud Hall and the Commerce Alliance joined forces to unify the Central Domain, her master had returned to the Cloud Hall. Now, the Cloud Hall was flourishing. Under the joint operation of the Commerce Alliance, the number of Zhen Yuanyu in the hall was not small.
For her ascension this time, her master, Granny Yu, had given her half of the supreme-grade Zhen Yuanyu stored in the Cloud Hall in case she needed it.
This half of the supreme-grade Zhen Yuanyu was worth more than two hundred thousand.
Just now, she was still considering whether to buy two Roaring Tokens. Now that she heard this number, she was shocked.
It turned out that the supreme-grade Zhen Yuanyu that she brought up was nothing in the Upper Realm …
In fact, when the warrior said that the Roaring Token was worth a hundred million supreme-grade Zhen Yuanyu, he was exaggerating the value of the Roaring Token by ten times.
The warrior couldn't be blamed. After all, the Roaring Token was a priceless item in the Upper Realm. Ordinary warriors could only see it once in their lifetime. Basically, only Realm Lords and Heavenly Venerables were qualified to use it. The warrior said that a million Zhen Yuanyu was just a rough estimate.
"Of course, even the Holy Lords and Realm Lords of the Jade Pure Holy Land only have one or two in their hands." The warrior shook his head and sighed. Compared to the Holy Lords of the Holy Land, warriors like them were like grass.
Ning Yudie pursed her lips, her face revealing a trace of melancholy. She wasn't counting on the Roaring Command, but she felt that it was somewhat familiar, as if she had heard of the Roaring Command somewhere before …
That warrior then sighed, "There is always an anomaly in this universe. A few years ago, there was a guy named Luo Zheng who used this Roaring Token to tell stories all day long. It is said that he was only at the Spirit Sea Realm. I don't know where he got this Roaring Token that can be used infinitely …"
Hearing this, Ning Yudie was stunned.
"Luo Zheng?" Ning Yudie stared at the martial artist and asked. She couldn't help but raise her voice.
The other martial practitioners around her also turned to look at Ning Yudie, including the red-clothed woman beside Ning Yudie.
Fortunately, that fat woman wasn't here. Otherwise, he would have been taught a lesson …
"Sister Die, what's wrong?" the red-clothed woman asked when she saw Ning Yudie's expression.
As for that martial artist, he chuckled and said, "That's right. That guy is in the limelight now. I'm afraid there is no one in this universe who doesn't know him. It is said that this guy ascended from the Lower Realm and has an Immortal Mansion. He used that Immortal Mansion to kill several Celestial Venerables …"
In the eyes of many grassroots martial artists, Luo Zheng had become a legendary figure.
Because Luo Zheng's identity as an ascender, it also meant that Luo Zheng didn't have much of a background in the Upper Realm. However, as a Spirit Sea Realm martial artist, many forces and Celestial Venerables were helpless against him. This in itself was a miracle!
Luo Zheng's anecdotes were also constantly being spread among grassroots martial artists. Among them, many exaggerated and even fabricated identities emerged.
"In the past few years, that guy has told many pleasant stories. For example, Su San killed the Snake Celestial, for example …" When the martial artist said this, his face was full of fascination, as if he himself was Luo Zheng.
When Ning Yudie heard the two words "Luo Zheng," her first reaction was that she had misheard. Her second reaction was that she thought it was someone with the same name.
After all, there were so many martial artists in the universe. It wasn't strange for them to have the same name.
However, when she heard about the Immortal Mansion, Ning Yudie was more certain in her heart. After Luo Zheng obtained the "Gen" token, he was indeed able to freely enter and exit the Immortal Mansion. However, although the Immortal Mansion was incomparably magical, could it kill Celestial Venerables?
In the past six years, Ning Yudie had also gone to the valley in the Eastern Region. However, she found that the exit had disappeared …
However, when she heard the story of "Su San killed the Snake Celestial," she was certain!
This was a story that had been passed down in the Eastern Region. Ning Yudie had also heard of it before. It was widely circulated in the Eastern Region.
Luo Zheng, with an Immortal Mansion, told the story of Su San killing the Snake Celestial …
When these pieces of information overlapped, she was almost 100% certain that Luo Zheng was her husband.
The Roaring Token!
At this moment, Ning Yudie recalled that one of the three treasures that Luo Zheng had received from his master was the Roaring Token.
However, at that time, even Luo Zheng himself wasn't clear about the use of the Roaring Token!
The woman in red saw that Ning Yudie's expression was becoming more and more complicated. She asked again, "Sister Die, what happened to you?"
Ning Yudie pursed her lips …
At this moment, a clamor came from the main hall in front of them.
All the martial artists stared at the huge pyramid.
"This Luo Zheng actually jumped from over 9,000 to 130!"
"What a powerful strength! I'm afraid this guy is going to reach the top!"
"Not necessarily. The higher he goes, the more difficult it will be. There are many geniuses in front of him. How can it be so easy to surpass them?"
The martial artist who spoke to Ning Yudie just now laughed and looked at the pyramid in front of them. He said, "It's that Luo Zheng. Previously, it was said that this guy was just lucky and inherited a magical Immortal Mansion. His strength and talent are a mess. Now, Luo Zheng is rising rapidly and has already reached 130. It's really surprising. I think this guy will be amazing if he can enter the top 10,000 …"
Luo Zheng's name …
Although the distance was not close, Ning Yudie could still see the densely packed names at the top of the pyramid.
At first, she had searched along the top of the pyramid, but she couldn't find Luo Zheng's name. Then she gave up.
Since the pyramid represented the ranking of all the Divine Sea Realm martial artists in the whole universe, and there were hundreds of millions of martial artists in the Illusory Battlefield, the possibility of finding Luo Zheng's name among them was not high.
Now, Ning Yudie glanced around and found Luo Zheng's name at the top of the pyramid. The two golden words Luo Zheng were slowly floating somewhere at the top of the pyramid …
"Didn't I hear that there are hundreds of millions of Divine Sea Realm martial artists in the Illusory Battlefield?" Ning Yudie suddenly asked, "With his ranking, he should be able to enter the Qingling Martial Hall to cultivate, right?"
"Pfft …" The martial artist couldn't help laughing. After looking up and down at Ning Yudie, he asked again, "You're an ascender?"
Ning Yudie nodded. Although she knew that there were hundreds of thousands of Great Realms in the Upper Realm, she didn't have a thorough understanding of them. She could see that the tenth-grade Qingling Sacred Ground was also a colossus!
The martial artist said, "No wonder. For someone like Luo Zheng, let alone the Qingling Sacred Ground, I'm afraid that even the famous Thirteen Palaces won't be able to accommodate him. It's said that after he ascended, he directly left the tenth-grade Holy Land and was brought to the Thirteen Palaces. Now that he has achieved such a ranking, I'm afraid he can already be considered a prodigy of our Human Alliance …"
"Is that so?" Ning Yudie's face revealed a trace of a smile.
When they were in the Central Domain, Ning Yudie had absolute confidence in her husband.
However, it was different in the Upper Realm where prodigies were everywhere. She didn't dare to have too much hope for Luo Zheng …
However, Ning Yudie never expected that Luo Zheng would have achieved so much in the past six years. The information she had just received was far beyond her imagination. It also far exceeded her expectations for Luo Zheng. At the same time, it also gave Ning Yudie the confidence to find Luo Zheng!
"Sister Die, why are you asking about the details? Do you know this person?" The woman in red beside her asked in confusion.
Ning Yudie smiled and looked at the pyramid. She said, "Did you forget what I told you? I ascended to find my husband."
The woman in red still had a strange expression on her face. "I know. So what?"
Ning Yudie said indifferently, "My husband ascended seven years ago. His name is Luo Zheng. If I'm not wrong, he's the person at the top of the pyramid!"

Chapter 1266
The red-clothed woman curled her lips. "Coincidence …"
This red-robed woman was a cultivator who ascended at about the same time as Ning Yudie, and the two of them could be considered fellow sufferers. Previously, when she heard that Ning Yudie ascended to the Immortal World to find her husband, she had advised Ning Yudie that the hope was too slim to even imagine and that it was better to give up.
Now that she suddenly heard this answer, how could she believe it?
The universe was so big, and there should be many cultivators with the same name.
However, she didn't know that Ning Yudie had completely confirmed Luo Zheng's identity.
Ning Yudie stared at the cultivator and said, "The story of Su San killing the Snake Immortal is a story that has been passed down in my Central Domain. Su San met the Snake Immortal in Bramble Mountain and fell in love with her. Later, the Snake Immortal's bestial nature couldn't be erased and caused harm to the world. In the end, Su San had no choice but to kill the Snake Immortal … This story should be like this, right?"
The cultivator suddenly nodded and looked at Ning Yudie with a look of trust!
The story of Su San killing the Snake Immortal was spread widely because of Luo Zheng's roar command. It was exactly the same as what the woman in front of him said.
And this story should be three or four years old.
This woman had just ascended, but she had heard of this story. At the very least, it showed that she and Luo Zheng did indeed come from the same lower realm.
This possibility …
In fact, Ning Yudie didn't want to prove anything to them.
She only knew that her husband was still alive, and she was already very satisfied to know where her husband was.
"This guy is still as unexpected as ever!" Ning Yudie looked at the countless names on the pyramid and thought.
As for the cultivator, after hearing these words, his heart was burning with passion.
This cultivator wasn't an ascender, and he didn't have any great background in the Jade Pure Holy Land. In order to earn a certain amount of compensation, he was arranged here to supply true essence to the dreamscape space.
It didn't matter if the woman in front of him was lying, but now it seemed that there was a fifty percent chance that it was true. If it was true, if he could help this woman … for a grassroots martial artist like him, it might be an opportunity!
Ning Yudie did not know what the martial artist in front of her was thinking. She thought for a moment and then asked, "But why did Luo Zheng use the Roaring Command to tell stories in the universe?"
"Heh, he wants to talk to a mysterious girl." The martial artist laughed. The conversation between Luo Zheng and the mysterious girl could be heard by all living beings in the universe, and this martial artist was no exception.
"Mysterious girl?" Ning Yudie's brows furrowed slightly as if it was a conditioned reflex.
The martial artist noticed Ning Yudie's expression and shrugged, "I don't know where that mysterious girl came from. The entire Huan Yu doesn't know her identity, but she did promise Luo Zheng to help him find his sister … in exchange, she told him to tell her a story." This matter was known by almost everyone in Huan Yu.
"His sister, Luo Yan?" Ning Yudie asked.
"Yes!" The martial artist's eyes suddenly lit up, and he became more certain in his heart, "It's this Luo Yan!"
"So they haven't found her yet …" Ning Yudie slightly lowered her head and said softly. Then she stared at the pyramid again.
She was interested in the pyramid at the top of the dreamscape space, so she looked down from above to observe the names on it.
These names were all Luo Zheng's competitors.
While she was observing, another sigh came from the other side of the hall …
"Luo Zheng's ranking actually rose by two places!"
Ning Yudie had a smile on her face. She swept past Luo Zheng's name and continued to look down, but not long after, she saw another familiar name.
Xi Youqin!
One hundred and sixty-fourth place!
When Ning Yudie saw this name, she was stunned again.
She actually had such strength?
Although Ning Yudie knew this girl's Extreme Violet Yin Body was very special, when she was in the Central Domain, Xi Youqin was only a Divine Core warrior, and without relying on her bloodline, her strength was a complete mess!
After that, this girl escaped into the Extreme Violet Realm.
How many years had it been?
She had already broken through to the Divine Sea realm? And she had such strength? She was almost the same as Luo Zheng?
How was this possible …
Ning Yudie couldn't accept this result.
But there were still some people in this world who had extraordinary talent.
Ning Yudie was indeed a genius with great talent, but compared to those Divine Sea geniuses from the Upper Realm, the gap was still very obvious.
But Xi Youqin was different.
Her First Grade Extreme Violet Yin Body was one of the supreme existences in the Extreme Violet Realm, and with the help of Old Man Wuding from the Wuding Tower, her cultivation and strength had improved at an incredible speed. With the ability of the First Grade Extreme Violet Yin Body, there were some abilities that others might not be able to cultivate in their lifetime, but for Xi Youqin, they were just a piece of cake!
Looking at Xi Youqin's ranking, Ning Yudie felt quite depressed. She couldn't help but sigh. How could this be …
… …
… …
After obtaining the reward from the third Karma Hall, Luo Zheng's ranking rose once again, but only by two places.
The reward from the third Karma Hall was only three million dreamy light spots, and everyone had one share. For Luo Zheng, the reward from the third Karma Hall was much less than the second Karma Hall. But, if the dreamy light spots from the Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao were considered as the reward, then it was still quite a lot …
After leaving the Karma Hall, the hooks in everyone's hands that were attached to the Laws of Heavenly Dao gradually disappeared.
It seemed that the hooks weren't allowed to be taken out of the Karma Hall. Otherwise, the Laws of Heavenly Dao contained in the hooks would have been of great help to everyone.
Regretfully, everyone continued to climb up the stairs.
Now, everyone was in the upper middle section of this majestic mountain. Standing on the stairs, they could enjoy the scenery outside the mountain.
Although this Karma Hall was constructed by the Heavenly Dao, everything was vivid and lifelike. Perhaps these scenes weren't illusions, but completely real existences. For the Heavenly Dao, this wasn't difficult.
At this moment, Luo Zheng took the lead, and Ji Nan, Jin Hai, Huan Ling, and the others followed closely behind.
After easily passing through the third Karma Hall, they were about to face the fourth Karma Hall, which was also the penultimate Karma Hall!
When Ji Nan first entered the Karma Hall, she was defeated in the fourth Karma Hall.
It could even be said that the penultimate Karma Hall had blocked almost 99% of the cultivators. It was really too difficult to pass through. Who knew what they would encounter in the fourth Karma Hall?
Soon after, everyone stood in front of the fourth Karma Hall …
The fourth Karma Hall was completely red, and there were faint flames swirling around it. Standing at the entrance of the hall, one could feel the scorching heat.
"So hot! So hot!" Azure Fiend cried out strangely.
"The rewards in the fourth Karma Hall are rich, but the difficulty cannot be underestimated. Everyone must be more careful!" Ji Nan lightly said.
"No matter how difficult it is, we can't give up now," Luo Zheng said.
At this moment, Luo Zheng's ranking was rapidly rising. Without hesitation, he took the lead and entered the Karma Hall. As for Ji Nan, Jin Hai, and the others, after sizing him up, they also followed closely behind.
The floor of the Karma Hall was covered with fine cracks. These cracks were only as thick as a finger, but within the cracks, there were streams of greenish-red magma that flowed. It looked like the blood vessels of a human body, and the greenish-red magma was equivalent to the blood of a human body.
Walking on the floor, one would also be roasted by the magma.
As for Ji Nan, Huan Ling, and the others, although they weren't injured, the heat was still unbearable. They all released their protective true essence and completely protected themselves.
Luo Zheng naturally didn't need this kind of protection. Ordinary flames couldn't harm him at all.
As the group continued to head deeper, the scenery of the Karma Hall kept on changing. It was as if they had walked into a huge volcanic cavern. They were no longer surrounded by the hall's walls but jagged, protruding rocks of irregular shapes. There was also a break in the path before them, and a lava lake appeared before them.
Looking at the tranquil magma lake, everyone stopped in their tracks one after another. Their eyes were filled with caution.

Chapter 1267
Previously, every time this group of cultivators entered the hall, everyone's gazes would be focused on Ji Nan.
After all, she was the only one who was familiar with the Karma Hall. Now, everyone understood that Luo Tianxing was their core!
Luo Zheng stared at the mountain-like giant and frowned for a long time. Everyone also stared at Luo Zheng, wanting to know what he planned to do next.
"What is this thing …" Luo Zheng rubbed his head and asked.
Ji Nan suddenly looked as if she was about to faint. This guy didn't know this lava giant at all …
As for Jin Hai, Huan Ling, and the others, they also showed helpless smiles.
Only Cang Mo didn't know what was going on. His bell-like eyes stared at the lava giant and said in a muffled voice, "This thing's strength is probably much greater than mine!"
"This is a life form of the Laws, a lava giant born from the Laws. It contains the Laws of Earth and the Laws of Fire. Such a huge lava giant can only be born from the Laws of Fire at the sixth level," Ji Nan said indifferently.
Life forms of the Laws weren't rare.
For example, Thunder Spirits were born from dense thunderbolts, and Flame Essences were formed from inextinguishable flames.
However, these were all relatively low-level life forms of the Laws.
High-level life forms of the Laws required a higher level of the Laws of Origin before they could be gradually cultivated.
For example, the golden wind blade in the giant storm that Luo Zheng encountered in the Chaotic Astral Ocean belonged to a relatively high-level life form of the Laws of Wind.
At this moment, everyone was looking for a way to pass through the lava lake.
Cang Mo moved his tall and sturdy body and walked two laps around the edge of the lava lake. Suddenly, he saw the surface of the lava lake fluctuate, spreading out fiery red ripples.
"What is this …" Cang Mo stared at the ripples, a strange look on his face.
"Gudong, gudong …"
At this moment, small lava bubbles appeared in the lava lake. After these lava bubbles burst, spiraling flames floated out. These flames danced in the air and then stopped spinning.
At this moment, everyone could clearly see that these balls of flames were actually small flame kittens about a foot long.
These little flaming kittens floating on the surface of the lake looked simple and docile. Their faint cat-like eyes glanced at everyone.
After floating for a while, they dived back into the magma lake. Magma bubbles kept bursting, and flaming kittens kept coming out of the bubbles …
"This little thing is quite cute." For some reason, Cang Mo stretched out a finger and waved it at the little flame cat.
"Meow …"
The small flame cat floating above the lava let out a low growl at Cang Mo. It slowly floated towards Cang Mo.
However, this little flame kitten's movement speed was extremely slow. Its four limbs glided clumsily in the air, and its speed was much slower than an ordinary person's walking speed.
However, just as the flaming kitten glided halfway, everyone heard a shrill meow!
"Meow!"
The originally docile flaming kitten suddenly swelled. In an instant, its entire body swelled to more than ten times its original size, and it pounced towards Cang Mo!
The flaming kitten originally gave off a docile and obedient feeling, but now it gave off a strong sense of oppression. The might of the Fire Laws contained within was so strong that it made it difficult for people to breathe normally. He felt as though a small sun had appeared behind him.
Cang Mo completely relied on his conditioned reflexes to turn and run. As he ran, there was a sizzling sound behind him.
The heat from the flaming kitten was transmitted to his armor. The armor on his back did not melt, but the heat was enough to burn his armor until it was boiling red!
"This flame … everyone, disperse!"
Everyone also realized that the flaming kitten was so powerful that they all retreated.
"Help!" Cang Mo also hurriedly called for help. The flaming kitten had been following Cang Mo all the way, and he could not shake it off. If the flaming kitten caught up to him, he would probably be reduced to ashes in an instant!
"Help him." Although Ji Nan and the others chose to retreat, it did not mean that they would abandon Cang Mo here.
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh!"
Jin Hai raised his hand and slapped out three icicles. Jin Hai's attainments in the Law of Ice were not low, but before the three icicles could touch the flaming kitten, they turned into a handful of water and were instantly evaporated by the powerful heat!
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng frowned slightly. He suddenly erupted with Vital Essence, and after accelerating his body, he rushed over from the other side. With a slight tilt of his body, he raised his leg and kicked Cang Mo's back armor!
The power of Luo Zheng's kick was not weak. He directly kicked Cang Mo out of the Karma Hall!
After being kicked, Cang Mo's speed more than doubled. After he rushed out of the Karma Hall, the flaming kitten slowly floated back. It did not choose to attack Luo Zheng and the others, but instead jumped and merged into the magma again …
"This flaming kitten … should be the pure power of fire born from the seventh level of the Law of Fire." Huan Ling took a step back, stared at the flying kittens, and whispered, "In other words, the Law of Fire contained in this magma lake is probably not the sixth level, but at least the seventh!"
Among the cultivators present, only Huan Ling majored in the Law of Fire, and only she had the right to speak.
"The seventh level of the Law of Fire! No wonder these kittens gave me so much pressure. "Ji Nan had said that the two Source Laws contained in the Lava Giant should be around the sixth level. She had indeed underestimated it.
"Who cares what level it is? The problem is how do we pass through it now!" Cang Mo stared at the huge magma lake and asked.
There was no bridge or rope in this magma lake. It was a dead end …
The key point was that this was not ordinary magma. With the strength of a Divine Sea Realm cultivator, they could directly swim across ordinary volcanic magma. How could ordinary magma break through their Protective True Essence?
But now, the power of the Law of Fire contained in the magma lake was far from what they could resist.
This was also in line with the rules of the Karma Hall. Starting from the first Karma Hall, it was basically impossible to survive any danger. The only time was when Luo Zheng used the Little Skulls to flip over the square boulder. That was also because the Little Skulls behind Luo Zheng contained power that was almost comparable to the Law of Heavenly Dao. Therefore, they were able to resist and even compete with the Law of Heavenly Dao itself.
Standing at the edge of the magma lake, Luo Zheng retreated a few steps as he sensed the intense heat. His eyes constantly scanned the surroundings of the cave, trying to find the secret to passing through the magma lake … …
