Chapter 1268
There were many craggy stone pillars and stalagmites protruding from the stone walls on both sides of the cave.
However, they couldn't form a path. It was obviously unrealistic to climb along these stone walls …
After observing for a while, Luo Zheng's face revealed a gloomy expression.
The third Karma Hall was still okay. At least there would be some hints. For example, those long hooks placed on the side in advance could at least let people understand a thing or two.
However, the fourth Karma Hall didn't have any hints. It just threw out a lava lake that was a hopeless place for Divine Sea Realm warriors. How could they pass through?
In the end, Luo Zheng's gaze fell on the huge lava giant.
There were countless cracks on the lava giant's huge body. From those cracks, one could faintly see flames spurting out.
Seeing this lava giant, Luo Zheng suddenly had an idea …
"Maybe the path through this lava lake is on this lava giant's body," Luo Zheng said indifferently.
Ji Nan smiled, "What a coincidence. I also thought of that."
On one side of the lava lake, there was a lava giant the size of a mountain. A few hundred feet away, there was another lava giant. These lava giants were arranged in a straight line. It was reasonable to see these lava giants as bridges to pass through the lava lake.
"But how do we persuade these guys to send us over?" Jin Hai asked while staring at the lava giant after hearing the conversation between the two.
The two's idea was indeed too crazy. However, under everyone's observation, the more they looked at it, the more it seemed to be the case …
"Attract that guy's attention and lead it here!" At this point, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. He had already taken the dark golden longbow in his hand. Then, he took out a long black arrow from his quiver and nocked it on the bowstring.
No one stopped Luo Zheng's actions. However, their expressions became serious. It was not a good idea to annoy this big guy. However, if they didn't take the risk, their trip to the Karma Hall would end here.
"Buzz …"
As Luo Zheng's bowstring made a sound, the black arrow left the bowstring and flew toward the lava giant!
"Sou!"
When the black light flew halfway, it once again released an explosive sound midway. It had undergone a second acceleration. Its speed was even faster and its power was even greater!
"Bang …"
After a short while, the black light exploded on the Lava Giant's chest! It was impossible for Luo Zheng to miss even if he wanted to …
At this moment, everyone, including Luo Zheng, held their breaths and stared motionlessly at the Lava Giant. If anything went wrong, everyone would run away. The remaining seven people had earned 5 million fantasy points from this trip to the Karma Hall. If they died here, their losses would be even greater.
However …
After a few breaths of time, the giant monster was still slowly rotating. Although Luo Zheng's arrow had caused quite a commotion, it didn't cause any damage to the Lava Giant. It simply ignored Luo Zheng's attack!
"It's useless," Luo Zheng curled his lips and turned his head to look at Phantom.
The bow in Phantom's hand was a Sovereign artifact. Its power couldn't be compared to the one in Luo Zheng's hand. Now, he could only pin his hopes on her.
She understood Luo Zheng's gaze. Before Luo Zheng could speak, she had already taken out the Moonbreak Bursting Flame Bow and aimed at the Lava Giant not far away.
Pull the bowstring, draw the bow …
Soon after, a rotating flame condensed in front of the bow.
"Pop!"
It was accompanied by the sound of the bowstring vibrating.
This intense flame also shot towards the Lava Giant ….
"Rumble …"
After the flame exploded, it spread out on the surface of the Lava Giant and burned fiercely.
The Moonbreak Bursting Flame Bow activated the Profound Meaning of Flame Burst, continuously bursting out balls of powerful flames …
However, this Lava Giant was originally a high-level Law Being born from the Laws of Fire and the Laws of Earth. These flames were absolutely fatal to other life forms, but they couldn't pose the slightest threat to this Lava Giant.
The Lava Giant was even constantly absorbing the flames. However, after the flames exploded, they spread along the cracks on the surface of the Lava Giant's body. Soon after, all the flames were absorbed by the Lava Giant.
"It's useless," Phantom shook her head and frowned slightly.
However, at this moment, everyone suddenly saw a large stone flying over their heads and smashing toward the Lava Giant!
Turning their heads, they saw Azure Demon maintaining his throwing posture. Azure Demon chuckled, "I also want to try …"
However, the Azure Devil found a three foot wide rock from who knows where and threw it out.
"This rock of yours is just a small pebble to the Lava Giant. What use can it have?" Jin Hai said disdainfully. Previously, Jin Hai had threatened to kill Azure Demon. Along the way, he didn't like Azure Demon. Firstly, Azure Demon was a Demon Race cultivator. Secondly, Jin Hai felt that Azure Demon was useless …
Azure Demon only smiled foolishly and pointed at the stone, saying, "It hit!"
"Bang …"
Everyone looked back and saw the large stone smash into the Lava Giant's shoulder and instantly shatter into pieces.
Just as Jin Hai said, such a large stone was just a small stone to the Lava Giant's mountainous body. What threat could it pose to the Lava Giant?
Jin Hai snorted disdainfully. Just as he was about to mock Azure Demon, the Lava Giant's body trembled slightly and it began to move.
"It can't be, it wants to chase me again? I was just joining in on the fun … "
Upon seeing this scene, Azure Demon was stunned. He did not expect that he could really trigger the Lava Giant. It was just an ordinary rock thrown over. Why was it chasing after him?
Luo Zheng, Jin Hai, Ji Nan, and the others all had baffled expressions on their faces …
Just now, Luo Zheng and Phantom's attacks had no effect on the Lava Giant. The Lava Giant was like an inanimate object, motionless. Perhaps in the eyes of the Lava Giant, Luo Zheng and the others were just mosquitoes, not enough to cause it any harm, so it didn't pay any attention to them.
But why did Azure Demon's stone have such an effect?
"Azure Demon, where did you get that stone from … where is he?" Ji Nan turned around and only saw Azure Demon's back. This Demon Race cultivator had already fled, rushing out of the Karma Hall. Obviously, he was afraid of the Lava Giant's pursuit!
Jin Hai snorted, "This guy was born in the wrong body!"
Although the Demon Race cultivators were not favored among the many races in the universe, this race had always been brave and good at fighting. They would even risk their lives to win. This was the greatest advantage of the Demon Race.
It was rare to see a Demon Race cultivator like Azure Demon running away at the slightest movement …
"Look, this Lava Giant shouldn't have been alarmed by Azure Demon's stone," Phantom suddenly said.
Everyone looked over and saw a large number of flame kittens floating above the magma in front of the Lava Giant. At this moment, the Lava Giant twisted its body and stretched out its giant hand to grab those flame kittens!
"Meow! Meow … "
Although these flame kittens were extremely agile, under the cover of the Lava Giant's huge palm, it was difficult for them to escape. Only a few flame kittens managed to escape, but even more flame kittens were firmly bound in the Lava Giant's hand.
After the Lava Giant caught a large number of flame kittens in its hand, it stuffed them into its magma-formed mouth.
"Boom, boom, boom …"
Under the Lava Giant's chewing, the little flame kittens let out mournful cries and exploded into balls of flames that danced wildly in the Lava Giant's mouth. Although the little flame kittens weren't very big, the power they erupted with before they died made everyone secretly shocked.
After seeing this scene, Ji Nan's pretty eyes flashed, "I understand, these flame kittens should be the Lava Giant's food! It feeds on these little things! "
"In other words, if we want to attract the Lava Giant's attention, we can only rely on these flame kittens …" Luo Zheng said lightly.

Chapter 1269
These flaming kittens did not look big, but since they were creatures born in this magma lake, they were also extremely terrifying creatures of the laws.
Otherwise, they would not be qualified to be the Lava Giant's food!
It could even be said that these flaming kittens were originally the Lava Giant's companion, but it was unknown why they had taken the shape of a cat.
Luo Zheng said that it was a good idea to use these flaming kittens to attract the Lava Giant's attention.
The problem was that these flaming kittens were definitely not to be trifled with …
In the magma not far away, a ripple was transmitted, and a magma bubble emerged. After it burst open, a flaming kitten emerged. The flaming kitten meowed and looked very cute.
Luo Zheng and the others stared at the flaming kitten.
Capturing the flaming kitten first and then attracting the Lava Giant was indeed very difficult.
But this was the fourth Karma Hall, after all, so it was normal for it to be so difficult.
"I can try, but I can't guarantee success," Ji Nan said indifferently as she stared at the flaming kitten. Then, with a flip of her hand, a piece of sacred wood appeared in her hand.
She mainly cultivated the Law of Wood, but she also dabbled in the Law of Ice …
The small piece of sacred wood kept twisting and growing crazily, and a layer of frost gradually appeared on its branches. Finally, the branches intertwined to form a small cage.
"The Builder Wood is omnipotent. It can melt fire and ice …"
Ji Nan did not bring the Ji Family's Supreme Artifact into the Illusory Battlefield this time.
It was not that the Ji Family did not have it, but Ji Nan did not need it.
There was a piece of wood in her body, and this piece of wood was her weapon. It was an existence comparable to a Supreme Artifact.
This piece of wood was called the Builder Wood.
It had the shape of an ox, and its skin was like a tassel, like a yellow snake.
Its leaves were like a luo, but it was actually like a luan.
Its wood was like a luan, and it was called the "Builder Wood".
According to legend, the Builder Wood was born in the Divine Territory. It was hundreds of meters tall and had the fate of all gods!
This piece of Builder Wood had been in her body since she was born. The Ji Family originally wanted to take it out. After all, this item was extraordinary. Even for the Ji Family, it was a rare sacred item. If it was forged with this piece of wood, it would be enough to create a Supreme Divine Weapon that was ranked in the top 500.
However, when the Ji Family tried to extract the Creation Wood from Ji Nan's body, they realized that Ji Nan seemed to have a special connection with the wood.
This Builder Tree had a spirit and refused to leave Ji Nan. When the Ji family forcibly extracted it, there was a natural phenomenon that day. A twelve-colored dragon-shaped lightning bolt streaked across the sky and shattered the entire Ji family's mourning hall …
Under such a phenomenon, the Ji family gave up on extracting the Builder Wood from Ji Nan's body …
As Ji Nan grew up, so did the Builder Tree. It had become a weapon that she could not part with.
As for why the Builder Tree was born in Ji Nan's body and why it would cause such a phenomenon when it was forcefully extracted, the Ji family had always kept these matters a secret. Even Ji Nan herself did not know what was involved in this matter.
"Using ice to counter fire, I can try to trap that little flame cat inside! But … who's going to lure that little flame cat over? "Ji Nan asked.

"Me? I'm not going! "Cang Mo's huge head shook like a rattle-drum.

"I'll do it." Luo Zheng stepped forward.
Although the fire laws in the Karma Hall were extraordinary, Luo Zheng had a divine artifact body after all. Although Luo Zheng was not sure if he could withstand the burning flames, he still had a considerable advantage compared to Jin Hai and the others.
Luo Zheng was the core of their team. Now that he had stepped forward, Jin Hai did not have any objections.
Next, everyone retreated a distance of about ten feet. Ji Nan waved her slender fingers and the Builder Tree fell to the ground. The Frost Cage woven from the branches and leaves of the Builder Tree was emitting a biting cold air, causing the temperature within a radius of ten feet to drop by a lot.
After the ice cage was set up, Ji Nan and the others retreated to the side.
Luo Zheng strolled toward the edge of the magma lake. As he walked, he lightly kicked the ground with the tip of his foot, and a piece of gravel on the ground flew high into the air and landed in Luo Zheng's hand. He held the small stone and stared straight ahead.
The bottom of the magma lake was probably covered with these little flame kittens.
As the magma bubbles exploded one by one, these nimble little spirits could be seen emerging from the magma. After playing around for a while, they dived back into the magma.

Not far to the side of Luo Zheng, another small magma bubble exploded, and a little flame kitten emerged from it.
"Meow …"

"Swish!"
Luo Zheng's fingers trembled slightly, and the gravel in his hand shot toward the little flame kitten!
The gravel had just gotten close to the little flame kitten when it was melted halfway. However, this gravel had already attracted the little flame kitten's attention.
At this moment, everyone held their breaths and stared at the distant Luo Zheng.
Ji Nan pursed her lips. Although the Builder Wood was a divine tool, her current cultivation was limited. She did not know if the ice cage formed by the Builder Wood could restrain it.
"Meow …"
The little flame kitten let out a soft cry. Its cat eyes glanced indifferently at Luo Zheng, and with a tumble above the magma, it sank into the magma!
"This …"
Luo Zheng's face revealed an expression as if he had seen a ghost.
As for Cang Mo, he wanted to express that he had seen a ghost even more! How could there be such a huge gap between martial artists? Cang Mo had not intended to provoke the little flame kitten, but the little flame kitten had pursued him relentlessly. However, the little flame kitten in front of him was extremely docile and did not even bother to pay attention to Luo Zheng.
It seemed that not only were these creatures of law intelligent, but they also had their own personalities …
The first one had failed, so they could only look for the next one.
Fortunately, there were many of these little flame kittens. Not long after, another little flame kitten floated up from the magma.
This time, Luo Zheng did not show any mercy. As he circulated the Primal Chaos Qi in his body, three sharp icicles condensed in his hand. With a flip of his hand, the three icicles shot towards the little flame kitten!
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh!"
The three icicles shot out, instantly alerting the little flame kitten.
It rolled, and the flame fur all over its body rose sharply. At the same time, it let out a shrill cry.
"Meow!"
Although Luo Zheng did not major in the Law of Ice, he cultivated all of his origin laws at the same time. Even if the icicles driven by the Primal Chaos Qi did not contain other profundities, their power was definitely not ordinary!
However, the little flame kitten did not dodge and directly crashed into the seemingly soft body …
Before the icicles could pierce into the flaming kitten's body, they turned into water vapor in the air and dissipated in the dry cave in the blink of an eye!
The little flame kitten's temper was clearly very irritable. After the icicles dissipated, the little flame kitten's momentum did not weaken in the slightest. The flames on its body burned brighter and brighter as it rushed towards the culprit, Luo Zheng!
"Retreat!"
Because he had already made arrangements, Luo Zheng calmly began to retreat in an orderly manner.
After the little flame kitten rushed ashore, it rapidly grew from the size of an ordinary kitten to the size of a leopard. How was this a fire kitten? It was a fire leopard!
Seeing this scene in the distance, Ji Nan raised her eyebrows slightly and waved her finger. The Builder Wood on the ground began to sway and the Builder Wood began to grow again. The Frost Cage she condensed with the Builder Wood was too small. Seeing the size of the flame kitten skyrocketing, she quickly adjusted the size of the Frost Cage.
"Meow!"
The little flame kitten chased Luo Zheng all the way from behind.
The speed of the man and the kitten was extremely fast!
The distance of ten zhang was covered in an instant. Luo Zheng's figure quickly swept past the Frost Cage formed by the Builder Wood!
"Now!"

Chapter 1270

The Five Elements promoted and restrained each other, and fire restrained wood. However, the Builder Wood was extremely special and did not fear fire.

This magma was different from ordinary magma. The magma in the mouth of an ordinary volcano was a dark red magma formed after sand and stones were burned and melted. It was more viscous.
But in this magma lake, the Earth and Fire Laws contained in the burning and melting sand and stones had reached the pinnacle. This red magma was like clear water and was not viscous. Therefore, the magma waves spread at an extremely fast speed!

Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng and the others began to quickly retreat.
No one was willing to be covered by the magma waves. Even Luo Zheng was very vigilant.

At the same time, its huge arm swung several hundred feet and directly smashed towards the little flame kitten in the ice cage!

Chapter 1271
This lava giant's fist was the size of a palace.

Chapter 1273

As for Jin Hai and Huan Ling, they each hugged a stone pillar, firmly fixed on it.
However, Ji Nan was holding the slender Builder Wood with one hand. She wanted to avoid the flaming kitten being involved in the fight between the two giants, so she could only use one hand to stabilize herself!
This sudden jolt caused her to lose her balance. After grabbing onto nothing, she was thrown up and flung backwards!
"Ah!"
Ji Nan's eyes showed a look of horror, and her other hand was still holding on to the Builder Wood!
The faces of Jin Hai, Huan Ling, and the others all sank!
At this moment, Luo Zheng slid down the top of the Lava Giant's head. His figure suddenly flashed, and he grabbed Ji Nan's arm. At the same time, he punched downwards!
The power that erupted from his fist was also the power of 20,000 dragon scales!
He directly nailed his fist into the top of the Lava Giant's head, firmly fixing himself, while his other hand held on to Ji Nan tightly.
Luo Zheng's punch did not cause much damage to the Lava Giant itself. After all, the Lava Giant was so huge that Luo Zheng's punch was like a mosquito bite.

Now that the two behemoths were fighting, Luo Zheng's group was probably the most miserable one.
They were still too insignificant in this lava lake. Now, they could only place their hopes on the lava giant under their feet and hope that it would not be defeated.

Boom!

A series of loud, muffled sounds reverberated in this spacious and huge cave.

This lava giant used its head to hit the lava giant. It was a good decision!
At the very least, the lava giant on the opposite side was directly lying on its back in the lava lake after being hit by the lava giant.

Chapter 1274

Chapter 1275

Currently, with their cooperation, they had only lost four people to reach this point. This result was already quite shocking.
After passing through the magma lake, everyone planned to continue forward. According to the rules, there should be a considerable reward after passing through the Karma Hall.

"This …" Luo Zheng was slightly taken aback.

Each fire wasp contained tens of thousands of dreamy light dots. The fire wasps in front of them were densely packed, and in their eyes, they were a huge amount of dreamy light dots!
Everyone's faces were filled with excitement!
Cang Mo, who was originally hiding at the back, immediately rushed up when he saw this scene.
The others were not slow either, and they each used their own methods to kill these fire wasps!
These fire wasps were indeed the reward for passing the Karma Hall. After all, they swarmed over and did not have any intention of attacking. They simply fluttered in the air, allowing them to kill them one by one. However, the dreamy light dots they contained were not few at all. Killing one fire wasp would give them tens of thousands of dreamy light dots. If they could kill a thousand, they would receive ten million dreamy light dots.
It had to be said that the Karma Hall was very difficult, and if one was not careful, they would die. However, once they passed it, the dreamy light dots would flow like water.
With this, Luo Zheng killed more than eight hundred fire wasps and absorbed more than eight million dreamy light dots. In comparison, it was actually not as good as the second Karma Hall.
However, this was somewhat unfair. In the second Karma Hall, most of the cultivators only obtained one or two million dreamy light dots. The stronger cultivators only obtained three or four million dreamy light dots. Luo Zheng was purely an exception, which was why he obtained more than ten million dreamy light dots.
Apart from Luo Zheng, the other cultivators' gains were not bad either. Ji Nan killed more than seven hundred fire wasps, and Jin Hai and company killed an average of six hundred fire wasps …
After obtaining the reward, everyone continued forward, passing through the hall and climbing towards the mountain peak.

Chapter 1276
The plundering of Fantasy Points in the entire Illusory Battlefield had reached a climax!
Most of the top-ranked warriors already understood that the most important way to plunder Fantasy Points was to enter the Karma Hall!
However, the Karma Hall was not so easy to enter.
The first was to obtain a metal voucher.
The second was to pay five million Fantasy Points.
Be it the former or the latter, they were not conditions that ordinary warriors could achieve.
The metal voucher had to be obtained from a mystic realm in the wilderness. It was definitely not a simple matter to pass through the mystic realm.
As for five million Fantasy Points, how many people in a main city could accumulate them?
Illusory Battlefield, in a main city of a large world.
A silver door of light appeared in the center of the main city. From this door of light, nine warriors slowly walked out.
The moment these nine martial artists came out, some of the surrounding martial artists revealed looks of awe on their faces! They were clear that these were true experts!
After passing through the Karma Hall, all the warriors would be transported to a specific main city. And this main city was usually located in the center of the large world!
This rule was deliberately arranged by the Illusory Battlefield.
After all, warriors who could pass through the Karma Hall's test without dying were basically the top 50 experts in this large world.
Waves of experts would leave the Karma Hall and gather in the central main city. There was a high possibility of an even more intense collision happening …
Nine people had entered the Karma Hall at the same time, but only one person had died. This strength was simply astounding.
"Boring. I didn't expect the reward of the fifth level of the Karma Hall to be this …" A long-haired man in the lead shook his head and could not help but grumble.
"Heh, I find it interesting to bring a treasure out of the Illusory Battlefield. Some treasures are extremely heaven-defying existences!" Another bald warrior laughed. He had obtained a resurrection token in a mystic realm of the Illusory Battlefield! Those who possessed this token could be resurrected once!
He had not died in the Karma Hall, so he naturally would not use this resurrection token.
However, the entire Illusory Battlefield was just an illusion. It was just a projection constructed by the Heavenly Dao. If he did not use this resurrection token, he would not be able to bring it out of the Illusory Battlefield. It would be a waste.
He never expected that the reward of the fifth level of the Karma Hall was to be able to choose any treasure to bring out!
Naturally, he chose this resurrection token.
With this token, it was equivalent to having an extra life. This was an effect that could only be obtained by "resurrecting the will of the heavens"!
And the person with the Mandate of Heaven's Resurrection was an Empyrean!
What's more, that Celestial Venerable couldn't resurrect himself. He could only resurrect others. Other than that, there were many other restrictions …
Without a doubt, even if this bald martial artist did not win in the Illusory Battlefield, he would still have profited greatly.
Among this group of people, there was a woman who was ranked fourth, but her face was gloomy.
This woman was dressed in a plain white robe and a long white dress that dragged on the ground. She had a pale white embroidered brocade draped over her shoulders. Her beautiful face gave people a feeling of indifference and elegance.
She was Xi Youqin who had entered the Illusory Battlefield.
From the beginning to the end, Xi Youqin had never paid attention to her ranking and Fantasy points.
Although she had promised her master that she would strive for a good result in the Illusory Battlefield, she wasn't very interested in these things.
However, she had entered this place through the Purple Extreme Realm after passing through a big world. After being assigned, she directly entered the main city of the Devil Clan …
What was even more troublesome was that this main city of the Devil Clan was surrounded by six other main cities!
Those were the true battlegrounds.
And just like that, Xi Youqin left the main city in a daze and began to kill along the way …
She also didn't remember how many of those smelly fellows she had killed. Xi Youqin's goal wasn't to kill those fellows. Her goal was to find Luo Zheng.
Just leaving the main city was not a simple matter. Before figuring out the rules, she even took the initiative to give up resistance and was killed once. In the end, she discovered that she had been reborn in a main city. Depressed, she could only vent her frustrations by slaughtering.
Thus, she used her sword array alone to slaughter seven main cities of the Devil Clan!
Only now did all the Demon Race cultivators understand that this seemingly beautiful, elegant, and clean Human Race woman was a completely nightmarish character.
The final result was obvious. No one dared to leave the main city.
However, in terms of plundering Fantasy points, Xi Youqin had a natural advantage!
She used 100,000 swords to set up a ring-shaped sword array and firmly encircled a main city.
This move could practically drive all the martial artists in a main city crazy!
Ordinary experts, no matter how strong they were, couldn't truly slaughter all the martial artists in a main city.
After all, there were exits everywhere in the main city. One could escape by jumping off the city walls. With countless martial artists running together, no matter how strong one was, it was impossible to kill all of them. At most, it would be good if one could kill one percent of them.
For example, Ji Luoxue had only obtained the title of City Lord with the help of a large number of guards and subordinates of the Ji Clan. Only then did he set the rules for a main city.
However, Xi Youqin was different. Her sword array encircled a main city …
Whether powerful or not, they were all unable to leave the city. Once they left the city, they would be killed. They could only obediently stay in the main city.
In the end, those Devil Clan martial artists couldn't take it anymore. They could only come to an agreement with Xi Youqin. Each of them would be killed by her three times!
After killing her three times, this group of Devil Clan martial artists finally sent her away …
After repeating this several times, she directly charged into the top 100 of the entire Illusory Battlefield.
However, she took the initiative to let others kill her once and lost half of her Fantasy points. After that, she slowly climbed back up …
From beginning to end, Xi Youqin didn't check her ranking.
She was only looking for Luo Zheng!
However, after learning that this Illusory Battlefield was divided into 50 great realms, Xi Youqin was unhappy.
This meant that the probability of Luo Zheng being in the same great realm as her was only one in fifty!
After entering the Karma Hall this time, she originally still had a trace of hope. However, she didn't encounter Luo Zheng in the Karma Hall …
The long-haired youth stared at Xi Youqin and asked, "Younger Sister Youqin, why are you unhappy?"
Xi Youqin glanced at him indifferently, then walked to the side of the city wall. She was too lazy to leave the city or fight with others.
As for the long-haired youth in front of her, it was said that he was a prodigy of the Human Alliance. His strength wasn't ordinary. In the Karma Hall, he had always been the leader. Along the way, he had tried his best to protect Xi Youqin. Thus, she hadn't displayed any strength in the Karma Hall.
"Nothing," Xi Youqin replied indifferently.
"Killing people is the happiest. This main city is the exit of the Karma Hall. There must be many powerful figures gathered here. How about I accompany you out of the city to kill people?" The long-haired youth smiled faintly.
Those who could completely pass through the Karma Hall were either prodigies or existences second only to prodigies. For example, the various great realm masters and the direct descendants of Heavenly Venerables. However, this long-haired youth didn't take them seriously at all. When his words spread out, it attracted the attention of many cultivators in the city. After all, this main city was different from other main cities. It was a place where super prodigies gathered!
Xi Youqin tilted her head and thought for a moment. "Alright, but I will kill you once we leave the city." Xi Youqin looked indifferently at the long-haired youth, her tone indifferent.
The long-haired youth chuckled. "That's not necessary. If Younger Sister Youqin wants to spar, I can accompany you!"
Xi Youqin didn't say anything else and walked out of the main city. However, she was thinking in her heart. The 50 great realms of the Illusory Battlefield would merge sooner or later. Otherwise, the prodigies scattered across the 50 great realms wouldn't be able to compete. It was better for her to raise her ranking. As long as she was at the top, she would eventually be able to meet Luo Zheng.
However, she didn't know what his ranking was now.
After an incense stick's worth of time, a sword light flashed outside the main city like a bolt of lightning.
Following the long-haired cultivator's pained scream, he was sent back to the main city by Xi Youqin to be revived …
The long-haired cultivator was wrapped in a curtain of light. He was also thinking bitterly. No matter how he thought about it, he couldn't understand how this gentle and weak girl with a scholarly aura was able to be escorted through the Karma Hall by him.
But how did this girl have such strength?

Chapter 1277

The Extreme Purple Realm was at the edge of the universe. It was a broken Small Realm, and it was very special. It was considered to be drifting outside the universe, so they couldn't hear the Roaring Command.

Women were indeed women. In such a short time, Ji Nan had already given the flame kitten a name: Little Fire.

But just as Ji Nan's voice fell, from the deep sky at the top of the mountain, a thin lightning suddenly fell. The lightning spread all the way down and wrapped around a stone pillar!

Chapter 1278
These thin bolts of lightning were like bolts of spring thunder, causing the Karma Hall to begin to reorganize.
A large number of fragments emerged from all the stone pillars. These fragments seemed to remember each other's positions, constantly reassembling …

Ji Nan, Jin Hai, Huan Ling, Cang Mo, An Si, and Luo Zheng.

Putting aside his own strength for the time being, his deeds had long been spread throughout the universe. For them, they could already recite it by heart.

If you accidentally got lost in it, you might have to endure for a few more months before you could hear the end. Because that guy said that he would break it down next time …
After killing the four Celestial Masters, Luo Zheng spent the past six years like this. The trillions of living creatures in the universe also spent their lives under the baptism of his "voice".
Ji Nan, Jin Hai, Phantom, and the others were no exception!

However, Luo Zheng's voice was endless. Moreover, Luo Zheng wasn't speaking seriously. He was telling a story!
The story was mysterious and exaggerated. It was always fascinating.
When Jin Hai was in seclusion, he heard the story echoing in his ears. His mind couldn't help but follow Luo Zheng's train of thought …
Comprehending the heart of Buddhism, the most important thing was to pay attention to peace of the six senses. There couldn't be the slightest distraction!
With Luo Zheng telling the story vividly, how could Jin Hai have peace of mind? It was a good thing that he didn't go crazy, but his cultivation would be greatly delayed.
After Jin Hai finished, Cang Mo also nodded hurriedly. "I've also encountered it! I … "
Phantom smiled and didn't speak. As for whether she was affected by Luo Zheng during her seclusion, it was unknown.
There were so many living beings in the universe. When Luo Zheng was telling the story, there were cultivators who were cultivating in seclusion. Even at a critical moment in their cultivation, the Roaring Command would definitely cause a lot of trouble for other cultivators. It was likely that many of them had suffered from Qi Deviation because of this.
Now, everyone finally understood why this unknown Luo Tianxing had such strength!
Ji Nan kept smiling. She was thinking of Ji Luoxue again.

Previously, she thought that Luo Zheng could fight with Ji Luoxue, but he would be defeated in the end.
Now, Ji Nan understood that Luo Zheng would definitely become one of the strongest opponents Ji Luoxue faced. This fellow hadn't just concealed his name, he'd concealed his strength all along the way!
Everyone was very interested in Luo Zheng. If they weren't asking him about the roaring command, they were asking about the immortal mansion …
Luo Zheng answered casually. If he could answer a question, he wouldn't hide it. If he couldn't answer a question, he would just gloss over it.

Luo Zheng didn't expect that this group of people would be so curious. They asked one question after another. Apart from Huan Ling, the others didn't stop.
If they continued asking like this, they probably wouldn't finish asking after several days and nights!
Thus, he had no choice but to correct everyone's questions. He moved the questions back on the right track and returned to the Karma Hall!

After speaking, Luo Zheng walked toward the corridor next to his. This corridor was marked with Ji Nan's name. In other words, this corridor belonged to Ji Nan.
The moment Luo Zheng walked into the corridor, an irresistible force blocked Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng didn't force his way in. This was the Karma Hall. Since Luo Zheng wasn't allowed to enter, it was impossible for him to force his way in …
Everyone knew that they would have to take the next path by themselves. After making some preparations, they all entered their respective corridors.

He didn't play any role along the way. It could be said that he had mixed in with everyone.
Now that this path was divided into six, he could only rely on himself. However, there was no confidence in his eyes. He could only enter his own corridor unhappily.
After Luo Zheng glanced at the corridor, he also stepped into it.
This corridor was very wide. Even if a carriage rushed over, it would be able to speed through this corridor.
Walking on the soft carpet, Luo Zheng had an illusion that this fifth Karma Hall didn't seem to have any difficulty.
However, after walking a few dozen meters, he suddenly stopped in his tracks.
In front of Luo Zheng, there was a torch hanging on the wall. The flame on the torch was still burning. It seemed to be used to illuminate this corridor.

Luo Zheng didn't pay attention to the flame. He planned to go around it.

Chapter 1279

Luo Zheng glanced at his hands. His body was shrouded in a jade-green radiance. Apparently, he was in his soul state.

Luo Zheng saw a small bell in the air!

When the small bell appeared, a figure gradually condensed in the wind and snow.
It was a woman with a graceful figure who was draped in an emerald blue ribbon!
This woman's legs were bare. She danced in the endless cold and quietly stared at him!
"Kekekeke …"
The world of ice and snow was still fine. Luo Zheng's soul was strong enough to resist the cold.
But under this woman's indifferent gaze, Luo Zheng felt like he had fallen into a ten-thousand-year-old ice cellar. At this moment, Luo Zheng's soul seemed to be sealed. The surface of his soul was frozen, and a thin layer of frost appeared. At this moment, Luo Zheng was almost unable to think.

Luo Zheng's eyes suddenly flashed, and the green light on the surface of his soul became even more intense!

Countless cracks suddenly appeared on the frost on the surface of Luo Zheng's body. Then, with a burst of popping sounds, the fragments fell to the ground!
The woman floating in the air saw this scene, and a smile appeared on her face …
She took the bell in her hand and gently shook it. A large amount of snowflakes drifted down from the sky!
"Again?"
Luo Zheng's eyes suddenly turned cold. His figure flashed, and he rushed toward the woman!
The cold wind whistled in his ears. As Luo Zheng rushed forward, he saw that the woman only swayed slightly. Like a snowflake drifting in the air, she floated behind him!
She retreated as much as Luo Zheng advanced.
The two of them chased each other in this boundless space, but the distance between them couldn't be closed.
"This movement technique … It's familiar …"
Luo Zheng's brows furrowed, but there was a glimmer of light in his eyes!
Baqu Feiyan?
Only this kind of strange movement technique could allow the user to be as light as a feather and maintain a certain distance from him.

If he had an extremely powerful technique, he would be able to break through the three inches distance! For example, Ji Luoxue had once broken his Eight Winding Smoke!
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng's soul began to emit a white mist. He also used the Eight Winding Smoke!
"Hu …"
When Luo Zheng used the Eight Winding Smoke, he felt as light as a feather …
Strictly speaking, Smoke was not a movement technique. It was more like a body refining technique. It was just that it was the opposite of ordinary body refining techniques.
He made himself as light as a feather, like a wisp of smoke. If the wind blew, the smoke would also drift with the wind!
Therefore, Smoke relied on external forces and not one's own strength.
At this moment, Luo Zheng was using Eight Directional Smoke Flying. His speed was not fast, but the woman's speed was also slowed down. She was originally 'pushed' by Luo Zheng's momentum, but with Luo Zheng's slow drifting, the thrust produced was almost negligible. The woman could not borrow the force to move forward, and the distance between the two rapidly shrank!
The woman looked at Luo Zheng, who was slowly drifting over, and her face flashed with a trace of doubt.

This Illusory Battlefield was an excellent cultivation opportunity for ordinary martial artists.
Countless martial artists experienced life and death transformations, and after leaving the Illusory Battlefield, their strength would explosively increase.
However, to those core geniuses and proud sons of heaven, this wasn't much at all. Anything that could be done in the Illusory Battlefield could also be done in the Illusory Battlefield.
But what they obtained in the Illusory Battlefield was much more important than what ordinary martial artists obtained. For example, this Emperor's Corridor!
The Divine Dao was naturally the path to becoming a True God!
In this universe, those that could transcend the Heavenly Dao and become a True God were few and far between. There were also very few factions and family clans that possessed the ability to transcend the Divine Dao. In the entire universe, there were no more than ten of them. And martial artists that truly had the qualifications to cultivate this ability were even rarer.
These people were called Dao Children.
They were existences that surpassed the proud sons of heaven. Even if all the family clans and factions of the universe were gathered together, they would only be able to gather a few people.
Even some Empyreans themselves didn't have the qualifications, much less their descendants.
Luo Zheng had previously encountered Ji Luoxue. She was a proud son of heaven of the Ji Family and also the Ji Family's most likely candidate to become a Dao Child! And what the Ji Family possessed was the Five Elements Divine Dao!
And the most important symbol of becoming a Dao Child was the complete blossoming of the Mahayana Lotus Flower!
There were some martial artists that had just stepped into the Divine Sea realm or had opened a seven-leaf or even nine-leaf Lesser Mahayana Lotus Flower. But in their entire lives, they had never opened a Mahayana Lotus Flower.
There were also some martial artists that had opened a Mahayana Lotus Flower at the Divine Transformation realm. But in time, they had stepped into the World Lord realm and had even shouldered the mandate of heaven and opened all the Mahayana Lotus Flower leaves.
This sort of situation was called a life form that was limited by the Heavenly Dao. The vast majority of Empyreans were like this; they had no hope of stepping into the Divine Dao in their lifetime.

Chapter 1280
The restrictions of the Heavenly Dao were almost unchangeable.
Some Empyrean powerhouses, in order to escape this restriction, had to pay a great price to refine a secret medicine that allowed them to escape the mystery of being in the womb and reincarnate with the memories of their previous lives.
They had to cultivate from the beginning. But when they reached the Life and Death Realm and opened their lotuses to cross minor heavenly tribulations, they still couldn't escape this restriction …
This was also why the great races attached so much importance to the Illusory Battlefield, and why so many proud sons of heaven were gathered.
Because only from this group of people could a True Divinity be born, and only then could they surpass the Heavenly Dao!
And now, all the possibilities were concentrated in the Divine Sea realm.
It wasn't that there weren't any geniuses in the Divine Transformation realm or the Divine Extreme realm. It was just that after the opening of this great world, many geniuses began to grow at an explosive rate. And, these geniuses were all concentrated in the Divine Sea realm. Most of their skeletal ages were between 20 and 40 years old …
So, some people called this generation the Golden Generation.
Snow Seal Divine Dao …
Cold Rain …
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed.
This woman probably wasn't from this era. He had never heard of this name before.
But at this moment, Luo Zheng grasped this opportunity to stare at the woman and ask, "After surpassing the Heavenly Dao and becoming a True Divinity, where will they go?"
Luo Zheng now had a rough idea of where Luo Yan was. Although the mysterious woman had promised to help him inquire about Luo Yan, she hadn't given him a response.
Thinking about it, the young man who took away Luo Yan was also a True Divinity existence. Then, after these True Divinities surpassed the Heavenly Dao, where did they go? It couldn't be in the chaos …
"The Divine Realm, the endless Divine Realm," the woman indifferently replied.
"The Divine Realm!"
Luo Zheng firmly engraved these two words in his mind.
At this moment, the rivers of ice that were pouring down had already stopped …
She placed her finger on the small bell and gently shook it again.
The blizzard stopped in an instant, and the whole world fell into silence. It was like the silence after a heavy snowfall in the middle of winter.
In this world, there was only Luo Zheng, and besides him, there was only a vast expanse of white …
At this moment, an invisible chill rushed towards Luo Zheng!
This chill directly penetrated Luo Zheng's spine and spread throughout his entire body. At this moment, his soul was frozen from the inside out, and he no longer had any strength to resist.
What he faced was absolute coldness!
At this temperature, not only did the energy completely disappear, even the mass disappeared.
Under this strange temperature, even the frozen ice and snow were extremely warm to Luo Zheng...
After the time it took for an incense stick to burn, the temperature began to rise. At the same time, the woman let out a soft sigh. "You are not compatible with the Snow Seal Divine Dao …"
Boom!
At the same time, a flame exploded in Luo Zheng's mind!
When he came back to his senses, everything in front of him suddenly disappeared. The lamp on the wall was still hanging there, emitting a lustrous light, illuminating the Emperor's Corridor.
Each lamp contained the imprint of a True God …
This kind of experience was indeed crucial to martial artists.
If a martial artist majored in ice-type cultivation methods, they would be able to comprehend a lot in that absolute coldness!
It was a pity that Ning Yudie didn't have the opportunity to enter this place. She majored in ice-type cultivation methods, and although the probability was very low, it wasn't impossible for her to be compatible with the Snow Seal Divine Dao.
The Real God named Yu Han had mentioned Divinity earlier on. However, he did not know what Divinity was.
If he returned to the immortal mansion, Luo Zheng would probably have to ask the Book Immortal and the others for guidance …
If he wanted to surpass the Heavenly Dao and pursue the Realm of the Gods, these things would be of utmost importance to Luo Zheng.
After pondering for a while, Luo Zheng walked past the lamp and continued forward.
Before he'd taken two steps, he discovered a series of engraved murals on the walls of the Emperor's Corridor.
The first mural depicted the birth of a baby girl. On that day, snow filled the sky, and there was an eclipse of the sun in the sky …
In the second mural, the baby girl had already grown up, stepped onto the martial path, and pursued the ultimate power …
The third mural …
"I understand now. The life of this True God is recorded in these murals."
After being titled as a True God, the Heavenly Dao would actually engrave these murals …
As Luo Zheng walked forward, he looked at these murals.
From this girl's step onto the martial path, to being valued, to opening the lotus flower, and so on, it was all recorded in the murals.
"When this girl entered the Life and Death Realm, she only opened one lotus leaf of the Hinayana Lotus Flower?"
Luo Zheng's face revealed a strange expression.
In the Upper Realm, the number of lotus leaves was regarded as the possibility of surpassing the Heavenly Dao. The more lotus leaves opened, the greater the possibility, and to a certain extent, it was equivalent to talent!
One lotus leaf belonged to a very ordinary genius. Even in ordinary tenth-rank Holy Lands, they wouldn't be regarded as a core disciple.
Just like now, any core disciple in a tenth-rank Holy Land would open five or even seven lotus leaves.
However, as he continued forward, Luo Zheng understood why this girl could become a True God.
Every time this girl experienced a heavenly tribulation, she would open one lotus leaf very steadily. After she passed nine minor heavenly tribulations, the Hinayana Lotus Flower would open nine lotus leaves!
As for the three incarnations, Divine Sea Realm, Extreme God Realm, and Divine Transformation Realm, she would open one lotus leaf for every heavenly tribulation …
Finally, the Mahayana Lotus Flower and Hinayana Lotus Flower would open completely, and she would become a Dao Child.
However, she became a Dao Child relatively late. She was already at the late stage of the Divine Transformation Realm, which was equivalent to a half-step Realm Lord.
As he looked at the murals, Luo Zheng sighed slightly.
When Luo Zheng went through his first minor heavenly tribulation, he had already opened 35 lotus leaves. The Mahayana Lotus Flower was only missing the final lotus leaf.
However, after enduring the heavenly tribulations, Luo Zheng's cultivation had increased a lot, but the final lotus leaf still hadn't bloomed.
Only by opening the final lotus leaf could one become a Dao Child!
Luo Zheng didn't know when the final lotus leaf would open …
As he walked past the murals, he saw the most exciting scenes in this girl's life, including the scene where she traveled through the universe and became a True God.
When Luo Zheng finished looking at the final mural, a torch appeared in front of him!
Luo Zheng stared at the torch, which also exuded a strong aura. However, this aura was different from the aura of the True God from before. This torch contained an extremely domineering will, which was an aura that looked down on the world!
"This flame also contains the imprint of another True God …"
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng took another step forward!
"Boom!"
The flame suddenly exploded, enveloping Luo Zheng once again.
At this moment, Luo Zheng was standing on an empty battlefield. In front of him was a lone city, and behind him was an army of thousands of men and horses!
In front of the gates of the lone city stood a tall and sturdy Demon Race martial artist with a large saber in his hand. The army of thousands of men and horses were also Demon Race martial artists.
That extremely domineering aura was emitted by this Demon Race martial artist!
"Attack the city!"
With a command, countless Demon Race martial artists began to roar and attack the city.
"This is … an internal war of the Demon Race?"
Luo Zheng's gaze sharpened slightly.
"Haha!"
With an earth-shaking laugh, the saber-wielding Demon Race martial artist swung his saber horizontally, resisting the army of thousands of men and horses with his strength alone!
"The aura of tens of thousands of martial artists, but it's not just one person. This person … is the True God of the second lamp?"
Just as Yu Han had said, every single lamp in the Emperor's Promenade had the mark of a True God recorded within it. These True Gods had been born within this universe, and they were memories that the Heaven Dao wouldn't obliterate.
"Kill!"
"Kill!"
"Kill!"
The soldiers roared. They were like a tide, not only surging towards the True God, but also towards Luo Zheng!
The first Demon Race martial artist rushed towards Luo Zheng. His figure flashed slightly, and he snatched the sword from the other party's hand. His eyes flashed slightly, and the huge sword reverberated. Instantly, more than a dozen Demon Race martial artists' heads flew into the sky, decapitated by Luo Zheng's sword!
But at this moment, Luo Zheng felt a strong sense of danger from behind him. In an instant, he rolled forward and lay flat on the ground!
"Whoosh —"
It was a huge saber aura that spread out.
This saber aura spread three feet above the ground. After Luo Zheng lay down, the saber aura shot past the tip of his nose!

Chapter 1281
In this soul environment, what was displayed was not the strength of a True God …

After this blade strike, no one in the Demon Race army stood up.
Of course, Luo Zheng was an exception …
However, after Luo Zheng dodged the blade energy and stood up, the Demon Race martial artist's gaze fell on Luo Zheng!

Under the suppression of this enormous force, Luo Zheng's knees slightly softened. This pressure contained a fierce and domineering aura, as if a mountain was pressing down on Luo Zheng's body.

The Demon Race martial artist roared!
The large blade pressed down by another two points, and the pressure increased by another two points. Naturally, the force Luo Zheng had to bear also increased by another two points!
In the face of such power, Luo Zheng straightened his legs, but still firmly stood in place. His eyes were fixed on the Demon Race martial artist, and there was not the slightest hint of compromise on his face.

"Whoosh!"
The large blade was brandished!
An incomparably thick blade energy, wrapped in a supreme aura, fiercely slashing towards Luo Zheng!

Luo Zheng also tit for tat.

He Luo Zheng Yang Luo Zheng.

"Yi Shen Sword!"
"Xiu!"
The Yi Shen Sword was originally a sword move that was based on the comprehension of the soul level.

He was a mark of a True God. Although he didn't have the strength of a True God, when he slashed out, the blade energy was displayed to the extreme. How could it be so easily broken by this junior in front of him?

Seeing the demon warrior heading straight for him, Luo Zheng's face was indifferent. His long sword circled in the air, and once again stabbed towards the demon warrior!
"Yi Shen Sword!"
This True God powerhouse, even if it was just a mark, was an existence that Luo Zheng couldn't defeat.

In the eyes of this True God, this was a very miraculous thing.

This enormous sword that he had seized at the last moment was actually a mediocre weapon. Under this collision, it shattered …
Seeing this scene, the demon warrior took advantage of the situation and rushed forward. The purpose of his existence was not to kill Luo Zheng. In the blink of an eye, he gently flicked his longsword. Not far from him, a sword on the ground flew towards Luo Zheng!

One slash! Two slashes! Three slashes …

When his voice fell, the thick clouds were cut apart. A ray of sunlight broke through the clouds, but it was as if the clouds were parted to reveal the fog!
At the same time …

Countless blade energies descended!
They constantly surged around Luo Zheng!

After a long time, the thick aura in the sky closed again.
There was no blade energy descending from the sky …
Luo Zheng looked at the True God named Mo Chen in front of him. He had already exited the state of severing emotions, and his eyes were a little more lively!
Mo Chen noticed the change in Luo Zheng and laughed, "You are not suitable for my Divine Way! Moreover … you already have your own Divine Way. However, the Divine Way that you cultivate is destined to be in vain! "

Chapter 1282
In the universe, there was an invisible limit to power.
For example, when cultivating the Law of Fire, the highest level was the tenth level, and the tenth level was perfection.
However, a Divine Sea Realm martial artist could reach the fifth level at most. An Extreme Divine Realm martial artist could reach the sixth level, a Divine Transformation Realm martial artist could reach the seventh level, a Sector Lord martial artist could reach the eighth level, and a Celestial Venerable martial artist could reach the ninth level!
This was the power of the boundary, and it was difficult to cross.
However, some Celestial Venerables who inherited the Mandate of Heaven regarding the Law of Fire could cultivate the Law of Fire to perfection, which was the tenth level of the Law of Fire.
This kind of perfection referred to the perfection under the Heavenly Way, but it was not the real perfection.
There was a higher level above the tenth level, which was a height that countless creatures in the universe could not touch or even understand.
To reach this level, one had to cross the Heavenly Way and become a True God.
However, not everyone could cultivate to become a True God.
The lotus flower floating above the heads of many martial artists directly announced the possibility of crossing the Heavenly Way.
Every martial artist cultivated a different Divine Path.
For example, the Five Elements Divine Path, the Snow Seal Divine Path …
"If I'm not wrong, you're cultivating the Emotion Severing Divine Path!" Mo Chen said lightly.
"Emotion severing … is a Divine Path?" Luo Zheng was slightly stunned.
Luo Zheng had faintly touched the essence of the Emotion Severing Divine Path, which was why he could use the Yi Sword to such an effect. However, he did not know that the Emotion Severing Divine Path was also a way to prove one's Dao. He thought it was just a method that he had comprehended from the Yi Sword.
"Yes, the Emotion Severing Divine Path is an extremely powerful Divine Path … but it is also a forbidden Dao!" Mo Chen continued, "If you really succeed in cultivating this Dao, you will attract the wrath of the Gods. This Dao cannot be taken!"
Mo Chen's character was a typical ambitious and ruthless person. In the beginning, he was also a fearless existence in the universe. He had used his wild nature to create a domineering Divine Path, which gave rise to an extreme comprehension.
However, when he mentioned the Emotion Severing Divine Path, his face revealed a cautious expression.
"Is that so?"
Luo Zheng's eyes flickered slightly.
His character was completely different from Mo Chen's. He was naturally not as arrogant as Mo Chen, but Luo Zheng's heart of martial arts was extremely tenacious.
In the face of the True God's advice, Luo Zheng would think carefully, but these words could not change Luo Zheng's original intention.
Mo Chen guessed Luo Zheng's thoughts. He laughed and waved his saber. "That's all I have to say. You … go!"
"Boom …"
This isolated city, this battlefield, and the demon army lying on the ground suddenly dissipated. Luo Zheng once more returned to the Emperor's Promenade.
However, Mo Chen's soul imprint was watching Luo Zheng's departing figure within the rising flames above the torch, and he continued to ponder …
"This person is Fateless."
"It just so happens that the Fateless cultivates the Emotion-Severing Divine Dao …"
"That's not right. He's not really a fateless person. His fate must have been concealed by someone using a great divine ability. He's trying to steal the sky and change the sun …"
"What is their true goal?"
"…"
Luo Zheng did not know that Mo Chen was thinking about this.
He just strolled in the promenade and continued to observe the murals on both sides of the promenade.
These murals recorded Mo Chen's life.
This person was also a big anomaly in the Demon Clan. His life was extremely bumpy, and he was rejected by the Demon Clan. Because the Demon Clan was in danger, he returned to the Demon Clan and saved the Demon Clan with his own strength … All of these were recorded in the form of murals.
Of course, one of the murals was the scene that Luo Zheng experienced in the flame illusion.
Mo Chen had once guarded an isolated city by himself. Moreover, he had faced an army of tens of millions of Devil Race soldiers alone. And, he had defended that isolated city for three whole years!
"This person became a True God by no means by chance. Others may rely on luck to become a True God, but this person's luck is extremely bad. In the end, he still achieved such an achievement. It's admirable and lamentable!"
Luo Zheng did not reject all the Demon Clan. Although almost none of the Demon Clan warriors that Luo Zheng encountered were good people, this clan had always had its own way of doing things.

Chapter 1283

The Sword-fighting Empyrean suddenly opened his eyes …

"Junior, Luo Zheng," Luo Zheng replied indifferently.
"You know who I am?" the Sword-fighting Empyrean asked.
"Sword-fighting Empyrean," Luo Zheng replied.
The Sword-fighting Empyrean stared at Luo Zheng, and after a few breaths of time, he said: "My name is Chen Huang Yi Jian, and I use the sword to prove my Dao and enter the Emotion Severing Divine Dao …"
Empyrean was only his title, Chen Huang Yi Jian was his real name.
Now that he had become a True God, it was no longer appropriate for him to be called an Empyrean.
"You, you seem to have walked on my path of the sword?" The Sword-fighting Empyrean stared at Luo Zheng and asked.
"Yes, Senior Sword-fighting." Luo Zheng nodded.
Chen Huang Yi Jian's face revealed a trace of a smile. He was still sitting upright on the mountain peak. With a grab of his hand, the two foot long Yi god sword flew back into his hand. After holding the sword in his hand, he thrusted it towards Luo Zheng.
On this sword, there was a green light hidden within.
This green light was also a sword intent …
But this sword intent was a thousand times purer than the sword intent Luo Zheng had comprehended!
This was the pinnacle of sword intent!
Facing this sword, Luo Zheng did not hesitate in the slightest.
He did not feel any killing intent from Chen Huang Yi Jian, and knew that the other party was only probing. He also thrusted his sword out.
The sword techniques of the two were almost exactly the same, and the simplest sword was also the pinnacle sword. It was just that Luo Zheng's comprehension of the Yi god sword was still lacking, and his sword intent had not been reborn to the pinnacle.
The two swords did not collide.
Chen Huang Yi Jian's sword stabbed halfway, then retracted. But in the instant it retracted, the two foot long Yi god sword stabbed out again.
Luo Zheng also did not hesitate. Chen Huang Yi Jian's sword retracted, and Luo Zheng also retracted. Chen Huang Yi Jian's sword stabbed out, and Luo Zheng also stabbed out …
These two people were stabbing at each other across the air, but there was no substantial sword technique!
One sword …
Ten swords …
One hundred swords …
One thousand swords …
Ten thousand swords …
One hundred thousand swords …
Time was already empty at this moment.
Luo Zheng did not know fatigue, and Chen Huang Yi Jian was also quite patient.
This was a test, and also a kind of training, but also a kind of silent teaching!
After a million swords.
Chen Huang Yi Jian suddenly retracted his hand, and looked at Luo Zheng indifferently, "If I did not see it with my own eyes, I would never believe that someone could see through the poem I left behind …"
"This junior is just a coincidence," Luo Zheng replied.
"No," Chen Huang Yi Jian smiled faintly, "No, the number of destiny can also be fixed. Someone is pushing you from behind."
Hearing this, Luo Zheng's face showed a puzzled look. He did not understand, "Pushing me from behind?"
"I don't know who it is … and I don't know what he is planning, but this is the arrangement of this person," Chen Huang Yi Jian continued.
At this point, Luo Zheng frowned. He did not understand the mystery in Chen Huang Yi Jian's words, but he faintly felt a trace of uneasiness.
He was not a fatalist. Even if he was in an irreversible adversity, he would still use his last bit of strength. At least, his fate was always in his own hands.
But from Chen Huang Yijian's words, there was another explanation. Was there someone behind him arranging his fate?
Seeing Luo Zheng's slightly conflicted expression, Chen Huang Yijian only faintly smiled, "This is not a question I want to discuss with you, and I don't care …" Then he opened his mouth and asked, "How many lotus leaves have you opened?"
"Nine-leaf Lesser Ascension, twenty-six Greater Ascension," Luo Zheng answered truthfully.
Hearing Luo Zheng's answer, Chen Huang Yi Jian nodded again, and a look of approval appeared on his face, "Not bad, the Divine Sea realm has opened thirty-five leaves. It won't be long before you become a Daoist disciple, not bad, not bad …"

Chapter 1284
Chen Huang Yijian did not have much interest in the martial artists that passed through the Emperor's Corridor.

Another example was Jin Hai, who could bring a paragon artifact like the Buddha Blade out of the Illusion Battlefield …

After Jin Hai handed over the original Buddha Sun Blade, the other Buddha Sun Blade would permanently belong to him!

Chapter 1287

Chapter 1288
The Yao Night Clan was divided into six factions.
These six factions were the most ancient clans of the Yao Night Clan.
It was because these clans possessed the bloodline of the ancient Yao Night Clan that the Three Kings were born from these six clans.
Heavenly Venerate Broken Shadow had risen to become the fourth Heavenly Venerate of the Yao Night Clan with Yao's help. Naturally, his clan had soared as well.
Even within the Yao Night Clan, where the Three Kings reigned supreme, there were still internal conflicts.
These six factions controlled the Elder Council and relied on the Three Kings to maintain the balance within the Yao Night Clan.

Among them, the two factions under the King of Life maintained their silence. Just like the King of Life, they didn't express any opinion on Yao's arbitrary decision.
As for the two factions that relied on the King of Massacre, one of them had sided with Yao, while the other had pledged to follow Xun to the death. Therefore, Yao had directly dealt with them.
This faction was the owner of the Yao Night Clan's Holy Spear. Generations of Massacre Kings had used their Holy Spear, so they were also known as the Holy Spear Faction.
The Holy Spear Faction's situation in the Yao Night Clan could only be described as extremely difficult. They either chose to hide or were forcefully enslaved by other factions. If they were men, they would be forced to do hard labor. If they were beautiful women, they would be treated as playthings.

When news of Xun had first spread through the universe, the Holy Spear Faction had not thought too much about it.

Not only had he killed Heavenly Venerate Broken Shadow, but he had also killed three Heavenly Venerates from other races …
Ming Dao was the son of Heavenly Venerate Broken Shadow.
During those days, Phantom secretly looked at Ming Dao's soulless appearance, but she was very happy in her heart!
At the same time, the name Luo Zheng was deeply engraved in the mind of the Phantom.

Luo Zheng was not strong enough now. He needed to go down his own path. Phantom regarded Luo Zheng as a cautious hope. They could not wait to protect him, so how could they be willing to disturb him?

Therefore, Phantom chose to remain silent and say such words.
However, she didn't know Luo Zheng's character.

Even someone as timid as Azure Demon chose to advance and retreat together with Phantom …

If you want me to accompany you, this Illusory Battlefield is really inconvenient. After all, it's just an ethereal projection. It's not as happy as in reality."

Ji Nan, Huan Ling, Jin Hai, Cang Mo, and Ansi stood on the city wall.

If you're willing to be a coward, I can't do anything to you if you stay in the main city. "

The corners of Luo Zheng's mouth curled up slightly. "I'm not regretting it. I'm just waiting for some things. If those things don't come, that's naturally for the best!"

"Cough, cough, cough …"

Chapter 1289

Therefore, although many of the sons of Celestial Venerates in the universe had excellent aptitudes, they still weren't heaven's pride …
The monstrous killing intent that escaped gradually dissipated in the air.

Black and white double-pointed spear …

Chapter 1290
When Ji Nan saw the Holy Spear of Slaughter, she felt her heart palpitate.
The piece of Builder Wood in her body even gave Ji Nan a warning. She could feel the uniqueness of this spear!
The black and white materials used to create the Holy Spear of Slaughter seemed to resonate with the Creation Wood in her body.
She naturally did not know that the black and white shaft of the Holy Spear of Slaughter also came from the other two Divine Woods.
The Eastern Hibiscus, the Western Ruo Wood, the Northern Star Creation Wood, and the Southern Dipper Seeker Wood.
These were the legendary Four Divine Woods.
The Four Divine Woods were existences that surpassed the Heavenly Dao.
Hibiscus had never appeared in the universe before.
The Creation Wood had only appeared once with Ji Nan's birth.
As for the Seeker Wood and the Ruo Wood, they had been placed here since the birth of the universe.
The reason was simple. Ruo Mu was a suitable place for the sun to reside in. When the many living beings in the universe were born, the sun that could illuminate all living things was incredibly important. It could be said that the final step in the creation of the universe was to light up the sun, and Ruo Mu was a crucial step.
As for the Seeker Wood, its reproduction speed was extremely fast. Its roots were almost everywhere, supporting the entire universe's 100,000 Great Realms and millions of Great Chiliocosms. However, although this kind of wood was everywhere, it was nowhere to be found. Therefore, it was called the Seeker Wood.
The Seeker Wood was white, and the Ruo Wood was black.
The Holy Spear of Slaughter was made from the Seeker Wood and the Ruo Wood.
Therefore, when Ji Nan sensed the power of these two Divine Woods, she could not help but remind Luo Zheng.
Even without Ji Nan's reminder, Luo Zheng knew that this spear shadow could not be resisted. His figure twisted in midair and quickly dodged to the side.

"This power …"
Even Luo Zheng was stunned.
Although the Holy Spear of Slaughter had only drilled a fist-sized hole in the city wall, it was enough to shock everyone present.
The city wall contained the Heavenly Dao rules.
With the Heavenly Dao rules, the city wall was an impenetrable existence.
Not to mention the Divine Sea Realm, even if an Extreme God Realm, Divine Transformation Realm, or even a World Lord personally came, they wouldn't be able to move the city walls in the slightest. It would be impossible for them to even peel off a single brick from the city walls.
Only an Empyrean who had inherited the Mandate of Heaven would be able to break through this wall. Because of the Mandate of Heaven, they would be able to control a portion of the Heavenly Dao.

Whoosh, whoosh, whoosh …

Puff!

Chapter 1294
Ming Dao, who had sacrificed himself to the Holy Spear, was like a puppet caught in a massacre.
As Luo Zheng retreated, Ming Dao took advantage of the situation and chased after him!
However, due to the protection of the Heavenly Dao, Ming Dao, who was holding the Holy Spear, was blocked outside the city wall just like those little skulls!
The difference was that those little skulls could not penetrate the thin film, but Ming Dao, who was controlled by the Holy Spear, had this ability!
"Bo!"
The invisible film was directly torn apart by the blood-red light released by the Holy Spear, and he actually drilled into the main city!
The strength of the sacrifice determined the power of the Holy Spear at this moment.
For example, after sacrificing himself, he could completely squeeze out the potential of the Holy Spear, and the power it erupted with was incomparable.
However, after Ming Dao sacrificed himself, his strength also soared greatly!
However …
Although Ming Dao broke through the invisible film, he was still suppressed by the Heavenly Dao in the main city!
Luo Zheng quickly retreated, and the Holy Spear spun wildly, going against the Heavenly Dao all the way as it chased after Luo Zheng!
At this moment, Luo Zheng could not fight back. After all, fighting was prohibited in the main city.
However, Luo Zheng did not want to fight Ming Dao at all. Flying and fighting were prohibited in the main city, so he just kept retreating …
Fortunately, under the suppression of the Heavenly Dao, Ming Dao's speed was not fast. Going against the Heavenly Dao required extremely powerful strength. After Ming Dao sacrificed himself, although he could resist the Heavenly Dao to a certain extent, it was still very difficult for him to move forward. His speed was not much faster than Luo Zheng's.
As for the onlookers, they could only choose to watch the show. After all, no one could make a move …
From the main city wall, Luo Zheng kept retreating while Ming Dao kept approaching. The spinning double-tipped spear was less than a foot away from the tip of Luo Zheng's nose!
100 feet, 200 feet, 300 feet …
When it was 1,000 feet away, the tip of the spear was less than two inches away from Luo Zheng's face!
However, at this moment, Ming Dao suddenly stopped moving and froze on the spot.
Everyone looked at the scene in front of them in silence. They had never seen a fight that could be fought in the main city before.
Whoosh …
A gentle breeze blew past …
Some fine powder fell from Ming Dao's body …
Soon, Ming Dao's entire body began to disintegrate, turning into dust. The Sacred Spear of Slaughter also fell to the ground with a clang.
Previously, the Underworld Dao had relied on the power of the Holy Spear of Slaughter to resist the Heavenly Dao's rules to a certain extent.
However, there was a time limit to the consecration. The stronger one was, the longer this time limit would be.
With Ming Dao's current Divine Sea Realm cultivation, if he offered himself to the Holy Spear of Slaughter, he would only be able to last for less than 50 breaths.
When all of his vitality was drained away by the Sacred Spear of Slaughter, the Heavenly Dao's laws formed an irresistible pressure that pressed down on his body. This was why his body was instantly reduced to dust!
"Buzz …"
A red light floated away. That was Ming Dao's soul. After this death, he naturally returned to the main city to be resurrected.
Luo Zheng furrowed his brows at this moment.
"No Fantasy Points?"
As a prodigy, Ming Dao had broken through the Karma Hall and fought in this core main city for a long time. The number of Fantasy Points in his body should be an astonishing number. However, no Fantasy Points appeared from Ming Dao's body. Luo Zheng also looked puzzled.
Ming Dao's ranking was indeed higher than Luo Zheng's. He was ranked fourth in this major world and 88th in the Illusion Battlefield!
However, no Fantasy Points appeared from Ming Dao's death. Everyone also looked puzzled.
"What's going on? Why didn't Fantasy Points appear after Ming Dao died?"
"Isn't this cheating?"
"Perhaps Ming Dao has other methods?"
Luo Zheng was not the only one puzzled. Everyone else was also puzzled, including Ji Nan, Jin Hai, and the others. They walked down from the city walls and surrounded Luo Zheng.
As for the Phantom, it heaved a sigh of relief. Although Luo Zheng was considered to have "retreated", he still won in the end …
Fighting prodigies was often accompanied by huge risks. Naturally, it was proportional to the rich rewards, such as a huge number of Fantasy Points.
However, at this moment, a sinister laugh was heard, and a cultivator walked towards Luo Zheng. "Hehehe, since the boss of the Underworld is willing to sacrifice himself, he must have thought of a way out. You are also very strange …"
This warrior was a warrior who followed Ming Dao. Previously, Luo Zheng had killed five people with two swords. The remaining three had retreated to the main city. This person was one of them.
As he walked, he casually glanced at the Holy Spear of Slaughter on the ground …
According to the rules, after dying in the Illusion Battlefield, a warrior's clothes, equipment, and spatial rings would reappear along with their resurrection.
However, the strange thing was that the Holy Spear of Slaughter did not disappear. Instead, it fell here.
This warrior naturally did not want the Holy Spear of Slaughter to fall into Luo Zheng's hands. Now that Luo Zheng had not picked up the Holy Spear of Slaughter, he approached the double-pointed spear as he spoke. In any case, there was no fighting in the main city. Whoever snatched it would own it …
However, although Luo Zheng was puzzled, when he saw the warrior walk over, he gently picked up the Holy Spear of Slaughter with the tip of his foot and then stuffed it into his spatial ring. He even smiled faintly at the warrior. "I am indeed very puzzled. Can you solve my doubts?"
Seeing Luo Zheng's actions, the warrior's face suddenly stiffened. He cursed in his heart and then said coldly, "Big Brother Ming Dao has obtained three Exemption Tokens! These three Exemption Tokens allow him to not lose any Fantasy Points when he dies! Heh … Don't mention killing him once, even if you kill him three times, you will gain nothing. I advise you to hand over Big Brother Ming Dao's weapon. Otherwise, he will never let you go! "
When everyone heard this person's explanation, they suddenly understood. Envy immediately appeared on their faces.
He actually had three Exemption Tokens. This thing was too excessive!
Luo Zheng felt relieved. He was just curious. Since he had the opportunity to obtain a Transfer Token, it was not strange for someone else to obtain three Exemption Tokens. As for this person's threat, Luo Zheng only smiled faintly. "Won't let me go? Without this Holy Spear of Slaughter, what qualifications does he have to not let me go? "
Regardless of Ming Dao's strength, he had already developed a cowardly awareness towards Luo Zheng. He had lost in terms of momentum. Now that the Holy Spear was in Luo Zheng's possession, he did not have the courage to confront Luo Zheng head-on …
Soon, Ming Dao, who had revived in the main city, rushed over. The moment he saw Luo Zheng, he asked with a gloomy expression, "Where is my weapon!?"
After enshrining the spear, his consciousness had been sealed. Thus, until his death, he did not know what had happened. Now that he saw Luo Zheng standing here and his Holy Spear could not be found, how could his expression be good?
"It was taken by this person!" the warrior said to Ming Dao.
Anger flashed in Ming Dao's eyes. He stretched out his hand and said sternly, "Again!"
"This Holy Spear of Slaughter originally did not belong to you. How can you talk about returning it?" Luo Zheng smiled faintly.
Since he had obtained this Holy Spear of Slaughter, Luo Zheng began to plan in his heart. Should he use five million fantasy points to make a trip to the Karma Hall?
If there was time …
Luo Zheng was willing to give Xun a surprise.
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, Ming Dao stared at Luo Zheng for a while, and a sharp glint flashed in his eyes. "I did not expect that the world-famous Luo Zheng was just a coward. He did not dare to face me head-on and even fled into the main city with his tail between his legs. Even with the suppression of the Heavenly Dao, he was almost killed by me. Haha!"
Since they could not fight in the main city, Ming Dao could only provoke him with words.
"We can fight again outside the city," Luo Zheng said faintly.
"Return the spear to me. Naturally, we can fight again," Ming Dao said again.
Luo Zheng smiled slightly and stared at Ming Dao with a mocking gaze. "Naturally, it is impossible to return the spear."
"Hmph, then forget about it. You can't do anything to me in the main city!" Ming Dao said coldly.
Although his weapon was confiscated from Luo Zheng, it was not a big deal. Now that Ming Dao was ranked fourth in this big world, he should be able to enter the next stage without any worries …
And this Holy Spear of Slaughter was just a projection. It did not matter if it was lost. However, Ming Dao could only use his life source magic weapon in the next stage.
At this moment, Ming Dao's gaze swept over and stared at Phantom. "You bitch! Come here! "
