Chapter 1295
When he explored the Secret Realm, he obtained three exemptions.
If he sacrificed himself with the power of the Sacred Spear, he would be almost invincible.
Not to mention ordinary prodigies, even Daozi could be killed at will after he sacrificed himself to the Sacred Spear of Slaughter.
But this situation should happen in the wilderness …
If Luo Zheng met him in the wilderness, far away from the main city, Luo Zheng would undoubtedly die!
Although the Underworld Dao worshipped him, he would not lose any Fantasy Points.
However, after being played by Luo Zheng just now, Ming Dao could not hold back.
In fact, his best choice should have been to lure Luo Zheng into the wilderness. However, at first, Ming Dao felt that he could defeat and kill Luo Zheng with the Sacred Spear. After falling into a passive position, he had lost this opportunity, so he chose to sacrifice himself at that time!
This approach was undoubtedly stupid. The main city was next to him. Even if he sacrificed himself and his strength soared, it was an impossible task to kill Luo Zheng in the main city.
The final result was that the Sacred Spear was seized by Luo Zheng …
At this moment, the knot in Ming Dao's heart could be imagined. At this moment, seeing Phantom standing silently not far away, he naturally had to find someone to vent his depression!
Phantom, who was not far away, heard Ming Dao's shout, and her body suddenly trembled.
"Don't go," Ji Nan whispered.
A complicated look flashed on Phantom's face, and she stood still.
"Bitch! Don't forget where you are now! "Ming Dao stared at Phantom and said coldly, a smile even appearing at the corner of his mouth.
Phantom was still in his palace. In the Illusory Battlefield, Ming Dao might not be able to do anything to her, but once she left the Illusory Battlefield, she was just a toy to him …
Phantom pursed her lips, and a sad look appeared in her eyes.
Although Phantom did not explain, with Jin Hai, Ji Nan, and the others' vision, how could they not see that Phantom was helpless?
In the end, Phantom moved away and walked toward Ming Dao …
The smile on Ming Dao's face grew wider and wider. Previously, the dispute between him and Luo Zheng was because of the Phantom. He had suffered a loss because of Luo Zheng, but Luo Zheng could do nothing to him in the end.
When Phantom was halfway through, Luo Zheng stood between them again and stared at Ming Dao with calm eyes.
"What do you think?" Ming Dao's voice carried a hint of sarcasm.
"Let her go," Luo Zheng said calmly.
"Sure." Ming Dao laughed heartily. "Give me back my spear!"
A trace of hesitation appeared on Luo Zheng's face … He really wanted to make a copy of the Holy Spear of Slaughter.
At this moment, Ming Dao also saw that although Luo Zheng looked indifferent, he actually valued his companions very much. Although Phantom had only gone to the Karma Hall with him once, Luo Zheng would not stand idly by.
"Hahaha …" Ming Dao's gaze fell on Phantom again, and he ordered, "Take off all your clothes. Let's have an erotic dance for everyone's entertainment!"
Hearing Ming Dao's words, many onlookers suddenly showed a touch of excitement in their eyes.
The women of the Demon Night Clan were all noble and elegant. It was rare to see their naked bodies.
Phantom gritted her teeth, "Ming Dao! I … "
"Remember, you are not my woman. You are just my plaything. Remember your identity. You have no choice!" Ming Dao's smile became more and more intense.
In Ming Dao's view, Phantom had no room to resist at all. This was the case in the outside world, and it was the same in the Illusory Battlefield!
The reason was very simple. The Holy Spear Sect had not only fallen into his hands, but also many of Phantom's relatives. With Ming Dao's status, it could be said that he controlled Phantom, including the life and death of the people around her. Phantom had no room to disobey his orders.
Phantom gritted her teeth, glanced at Luo Zheng, and asked softly, "Luo Zheng, will you return to the Demon Night Clan with my King?"
My King was naturally referring to the King of Slaughter supported by the Holy Spear Sect, Xun.
Hearing this question, Luo Zheng was slightly stunned. Phantom was a woman of few words. Although he could guess the general situation like Jin Hai and Ji Nan, it was impossible for him to guess the ins and outs of the matter. After all, Luo Zheng was not a prophet.
"Six years ago, I had always hoped that Luo Zheng and Xun could return …"
"To be able to rescue me from that damned Dark Winter Palace …"
"I am still very young. My skeletal age is only 28 years old. I still have a chance to become a heaven's pride of the Demon Night Clan. I don't want to be Ming Dao's plaything. My talent … is better than his!"
"But I also know that a few years is too short. It's impossible to return so quickly …"
"I might … not be able to wait until that day …"
As Phantom spoke, tears welled up in her eyes.
She was not the only one who was looking forward to it. The entire Holy Spear Sect was enduring humiliation as they waited for the day when Xun returned.
Phantom was very clear about the consequences of disobeying Ming Dao today. When she left the Illusory Battlefield in the future, her fate would be very miserable.
But Phantom also understood that although Ming Dao seemed to be making things difficult for her, he was actually using her to threaten Luo Zheng.
Phantom now understood that she did not want to affect Luo Zheng in any way. At this moment, she wanted to get an answer from Luo Zheng.
Looking at Phantom's current expression, Luo Zheng felt a soft spot in his heart being touched.
Helping Xun return to the throne was still a mess. Although he went to the Asura World, sent people to infiltrate the Demon Night Clan, and then killed Xun to tell him a secret hidden in the stars, Luo Zheng was trapped in the Immortal Mansion and could not do anything for six years!
He did not know that there were so many people in the Demon Night Clan who had such great expectations for him …
Luo Zheng stared at Phantom and reached out to wipe away the tears on her face. He nodded and said, "I will!"
Just these two words made Phantom stop crying. After she nodded, she rubbed her eyes and walked towards Ming Dao. There was an almost resolute sneer on her face. After sniffing her delicate and tall nose, she said, "Ming Dao, in fact, I have always wanted to tell you that I think your talent in martial arts, if it weren't for your father, is almost a waste …" She paused for a moment and continued, "When you are in bed, you are even more useless."
After saying that, a relieved smile suddenly appeared on Phantom's face. She smiled very gently, as if she was relieved.
She had endured enough over the years.
She was a person of few words, but she had said so much in a row.
The Demon Night Clan was much more bold and unrestrained than the Human Race when it came to matters between men and women, so her words were unrestrained.
Regardless of whether the style was bold and unrestrained, as a man, the most intolerable thing was for a woman to expose his shortcomings in front of so many people. Moreover, Phantom was just one of his many playthings!
"Pfft …"
Ji Nan, who was not far away, blushed slightly, but she could not help laughing.
As for the many warriors around, they all had some strange expressions on their faces …
This was a dignified genius, a Divine Sea Realm warrior. How could she not be good in that aspect?
Jin Hai put his hands together. His face was calm, but the corners of his mouth were slightly raised.
As for Cang Mo, he could not help but burst into laughter. The laughter from his rough throat was very harsh.
Ming Dao's face stiffened. He stared coldly at Phantom, wishing he could cut her into pieces with his gaze. In a fit of anger, he actually laughed, "Hahaha … Phantom, I didn't expect that after letting you enter the Illusory Battlefield, you would grow up! Very good, very good … Do you know what the price will be? "
"Death," Phantom said calmly. At this point, her wish seemed to have been fulfilled by Luo Zheng's promise. Anyway, she was tired of living like this and being a plaything of this kind of person.
"No," Ming Dao shook his head, "How can I let you die so easily? You should know the most terrible punishment of my Demon Night Clan!"
Phantom replied faintly, "The Eternal Night Punishment."
"Yes, I will throw you and your entire clan into the Eternal Night Punishment … I will let you experience an unforgettable torture! Hahaha … "Ming Dao's emotions were abnormally agitated. He roared wildly. He could only use threats to vent his anger.
Luo Zheng sighed softly in his heart. He could only blame himself for not being strong enough. The sooner he became strong, the sooner he could return to the Demon Night Clan with Xun …

Chapter 1296
This was the voice of the mysterious girl.
The rules of the Illusion Battlefield had been announced by this mysterious girl several times …
But now, the rules had suddenly changed.
In the first stage of the Illusion Battlefield, the various main cities could still be protected by martial artists because of the existence of the powerful Heavenly Dao. As long as you didn't leave the main city, you would be safe.
Even if you were a prodigy, the Daozi wouldn't be able to do anything to you …
However, the later stages of the Illusion War, the more intense the competition would be.
The Illusion Points would start to tilt towards a small number of people.
For example, Ming Dao had a large number of Illusion Points and was ranked fourth in this world. As long as he didn't leave the main city, it would be very difficult for others to catch up.
However, at this moment, the rules of the Illusion Battlefield changed.
The main city was no longer a safe haven for martial artists. On the contrary, it would become an Asura Battlefield …
After all, almost all the martial artists were concentrated in the main cities. Only a small number of martial artists would leave the main city to explore the endless wilderness.
Once all the restrictions were removed, all martial artists would be able to fight freely!
At the moment of contact, the little skulls wandering outside the main city began to rush into the city and bite Luo Zheng's back row. Those little skulls were densely packed and formed a cloak of skulls.
Ming Dao was originally laughing wildly, but after hearing the mysterious girl's voice, he was suddenly stunned.
Luo Zheng looked at Ming Dao indifferently, and a warm smile appeared on his face. "Why aren't you laughing?"
Previously, Ming Dao didn't care about his defeat. He had three exemptions in his hands. Even if he was killed by Luo Zheng three times, he wouldn't lose any Illusion Points.
Moreover, after the Sacred Massacre Spear was taken away, he wouldn't foolishly rush out of the city. Luo Zheng wouldn't be able to do anything to him.
Who knew that the battle restrictions in the main city would be removed at this moment?
A chill suddenly swept across the back of his neck. As long as he could maintain his position as the fourth in the world, he would definitely be able to advance to the next stage of the Illusion Battlefield!
However …
After realizing the danger he was in, Ming Dao shot backward almost like a conditioned reflex.
He had already lost the courage to fight Luo Zheng head-on. Now, all he wanted was to escape!
"Huala …"
At the same time, the entire main city began to fall into chaos.
This main city was not the only one in chaos.
In the entire Illusory Battlefield, the fifty great realms would fall into chaos …
The martial artists in every main city began to recklessly kill each other. There were also a large number of martial artists who began to flee in all directions. The main city was no longer their shelter, but a slaughterhouse!
Somewhere in another great world.
His long and tender fingers gently intertwined and waved, and huge swords began to dance along with his fingers at the same time, forming a complicated sword array and descending towards a main city!
Weak, strong, good at hiding, good at surprise attacks …
Eighty percent of the martial artists in this main city had perished under the sword array!
Xi Youqin cocked her head to the side as she looked at the main city that had been reduced to rubble. There was a look of yearning on her face.
"My luck was pretty good. They all hid in the main city and refused to come out. I was about to leave … but the rules changed!"
…
Xi Youqin's act of instantly annihilating an entire main city by herself was rather extreme, but it was not uncommon.
Many experts reacted at this moment and tried their best to kill the martial artists in the main city!
In the outside world, under everyone's observation, the name rankings on the pyramid began to fluctuate violently once again! Countless names kept surging up and down in the pyramid like a tidal wave.
The lower the ranking of the martial artists, the greater the fluctuation.
On the contrary, the names of the martial artists near the top of the rankings fluctuated much less.
After all, the martial artists at the top of the food chain in the Illusory Battlefield were at the top of the food chain.
This change in the rules was not only harmless to them, but it was also very beneficial.
Under such circumstances, the experts could plunder a greater amount of Illusory Points …
Just like before, when Ji Luoxue controlled a main city, she could only collect two Illusory Points for each martial artist she targeted.
If she collected too many Illusory Points, they might as well not leave the city. After all, if they were killed by Ji Luoxue once they left the city, it was equivalent to giving her free Illusory Points.
However, at this moment, Ji Luoxue did not need to collect Illusory Points. She would start plundering the entire main city at an extremely fast speed. In any case, no one could stop her.
However, this situation still depended on which main city it was. For example, the core main city that Luo Zheng was in was the exit of the Karma Hall. There were countless experts there. Apart from Ming Dao, there were also a few other prodigies.
No one could easily kill 80% of the martial artists like Xi Youqin. Even Luo Zheng could not do it …
"Huan Ling, Ji Nan … Protect yourselves." After Luo Zheng finished speaking, his figure suddenly shot out and chased after Ming Dao!
Ming Dao retreated at an extremely fast speed. After retreating several hundred feet from Luo Zheng, he flipped over and sped into the sky. However, Luo Zheng's reaction was not slow at all. Almost at the instant that Ming Dao rushed out, he followed closely behind.
Although Luo Zheng had taken away this person's Sacred Spear, he did not take away his Illusory Points. At this moment, Luo Zheng only had one thought in his mind, and that was to completely plunder all of Ming Dao's Illusory Points!
…
Ji Nan, Jin Hai, Cang Mo, Huan Ling, and An Si quickly gathered together at this moment!
The martial artists who had originally planned to watch either started fighting or fled like Ming Dao. Many of them quickly flew into the sky.
The group of cultivators who reacted the fastest had succeeded. They had escaped into the vast wilderness to seek refuge for the time being.
As for the martial artists who reacted slightly slower, they were intercepted and killed. After contributing half of their Illusory Points, they slowly began to "revive" in a corner of the main city.
Ji Nan and the others were not ordinary after all!
If the five of them worked together, perhaps even Luo Zheng would be helpless.
Except for a few geniuses in the main city, they would not encounter any natural enemies here. To put it bluntly, they were the only ones who could plunder other people's Illusory Points. No one could plunder them!
Streaks of sharp light dissipated in all directions, and strong waves of True Origin attacked. This kind of scene could only be seen in a racial war. Although there had been rumors over the years that many races in the universe would definitely fall into chaos and launch an unprecedented global war, this war was the real battle of the world!
At this moment, in the Illusory Battlefield, it was a rehearsal in advance.
On the other side, Luo Zheng did not let Ming Dao escape too far away. When Ming Dao escaped to an altitude of thousands of feet, he was intercepted by Luo Zheng. Luo Zheng deliberately took the Sacred Spear of Slaughter in his hands. Holding the spear in one hand and the sword in the other, he looked at Ming Dao with a faint smile on his face. "Should I kill you three times? Or five times? You choose one? "
If he was killed once, he would lose half of his Illusory Points. If he was killed twice, he would lose three-quarters. If he was killed three times, he would lose seven-eighth …
Ming Dao's face was gloomy. If he was really killed by Luo Zheng three times, his ranking would probably fall out of the top 100,000 …
That was a loss he could not accept. With a flip of his hand, he took out a purple spear again. This was a first-grade divine artifact, and it was Ming Dao's life weapon!

Chapter 1297
At this moment, Ming Dao's face was livid. He stared at Luo Zheng, thinking about how to deal with it.
According to the prediction of the powerful figures in the clan, the first stage of the Illusory Battlefield was about to end!
The first stage would eliminate most of the warriors. In each big world, perhaps only 50 people could enter the second stage!
In the 50 big worlds, there were a total of 2,500 people …
There were trillions of warriors in the whole world, and only a few would survive. These 2,000 people were the elites of the elites in the Divine Sea Realm. They were either the descendants of some big worlds, the sons of Celestial Venerables, heavenly talents, or Daoists!
These predictions were based on the fragmented information left behind in the last round of evolution.
But they were still mostly correct.
Just like how Ming Dao knew about the existence of these main cities and the Karma Hall before he entered the Illusory Battlefield!
In this Illusory Battlefield, Ming Dao's journey was smooth sailing. Without too many accidents, he climbed all the way to the fourth place in the big world.
This ranking was more than enough for him to enter the second stage!
There might be people who could push him down a few places, but it was impossible for him to fall out of the top 50.
He could choose a place with good scenery in the main city to sleep …
However, he didn't expect that the rules of the Illusory Battlefield would suddenly change. The powerful figures in the clan didn't predict that people could kill each other in the main cities. Maybe it wasn't recorded in the historical records, or maybe the records were incomplete.
Now Ming Dao was really regretting it.
If it weren't for the Phantom, he wouldn't have provoked this damn Luo Zheng!
And because of his impulsiveness, the Sacred Spear fell into Luo Zheng's hands. What could he use to fight against Luo Zheng?
"Whoosh!"
From his Sumeru Ring, a blood-red light shot out. That was his natal magic treasure, the Flying Crane Spear. It was a first-grade divine weapon. In the hands of an ordinary warrior, it was a precious treasure. But compared to the Sacred Spear of Slaughter, it was naturally thousands of miles away.
Even if Ming Dao knew that he couldn't defeat Luo Zheng, as a heavenly talent, how could Ming Dao be tied up without a fight?
Besides, if he used his full strength, he might not lose to Luo Zheng!
"Blood Crane Dance!"
As the Nether Dao spear swept out, rays of blood-red light spread out, and mixed with countless spear shadows, shot straight at Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng's figure flashed slightly, and he activated [Eight Paths of Flying Smoke] with a calm expression on his face …
Ever since he had played Go with Chen Huang, his understanding of swordsmanship had reached a new level.
Not only had Luo Zheng's swordsmanship improved, but his thinking in the battle had also improved qualitatively. For example, when he saw Ming Dao's spear sweeping toward him, he could calmly determine the best way to deal with it!
But this time, Luo Zheng did not draw his sword!
Instead, he raised the spear in his left hand and aimed it at Ming Dao.
Luo Zheng wasn't good with guns!
But when one cultivated to Luo Zheng's level, they could use grass, trees, and flowers as weapons. In the eyes of mortals, they could use all kinds of weapons …
Moreover, the Holy Spear of Slaughter was originally very powerful. When he held the Holy Spear of Slaughter in his hand, there was a huge will in the Holy Spear of Slaughter that was connected to Luo Zheng. It was this will that transmitted a very hungry feeling, desperately asking for something from Luo Zheng!
"Huh?"
When Luo Zheng obtained this spear before, he had only placed it in his Sumeru Ring. He didn't expect that after activating the Holy Spear, there would be such a mystery.
The Holy Spear was alive …
It was like a person who had been hungry for three days and three nights and was eagerly waiting for Luo Zheng to feed it.
"This Holy Spear of Slaughter was owned by the kings of the Night Demon Clan. I'm afraid that it's not just True Essence that drives this Holy Spear, but also the power of faith …"
"I don't have the power of faith, and I don't have a trace of True Essence in my body. I can only try using Chaos Qi."
Luo Zheng extracted a wisp of Chaos Qi from his body and circulated it through his meridians, pouring it into the Holy Spear of Slaughter!
"Crackle …"
When Luo Zheng poured the Chaos Qi into the Holy Spear of Slaughter, the Holy Spear of Slaughter suddenly emitted the sound of frying beans.
Opposite him, Ming Dao's hand bloomed with several spear lights, but how could Luo Zheng, who was using Eight Winding Smoke, be easily pierced by him? Every time, Luo Zheng was able to dodge Ming Dao's attack by three inches …
At this moment, Ming Dao heard the Holy Spear of Slaughter erupt and was slightly stunned. He stared at the Holy Spear of Slaughter …
"What's going on with this spear?"
However, Luo Zheng's face was also full of confusion …
He only felt the moment the Chaos Qi poured into the spear, the Chaos Qi was completely devoured by the spear!
"Not enough?"
Although he didn't know what had happened, Luo Zheng understood that this Holy Spear of Slaughter was definitely a glutton. Even with the Chaos Qi's condensed form, it still wasn't full?
Fortunately, Luo Zheng's Chaos Sea was not small. This time, the Chaos Qi was continuously poured into the Holy Spear of Slaughter …
At this moment, Luo Zheng didn't think too much. Feeling that the Chaos Qi was enough, he lightly shook the Holy Spear of Slaughter, held it with his left hand, and stabbed it towards Ming Dao!
"Whoosh …"
Using the Chaos Qi, a huge spear shadow appeared on the side of the Holy Spear of Slaughter.
This Single Thought was the Holy Spear of Slaughter's spear skill. It didn't need to cultivate any special martial skills. It could be directly used with the Chaos Qi!
Whether it was the size or the degree of its condensed form, this huge spear shadow far surpassed the Single Thought that Ming Dao had used before!
After noticing the spear shadow, Ming Dao's eyes flashed with shock. This Luo Zheng's Single Thought spear skill was more than a hundred times stronger than his? And this was the first time that Luo Zheng had used it …
For a moment, Ming Dao thought that his eyes were playing tricks on him!
Why?
Ming Dao was naturally aware of the characteristics of the Sacred Massacre Spear. Even though he did not know why the Sacred Massacre Spear had exploded, the spear shadow that was created with a single thought was related to the amount of vital essence that was poured into it.
Even if Ming Dao poured all of his True Essence Sea into it, it was impossible to achieve such an effect!
"This, this … is impossible!" Ming Dao roared.
"Nothing is impossible." Luo Zheng smiled slightly and suddenly thrust the Holy Spear of Slaughter toward Ming Dao!
The majestic illusory spear shadow spun wildly and strangled toward Ming Dao!
Previously, Ming Dao's Single Thought could leave a small hole on the city wall, and Luo Zheng's spear shadow could probably destroy the city wall under the protection of the Heavenly Dao's rules.
Facing this spear, Ming Dao couldn't resist at all!
"Run!"
This was the only thought in Ming Dao's mind at this moment.
However, he couldn't escape in the end, and Luo Zheng wouldn't let him escape either!
The huge spear shadow directly swallowed Ming Dao and disappeared completely in the blink of an eye.
Then, a red light spot flew toward the main city below with Ming Dao's soul. Although the rules had changed, cultivators could still be resurrected in the main city.
"There are still no Fantasy Points. Ming Dao has three exemptions … If I want to obtain his Fantasy Points, I have to kill him three times first!"
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng followed the red light spot. As long as Ming Dao was resurrected, he would kill him again!
The battle in the main city was already unusually intense …
Many cultivators in the main city were experts who had returned from the Karma Hall, and there were also a few prodigies among them. Now that everyone was fighting in the main city, one could imagine the destruction caused.
Under the protection of the Laws of the Heavenly Dao, all the buildings in the main city were originally intact, but in less than an incense stick's worth of time, the huge main city had been reduced to a pile of rubble.
"Boom!"
Bam!
"Huala …"
All kinds of nomological powers flew in all directions. If one wasn't careful, they might die without knowing who killed them.
In the streets of the main city, circular balls of light appeared. They were cultivators who had been resurrected after being killed.
Near these balls of light, there was a group of cultivators standing guard. When the cultivators who had been killed were resurrected, they would instantly attack and kill them again …
Some of the weaker martialists fell into this vicious cycle. They were resurrected, killed, resurrected, and killed again. The fantasy points in their bodies were also exhausted in the repeated deaths.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1298
Luo Zheng flew into the main city, and the aura he emitted attracted the attention of many martial artists.
Fear appeared on the faces of these martial artists …
His fight with Ming Dao was still vivid in their minds. This person was definitely not an existence that ordinary martial artists could provoke.
However, Luo Zheng's gaze swept past these people and passed over their heads …
The Fantasy Points in these martial artists' bodies ranged from a few hundred thousand to a few hundred. Luo Zheng naturally had no interest in them. At this moment, he had already set his sights on Ming Dao.
The red dot flew over and landed on the west side of the main city. When Luo Zheng arrived, he happened to see the red dot land and expand, turning into a round ball of light as tall as a person. Ming Dao would reconstruct himself within it …
Even with the power of the Heavenly Dao, it was impossible to revive so many martial artists at the same time and reconstruct their bodies.
All the martial artists who entered the Illusion Battlefield were currently enveloped by the light of the Heavenly Dao. They only needed to project their bodies again.
However, Luo Zheng did not know why the equipment they carried with them would not be projected after reconstructing the projection …
He still held a sword in one hand and a spear in the other, guarding one side of the ball of light.
After a short while, the ball of light that enveloped Ming Dao began to fluctuate, and a figure stepped out of the ball of light!
When the figure stepped out, Luo Zheng smiled and suddenly thrust the spear in his left hand …
Ming Dao's face was full of misery. He had no chance to escape or fight back. The moment he stepped out of the ball of light, he was killed by Luo Zheng without any resistance!
Whoosh …
This time, Ming Dao still did not produce any Fantasy Points.
The red dot of light, including Ming Dao's soul, flew toward the other side of the main city.
"I've killed this person three times. His three immunity tokens should have been used up." Luo Zheng swung the spear in his hand and chased after the red dot again!
Not long after, Ming Dao resurrected from the ball of light again. Without exception, he welcomed Luo Zheng's spear again!
The three Immunity Tokens that Ming Dao had obtained through a great opportunity had all been used up. After being killed by Luo Zheng, he was unable to keep the fantasy points in his body!
"Buzz buzz buzz …"
When Luo Zheng took back his spear, a sea of dreamy light spots suddenly exploded!
The dreamy light spots were overwhelming and eye-catching. When the martial artists scattered around saw so many dreamy light spots, their eyes went wide.
If they could absorb these dreamy light spots, they might be able to instantly enter the top 100 of this great world!
However, Ming Dao had been killed by Luo Zheng, so his Fantasy Points were directly handed over to Luo Zheng. If others wanted to obtain these Fantasy Points, they could only kill Luo Zheng. At this moment, it was fine if Luo Zheng didn't come to plunder their Fantasy Points, but who would dare to plunder Luo Zheng's Fantasy Points?
Luo Zheng's gaze didn't stop at these Fantasy Points. A red light spot burst out from Ming Dao's corpse and continued to fly toward a corner of the main city. At the same time, Luo Zheng also followed.
Killing Ming Dao once only squeezed out half of his Fantasy Points, but Luo Zheng planned to squeeze out all of Ming Dao's Fantasy Points!
As for the sea of dreamy light spots, they were like thousands of little fairies that followed behind Luo Zheng …
Ming Dao was wrapped in the ball of light, and his face was pale.
He had a total of 70 million fantasy points in his body!
After being killed by Luo Zheng, he had lost half of them and only had 30 million fantasy points left!
With a thought, he checked his ranking. The overall ranking of the Fantasy Battlefield was 2,400, and he was ranked 47th in this great world!
Fortunately …
He didn't fall out of the top 50!
While he was rejoicing, Ming Dao felt an even bigger headache.
The problem was that Luo Zheng was still chasing him. If he was killed again, he was afraid that he would completely fall out of the top 50 and have no chance to enter the second stage of the Fantasy Battlefield!
As a prodigy, he couldn't even squeeze into the top 2,500?
This was no longer a matter of shame …
After stepping into the ball of light, he stared at Luo Zheng and gritted his teeth. "Luo Zheng, don't go too far!"
However, Luo Zheng didn't care about Ming Dao at all. He still had a faint smile on his face. The long spear in his hand pierced out again, and Ming Dao was once again swallowed by the spear shadows produced by One Thought …
"Pfff!"
At this moment, a huge amount of dreamy light spots burst out from Ming Dao's body again.
However, compared to before, the number of dreamy light spots had decreased by half. There were only about 17 million dreamy light spots left, and Ming Dao only had 17 million dreamy light spots left. It was certain that he would fall out of the top 50.
"Whoosh …"
Luo Zheng still didn't intend to let Ming Dao go. He chased after him again, preparing to kill Ming Dao for the sixth time.
"Luo Zheng, if you are willing to let me go, I can let Phantom go!" Ming Dao roared loudly this time.
With Ming Dao's status, he might not be able to control the Sacred Spear Sect's position. However, he could control Phantom's fate. He realized that Phantom was the only bargaining chip in his hand!
Although he had fallen out of the top 50, as long as he kept these 10 million fantasy points, he still had time to catch up!
"Pfff!"
Luo Zheng still didn't respond and pierced Ming Dao's body with his spear again.
"Can I really let Phantom go …" Ming Dao said weakly.
"No need," Luo Zheng said lightly.
Luo Zheng didn't believe in such a person's promise. He didn't believe that his compromise could be exchanged for the other party's tolerance.
Some grudges were unavoidable. He would bring Xun back to the Fiend Night Clan to settle the score.
After the sixth kill, Ming Dao only had 8 million fantasy points left in his body …
"Continue …" Luo Zheng's eyes flashed, and he flew away again.
After the seventh kill, Ming Dao only had 4 million fantasy points left in his body …
After the eighth kill, 2 million fantasy points …
After the ninth kill, 1 million fantasy points …
After the tenth kill, 500,000 fantasy points …
Towards the end, Luo Zheng no longer cared about how many fantasy points he could obtain from Ming Dao.
He only killed the Underworld Dao experts time and time again to wash away all the fantasy points on him.
After killing Ming Dao for the twelfth time, he only had around 100,000 fantasy points left. At this moment, Luo Zheng finally stopped killing Ming Dao. As for Ming Dao, his fantasy points had been plundered from 70 million to around 100,000. He had fallen from fourth place in the Great World to around 80 million. At this moment, Ming Dao could only be described as wanting to cry but having no tears!
"Luo Zheng!"
Phantom's voice sounded behind Luo Zheng.
Ji Nan and the others appeared together with Phantom.
In the chaotic battle in the main city, the five of them huddled together. Not only did they not die, but they also obtained a considerable amount of fantasy points.
Others might not dare to kill them, but they could kill others …
The 'sheep', who had lost the protection of the Laws, surrendered their fantasy points at an unprecedented speed without any leeway to resist.
Luo Zheng nodded faintly at Phantom. "I have already washed away Ming Dao's fantasy points."
"I saw it." A relieved expression appeared on Phantom's face.
Luo Zheng stared at Phantom and asked, "Are you mentally prepared?"
Phantom hesitated for a moment before saying, "Even if it's the Eternal Night Punishment, I will bear it. I will try my best to live. I … still want to see that day."
The pain of the Eternal Night Punishment was simply indescribable. Those who were not from the Fiend Night Clan would not know how terrifying it was!
"That day won't be too far away," Luo Zheng said.
Hearing this, Phantom's eyes shone brightly.
What people were most afraid of in this world was not pain or setbacks. Pain could be endured, setbacks could be overcome, but what people were most afraid of was having no hope.
Just like Liu Yu, who was on the verge of collapse and had been imprisoned for a long time, only with hope would there be the motivation to live. What Luo Zheng could give them now was his promise, which was the hope they looked forward to the most.
Luo Zheng was not a person who would break promises.
Every time he made a promise, the pressure on him would increase.
But things had already come to this point. Regardless of whether he made a promise to others or not, he would take this step …
At this moment, Luo Zheng checked his ranking again. After checking, he was slightly taken aback.
His ranking in the Dream Battlefield had finally rushed into the top 100, ranking 77th.
And in this large world, he was ranked second? He had squeezed out all of Ming Dao's fantasy points, leaving only 100,000 fantasy points for him. Luo Zheng also had a lot of fantasy points!

Chapter 1299
At this stage, the ranking competition in the Illusory Battlefield had reached its most intense moment.
The names on the pyramid were constantly vibrating, and the attention of the outside world was also unprecedentedly huge …
"Lie Qianhan actually jumped to first place! Unbelievable, didn't he drop to the 200th place previously? "
"Xuanyuan Chenfeng also returned to the top three …"
"What's that Purple Conch thing? I have never heard of this person, but he actually entered the top ten … "
The top 100 in the Illusory Battlefield were the subject of everyone's discussion.
After all, the entire Illusory Battlefield was divided into 50 large worlds. At this stage, the geniuses of the 50 large worlds were still unable to meet each other. Therefore, the strength of the top 100 could not be directly measured. They were all the top experts of each large world.
Ning Yudie stared at the name on the pyramid while transferring her True Qi to Yang Kai.
Seeing Luo Zheng's name always made her feel at ease.
As she understood the Illusory Battlefield, she understood more and more what kind of state her husband was in now, and what kind of future he would have.
These top 100 geniuses were the favored children of heaven and earth. Even the gods had to take care of them. Any one of them would be valued by the various large races, because the future Dao Children would be born from these people. The True Gods of the universe would also be born from them …
Previously, Ning Yudie had heard of the Battle of the Great Era from Luo Zheng.
Then what was this Battle of the Great Era?
Not only was Ning Yudie unclear, but Luo Zheng himself was also not very clear.
Now, she believed that this Battle of the Great Era was the Divine Dao!
However, Ning Yudie did not know that this understanding of the Great Era was very narrow.
In fact, not only Ning Yudie, but the majority of the martial artists in the universe were also not very clear about this.
They only knew that after the Great Era descended, geniuses were like a fountain, and ordinary geniuses were not qualified to be called geniuses …
What was a genius? Being different from the masses, possessing incomparable talent and ability, this was a genius.
When the number of geniuses in the Great Era increased, then everyone was not considered a genius.
In the current universe, the true geniuses could only be the geniuses of the various large powers and races.
These people had all opened the Mahayana Lotus Flower. If this Lotus Flower was perfected, they would become Dao Children. This was the possibility of becoming a True God.
Not only did these people have the possibility of becoming True Gods, but they also carried the fate of the universe.
As the Battle of the Great Era continued to evolve, the crisis in the universe would erupt sooner or later. This kind of crisis was something that every cultivator and every living being in the universe could not avoid.
Every generation of Yan Ji was like this.
In the last evolution cycle, the danger that erupted in the great world was the fall of the stars and the arrival of the fire devil.
In the last cycle of evolution, the crisis that broke out in the world was the God's Punishment Plague …
The crisis that erupted in the Evolution Era before that was the fall of the stars …
One Era of Evolution was 3.6 billion years old, and before that was 10 billion years ago. There was no longer any historical data that could be found. The martial artists in the universe could only trace back to three Era of Evolution, and this history was still incomplete.
After all, after each cycle of evolution and extinction, all civilizations would disappear, and the things that could be passed down to the next evolution era were few and far between.
"Which force does Xi Youqin belong to? Why is she ranked sixtieth? "
Ning Yudie's face was a little gloomy. Naturally, she was not convinced that Xi Youqin was better than Luo Zheng.
"This Xi Youqin seems to be very unfamiliar, but I heard that this girl seems to have some background. She seems to be from the Extreme Purple Realm," someone replied.
"The Extreme Purple Realm, huh? The Extreme Purple Yin Constitution, so that's how it is." Although Ning Yudie knew that Xi Youqin had the Extreme Purple Yin Constitution, and that the giant swords that she used to control were very powerful, she did not know that the Extreme Purple Realm was so powerful in the Upper Realm that they could cultivate Xi Youqin to enter the top hundred of the universe?
"Whoosh!"
However, at this moment, a whip shadow suddenly lashed toward Ning Yudie!
"Smack!"
The whip shadow exploded in front of Ning Yudie's face!
Although it did not hit Ning Yudie, the strong wind brought about by the explosion of the whip shadow was like a knife that attacked her!
At this moment, faint ice crystals appeared on Ning Yudie's face. These ice crystals were arranged according to a certain distance, forming a special protective layer of vital energy.
"Crack …"
The blade-like strong wind cut the ice crystals, and the surface of these ice crystals cracked, revealing Ning Yudie's angry face.
If it were not for the protection of these ice crystals, her face would have been cut into pieces. Although there was a way to restore her appearance, she would have suffered a lot of damage!
"You, what are you trying to do!"
Ning Yudie suddenly stood up and glared at the middle-aged woman not far away with a face full of anger.
Although she was serving as a laborer here, pouring her vital energy into the illusory space of the Jade Pure Sacred Ground for free, she was not a slave of the Jade Pure Sacred Ground!
The middle-aged woman who was holding the whip not far away stared at Ning Yudie with a cold smile. "I've said it before, no noise is allowed here, but you've done it again. This whip is just a warning. If there's a next time, it won't be just this whip."
"I was only discussing in a low voice. It won't affect the pouring of vital energy. Besides, someone else is also talking. You … Why are you targeting me?" Ning Yudie asked coldly.
"Heh." The middle-aged woman held the whip in her hand as a disdainful smile appeared on her face. "Someone else? You're just an Ascended cultivator who has come from the lower realm to serve as a laborer. What right do you have to compare yourself with someone else? A slut like you … "
At this moment!
A black broken blade spiraled over and silently flashed a few times in the air. It then fluttered next to the middle-aged woman!
The broken blade was extremely fast and almost unavoidable. It instantly cut off the middle-aged woman's right arm!
Puff!
As blood sputtered out, the middle-aged woman let out a squeal like a pig being slaughtered.
As the martial artists who were pouring their true essence saw this, their complexions didn't change much. But, they all stood up and stared at the scene in front of them.
To a martial artist, losing an arm wasn't strange at all. But, if someone dared to injure someone here, that meant there was a problem. This person definitely had a considerable status within the Jade Pure Holy Lands.
After all, in front of the great hall of the dreamland space, even the Holy Lords of the Holy Lands had personally come to observe. How could an ordinary person dare to do anything here? Wasn't that the same as seeking death?
"Who is it? Why did you injure me!? "
The middle-aged woman clutched her arm, her eyes filled with bewilderment. This middle-aged woman could be considered an intelligent person. She knew that the person who dared to cut off her arm was not someone she could afford to provoke!
But, she hadn't done anything wrong, so why did they want to cut off her arm? Based on what?
The middle-aged woman couldn't accept this and thus opened her mouth to question this person!
Before long, a person walked out from the void. This person had a Divine Transformation realm cultivation. Many sharp-eyed martial artists recognized this person. This person was the Ninth Palace Master of the Jade Pure Holy Lands. She could be considered a top 100 ranked character of the Jade Pure Holy Lands!
"The reason I cut off your arm is to teach you some manners." The Ninth Palace Master coldly said. Then, she turned to look at Ning Yudie. Her cold face seemed to melt away as if it had met the sun, replaced by a warm smile. "Is this … Miss Ning?"
Ning Yudie was stunned. This person's cultivation was unfathomably deep, so why was she being so polite to her?
After she ascended, she had been despised everywhere and treated unfairly. Regret filled her heart. If she had known this would happen, she would have never ascended. In the Jade Pure Holy Lands, the Divine Sea realm wasn't anything at all!
"I am Ning Yudie." Ning Yudie truthfully replied.
The Ninth Palace Master kindly smiled. "Miss Ning, can you come with me?"
Being so polite, Ning Yudie had a strange expression on her face. But, she still nodded. "But, I still have to pour my true essence into this place …"
"Miss Ning's words are too polite. There is no need to mention this matter again." The Ninth Palace Master smiled.
Thus, Ning Yudie was led away by the Ninth Palace Master.
Under the watchful eyes of everyone present, the Ninth Palace Master personally brought away a woman who had just ascended. Naturally, everyone began to discuss this.
"Just where did that woman come from? She was actually taken care of by the Ninth Palace Master? "
"Look at how beautiful she is. Could some great figure of the Jade Pure Holy Lands have taken a liking to her?"
"What's the use of being beautiful? Not to mention those great figures, even you and I have many beautiful women by our side. "

Chapter 1300
Ning Yudie followed behind the Ninth Palace Master, feeling a little uneasy in her heart.
With her intelligence, how could she not guess what was going on?
She was just an ascender with no background in the higher realms. What good thing would happen to her?
There was only one possibility, Luo Zheng.
But after some understanding, she also knew about Luo Zheng's current situation.
He was indeed very famous in the universe, but this fame didn't come from any power. Moreover, he seemed to have offended many big powers …
Although Ninth Palace Master was very polite, she wasn't sure if this was a good thing or a bad thing.
Ning Yudie was more afraid that she would fall into the hands of Jade Pure Sacred Land and be used as a bargaining chip to threaten Luo Zheng. This was what she didn't want to face …
But she didn't have any room to resist!
She followed Ninth Palace Master around the illusory space and came to the plaza in front of the hall.
At this moment, all the core figures of Jade Pure Sacred Land were gathered here.
32 Palace Masters, various Palace Masters, Deacons, Elders …
The auras emitted by these people were abnormally strong. The weakest was at the Divine Transformation realm. There were even a few martial artists whose strength was unfathomable.
Ning Yudie was at the Divine Sea realm. Those she couldn't see through were at least half-step Sector Lords.
But under Ninth Palace Master's lead, these people all gathered their gazes on Ning Yudie …
"Is this woman really Luo Zheng's wife?"
"Luo Zheng is also an ascender. It isn't impossible for him to marry and have children in the lower realms. But the chances of him impersonating her are higher!"
"Impersonating? Hehe, wait until the Sacred Lord uses her Spirit Heart God Art to check. There's a price to pay for impersonating! "
Seeing Ning Yudie being brought here, the martial artists in the plaza began to discuss.
The Illusory Battlefield had reached its climax. They were especially concerned about the overall ranking.
The strongest core genius of Jade Pure Sacred Land, Shen Qing, had already entered 70,000 and his ranking was still rapidly climbing!
According to everyone's expectations, Shen Qing being able to rank around 100,000 was already a very impressive result. But now, Shen Qing had already rushed to 70,000. This naturally surprised the higher-ups of the Jade Pure Sacred Land.
After all, they were only a tenth-grade Holy Land, and there were hundreds of thousands of tenth-grade Holy Lands like them in the universe.
Of course, Shen Qing's results couldn't be compared to Luo Zheng's.
With Luo Zheng's current ranking, the entire Human Alliance would place great importance on him!
After all, less than a hundred out of the three thousand major worlds of the Human Dao Alliance had made it into the top ten thousand.
Under the gazes of so many powerhouses, Ning Yudie felt a little unnatural.
In the end, she followed Ninth Palace Master to an old man in a Daoist robe.
This Daoist robed old man sat on an immortal rattan chair. His gaze was fixed on the pyramid at the top of the Dream Space until Ninth Palace Master bowed deeply towards the old man. "Sacred Master, I have brought the person."
The Daoist robed old man was the Jade Pure Sacred Master. He was the master of this tenth-grade Holy Land.
After hearing Ninth Palace Master's words, the Jade Pure Sacred Master slowly withdrew his gaze. Perhaps it was because of the cultivation method he cultivated, but the Jade Pure Sacred Master looked exceptionally old. His movements were also incomparably slow. However, within his wrinkled eyes, there was a pair of bright eyes.
He glanced indifferently at Ning Yudie, then said in an aged voice, "Are you really Luo Zheng's wife?"
The cultivator by Ning Yudie's side was originally a grassroots cultivator. However, he was still a cultivator from the higher realms who had been born and raised in the Jade Pure Sacred Land.
After learning of Ning Yudie's background, he took this as an opportunity and used his connections to report it to his superiors.
He didn't expect that this matter would actually be reported by the Jade Pure Sacred Land's higher-ups. The cultivator who reported it also promised that if Ning Yudie's identity was confirmed, he would be given a decent reward.
Ning Yudie replied, "I'm here to find my husband."
The Jade Pure Sacred Master nodded slightly. "Look into my eyes …"
The moment Ning Yudie focused her gaze, she felt that she couldn't pull her gaze away from the old man's eyes. It was as though her entire body had sunk into them.
This was a Soul Divine Power!
Ning Yudie instantly came to this conclusion.
However, with Ning Yudie's cultivation, how could she resist the Soul Divine Power of a Sacred Master?
However, other than feeling her gaze being attracted, Ning Yudie didn't feel any discomfort.
After a while, the Jade Pure Sacred Master closed his eyes, and Ning Yudie's body suddenly trembled. Then, she let out a sigh of relief.
The other higher-ups of the Jade Pure Sacred Land also stared at the Jade Pure Sacred Master. The Jade Pure Sacred Master slowly said, "Luo Zheng ascended to the Spirit Channeling Realm of the Human Dao Alliance and was sent to the Thirteen Palaces to cultivate … He's considered a member of the Human Dao Alliance. Since you're Luo Zheng's wife and have the opportunity to ascend to the Jade Pure Sacred Land, I naturally have to take care of you. However … back then, Luo Zheng was sent directly to the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace. If you're willing, I can send you to the Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace."
Although the Jade Pure Sacred Master was old and clumsy, he could see the situation very clearly.
If Luo Zheng hadn't climbed to the top of the pyramid, he wouldn't have asked about this matter. At most, he would've sent someone to investigate and casually settle Ning Yudie down.
Although Luo Zheng's reputation had been great before, he'd only displayed the power of the immortal estate. Now, Luo Zheng had completely proven his strength and was ranked seventy-seventh in the Dream Battlefield.
With the background of the great world, if this child were to mature, he would definitely become an extremely important figure in the Human Dao Alliance.
If he could improve on this ranking, he would only need one more step to become the main character of this great world ….
The significance of this would be extraordinary.
Then, his achievements would surpass Celestial Sovereign Mirage Sea's achievements in the past.
Taking care of Ning Yudie now was just a meager effort for the Jade Pure Sacred Land. However, if they could gain Luo Zheng's goodwill, even a fool would do such a deal.
"The Cloud Mist Heavenly Palace?" Ning Yudie asked curiously.
The Jade Pure Sacred Master nodded. "Yes, that's the cultivation sacred land of the Human Dao Alliance. Only the most outstanding cultivators can set foot there."
"I'm not going …." Ning Yudie shook her head. "I think I'll stay here and wait until Luo Zheng arrives."
Hearing Ning Yudie's answer, the Jade Pure Sacred Master smiled faintly. "You can stay here. If the battle in the Dream Battlefield ends, I can help you send out a roar command to summon Luo Zheng. However, you know Luo Zheng's current situation. The major races aren't willing to let him go. He's trapped in the immortal estate, and I'm afraid he won't be able to escape in a short time."
"I understand." Ning Yudie nodded. "Then I'll thank you in advance, Sacred Master."
The Jade Pure Sacred Master said politely, "It's nothing. You can also enter the Jade Pure Martial Hall to cultivate. Ninth Palace Master, she'll be under your palace …."
Hearing the Sacred Master's words, Ninth Palace Master's face lit up.
This kind of thing, anyone would be willing to do. It was just a matter of accepting a disciple from the Imperial Palace. Regardless of Ning Yudie's aptitude, everyone knew that Luo Zheng was a person of great importance. If they could make him owe them a favour, it would definitely not be a bad thing!
Moreover, the Sacred Master had already used his Spirit Heart Divine Ability to sense her and confirmed that this woman was Luo Zheng's first wife.
A martial artist could have many women, but he could only have one wife, and she was probably a very important existence to Luo Zheng.
"But I don't want to cultivate now." Ning Yudie's gaze landed on the pyramid. "I want to watch from here …."
"It's a piece of cake." Ninth Palace Master smiled and found a good spot for her in the square ….
From this moment on, Ning Yudie was considered an official disciple of the Jade Pure Sacred Ground. She had also stepped into the core of the Jade Pure Martial Hall!
Previously, when Ning Yudie heard about the Jade Pure Martial Hall, she also looked forward to it. She was even wondering if her husband was qualified to enter it. But now, the Sacred Master just knew about her relationship with her husband and casually placed her there.
At this moment, Ning Yudie felt as if she had fallen into a dream ….
What did her husband do to make them value him so much?
She naturally didn't know about Luo Zheng's performance at the Torch Flame Inheritance. She also didn't know that the several Heavenly Venerables of the Human Dao Alliance were silently watching Luo Zheng's every move!
"That little fellow …" Looking into the distance and looking at Luo Zheng, who was ranked 77th, a gratified look appeared on Ning Yudie's face. "Unknowingly, he has already reached such a stage."
Thinking back to when she first met Luo Zheng, he was only in the Xiantian Mystic Realm, she felt very emotional.
＝＝＝＝＝
